# 351: Chapter 348: vs Lance!

"Hello, I'm D, I've heard a lot about you."

D walked up to Lance and greeted him.

The surprise on Lance's face slowly faded, and he politely replied, "Hello."

The short Nemona jumped up excitedly, shouting, "Battle! Battle! Battle!"

Lance looked at Nemona, seeing the little girl so enthusiastic, he couldn't help but ask with a smile, "Do you also like Pokemon battles?"

Nemona nodded, grinning broadly, showing her big white teeth: "That's right! I can't battle you just yet, but Mr. D will battle you for me!"

The thought of a Champion Level battle is enough to get anyone's blood pumping!

Lance smiled and then looked at D, asking in a casual tone, "Would you like to battle me?"

"May I?" D nodded reservedly, Lance didn't seem as serious or unapproachable as he appeared.

"Of course you can." Lance's gaze turned to the red Pokemon beside D, "Is it also a Dragon Type Pokemon?"

Born into a Dragon Master family, he had a unique intuition for Dragon Type Pokemon.

D: "That's right, it's called Koraidon."

Lance: "I knew it, it must be a very strong Pokemon, so, Dragon Emissary Lance, now accepts your challenge!"

"I'll be the referee!" Nemona immediately raised her small hand high and eagerly led the way for the two.

D couldn't help but smile, and then followed Nemona with Lance to the previous battle area.

A trainer's battle is like this, even if two strangers have never met before, as long as they reveal their identity as trainers, they can engage in an exciting Pokemon battle at any time.

Nemona took the small flag from the hand of a waiter and then stood in the center of the battlefield, mimicking a referee's tone:

"Next up is a Pokemon battle between Dragon Elite Lance from the Kanto Region and Mr. D from the Paldea Region!"

"The rules for this battle are 6v6, with each person limited to using six Pokemon. The battle ends when all Pokemon on either side lose their ability to fight!"

Hearing the noise from this side, the surrounding trainers, who had been chatting away, gathered around.

So when Alder arrived with Boss Clay and Nikar, they saw D already standing on the battlefield with Lance.

Alder was a bit surprised: "Oh? It's already starting?"

Boss Clay looked at the red-haired youth with interest: "That's the Champion from the Kanto Region, Lance, right?"

Nikar looked at the lively Nemona jumping around the field like a Rillaboom with helplessness: "That girl... She's so lively."

Alder didn't forget the main matter, he pointed at D across from Lance, and said to the two:

"Look, that's him, D. It's thanks to him that my Volcarona was able to receive timely treatment."

Upon hearing this, the two cast curious gazes in that direction.

They then saw the tall, steadfast, and handsome black-haired youth alongside his red Pokemon.

Nikar doesn't understand Pokemon battles; he's only focused on the electronic device called a phone.

But Boss Clay showed a look of interest, a trainer praised by Alder... this person is unusual!

On the field, after Nemona finished introducing the match rules, she suddenly shifted topics and excitedly said to Lance:

"Mr. Lance, don't underestimate him! And don't take the match lightly just because Mr. D isn't well-known! Mr. D, like you, is very skilled with Dragon Type Pokemon! He has lots and lots of Dragonites on his ranch too!"

D: "..." The social butterfly Nemona nearly exposed everything about him.

Hearing this, Lance showed an expression of surprise. He looked at D opposite him, his eyes grew serious, and he said to Nemona:

"Thank you for the reminder. I will give it my all."

"Hehe!" Nemona smartly smiled and then formally announced: "The first match has officially begun!"

Originally, D just wanted to compare Dragonite with Lance: "..."

But now, it seems that's not going to work!

This clever little Nemona surely wants to see more exciting Elite Level and Champion Level Pokemon battles, so she set the rules to 6v6.

D sighed silently.

But, it doesn't matter!

Since he's here, he might as well have a great battle! Using his past life experiences and the efforts of his Pokemon!

Thinking of this, D's eyes slowly became determined.

In the first match, Lance may not send out his ace Pokemon, Dragonite.

D wasn't sure which Pokemon Lance had, so he thought for a moment and decided to let Pawmot go up first to test the waters.

Both nearly simultaneously threw out their Poke Balls.

Two beams of white light flashed simultaneously, a large, fierce-looking Gyarados appeared on the battlefield.

And on the left side, there was a cute-looking Pawmot with adorable pink fur, holding a small unassuming hammer, and wearing a Red Cloak.

Seeing this scene, the surrounding trainers couldn't help but exclaim in surprise.

---

"What kind of Pokemon is that? It looks so cute, is that a weapon it's holding?"

Another Trainer showed an incredulous expression: "A Pawmot from the Paldea Region? No! How can this be?"

"What do you mean?" someone asked quickly.

The person explained: "As far as I know, a normal Pawmot's fur is yellow like Pikachu's, and it doesn't have any cloak on it, nor does it use weapons. Most importantly, a normal Pawmot doesn't fly at all!"

The Trainers present weren't novices. As soon as they heard this, they immediately understood the person's meaning and looked at the Pawmot with disbelief.

"It's here! D's ace Pawmot!" Nemona exclaimed.

On the field, D and Lance almost simultaneously gave their commands.

"Gyarados, Twister!"

"Pawmot, Electro Ball!"

Gyarados roared, Dragon Type Energy surged wildly around its body, transforming into a terrifying Twister that swept towards Pawmot.

Pawmot's Red Cloak rustled in the wind, it quickly shifted its position, cleverly dodging the Twister, then rubbed its left hand to create a ball of flashing Light Ball, and threw it towards Gyarados.

Gyarados was so large that it couldn't avoid the rapidly flying Electro Ball.

The fourfold restraining Light Ball instantly dealt a heavy blow to Gyarados.

Lance frowned slightly, the attributes really weren't in his favor.

He had never seen that Pokemon before, from its recent performance, it seemed speed was its advantage, plus its small size, unless using wide-range moves, it was hard to hit.

After a brief thought, Lance decisively switched from offense to defense, to gather more intel on that Pokemon: "Leer! Stay alert!"

Changing tactics, huh? D still chose to attack: "Spark!"

"Pa-mo!"

Pawmot's body surged with sizzling Electric Type Energy, it swung the small hammer in its hand vigorously, charging towards Gyarados.

"Hydro Pump!" Lance immediately shouted, trying to prevent the Pokemon from approaching.

With the command given, Gyarados opened its wide mouth, a powerful Water Type Energy gathered inside, then transformed into a massive beam shooting out.

Pawmot anticipated this, flickered its body, and dodged the Hydro Pump in time.

The strongly charged electricity hit Gyarados heavily.

Electric Type Energy instantly spread across Gyarados's entire body, pierced through its scales, burrowed inside it, like a thousand words were frantically gnawing in its flesh.

Gyarados's face was full of pain, and its massive body crashed to the ground with a boom.

Is it over?

The spectators were full of disbelief.

That was Lance from the Kanto Region! He was defeated by this opponent that came out of nowhere in just two rounds!

What is this guy's background? How come we've never heard of him before?

"Gyarados's type was too severely restrained." Boss Clay remarked with a tsk.

Alder nodded in agreement, eyes shining with anticipation: "This is just the appetizer, the real show is yet to come!"

On the battlefield, after waiting for about thirty seconds, Gyarados still hadn't stood up.

Nemona ran up to check on Gyarados's condition, then announced: "Gyarados is unable to battle! Victory for Pawmot in the first match!"

"Pa-mo!"

Pawmot flew back to D's side, celebrating with a high five.

Lance remained calm, he returned Gyarados to the Poke Ball, then took out a second Poke Ball:

"The battle is not over! You are a worthy opponent, from now on there's no need to say anything, let's use battle to decide who's stronger!"

"Come on!" D nodded, just about to let Pawmot continue to fight, when the Poke Ball containing Ogerpon on his waist suddenly shook a few times.

Immediately afterward, Ogerpon voluntarily came out of the Poke Ball, and expectantly: "Bo-nie!"

Pawmot was slightly stunned: "Pa-mo?"

Ogerpon looked at it, eyes like stars, filled with anticipation: "Bo-nie Bo-nie!"

"Pa-mo~" Pawmot understood, and then returned to D's side.

D was a bit surprised: "You want to battle too?"

Ogerpon nodded happily: "Bo-nie!"

It mimicked Pawmot's movements and cheerfully ran onto the battlefield.

"Wow! A Pokemon I've never seen before!" Nemona's eyes lit up.

The spectators all focused their gaze on Ogerpon.

"It's another Pokemon we've never seen before!"

"Hey! Just now, that Paldea guy, is this Pokemon also from your region?"

"No, it's not! I've never seen this Pokemon either!"

"Does this mean it's a Pokemon that humanity hasn't discovered yet?!" someone exclaimed in shock.

"Mr. D, what's this Pokemon's name?" Nemona asked excitedly.

"Ogerpon, its name is Ogerpon." D replied with a smile, looking at Ogerpon's back.

"Bo-nie~"

"Ogerpon, huh?" Lance also showed interest, "In that case, I'll use this Pokemon!"

As the words fell, Lance tossed out his Poke Ball.

Accompanied by a flash of white light, a Charizard with fiery bright eyes and a scorching atmosphere all over its body appeared on the battlefield.

---

"Looks like we're about to face some type matchups!" Boss Clay said from a distance.

The green Pokemon, obviously Grass Type, had no advantage against the flying and Fire Type Charizard.

However, in the next moment, everyone saw the Pokemon make a strange move.

It fiddled with its green coat, then unexpectedly pulled out a sorrowful, teary-looking blue mask and put it on its face.

Immediately after, people saw the Pokemon's body change from green to blue.

Boss Clay: "???"

Alder: "???"

Lance: "?"

Nemona opened her mouth in shock: "What's going on? Did its form change?"

D couldn't help but face-palm, wondering if Ogerpon's transformation right in front of everyone might be a bit inappropriate...

But it was too late, the battle had officially begun.

Facing the Grass Type Ogerpon, Lance decisively commanded: "Flamethrower!"

D wasn't very sure of Ogerpon's actual combat power, but recalling how it managed to defeat four opponents single-handedly, Ogerpon definitely must be strong.

Therefore, D tentatively ordered: "Use Thorn Stick?"

"Boni!"

Ogerpon immediately responded.

It joyfully swung its small hands, and soon a large, thorn-covered stick formed in its hands.

The Thorn Stick was enveloped in a thick Water Type Energy.

Facing Charizard's Flamethrower, Ogerpon excitedly brandished the Thorn Stick and charged forward.

Witnessing this scene, D's heart skipped a beat.

Too aggressive!

However, in the next moment, a miracle occurred.

Ogerpon swung the Thorn Stick in front of it, continuously deflecting the scorching Flamethrower, turning them into fiery snakes that were dissipated by the Water Type Energy of the stick.

Charizard, seeing this, was shocked and unsure if it should continue its attack.

Lance frowned: "Increase the firepower!"

Charizard immediately steadied itself, boosting its Flamethrower output.

Yet Ogerpon just barreled straight through the flames, dodged aside at the right moment, and raised the Thorn Stick high, catching Charizard completely off guard.

"Boni!"

With a bang, Ogerpon hit Charizard square on the head with the stick.

After enduring such a heavy blow, Charizard collapsed to the ground, its pink tongue hanging out helplessly, rendered unable to battle.

Everyone: "???"

Boss Clay: "Did it end in just one turn?"

Wasn't there supposed to be a type matchup? What move was that? How did it defeat Lance's Charizard in one stroke?

Alder's eyes gleamed brightly; he knew he hadn't misjudged them!

Seeing this scene, everyone except Alder couldn't help but be stunned.

What had they seen?

A single swing took down Charizard?

That Pokemon seemed so cute, why was it so violent? What was its origin?

"Boni Boni!"

Having won the battle, Ogerpon gleefully put away the stick, ran back to D's side seeking praise, and asked with starry eyes:

"Boni?" Wasn't it amazing?

For a moment, D didn't know what to say, so he simply patted Ogerpon's head, now reevaluating its strength.

D glanced at Lance across from him, noting that Lance hadn't yet regained his composure.

After a long time, Lance came to his senses, his face showing a serious expression, "Wow... So strong! D, you're really strong!"

---

---

---

---

# 352: Chapter 349: Meowth's Hammer Shows Its Divine Might!

"Whoa! The match is getting more and more exciting!" Nemona expertly took on the role of referee: "Charizard is unable to battle, Ogerpon wins. Mr. D, will you switch the Pokemon for the next match?"

D glanced at Lance and said, "Switch!" Just as well to let Flutter Mane, from ancient times, experience modern Pokemon battles.

"Then, the third match is about to begin!" Nemona raised her flag high.

Meanwhile, Professor Oak, who had been chatting idly with other researchers on the other side of the party, was invited over by several Trainers.

They said there were many unseen Pokemon here and wanted Professor Oak to take a look and introduce these Pokemon to them.

Initially, Professor Oak was a bit confused.

But as he squeezed through the crowd, he saw several Pokemon he had never seen before beside one of the Trainers.

A blue Pokemon wearing a mask, bouncing in place,

A mysterious Pokemon with pink fur, holding a small hammer and wrapped in a cloak,

A completely crimson, muscular unknown Pokemon,

And that mysterious Pokemon with fierce eyes, similar in shape to Misdreavus but much larger.

"What Pokemon is that?!" Professor Oak was shocked: "Is it an exaggeratedly developed Misdreavus?"

But no one around could answer him.

Although everyone could see some connection between that Pokemon and Misdreavus, it was obvious that its appearance was much different from the Misdreavus they were familiar with.

That was not Misdreavus.

That Pokemon is...

"Flutter Mane, it's up to you!" D called out.

"Mm..." Flutter Mane opened its mouth, responding silently.

Its gaze fell upon the unfamiliar Pokemon with a gray body and a fierce face standing opposite.

The third match, Flutter Mane vs. Aerodactyl, officially begins!

This time, Lance made the first move: "Aerodactyl, Scary Face!"

"Awoooo!"

Aerodactyl opened its blood-red maw, revealing the fierce visage of an ancient predator, and an intangible pressure instantly swept across the field.

Flutter Mane felt as if it had returned to the feeling it had long ago when facing Koraidon, which had seized its territory.

Flutter Mane was overwhelmed with pressure, momentarily intimidating it into freezing in place.

"Calm down! Flutter Mane!" D quickly called out to remind.

Lance wouldn't give it time to adjust: "Aerodactyl, Ancient Power!"

"Awoooo!"

Aerodactyl roared, and purple-glowed rocks instantly appeared around its body.

Under its control, these rocks shot towards Flutter Mane like meteorites.

Seeing this, D hurriedly said, "Flutter Mane, dodge! Phantom Force!"

Flutter Mane finally snapped out of its frightened state, but because of the negative impact of Scary Face, its evasion speed was slightly slower, forcibly taking Aerodactyl's Ancient Power hit.

The pain made Flutter Mane more clear-headed, and also more furious.

It bared its little fangs, growling again and again.

"Power Gem!" D ordered.

Flutter Mane immediately took action, a beam of gem-like radiant light burst from its body, shooting towards Aerodactyl.

Aerodactyl was already wary, it spread its wings wide, then flew straight into the sky, avoiding this Power Gem move.

"Continue!" Lance said gravely.

In the sky, Aerodactyl summoned the ancient power again, and condensing it into several meteorites, hurled them towards Flutter Mane below.

"Phantom Force!" As soon as he saw Aerodactyl's lifting motion, D reacted.

This time, Flutter Mane wasn't affected by any negative status, it promptly executed Phantom Force, successfully dodging Aerodactyl's Ancient Power.

"Stay alert!" As Flutter Mane vanished, Lance immediately reminded.

Aerodactyl vigilantly scanned around, trying to capture Flutter Mane's figure.

However, in the next second, Flutter Mane launched a sudden attack from behind.

Flutter Mane's massive body crashed into Aerodactyl like a small calf, sending Aerodactyl flying and almost out of the battlefield.

Thankfully, it regained its footing at the crucial moment and flew back up.

"Charge!" Lance said gravely: "Use Wing Attack!"

"Beware of dodging!" D immediately reminded.

But in the next instant, he discovered something alarming.

Too late!

Because that Aerodactyl's speed had increased!

Thinking of this, D immediately had an answer in his heart, it was Ancient Power that boosted all of Aerodactyl's abilities!

In mid-air, Aerodactyl's speed had clearly surpassed that of Flutter Mane; its wings were coated with a layer of white light, then suddenly collided with Flutter Mane.

Flutter Mane was directly sent flying.

Aerodactyl's form didn't pause for a moment, immediately launching a pursuit of Flutter Mane.

Ghost Type Pokemon, huh? In that case, use this move! Lance shouted, "Crunch!"

"Roar!"

Aerodactyl let out a roar, then opened its bloody maw, biting hard on Flutter Mane's body.

---

Flutter Mane cried out in pain and fell to the ground in a sorry state.

One second, two seconds, three seconds...

Flutter Mane remained unable to get up, losing the battle against Aerodactyl.

Nemona stepped forward to check on Flutter Mane's condition, then announced, "Flutter Mane is unable to battle! The victory goes to Aerodactyl in this matchup!"

D returned Flutter Mane to the Poke Ball for rest, then admired, "So this is your true strength, Lance!"

Lance maintained his calm and composed demeanor: "The opponent is such a formidable Trainer, if I don't take it seriously, I'll be thrashed through the ranks!"

D: "..." Truth be told, the Pokemon he brought along this time matchup very well against Lance's Pokemon in terms of type.

Ogerpon, Pawmot, Dragonite, Koraidon, Glaceon, Alcremie...

And Dragonite's Pokemon are all Flying and Dragon Types.

If he remembered correctly, during the Master Tournament on his journey, Lance was also knocked out by Diantha's Mega Gardevoir due to type matchups.

However, the battle was not over yet.

Round four, Pawmot vs. Aerodactyl.

After the previous battles, Lance had a preliminary understanding of this unfamiliar Pokemon, Pawmot.

This time, he didn't let Aerodactyl engage Pawmot head-on, but instead used his speed advantage to adopt a guerilla strategy.

"Use Agility! Just like that, keep your distance with speed, then seize the moment to counter with Ancient Power!" Lance calmly commanded.

Aerodactyl was quite lucky; during the two uses of Ancient Power, it triggered a stat boost twice, coupled with the speed increase from Agility.

This made Aerodactyl's flying speed extraordinarily fast now.

Pawmot's Electro Ball and Thunder Shock couldn't hit the opponent at all, with all attacks missing Aerodactyl.

On the field, the two Pokemon chased each other relentlessly, with their stamina gradually depleting.

The scene fell into a stalemate for the moment.

Lance was intensely focused on the situation on the field, ready to order Aerodactyl to use Ancient Power for an attack the moment Pawmot revealed a flaw.

Fortunately, Pawmot's reactions were quick, swiftly increasing its flying speed to dodge the boulders hurled at it.

It seemed Lance had found a method to counter Pawmot! Watching this unfold, D couldn't help but curl his lips into a smile.

However, they weren't entirely without a countermeasure.

"Pawmot!" D shouted aloud, "Mjolnir!"

Pawmot had not used this move before, but upon hearing D, it instantly understood.

"Pawmot—!"

Pawmot's slightly tired eyes immediately filled with fighting spirit as it looked up at the Aerodactyl circling in mid-air.

"A new move?" Lance's expression turned serious, warning, "Stay alert!"

Aerodactyl immediately focused its attention.

At the same time, a strong current erupted from Pawmot's body.

In this moment, all of its Electric Type Energy began to surge madly into the small silver hammer.

A sizzling crackling sound resonated in the air, and in an instant, the metallic hammer shone brightly with sparks.

"What's that..."

The onlooking Trainers immediately shifted their gaze to the hammer.

In the next second, they saw Pawmot hurl the hammer with all its might.

The hammer, enveloped in terrifying currents, spun rapidly towards Aerodactyl.

Aerodactyl's attention had been fixed on Pawmot. As soon as it saw movement, Aerodactyl promptly reacted.

It flashed its body, evading the attack in time.

Upon witnessing this scene, the onlookers couldn't help but feel their hearts in their throats.

The move missed, and the use failed!

Looking at Pawmot, exhausted and panting from using the move, Aerodactyl relaxed its nerves slightly.

Lance also breathed a sigh of relief, observing this scene.

He caught a glimpse of D on the opposite side but didn't see any disappointment on his face; rather, he appeared full of anticipation, as if expecting something.

Lance: "?"

Something was wrong!

Suddenly, he heard D shout, "Now! Retrieve it!"

"Pawmot..." Pawmot's previously tightly clenched little pink fist suddenly opened, reaching forward.

The audience was puzzled, not knowing what happened yet.

However, in the next moment, someone noticed an unbelievable scene.

"W-what's happening?! That hammer is flying back on its own!?"

The other Trainers also cast their gaze in that direction.

They saw the hammer that Pawmot had thrown far away returning at an incredibly fast speed!

Seeing this, Aerodactyl was greatly shocked, instinctively wanting to dodge.

However, this unexpected move struck before it could react, and the returning hammer smashed into its back with a thud.

Aerodactyl was hit instantly, its large body falling forward and then crashing to the ground, losing its ability to battle.

Lance stared blankly at this scene.

"L-lost..."

"What move was that just now?"

"No, I want to know how Pawmot managed to do that! Not only flying but also using a weapon, imbuing Electric Type Energy into the hammer!"

"Unbelievable!"

Compared to the astonished crowd, Alder's expression was much more vibrant.

He clapped enthusiastically and praised, "What a spectacular match!"

At the edge of the arena, Professor Oak finally shifted his gaze from those unfamiliar Pokemon to the trainer.

As a native of Kanto, Professor Oak naturally knew Lance and was aware of his formidable prowess as a Pokemon Trainer.

Yet now, Lance used three Pokemon and only managed to defeat one of the opponent's Pokemon.

This trainer named D is something else!

I really want this match to end quickly; he wants to take a good look at those unfamiliar Pokemon!

On the second floor of the manor, Caitlin, Shauntal, and Cynthia leaned against the terrace, observing the lively scene below.

Seeing Pawmot defeat Aerodactyl, who held the advantage, Caitlin showed a look of surprise.

Beside her, Shauntal, holding a novel manuscript, felt a sudden burst of inspiration.

Perhaps she could write a novel about an originally unknown trainer defeating the strongest champion to become a Pokemon Master!

"Cynthia, what do you think?" Shauntal instinctively asked Cynthia beside her.

Cynthia, gazing at the scene below, softly spoke, "Electric Type, Water Type, Dragon Type... Lance's Pokemon are completely at a disadvantage, but you can tell he hasn't gone all out yet."

After all, Lance's most prized Pokemon hasn't yet appeared.

But...

Cynthia's gaze shifted to the young man on the other side of the field and the mysterious Pokemon beside him, eyes showing a hint of hesitation.

The trainer named D also seems not to have given it his all.

"Giovanni? Giovanni? Are you listening?"

By the garden fountain, a stout, somewhat overweight middle-aged man looked at the cold-faced man before him and called out.

Upon hearing the voice, Giovanni turned his head and coldly nodded, "Hmm."

The middle-aged man smiled apologetically, "Oh, look at my memory, I almost forgot, you are also a powerful Pokemon Trainer; being a Gym Leader must be tiring, right?"

"When in position, fulfill your responsibilities; it's not tiring at all," Giovanni replied with calm confidence, "Only those lacking capability would find such work tiring."

"Oh, that's good, that's good," the middle-aged man quickly replied with a smile, "You truly are remarkable, Giovanni!"

On the battlefield, after waiting approximately thirty seconds, Nemona stepped forward to check on Aerodactyl's condition and then excitedly raised her hand to announce:

"Aerodactyl is unable to battle; this match is won by Pawmot!"

"Pawmo!"

Pawmot was ecstatic and immediately flew back to high-five D.

Across from him, Lance looked helpless.

He retrieved Aerodactyl into its Poke Ball, took a deep breath, and then said to D, "Come on, there's one last battle!"

D was slightly taken aback.

Lance's face broke into a smile, "I'm sorry, I didn't bring all my main Pokemon this time; so far, I have only one Pokemon left that can fight."

Saying so, Lance took out his last Poke Ball.

"This Pokemon has been with me from the start, pushing me toward becoming the strongest trainer, and it is also my most powerful Pokemon."

"Never give up, that's the creed this Pokemon taught me."

"D, you know this too, don't you?"

D looked at Lance opposite him and suddenly had a revelation.

Lance is about to send out his ace!

No need to guess; it's Dragonite!

Knowing this...

D nodded gently, "Yes, I know."

He let Pawmot rest aside and then took out a new Poke Ball from his waist.

"I've been eagerly awaiting this moment for a long time," D said, gripping the Dragonite's Poke Ball in his hand.

Seeing him opt to switch Pokemon again, Lance wasn't surprised; his gaze only became more resolute.

He forcefully threw the final Poke Ball, and in that dazzling white light, a Dragonite with eyes as resolute and composed as its Trainer's appeared before Lance.

Lance's gaze passed over Dragonite's shoulder to the opposite side, then he spoke solemnly:

"Lance, the Dragon Master, is here!"

---

---

# 353: Chapter 350: Twin Dragons, General!

In the villa courtyard, surrounded by people on the battle arena.

Two Dragonites with distinctly different looks stood facing each other, both filled with fighting spirit as they gazed at their opponent.

"Dragonite too?" Lance didn't expect D to send out a Dragonite to battle him in this last match.

The many trainers and the professor watching also showed surprised expressions.

"Two Dragonites, huh…" Professor Oak's gaze wandered over the two Dragonites, thinking this is going to be interesting.

As everyone knows, the Dragon Taming Clan's specialty is the Dragon Type Pokemon.

In terms of breeding and training Dragon Type Pokemon, not many trainers in the world can match them.

But now, another trainer has brought out the same Pokemon to battle the Dragon Elite...

For a moment, Professor Oak didn't feel like researching those never-before-seen Pokemon.

He suddenly became very curious about the outcome of this match.

"Wow wow wow! That guy also brought out a Dragonite!"

On the second floor of the manor, Shauntal, who constantly watched the battle below, shone brilliantly in her eyes, adjusted her glasses, and focused entirely on the coming battle.

Caitlin's eyes showed an interested look: "A champion from the Paldea Region? Cynthia, have you heard of him?"

Beside her, Cynthia shook her head: "I haven't heard of him." Paldea was too far from the Sinnoh Region, and the connection between the two regions isn't as close as Kanto and Johto.

"Then who do you think will win?" Caitlin changed the question.

Upon hearing, Cynthia cast her gaze at the two Dragonites warming up below, then honestly shook her head: "I can't tell, but soon there will be an answer."

Because the little girl serving as the referee had already raised the flag high and then blew the whistle:

"Pheeeew—"

Nemona's eyes gleamed: "The final battle, Dragonite versus Dragonite, officially begins!"

"Dragonite, it's time to test your special training results. The opponent is a famous figure in the Kanto Region, give it your all!" D fiercely extended his hand, pointing at the opposing Dragonite.

"Roar!" Dragonite responded, clearly feeling D's mood, his hard training over the past two years had made a marked difference!

D: "Come on, greet the opponent with Extreme Speed!"

"Roar!"

Dragonite's body instantly turned into a white blur, the accumulated experience over time made it very familiar with this move.

Everyone only saw a flash of white light, and D's Dragonite had already reached Lance's Dragonite.

Lance's Dragonite was on guard in advance, quickly crossing its arms in front to try to defend, but still staggered from the strike.

"Full of fighting spirit, huh?" Lance's expression remained unchanged, taking the chance while D's Dragonite hadn't escaped, he immediately ordered: "Slam!"

"Roar!"

The Dragonite that had retreated several steps quickly adjusted its stance, its wings flapping swiftly, it suddenly charged above Dragonite, a surge of white light erupted from its long tail and then slammed down on Dragonite's face.

With a crisp 'snap' sound, Dragonite retreated several steps, but soon, it stabilized its form and then charged towards the opposing Dragonite.

"Thunder Punch!" D's command intertwined amidst the chaos.

Dragonite clenched its right claw into a fist, the power of thunder rapidly surged, swinging forcefully towards the opponent.

Facing this frontal strike, Lance's Dragonite showed its battle-hardened advantage, it slightly tilted its head, letting the thunder-powered fist brush past its body.

"Strong enough, but lacking battlefield experience." Very familiar with Dragon-type Pokemon, Lance instantly recognized the shortcomings of that Dragonite through the contact just now.

Seeing the Thunder Punch miss, he coolly ordered: "Fire Punch!"

Covered in burning flames, the fist smashed into Dragonite's abdomen, Dragonite grimaced, retreating continuously, breathing heavily.

"No, Dragonite's practical experience gap with the opponent is too big!" Seeing this scene, D frowned slightly.

Dragonite's close combat was clearly no match for the other Dragonite.

He quickly pondered strategies in his mind.

What if using Rain Dance and Thunder? After all, Thunder is guaranteed to hit during rain, if executed properly, it might deliver a fatal blow to the opponent.

"No, no! Lance's Dragonite knows Thunder too… once Dragonite uses Rain Dance, given his experience, he would definitely react immediately, by then Dragonite might suffer a backlash!" D immediately dismissed this idea.

On the other side, Lance didn't plan to give them any breathing room.

"Dragonite, Hyper Beam!"

"Roar—"

With the command issued, that determined-looking Dragonite immediately opened its mouth, a beam filled with a desolate aura rapidly gathered within, and then transformed into a robust beam, shooting out fiercely.

"Quick, dodge!" D was forced to stop thinking, commanding Dragonite to try to evade.

—No, it's not just Dragonite's battle experience, compared to Lance, his own command experience was lacking too!

Dragonite felt a bit nervous at the moment, staring at the oncoming Hyper Beam, instinctively flying upwards.

A second after it left the ground, that Hyper Beam brushed past where it had just been.

Dragonite narrowly avoided the move, swiftly turned back while shouting: "Roar!"

---

D suddenly understood Dragonite's intention; it wanted to use its signature move!

"Not yet, the best timing hasn't arrived!" D quickly shook his head; they need to drain a bit more of the opponent's stamina first!

Otherwise, Thunder won't deal a fatal blow to Lance's Dragonite, and they will surely face a counterattack.

Just like when Ultraman fights the monsters, he always drains their energy before using the finishing move.

You have to save the ultimate move for last!

For now, D kept calm: "Thunderbolt!"

Even though Dragonite didn't understand why D wouldn't let it use Rain Dance, it obediently followed the command, harnessing the Electric Type Energy on its body, unleashing a powerful current, aiming at Lance's Dragonite.

Facing the overwhelming current, Lance and Dragonite's expressions remained unchanged.

"Dragon Dance!" Lance's voice was steady.

"Roar—"

The Dragonite responded, its determined eyes fixed on the incoming current, and then suddenly started spinning rapidly on the spot.

Its spinning speed was so fast that the overwhelming current was deflected away.

Watching this scene, the spectators were slightly astonished.

"Blocked it!"

"Can Dragon Dance be used like this?"

Everything unfolding in the battle area was reflected in Professor Oak's eyes, he murmured: "If this is the full strength of that Dragonite, then it's in danger..."

"Using Dragon Dance to deflect energy-based move attacks?" D suddenly recalled that this technique seemed to have appeared in the anime.

Thunderbolt ended, as did Dragon Dance.

Lance gazed at the other side's Dragonite, gasping for breath, covered in wounds, and squinted slightly: "It's time to end this, Dragonite, unleash Outrage Burst!"

"Roar—"

Upon receiving the command, Dragonite suddenly erupted with an incredibly terrifying aura, its scales bristled sharply, then charged at its opponent.

Seeing this, D's heart set off warning bells instantly, "Beware of dodging! Fly up! Agility!"

Dragonite also sensed the danger, not daring to slack off, and exerted all its strength, using the Agility honed during delivery duties to soar into the sky.

Lance's Dragonite followed closely behind, its attacks devoid of any pattern, but every punch, every collision, was imbued with an unstoppable force.

That is Outrage, a Dragon Type move with a power of 120, and when enhanced by Dragon Dance, once hit, combined with Type Matchups, Dragonite was bound to lose its ability to battle.

Right now, D could only pin his hopes on Dragonite.

As long as it withstands this attack, then that Dragonite will fall into the negative state caused by the excessive use of Outrage.

Then it's the perfect chance for them to turn the tide!

High in the sky, above the manor, two Dragon Type Pokemon were engaged in a high-speed chase.

D watched the scene, his heart in his mouth.

"Come on, Dragonite!"

The airflow surged around Dragonite, its speed maxed out, barely widening the gap with the Dragonite behind.

As time ticked by, everyone attending the banquet was watching the battle between the two Pokemon in the sky.

A storm started brewing.

Suddenly, high in the sky, the wind raged, milky-white air currents surged, a terrifying hurricane was brewing.

Some noblewomen had their hats blown away by the wind, hurriedly covering their expensive evening gowns.

"Is that... Hurricane?"

Some trainers noticed the changing surroundings, their voices filled with shock and confusion.

"But how is it possible?! Why is that Dragonite's Hurricane so exaggerated!"

Lance's Dragonite was using Outrage, which meant that for a while it would only use Outrage.

Which meant the Dragonite using Hurricane must be the one being chased!

Hurricane slightly hindered Lance's Dragonite's pursuit.

D took a moment to glance at Lance across, finding Lance's face equally solemn, seemingly not expecting the situation to unfold like this.

That Dragonite's stamina, speed, and endurance... are too exaggerated!

D guessed Lance's thoughts, grinning widely.

Looks like having Dragonite deliver milk every day paid off after all!

High in the sky, the erect scales on Lance's Dragonite slowly lowered.

Dragonite successfully dragged out the duration of Outrage, flying swiftly utilizing the favorable air currents, occasionally glancing back at its opponent.

This action was akin to taunting Lance's Dragonite, which was already slightly embarrassed and angry after chasing for so long.

Angrily, it unleashed Extreme Speed at its front, crashing into Dragonite.

Dragonite's body stumbled from the impact, plummeting toward the ground.

But luckily, just before hitting the ground, it managed to stabilize itself.

Lance's Dragonite landed in front of Lance right after.

Its body was marked by numerous scars from the fierce air currents earlier, looking quite battered.

Both Dragonites were panting heavily, staring at each other across the battlefield, seemingly back to the beginning of the match.

Watching this, D's eyes sparkled.

It's almost time!

Next, it's time to lure Lance out for the next move!

D shouted: "Dragonite, very tired, right? Hang in there a bit longer!"

---

Upon hearing these words, Lance immediately judged that the stamina of the opposing Dragonite was already nearing its limit.

The chase just now had consumed a lot of its endurance, and now it couldn't respond as quickly as before!

Thus, Lance waved his hand and issued the final command: "Dragonite, give it the final strike, use Hyper Beam once more!"

Hooked!

The terrifying white energy swiftly condensed in Dragonite's mouth.

Seeing this scene, D immediately shouted, "Now's the time!"

Dragonite understood instantly.

Its eyes gleamed with excitement, the usually gentle gaze suddenly became resolute, as it locked onto the white beam coming straight at it.

At the moment it was about to be hit, Dragonite suddenly soared high, flying into the sky.

Witnessing this, Lance's heart tightened, his face showing shock: "How is this possible?!"

How does it have the strength to evade?!

Yet in the next second, the scene he feared the most occurred.

Suddenly, it started raining heavily in the sky.

Some trainers still didn't know what was happening, because Dragonite's Rain Dance had reached a level of mastery, barely needing to lift a hand, just a thought, and it could make the sky rain.

But those with sharp insight realized what was happening.

Professor Oak stared at the Dragonite in surprise, murmuring, "I really didn't expect its mastery of Rain Dance to be so high."

"Boom!"

Thunderclouds surged in the sky, and terrifying lightning brewed madly within.

Too late — Lance's mood instantly sank to the bottom.

D's gaze focused on the opposing Dragonite, a smile finally appeared on his face.

Checkmate.

A deafening clap of thunder exploded, and a thick bolt of lightning fell straight down, the dazzling Spark instantly engulfing the Dragonite below.

Strong electric currents seeped through the gaps in Dragonite's scales, spreading a strong sense of paralysis through its entire body.

Dragonite then lost consciousness, its massive body collapsing heavily, unable to rise again.

At this moment, all the spectators rose in amazement, speechless from shock.

The rain stopped, and the thunderclouds dispersed.

Giovanni, with his suit soaked from the rain, withdrew his gaze and turned to walk toward the interior of the mansion.

On the second floor of the mansion, Caitlin, who had been sitting to watch the showdown, also stood up in shock, murmuring in disbelief:

"He really won..."

Beside her, Shauntal adjusted her glasses, her eyes revealing a glimmer: "Brilliant! He used the strategy of increasing Thunder's accuracy on rainy days?"

Did he not use it from the start because he was worried Lance's Dragonite would also use Thunder?

"That Dragonite's mastery of Rain Dance must have reached a level of mastery beyond imagination. I didn't expect there to be trainers who would have their Pokemon specifically practice this move." Cynthia murmured.

Caitlin nodded in agreement: "Not only that, but Dragonite's stamina is also extraordinary, these strengths just happen to make up for its lack of practical experience, truly astonishing."

"Dragonite is unable to battle, the match is won by Mr. D!"

On the field, Nemona excitedly announced the result of the match.

Truly a Champion Level Pokemon match, she learned quite a bit from it!

She thought about applying these lessons to her own Pokemon later! Nemona thought.

Dragonite happily ran back, with an expression begging for praise.

D smiled and patted its head, then took a bottle of goat's milk from his backpack to feed it: "Well done, now take a good rest!"

"Roar~"

Dragonite tilted its head back and gulped down the goat's milk, then licked the milk residue off its mouth with its tongue, acting like a good baby as D returned it to its Poke Ball.

On the other side, Lance also returned his Dragonite.

He gazed at D with admiration in his eyes.

"I lost... it's incredible! I don't feel sad now..." Lance pondered, a slight smile appeared on his handsome face: "Maybe fighting with an excellent trainer makes me happier than the frustration of losing!"

"Same here!" D felt Lance was quite easygoing and friendly.

He smiled, leading Koraidon and the others forward, taking out several bottles of goat's milk and handed them to Lance:

"Feed your Pokemon with these, they help heal injuries and restore health."

---

---

---

---

# 354: Chapter 351: Rotom Phone, Senior Dragon Enthusiast

A spectacular battle concluded, and numerous guests and Trainers headed to their rooms to change out of their rain-soaked clothes.

Seeing this, D couldn't help but feel a bit embarrassed.

In its excitement, Dragonite forgot to control the range of its Rain Dance...

Fortunately, no one blamed him. Everyone was accustomed to such occurrences and left laughing and chatting.

Lance didn't rush to change his clothes. He released a few injured Pokemon and fed them some bottles of healing milk.

As the bottles of milk went down, the weakened Dragonite and others began to open their eyes, and their injuries started to heal visibly.

"This is..." Lance was slightly surprised. He had never seen a remedy as effective as this.

"It's special healing milk from my ranch, very effective for injured Pokemon," D replied with a smile.

Healing milk... huh? Lance looked at the remaining bottle of milk, slightly dazed.

The transparent glass bottle was adorned with caricatures of three Skiddo, their heads close together, displaying pure, innocent smiles, looking extremely cute.

The bottle was filled with a milky white liquid, gently swaying under the moonlight.

At that moment, a loud, carefree voice called out from the side:

"Hey, hey, hey! You're just giving away such precious milk? I had to pay a high price to get it!"

Lance looked away from the milk and, along with D, turned towards the approaching Alder, Boss Clay, and Nemona's father.

Alder said jealously, "I never got such special treatment."

D: "..."

Volcarona beside them made a sound to remind Alder that it was thanks to the milk gifted by D that it had recovered.

Alder laughed heartily: "Just kidding! Just kidding! Long time no see, Lance!"

Lance nodded towards Alder, greeting, "Same here, Mr. Alder."

"Dad! This is the Mr. D I told you about, who runs a ranch and delivers goods with a whole group of Dragonite!" Young Nemona ran over, jumping into her father's embrace.

Nikar nodded repeatedly: "Yes, yes, of course, I remember."

Not only did he remember, but through Alder he also gained a deeper understanding of this young man.

Seeing that they had finished their battle, Nikar's eyes lit up, finally seizing the opportunity to ask the question he was curious about:

"Hello, D, I am Nikar, the owner of Rotom Technology Company. Are you the author of this proposal about the Rotom Phone?"

"That's right." D's heart stirred slightly, and he asked smoothly, "With the current technology, is it possible to create such electronic devices?"

Although Nikar was very intrigued, he couldn't give a definite answer at that moment:

"I can't decide on the specifics yet. I'll need to return and discuss it with the R&D team before reaching a conclusion."

"I see..." D nodded thoughtfully, not surprised.

Nikar cleared his throat, his voice somewhat excited: "So... are you really going to give this proposal to our company? To collaborate with us?"

This banquet gathered many big bosses, including some from other regions developing electronic devices.

The Rotom Phone has great potential. If done well, their company would certainly have a significant presence worldwide in the future.

D wasn't thinking about those complexities; he just wanted to get to use a smartphone soon, so he nodded: "Because we're all from Paldea!"

Nikar immediately sighed in relief: "I understand. Please leave me your contact information, and once we have results, we'll contact you immediately to discuss your share of the rewards."

D nodded politely, not refusing.

Nikar saw that the Trainers here still had more to say, so he didn't disturb them further and left decisively.

Soon, only D, Nemona, Alder, Lance, and Boss Clay were left.

They all heard the previous conversation, and Alder couldn't help but curiously ask, "Rotom Phone?"

He connected it with the already developed Rotom devices and asked, "Is it similar to those Rotom appliances like the washing machine?"

Beside them, Boss Clay also cast a curious look this way.

D pondered for a moment, then explained: "Yes and no. Unlike a washing machine, it isn't a household appliance but a portable communication device similar to a video phone."

Boss Clay, Lance, and Alder instantly turned curious. Though they were Gym Leaders and Champions, there were still many things in this world they didn't know.

Seeing this, D continued: "Besides basic communication functions, it has many other features, such as online shopping, navigation via satellite signals, and video communication."

Navigation and video communication are easy to understand, but what about online shopping?

Nemo innocently asked: "What is online shopping? Is it shopping on a web woven by Ariados?"

D: "...No, online shopping is about connecting every region via the internet and then using messaging functions to contact people in other areas, allowing you to buy the items you need from them."

---

Nemona's eyes lit up instantly: "Wow! How amazing! So, does that mean I can buy fresh milk and vegetables from D Ranch right at home?"

D found Nemona adorable and couldn't help but reach out to tousle her hair, nodding with a smile: "That's right, when I see the message, I'll ask Dragonite to deliver the items you ordered to wherever you are."

The remaining trio of Alder, Lance, and Boss Clay were still limited by their era, unable to quite picture the scenario D described.

And... having Dragonite deliver goods, what kind of crazy idea is this?!

Boss Clay couldn't resist throwing a bewildered look at D, wondering if the ranch really had that many Dragonite.

However, Alder on the side laughed and explained, "Indeed, I've seen it with my own eyes; many Dragonite fly into town with little satchels around their necks, delivering milk and veggies from the ranch to the townsfolk!"

Nemona also nodded: "Exactly!"

Boss Clay was shocked.

Lance was equally surprised.

His gaze swept over the Dragonite and the Pokemon named Koraidon, finally unable to resist his curiosity, he asked:

"Are you... part of the Dragon Master family as well?"

He vaguely remembered there seemed to be a Dragon Master family over in the Paldea Region too.

D was puzzled for a moment before understanding what Lance meant.

He shook his head: "No, I'm not familiar with any Dragon Master family; it was pure chance that brought me together with those Dragonite."

Lance nodded and didn't inquire further; he got it.

Alder was still amazed by the earlier topic.

He gave it a thought in his mind and then asked:

"If I bought milk over in the Unova Region, could Dragonite deliver it across regions too?"

This brought up a regional security issue, where Dragonite might come to help with deliveries but then be forcibly 'liberated' and seized by Plasma Team as a Pokemon being exploited.

D thought for a moment, choosing not to divulge this matter but instead tactfully replied:

"What I mentioned earlier was just an idea; I don't know if it could be realized."

"Got it!" Alder nodded with a grin: "Though I don't understand this area much, you can contact me anytime if you need help!"

"Oh, by the way, a Rotom Phone must need a Rotom like the Rotom washing machine, right? You can come find me then; I'll take you to Chargestone Cave, where many Plasma State Rotom reside!"

"Then I'd really appreciate that," D said with a smile.

If planning to equip Giratina and the others with Rotom Phones, they indeed needed Rotom Pokemon.

Because a regular Rotom Phone is just a smart phone resembling Rotom in appearance, but doesn't actually contain a Rotom.

Only when a wild Rotom enters does a Rotom Phone become a true Rotom Phone.

Rotom's function acts akin to a smart AI like Xiao Ai or Xiao Du, translating and encoding Pokemon text to communicate the phone's content to Giratina and the others.

Additionally, it can follow Pokemon usage intents for intelligent touch control, allowing them to surf the internet like humans.

Upon hearing this, Boss Clay became deeply interested in this young man before him.

He wanted to say more, but as the host of the banquet, many were waiting for him to attend to them.

Reluctantly, Boss Clay had to bid farewell to Alder and D, turning to converse with other guests.

Alder originally intended to stay with D, but many from the Unova League noticed his arrival and were amazed, leading to a large crowd coming to drink with him.

Seeing this, Alder had thought to decline, but recalling his previous irresponsible departure from the Pokemon League and leaving affairs to the Elite Four, he dismissed that notion immediately.

Now that he was back, it was time to resume his Champion's duties.

Thus, Alder let D remain here while he went to discuss Unova League's current affairs with the crowd.

After a while, Nemona was also whisked away by her father.

Before leaving, Nemona longingly said that once she returned to the Paldea Region, she would go to D Ranch to buy fresh milk and vegetables.

D: "..." Anyone could see that Nemona's motivation lay elsewhere.

Lance didn't leave, though; he looked at Koraidon and Pawmot and asked: "Are they Pokemon from the Paldea Region?"

D looked at his several Pokemon playing in the garden and nodded with a smile: "Yes, they are."

"There aren't such Pokemon in the Kanto and Johto Regions," Lance remarked honestly: "Today is the first time I've seen them, and honestly, at first, I couldn't determine their types."

D pointed to several Pokemon to introduce them: "That pink one is called Pawmot, an Electric and Fighting Type Pokemon."

"The one that resembles Misdreavus over there is named Flutter Mane, a Ghost and Fairy Type Pokemon."

"Here, this one is Koraidon, a Dragon and Fighting Type Pokemon; they are exclusive to the Paldea Region and rarely seen in other regions."

---

Lance's gaze swept over Pawmot and Flutter Mane, finally resting on the muscular and strong Koraidon.

Lance's eyes gradually became eager, as if he were looking at some sacred relic.

D: "?"

Lance praised: "What a perfect and holy Pokemon!"

Koraidon: "Ahh...?"

Lance asked: "D, you also really like Dragon Type Pokemon, right?"

D was momentarily stunned, then glanced at Koraidon and Dragonite, nodding: "Yeah."

Lance seemed to find a fellow enthusiast and then began to share his views on such Pokemon:

"Dragons are sacred legendary creatures. Although they are not easy to catch, if trained seriously, they can be unbeatable all over the world! They're the best companions! But..."

Lance didn't speak too absolutely: "But Dragon Type Pokemon are very afraid of the cold. This is one of their few weaknesses, isn't it? So, whenever winter comes, I always huddle with Dragonite to keep warm."

D: "..."

D understood that Lance was clearly a seasoned dragon enthusiast.

But he really wanted to say that even though Charizard and Aerodactyl have 'dragon' in their names, they aren't Dragon Types.

"Do you also huddle with Koraidon to keep warm?" Lance looked at Koraidon and asked.

D: "In my ranch, we light the fireplace in winter, and the whole house becomes cozy and warm. It's not cold at all; if we huddle together, we'd end up sweating profusely."

Koraidon had once done something like that, sneaking onto the bed while he was sleeping.

The result was that it pressed on the blanket, making him sweat all over but unable to kick the blanket off, nearly suffocating him from the heat.

"I see." Lance finally realized, a slightly embarrassed smile appearing on his handsome face: "I almost forgot, you run a ranch, while I'm often out traveling, so I frequently encounter such situations."

D sometimes envied that kind of life of going on adventures.

He loved the storyline from the anime where Ash runs into a cave with his Pokemon during a snowy blizzard.

Thus, D curiously asked: "Do you hide in caves during snowy days?"

"We do!" Lance reminisced: "Back then, I often thought how great it would be to have a kotatsu that could fit a Dragon Pokemon, so Pokemon and their Trainers could snuggle in for warmth... Haha, how big would that kotatsu have to be? Really hard to imagine."

D imagined that scene and couldn't help but laugh: "That's great."

"Huh?" Hearing his tone, Lance was a bit surprised: "D, haven't you experienced something like that?"

"No." D shook his head: "I've been running a ranch in the Paldea Region. I've hardly ever traveled."

Lance looked at the Pokemon and said: "Then you should try traveling. The feeling of keeping each other warm with your Pokemon in the natural environment is wonderful."

D thought for a moment, then shook his head: "No, my Pokemon at home would miss me."

To gain some, you must lose some. He's already satisfied with his current life.

Plus, he couldn't leave his family behind. Every time he goes out for a few days, he wants to return to the ranch, thinking about the fields, the Pokemon, and whether Teddiursa and Mew have sent any letters home.

Perhaps it's the same feeling as those old people who originally lived in the countryside and constantly think about returning home after moving to the city?

Except those elderly worry about their chickens and ducks not being fed, and their big yellow dog going hungry.

"I see..." Lance could somewhat understand that feeling: "Having Pokemon waiting for you at home, that feeling of attachment is really nostalgic."

"Yes!" D looked up at the night sky, nodded with a smile.

The night sky today was beautiful, the moon was full, emitting a bright and gentle glow.

Many Pokemon on the ranch liked such scenery. At this moment, perhaps they were also looking at the moon above, just like him?

Just then, a middle-aged man walked over.

Spotting that familiar face, a name immediately popped up in D's mind—Professor Oak!

Perhaps because he had just been caught in the rain, Professor Oak had taken off his lab coat, leaving only a round-neck sweater, exuding a friendly and harmonious aura.

Lance recognized Professor Oak and greeted him politely: "Professor Oak."

Professor Oak nodded at Lance and then looked at D with a jovial smile.

"Hello, Trainer from Paldea, I'm Professor Oak from Pallet Town!"

---

---

---

---

# 355: Chapter 352: Misdreavus and Flutter Mane

D, however, had already recognized Professor Oak. Even without memories from his past life, he had seen "Professor Oak's Pokemon Lecture" on TV while at the ranch.

Therefore, D spoke up, "I've seen you on TV! Hello, Professor Oak!"

Professor Oak, hearing this, understood immediately and nodded with a smile. "Those were programs invited by the TV station for me to give lectures."

These programs are meant to introduce Pokemon to children who aren't yet ten years old.

Since he is an authority in the Pokemon Research Realm, almost every region broadcasts this show on TV to guide and educate the young children.

After a brief chit-chat, Professor Oak couldn't wait to shift the topic to the Pokemon beside them.

No Pokemon Doctor could resist these unseen Pokemon.

With D's permission, Professor Oak eagerly moved forward to carefully examine the Pokemon.

"This Pokemon's physique is so strong! It has dense red scales... are those tires on its chest?"

Professor Oak, lying on the ground without any regard for his image, reached out, poked Koraidon's chest airbag, and asked curiously.

Koraidon found this posture too bizarre, and with a look of fright, stepped back two steps and subconsciously transformed into a tall and mighty battle form.

"Gah-siii!"

Seeing this, both Professor Oak and Lance were stunned.

"Its appearance... changed?!"

After the brief surprise, Professor Oak's eyes lit up intensely, showing a keen interest in researching this never-before-seen Pokemon.

D stepped forward helplessly, patting Koraidon on the shoulder, prompting it to obediently revert to its ride form.

D said, "This is Koraidon, a very rare Pokemon in the Paldea region, discovered and named by Professor Sada and Professor Turo of Paldea."

"Koraidon..." Professor Oak muttered, then asked, "Is its name Koraidon?"

"Gah-siii!" Koraidon loudly nodded.

Professor Oak's ears were buzzing from its roar, quickly covering them. "Alright, alright, I know, little Gah-siii!"

Koraidon stopped speaking.

Professor Oak breathed a sigh of relief and said to D with a smile, "It seems to be a Pokemon with a very strong personality!"

Ogerpon and Pawmot chuckled on the side.

Professor Oak then turned his gaze towards them, recalling the battle just now, and asked,

"This Pokemon can change its attributes through the mask it wears, am I correct?"

D nodded, "It's called Ogerpon, and the mask contains a mysterious energy from Paldea named [Tera Energy]. By wearing the mask, Ogerpon can use this power to change its attributes."

"Tera Energy?" Professor Oak felt it was a term he hadn't heard before: "The ability to change a Pokemon's attributes... I didn't expect such power to exist in the Paldea region!"

The distance between Kanto City and Paldea Region is too great, so basically, there isn't any contact between these regions.

Professor Oak knew very little about everything over there.

Seeing Professor Oak looking at it, Ogerpon lively greeted him.

Professor Oak responded with a smile, "Hello to you too, Ogerpon!"

Ogerpon felt even happier, not expecting people here to not dislike it either.

The joy and excitement in its heart made Ogerpon happily circle around them.

Watching this scene, D couldn't help but recall Ogerpon's past experiences, feeling a bit complex inside.

But no matter what, those things were in the past, and what's important is to seize the moment.

At this point, Professor Oak then turned his gaze to Pawmot, dressed like a superhero with a cape and hammer.

Professor Oak had seen this series of movies before, immediately recognizing the outfit.

He greeted with a smile, "Hello there, little hero!"

Hearing a stranger call it a hero, Pawmot froze for a moment, then showed a shy expression.

It quickly flew behind D, feeling embarrassed.

D chuckled, "This Pokemon is Pawmot, and these costumes were specially crafted by me."

Professor Oak said, "Pawmot... hoh, it's so spirited!"

After introducing the Pokemon outside, Professor Oak suddenly turned to check around and asked,

"Where's the Pokemon that looked like Misdreavus earlier? I saw it over here just now."

D paused, then turned to look around, realizing Flutter Mane had disappeared without notice.

He quickly searched the area and asked the Pokemon if they had seen where Flutter Mane went, but they were all chatting with Professor Oak and hadn't noticed Flutter Mane's situation.

This is bad! D frowned.

Noticing the expression on his face, Professor Oak was a bit surprised, "What's wrong? That Pokemon shouldn't have gone far, let's search the manor for it."

---

D: "… No, there's something special about that Pokemon. Its temperament is quite different from other Pokemon. I'm worried it might get scared by something and end up hurting others."

After all, this environment is completely unfamiliar to Flutter Mane.

With a slight pause, D calmly said to Pawmot, "Pawmot, you fly over the manor and see if you can find Flutter Mane. Pay attention to the dark corners."

"Pawmot!" Pawmot nodded and immediately took to the skies.

Though Professor Oak and Lance weren't quite sure what was happening, they said, "Then let's help you search together."

So, the group and the Pokemon began urgently searching throughout the manor.

Meanwhile, on the third floor of the manor, in a dark room far from the noise, a low sobbing sound could be heard, like the cry of a young cub.

Originally feeling a bit hungry and looking for meat to eat, Flutter Mane was drawn to this sound.

The room's window was wide open, and the wind blew the curtains into a constant sway, creating a very eerie atmosphere.

Flutter Mane floated towards the window at the sound, making the crying even clearer.

Scanning the room, Flutter Mane showed a puzzled expression.

The room was clearly empty, so where was the sound coming from?

With that confusion in mind, Flutter Mane drifted into the room.

It seemed as if the sound heard the commotion of someone entering, pausing for a moment, before wailing even louder, as though trying to lure an intruder to investigate.

Being an Ancient Hunter, Flutter Mane immediately sensed the intent behind it.

Clearly, it was a trap.

Its body quietly slipped into the shadows, then stealthily moved behind the European-style sofa.

The next second, Flutter Mane saw there a Pokemon with a dark green body, a hairstyle resembling willow wisp, and an eye-catching string of red beads around its neck.

No, it wasn't too unfamiliar...

Because this Pokemon looked a bit like it! It was like a smaller version of itself!

Though the Pokemon was hiding behind the sofa and crying, its face didn't show any signs of sadness, instead looking mischievous, as if waiting for prey to be hooked.

Flutter Mane silently emerged from the shadows.

The crying immediately stopped.

The Pokemon finally belatedly realized something, and with a swoosh turned its head around. The next moment, two vastly different gazes met.

Flutter Mane: (●_●)

Misdreavus: Σ(°△°|||)︴

The Misdreavus, planning to scare humans with a prank, turned and ran in fright.

In a panic, it knocked over a vase in the room, which crashed loudly to the ground.

Flutter Mane looked calmly at the cub. As a Pokemon that had lived alone for a long time, it had no interest in other cubs of its kind.

It initially didn't want to interfere, but suddenly D's image flashed in its mind.

If it were D, he would surely take this young cub back to care for it, right?

For some time, Flutter Mane had sensed D and Koraidon's efforts in teaching it about this world's knowledge, helping it integrate into this place.

To live up to their trust, it had been diligently learning.

And now, it was time to test what it had learned.

Flutter Mane followed.

When the fleeing Misdreavus looked back, it saw that terrifying creature following closely behind. Misdreavus felt like it was going to wet itself from fear.

Mom! Help!

It quickly tried to float away in the air, hoping to escape.

However, that creature was faster, grabbing it with its clawed head.

Misdreavus was too scared to move.

Flutter Mane didn't move either, pondering how to care for the cub.

After a long while, Misdreavus gradually calmed down. It finally realized that the big creature didn't seem to intend any harm.

Growing bolder, Misdreavus tentatively asked, "Misdreavus?" (What do you want?)

Flutter Mane recalled a program on the thing called "television" it had previously watched with Calyrex at the ranch, "Pokemon World."

It depicted how wild Pokemon met, fell in love, laid eggs, hatched cubs, and cared for them.

What did the Pokemon do in that show, again?

At that moment, Misdreavus's stomach growled.

Terrified, Misdreavus said, "Misdreavus..." (I'm hungry...)

It intended to scare a few humans with a prank, then feed on the fear they generated.

Unexpectedly, instead of attracting humans, it attracted this odd creature.

Hearing this, Flutter Mane glanced at it and remembered.

The first step in caring for a cub is to fill its stomach.

So, Flutter Mane brought out a Berry it had secretly stashed away, and like D caring for other Pokemon, fed it to the little Misdreavus.

Misdreavus felt slightly honored; though it didn't like the taste of the Berry, it ate it anyway.

Seeing the cub eat the Berry, Flutter Mane was satisfied, releasing Misdreavus, while watching its actions intently.

Misdreavus dared not move an inch.

---

At this moment, Flutter Mane suddenly heard the voice of Pawmot calling it.

It looked in that direction, then turned back to signal Misdreavus to follow.

Misdreavus dared not run, as it could sense that this big creature in front of it was a very terrifying presence. If it tried to escape, it might be caught immediately.

Under such circumstances, it was better not to provoke it.

So, when Pawmot led Flutter Mane back in front of D, D saw a timid Misdreavus following behind Flutter Mane.

D was taken aback, "It is?"

Flutter Mane: "Dream."

Professor Oak, observing the two Pokemon's actions, then said with interest:

"This... this Pokemon named Flutter Mane seems to be taking care of this Misdreavus as if it were its offspring."

D was dumbfounded, looking incredulously at the calm Flutter Mane with a small fang revealed: "Offspring?"

Impossible, right? To his knowledge, such Paradox Pokemon as Flutter Mane cannot lay eggs.

But looking at Flutter Mane, it seemed to know about the "offspring" matter?

In that instant, a speculation burst into D's mind.

Could it be that Paradox Pokemon are not conjured creatures, nor fantasy entities created by Terapagos according to human wishes?

Each Paradox Pokemon might have been a real creature, much like the dinosaurs of the Jurassic and Cretaceous periods.

Just that they all went extinct for some reason, with the remaining Paradox Species gradually evolving into their modern form.

Or there's another possibility.

Could these Paradox Species Pokemon not belong to this world at all?

Is there a chance that the Time Machine summoned these Paradox Species from another dimension, other time and space?

And each Paradox Species has its unique egg group, so they can't breed in the game.

Otherwise, the unbreeding Paradox Pokemon would just become extinct themselves.

Professor Oak's gaze was fixed intently on Flutter Mane, murmuring: "Flutter Mane... is this a special form of Misdreavus in the Paldea Region? Fascinating, it seems to be twice the size of a regular Misdreavus."

D also found it difficult to answer this question.

He thought for a moment, then took out a piece of Translation Nuts Cake from his bag and ate it before asking: "Flutter Mane, what's up with this Misdreavus?"

Flutter Mane couldn't quite coherently convey its meaning: "Dream." (Offspring, alone, crying, I help it.)

D looked again at the Misdreavus, which wanted nothing more than to hide far away, and heard it say miserably: "Dream!" (I wasn't crying! I was pr-playing tricks, and this guy came over and caught me!)

D: "..."

He understood, showing a speechless expression, and explained to Flutter Mane:

"It isn't an offspring, even though you look alike, you're not the same kind of Pokemon."

Flutter Mane showed a puzzled expression: "Dream?" (Not, offspring?)

D nodded: "It's called Misdreavus, and it naturally looks like this."

Saying this, D apologized to the little Misdreavus on behalf of Flutter Mane:

"I'm very sorry, it seems there's been a misunderstanding between you and Flutter Mane. It meant no harm, it simply mistook you for a newly born baby and wanted to help you, which is why it acted that way."

Misdreavus, looking quite irritated, saw that this human was reasonable, and its courage gradually grew.

"Dream!" (I'm not a baby! But since you didn't mean it, I'll forgive you!)

After speaking, Misdreavus darted away swiftly.

Flutter Mane didn't stop it, just looked at its retreating figure in confusion, seemingly unable to fully comprehend D's earlier words.

Meanwhile, Lance and Professor Oak, who had witnessed the entire process, were astonished.

Professor Oak: "Were you just talking to the Pokemon?"

D nodded calmly: "This is my ability."

Ability...

Professor Oak immediately thought of the Power of Viridian in the Kanto Region.

It's said that some people born in Viridian City receive the blessing of Viridian Forest.

These individuals have the ability to perceive Pokemon intentions and heal Pokemon injuries.

Lance, on the other hand, thought of the legendary Dragon Power; within the Dragon Taming Clan exists an ancient legend that those who awaken the Dragon Power would naturally be Dragon Envoys.

This ability greatly enhances the affinity with Dragon Type Pokemon, allowing them to be better controlled.

---

---

---

---

# 356: Chapter 353: Humans and Pokemon

Seeing Professor Oak and Lance quickly accept the fact that he can understand Pokemon talking, D couldn't help but mutter to himself that it's indeed the Pokemon World.

It seems like anything could happen in this world.

The evening banquet gradually came to an end. The sound of guests laughing and toasting gradually decreased.

D glanced at the time and found out it was already half-past ten in the evening.

Time really flies, doesn't it?

Alcremie and Pawmo were yawning sleepily. Seeing this, D returned several Pokemon to their Poke Balls to let them sleep.

As the host of the banquet, Boss Clay began to bid farewell to the guests.

Lance and Professor Oak also prepared to leave.

Being the Champion of the Kanto League, Lance's daily work is not easy. Besides handling league affairs, he also works as a righteous inspector investigating Team Rocket's activities across Kanto.

After saying goodbye to D, Lance stylishly flicked the cape behind him and turned to leave into the heavy night.

Professor Oak looked at the Koraidon beside D and invited,

"D, if you have some free time in the future, you can come to Professor Oak's Research Institute in Pallet Town in the Kanto Region to have fun. I'll make sure to host you and your buddy Pokémon well."

Pallet Town?

Upon hearing this, D's heart was moved. That's a famous place in the anime, Ash's hometown! He really would like to visit and have a look.

But thinking carefully, the Kanto Region is on the other side of the planet, and going there would probably take a lot of time, so it's better to plan for another time in the future!

Thinking this in his heart, D politely replied, "Sure, I'll definitely bring the folks from the ranch to visit you then."

Professor Oak immediately broke into a delighted smile.

The Pokemon on D Ranch, they are surely more than those in the Kanto Region!

As a professor who specializes in Pokemon research, Professor Oak simply couldn't turn down such a thing.

If D didn't have his own ranch that could accommodate so many Pokemon, Professor Oak would even want to invite D to transfer the captured Pokemon to the research institute for him to help take care of them.

Professor Oak: "Alright, that's it for today! I need to return to Pallet Town too. In a few days, I have to go to the Johto Region to attend a Pokemon lecture. You should get some rest early too!"

D nodded, watching Professor Oak turn and leave, with a thoughtful expression on his face.

Professor Oak researching Pokemon... an authority in the Pokemon Research Realm...

If I visit him with Calyrex and let Professor Oak upload information about Calyrex to the Pokedex or publish it in the form of a paper,

wouldn't that allow more people to know about Calyrex, thereby enhancing its faith?

The answer is obvious, it's feasible.

Unfortunately, Calyrex didn't come this time. Thinking of this, D couldn't help but feel a bit regretful.

But it doesn't matter, there will be plenty of opportunities to further the influence of the Calyrex Crew in the future.

D turned his head to look around, not seeing Alder's figure, probably off chatting with others.

Finding himself with nothing to do, he leaned casually against Koraidon, waiting for Alder where he was.

Cynthia and Caitlin seem to have already left, and Nemona and her dad are nowhere to be seen.

D looked around, then saw Giovanni, who had changed into a suit, passing by from a distance.

Giovanni had changed into a brown suit, with deep-set features and sharp eyebrows, looking very much like an elite businessman.

Without stopping his steps, he walked away powerfully, leaving the estate straight away.

Watching Giovanni's departing figure, D marveled, "Tsk, such a strong presence!"

"What do you mean by strong?"

At this moment, Alder finally finished his own affairs and approached D.

"Nothing much, just thought that person's presence was really strong." D withdrew his gaze towards Giovanni.

Alder followed his line of sight, seeing only an unfamiliar figure, unable to recognize who it was.

"Are you done?" D asked.

Alder nodded, taking D and leaving the estate, and they boarded a taxi heading to the hotel.

On the way, he asked, "It's over! Are you planning to go straight back to Paldea or stay in the Unova Region for a few more days?"

D thought of the Legendary Pokemon in the Unova Region. Currently, the one he could most easily encounter is Kyurem residing in the Giant Chasm.

After some thought, D asked, "Is Lacunosa Town far from Driftveil City?"

"Lacunosa Town?" Alder looked a bit surprised. "That place is in the northeast of Unova! It's quite a distance from Driftveil City. Why? Do you want to go there?"

D explained, "I once heard stories from Lacunosa Town and found them fascinating, so I want to visit and see for myself."

Lacunosa Town's stories? Alder was initially bewildered but soon had a sudden realization on his face.

"You mean the legend of the carnivorous beast, right?"

It's said that a long, long time ago, a meteorite fell from space, landing in the towering mountains just north of Lacunosa Town.

Inside the meteorite was a ferocious beast, and it's said that at night, the beast would appear in the town, accompanied by a cold wind, snatching away people and Pokemon to devour them.

---

To prevent such incidents from happening again, the residents of Lacunosa Town built walls to enclose the town and established rules prohibiting going out at night, encouraging people to stay indoors.

In modern times, even though some townsfolk no longer believe the story, they still stay home at night and rarely go out.

"Mr. Alder, you know that story too?" D asked.

Alder nodded and replied with a smile, "Before I became the Unova League Champion, I traveled around the Unova Region like those Trainers. What you're interested in must be the man-eating beast from the story, right?"

D asked with interest, "Do you think that beast could be a Pokemon?"

"That, I don't know." Alder shrugged, "After all, no one has ever seen that beast, and some say the story was made up by adults to scare children into going home and sleeping after dark."

D had never visited the real Lacunosa Town, and thinking of the legendary walls around it, he asked, "Is Lacunosa Town really surrounded by walls?"

Alder shook his head, "No, maybe there were once, but they've been dismantled. The current Lacunosa Town is just a normal small town, no different from Porto Marinada Town."

"I see..." D said no more.

The taxi stopped beside the hotel, and Alder looked at D and Koraidon with a smile:

"Go have a good sleep first! Make your decision tomorrow after you wake up!"

"Alright." D nodded, then bid Alder goodbye and took the elevator to his room.

After a simple wash, he lay on the bed, contemplating his next move.
