This trip had already lasted about a week, and if he were to head to Lacunosa Town, the travel and search would take an unknown amount of time.

Most importantly, the north of Lacunosa Town is a vast range of towering mountains, incredibly expansive. If one were to wander around blindly, it would be nearly impossible to find the exact location of the Giant Chasm.

Moreover, the compatibility between Kyurem and the ranch isn't very high...

With this thought, D had his answer.

"Never mind, this trip has already been quite fruitful."

Not only did he learn how to make Pokeblock, but he also sent out the proposal for the Rotom Phone...

If he delayed any longer, it felt like it would take a month to finish everything, and in that time, everyone at the ranch would probably be worried to death.

The food left in the fridge was likely almost eaten up by some Pokemon. Though the Indeedee Sibling were adept at chores and managing the ranch, having them go to town to buy groceries with money was simply too much.

Moreover, the couple of barrels of fruit wine brewed in the cellar last month should be about ready.

He needed to get home.

With an answer in mind, D felt settled, closed his eyes peacefully, and prepared to return to Paldea directly by ship from Driftveil City the next day.

The night passed wordlessly, and early the next morning, D told Alder about his decision.

To this, Alder looked regretful, "I had wanted to show you around Unova, maybe get some Casteliacone!"

D laughed and said, "Next time, perhaps. If I can't come this way, Mr. Alder can visit Paldea."

At this, Alder immediately gave a bitter laugh, "I might not have any time later! Those four people, as soon as they heard I was back, dropped everything on me."

"Caitlin left with Shauntal and Cynthia last night to vacation at her villa."

D: "..."

Why did he feel that compared to Geeta and Alder, Cynthia, the Sinnoh Champion, seemed so relaxed?

Steven too... and let's not even mention Diantha; she's got time to film movies and have afternoon tea.

D patted Alder's shoulder sympathetically, "You've got it tough!"

Alder sighed heavily, "Sigh, being a Champion isn't for the faint of heart. If someone can defeat me, I want to do like you, go back home with Volcarona and live a leisurely life."

D smiled slightly, "Soon, perhaps in a few years, a new Unova Champion will emerge."

Doing the math, Iris was almost going to be ten years old.

"Thanks for the kind words!"

Alder escorted D to the pier, bought him a ticket directly to Paldea, and saw him onto the ship.

With the mellow sound of the horn and puffs of white smoke drifting toward the blue sky, the luxury cruise ship docked at the pier gradually sailed into the ocean.

D stood on the deck, waving goodbye to Alder, "Goodbye, Mr. Alder!"

Alder crossed his arms and watched the ship depart, "Goodbye." My friend.

Once the ship was slowly swallowed by the ocean's horizon, Alder withdrew his gaze.

He looked at Volcarona beside him, warm and radiant like the sun, and his gratitude toward D filled his heart.

Had D not reached out to help them, Volcarona's condition at that time wouldn't have possibly lasted until now.

As if sensing the Trainer's emotions, Volcarona lightly flapped its wings, silently comforting Alder.

Alder closed his eyes, took a deep breath, and when he opened them again, they were clear and bright.

"Whew—time to go, old friend. We have to get serious now and do what only we can do!"

---

The relationship between Trainers and Pokemon is definitely not just about endless battles.

Beyond that, Trainers should focus more on maintaining the emotional bonds with their Pokemon partners.

The first thing Alder did upon returning to the Unova League was to host a conference through the television station for all Trainers in the Unova Region.

In the studio, Alder and Volcarona faced the camera and earnestly shared the story between him and Volcarona.

"...When I saw the Volcarona that had accompanied me for many years become so weak, I felt like my life was about to be destroyed," Alder said.

"The relationship between Trainers and Pokemon is definitely not just about endless battles. From my own experience, I tell everyone that I hope every Trainer in the Unova Region will treat their Pokemon companions well."

"If nothing unexpected happens, they will accompany you for a lifetime, existing alongside you longer than anyone else in the world except your parents."

"Nothing remains strong forever, and the lives of Pokemon are not eternally unweakening, but the time and memories you live with them are everlasting happiness for us."

Alder's words were sincere and heartfelt.

Some Trainers watching the program were moved to tears uncontrollably.

These still young boys and girls, not yet twenty years old, turned to look at their Pokemon beside them.

Thanks to the Champion's insight, they quickly realized the significance of the Pokemon they caught during their journeys.

They will accompany each other, sharing a lifetime together.

This relationship and these bonds, from a certain angle, even surpass those of partners.

The ship D was on had not yet left the Unova Sea Region.

Through satellite equipment in space, the television on the ship successfully received the program signal from the Unova Region.

Looking at Alder and Volcarona on the screen, D and the group of Pokemon beside him were somewhat moved by those heartfelt words.

But soon, D thought of something more interesting.

The storyline in the Unova Region has changed.

Because of his intervention, Volcarona did not die, and Alder not only did not fall, but became more focused on maintaining the bonds between humans and Pokemon.

From his actions today, one can see that the future Alder will surely convey his insights and beliefs to every Trainer in the Unova Region.

"I wonder if, under these circumstances, Plasma Team's [Pokemon Liberation Speech] could still have as much impact as it did in the game..."

Since Alder's strength hasn't declined, he might not lose to N in that belief-staking Champion battle.

It's possible that either Reshiram the Dragon of True or Zekrom the Dragon of Ideals might even choose Alder.

It's truly exciting to anticipate how the future of the Unova Region will unfold!

*

Accumula Town, in front of a convenience store by the street.

A man with fair skin, long green hair, and a black baseball cap stood in front of the store's glass window.

His gaze passed through the glass, watching the television screen on the shop's wall showing the program.

After a moment, the man lowered his head slightly, and the shadow cast by the brim of his hat obscured his face, making it hard to see clearly, before he turned and left.

---

---

---

---

# 357: Chapter 354: Gifts

The year 199 of the League, April 5th.

The ship successfully arrived in the Paldea Region.

White Wingull called out as they flew over the horizon, landing on top of the lighthouse.

Golden beaches, blue seas, and modern skyscrapers...

This is Levincia City, the most technologically advanced, commercially prosperous city in the Paldea Region!

D brought Koraidon to step foot in the city once again, returning to his homeland, making him feel relaxed all over.

Feeling cheerful, he looked around, and said to Koraidon, "Come on! Let's go to the mall and buy some gifts to bring back for everyone!"

"Aga~" Koraidon replied joyfully.

This time, when coming back, D didn't call the ranch to notify them. He's planning to give everyone a surprise.

Through bustling streets, D headed straight to a skyscraper.

This is the most bustling and lively mall in Levincia City.

From furniture appliances to fruits and vegetables, to clothes and daily necessities, even things for Pokemon can be found here.

D had visited here several times before and already knew this place like the back of his hand.

Before going to choose gifts for everyone, he first went to the third floor of the store, where he bought a Berry blending machine for making Pokeblock.

Then he took the elevator all the way up, arriving at the gift area on the twelfth floor.

To avoid catching attention, D divided his Pokemon into two batches.

The first batch's members were Alcremie, Glaceon, Ogerpon, and Pawmo.

The Pokemon immediately lined up, with Alcremie perched atop Pawmo, looking at D together.

As the elder of the Pokemon, D smiled and introduced to many Pokemon:

"Go ahead, this place specializes in selling Pokemon toys. Look for anything you like, feel free to take it, and after that, I'll pay for them."

"Pamo!"

"Mama!"

"Bonnie!"

"..."

The Pokemon collectively let out a cheer and then scattered like children, roaming between shelves, selecting things they liked.

D pushed a trolley with a laugh following behind them.

He watched Alcremie standing with hands on hips in front of a can of delicately shaped candies, then turning back to point at the candy jar.

D immediately helped Alcremie put the candy jar into the trolley.

Alcremie also hopped over and into the basket of the trolley, letting D push it.

Soon, Pawmo came flying back hugging a can of cereal.

Seeing Alcremie sitting in the trolley, it was immediately drawn to it, then casually tossed the cereal into the trolley and plopped down next to Alcremie.

The two Pokemon looked forward with joy, letting out rounds of cheers.

"Mama~!"

"Pamo~!"

"Two little fools." D said with a smile, then looked at Glaceon running back with a small dinosaur plush toy.

Glaceon glanced at the two Pokemon on the trolley, showing some desire, seemingly wanting to sit on it too.

But owing to its character, it felt a bit shy to ask.

Seeing this, D scooped up Glaceon from the ground amidst its surprise and placed it into the trolley.

Koraidon nearby looked heartened.

It nudged its large head over, wanting to sit on it too.

D was immediately sweating heavily, using his hand to push Koraidon's large face: "No way, you'll break the trolley!"

At these words, Koraidon's head immediately drooped down, showing an expression of grievance.

"Aga..."

D's heart softened, thought for a while, and changed his tone: "This time it won't work, when we get home I'll get a pickup truck, and then take you out for a ride."

"Aga!"

Koraidon was easy to placate, the dark cloud over its head instantly turned sunny, revealing a bright and radiant sun.

At this time, Ogerpon came running back with a Pikachu mask, seeing Pawmo and the others having fun in the trolley, its face immediately showed an eager expression.

"Bonnie!"

D: "..."

He observed the trolley's structure briefly, then had Koraidon bring over a second one.

D directed Ogerpon to sit inside, then transferred Alcremie, Pawmo, and Glaceon over to its side and into its embrace.

The trolley loaded with goods was handed to Koraidon to push, while D pushed a group of Pokemon leisurely wandering between the shelves.

As he wandered, he remembered the preferences of the Pokemon in the ranch and selected gifts for them.

"This Pokemon nest is nice. Get a few to serve as Cyclizar's nests."

"This Christmas reindeer hat set is quite cute, buy it for Dragonite and Dragonair to dress up."

"Oh! Are these Wise Glasses? They have a calm, mature look that I think Indeedee would like."

"Then this blue silk scarf goes as a gift for the sister Indeedee."

"Is this the Charcoal that Fire Type would like? Buy it for Slugma."

"..."

After several rounds, D finally finished selecting gifts for everyone.

Koraidon's trolley was already filled to the brim.

Seeing Pawmo and Ogerpon a bit tired from playing, D recalled them into Poke Balls, then switched them with Dragonite, Cyclizar, and Flutter Mane.

D repeated to them what he just told Pawmo and the others earlier.

---

The three Pokemon happily ran out immediately.

In the end, Dragonite chose a salted fish plushie, planning to hug it while sleeping later.

Cyclizar looked left and right, and finally picked a box of snack gift sets, filled with lots of tasty treats to share with everyone later!

Cyclizar showed a happy smile.

Flutter Mane followed the aroma down the escalator, stopping at a marketplace selling meat products, gazing intently at the fresh meat steaks and bones.

The butcher was startled by the Pokemon.

D ran over and saw Flutter Mane's state, quickly pushing it back:

"We can buy these things back home too; no need to get them specifically from here!"

"Mane..."

Flutter Mane seemed confused, then turned around and floated back with a specially made large bone for canine Pokemon to gnaw.

D reached out to take it, looked at the description, then at Flutter Mane's two sharp little fangs, and suddenly understood.

It seems Flutter Mane wants to grind its teeth!

Since coming to the ranch, Flutter Mane had been eating only cooked food, almost not having to hunt separately.

This led to it not tearing into prey for a long time, making its teeth itchy from lack of hunting, which made Flutter Mane feel uncomfortable.

This was indeed his oversight, D thought.

"In that case, I'll pack this big bone up!" D said.

Flutter Mane responded lightly and then voluntarily returned to the Poke Ball.

D took out the Poke Balls for Dragonite and Cyclizar, eagerly saying, "We've bought everything, time to go home!"

The two Pokemon naturally had no objections, obediently returning to the Poke Balls.

"What about you? Do you want anything?" D asked Koraidon, who chose nothing, smiling and saying, "Everyone's finished choosing, now you're the only one left."

"Ah gah hiss!" Koraidon happily shook its head; it didn't want anything.

Seeing this, D raised an eyebrow in surprise, "Really nothing?"

Koraidon honestly shook its head, "Ah gah hiss."

D couldn't help but ponder.

Koraidon nudged his back with its head, walking out.

D now understood what Koraidon meant.

It wanted to go home.

"Oh well, I'll study Pokeblocks for you when we get back." D said in a resigned manner, having to settle the bill and forcefully pack everything into his travel backpack until it practically burst.

After all this hustle, it was noon.

Koraidon's stomach rumbled, and D felt hungry as well, deciding to fill his belly here in Levincia City before heading back.

One man and one Pokemon strolled through the streets of Levincia City, with Koraidon drawing many curious glances from passersby.

Having grown accustomed to this, D ignored those looks, intending to find a Chinese restaurant serving Paldean-style hometown dishes.

Yet, as he passed a dessert shop, he suddenly heard a slightly surprised familiar voice from inside.

"Oh? D-san?"

D turned to see Lono with a Magnemite hovering above, standing not far away with a face full of surprise.

"It's really you! I almost thought I'd mistaken you!" Lono's eyes shined brightly, "D-san, how come you're suddenly in Levincia City?"

D: "I just returned from the Unova Region, you know, Levincia City is the only place with direct ferries to the Unova Region."

Lono instantly understood, and seeing Koraidon, she quickly asked, "Have you had lunch yet? If not, let me treat you! As a thank you for your hospitality last time!"

D thought about having many Pokemon with him, which might be inconvenient, so he planned to say he'd eaten.

But before he could speak, he heard Koraidon's stomach rumble beside him.

D: "..."

Lono burst into laughter with sparkling teeth, "Seems like you haven't eaten yet! Perfect, I haven't either; I know a great Chinese restaurant!"

D: "..."

Lono indeed was enthusiastic!

Unable to resist her kindness, he decided to accept and followed Lono to a Chinese restaurant located at the city center.

Lono energetically scribbled on the menu: "D-san, do you eat Mapo Tofu?"

"Sure."

"Green Pepper Beef?"

"Also fine."

"Baked Eggs?"

"...I'm not picky."

Lono: "Haha, that's great, then we'll have these dishes, waiter, also five bowls of rice and snacks for the Pokemon!"

"Got it!" The waiter took the menu, only to see this lady marked every dish on it.

The waiter was shocked, "Miss...isn't this a bit too much? Can the two of you finish it?"

D was puzzled upon hearing this.

The waiter handed the menu to him.

D wasn't surprised until he saw Lono marked everything.

"We won't finish, right?" D said, speechless.

Lono smiled sheepishly, "Because I don't know what you like, so I ordered everything, and it's not just us, but also the Pokemon!"

D thought it over and felt maybe they could finish, as Koraidon and Flutter Mane are big eaters: "All right, let's start with that."

The waiter nervously took the menu and left.

Lono then began chatting about the ranch trials.

She asked expectantly, "By the way, D-san, after I challenged, did any other Gym Leaders visit your ranch?"

"I don't think so? There weren't any before I left the ranch," D shook his head, "But I'm not sure since I haven't been back for a week."

Lono inexplicably felt some regret, "I wanted to see their expressions!"

D was speechless, "It's not that exaggerated, really? Although it sounds intimidating, familiarizing oneself with the trial's contents and utilizing type matchups, it's not impossible."

Just like Leon before, his Gogoat was completely countered by Charizard.

Not surprisingly, Charizard breezed through the Grass Trial.

If Leon's Charizard could do it, Alder's Volcarona surely can too.

After all, the Bug+Fire-type Volcarona equally counters Gogoat decisively.

It's merely that at the time, Alder was still in shock and didn't wish for Volcarona to immediately battle other Pokemon after just recovering.

Lono wore a resigned expression, "But most Gym Leaders specialize in only one Pokemon type! If the Cascarrafa Gym Leader Kofu tackled the Grass Trial, they'd probably be worse off than me."

Surely they can't raise Pokemon of other types solely for the trials, right?

Upon hearing this, D started to ponder, then showed a capitalist's smile:

"On that point, I'll gradually improve the future trials, adding more varied-type trials, allowing challengers to choose trials according to their situation."

Lono: "..."

She switched topics, casting her gaze on D's hairstyle and teasingly asking, "D-san, did you cut your hair? Your beard's gone too."

Hearing this, D perked up, sat up straight, and asked proudly, "Now I don't look that old, do I?"

He's quite sensitive about being called an uncle.

Children a few years younger are excusable, but not a peer like Lono.

After all, he's only four years older than Lono.

Lono gave a thumbs-up, "It's great!"

Koraidon chimed in, "Ah gah hiss!"

"Hmph!" D felt Lono was quite likable and said, "Just call me D from now on, don't use Mr., it sounds weird."

In both his past and current lives, people directly addressed him by name, without prefixes like uncle or brother.

Upon hearing his prideful hmph, Lono suddenly found D's personality rather endearing.

She beamed a joyful smile and enthusiastically said, "Okay, I got it!"

---

---

---

---

# 358: Chapter 355: Dragonite's Egg

Soon, the waiter began serving the dishes one after another until the table was completely filled.

Spicy and fragrant Mapo tofu, stir-fried shredded pork with green peppers, a large bowl of golden yellow and tender egg omelet, tender and bright red braised pork, aromatic white radish and rib soup...

Then, the waiter brought out dozens of snacks and sandwiches for Pokémon.

The two released their respective Pokémon, gathering around the table and enjoying the meal together.

The Pawmo had met Lono's Pokémon before, so upon meeting again, several Electric Type Pokémon gathered friendly to Nuzzle each other's cheeks, exchanging power as a greeting.

Lono's Luxray sprawled on the floor, with a plate of Poffin-like cookies between its paws, burying its face into it and eating noisily.

Glaceon stood beside it, startled by Luxray's unrefined eating style, then thought to itself that it should not eat like that, and elegantly began to chew slowly.

Flutter Mane looked at the floating Mismagius with a puzzled expression.

Mismagius noticed its gaze and turned to look over, equally perplexed.

The two Pokémon stared at each other, neither willing to look away first.

Looking at the feast in front of him, Lono grinned with an animated tone: "I'm digging in!"

D watched Lono eating with amusement, then began his meal as he usually did.

As he ate, he glanced at the Pokémon beside him.

This was D's habit, to check if any of the Pokémon needed help during meals, like wanting a certain dish, then he would help them get it.

Instead of seeing Pokémon in need of help, he saw a harmonious scene among them.

They seemed to get along quite well!

Enjoying the delicious food, Lono looked satisfied and said, "So tasty!"

D reassuredly withdrew his gaze from the Pokémon and turned to Lono, "Did you encounter Trainers challenging the Gym again this morning?"

Lono's cheeks were stuffed full like a little hamster: "Hmm, hmm! This morning I hosted three Trainers at once, and Mismagius and Bellibolt were exhausted!"

"That's really tough!" D suddenly felt that Gym Leaders and Elite in the Paldea Region were very busy.

"Hehe!" Lono laughed, "Actually, it's not too bad! Gym Leaders also have rest time occasionally; it's not like you imagine, staying in the Gym all day accepting challenges—that would be too boring!"

D nodded thoughtfully.

He remembered that among all the Gym Leaders in the Paldea Region, Lono's strength was not very strong.

No, maybe she's more focused on audience reaction than fully engaging in battles with challengers.

It's just that Lono hasn't become a livestream host yet.

Thinking about this, D suddenly felt curious, so he asked, "Do you like your current job?"

"Current job?" Lono mumbled unclearly, "You mean being a Gym Leader? I really like it! Whenever I see the audience cheering for our battles, it makes me very happy!"

No wonder! D realized, as he knew that the League's salaries are quite generous, Gym Leaders are never short on money.

Lono's livestreaming must be purely out of interest or simple affection.

However, thinking about livestreaming and editing videos to post online, D got a new idea from Lono.

Perhaps he could create a personal channel later to post short videos of his ranch to promote his farm products and fruits.

Like a channel called "D's Country Orchard".

"And what about you, D?" Lono asked in return.

"Me?" D was stunned for a second, then regained his composure and replied with a smile, "I also really enjoy my current job, always felt that city life doesn't suit me, with one small room after another in skyscrapers like pigeon coops, nothing like the leisure of the ranch side."

But it's undeniable that city life is quite convenient.

However, considering the Pokémon World has ride Pokémon that replace vehicles, D felt there wasn't much difference between the two.

Not to mention Koraidon—and with Dragonite's speed—it's only a ten-minute ride to the neighboring city.

Lono imagined that lifestyle and couldn't help but sigh, "It feels like you and I are complete opposites, D!"

D raised an eyebrow, suddenly remembering something: "Do you eat out every day?"

Lono immediately showed an embarrassed smile: "Not really! Just occasionally, once in a while… I'm quite good at cooking!"

D looked doubtful.

Lono had to concede, "Alright... my culinary skills aren't very good, but I'm still skilled at some basic dishes! Yet Mismagius and Bellibolt don't like the food, so I can only dine out and occasionally cook for myself."

"Alright then!" D nodded, "That's indeed convenient for city living."

"Right? Hehe!" Lono nodded in agreement with a grin.

D gave a helpless laugh, and the atmosphere turned quite harmonious.

After the meal, Lono quickly settled the bill before D could.

---

D looked speechless, the education he received since childhood made him subconsciously think that these things should be done by him, especially since he was four years older than Lono.

Having a young girl pay for the bill felt strange in a way that's hard to describe.

Lono turned her head to see his expression and said with a smile: "It's okay! We agreed that this time it would be my treat!"

"If you feel embarrassed, D, you can repay me next time during your vacation when you come to the ranch for the trial!"

D looked at Lono and after a while nodded helplessly: "Alright, then I will definitely treat you well at that time."

"Hehe, it's settled then!"

After lunch, D said goodbye to Lono and rode Koraidon out of Levincia City.

At three in the afternoon, Koraidon flew over Porto Marinada Town and landed in the ranch.

Butterfree was the first to notice him, and soon a large group of Pokemon surrounded him.

A smile appeared on D's face as he petted this one and that one.

With Pokémon like Alcremie and Glaceon, little lively darlings, absent, the atmosphere at the ranch was quite listless and many Pokemon appeared to lack energy.

Seeing this, D quickly released all the Pokemon from their Poke Balls, then said to Alcremie and Pawmot:

"Go play with everyone! Since you're not here, no one has been playing with Garganacl and the others."

Upon hearing this, Alcremie turned its head to look at the five large Pokemon standing in line before it.

They who used to be towering figures now appeared all pitifully meek, like bullied little ones.

Alcremie couldn't stand this sight, felt a pang of sympathy, and immediately jumped onto Garganacl to go and play with them.

"Alright, you go play too!" D turned and patted Cyclizar's head, then placed the backpack filled with gifts in the living room.

After greeting Calyrex who was watching TV, brother Indeedee came to report on the ranch's situation over the past few days.

D ate a piece of Translation Nuts Cake and heard brother Indeedee say:

"Indee Indeede." (Nothing happened at the ranch that couldn't be handled, just a few fences on the other side of the river accidently damaged by some wild Pokemon.)

"Indee." (Also, a girl named Grusha came to the ranch the day before yesterday, but upon hearing that you were out, left.)

"Grusha? Girl?" Hearing this, D was slightly stunned.

Brother Indeedee was somewhat puzzled.

D instantly understood, and laughed: "So you differentiate gender through human hairstyles and looks!"

Brother Indeedee looked bewildered: "Indee?" (Did I get it wrong?)

D nodded: "Grusha isn't a girl, he's the Gym Leader of Glaseado Gym, a genuine man, just like me."

Brother Indeedee: "..."

D couldn't help but laugh, he didn't mind this matter at all, knowing Grusha would definitely visit again.

He opened the travel backpack and took out the gifts prepared for the Pokemon, selecting glasses and scarves for the brother and sister pair.

"Here's your gift, see if you like them."

The siblings took them with extended hands, smiles blossoming on their faces.

Seeing this, D knew he had chosen the right gifts.

He then handed over the gift prepared for Calyrex, and said to him:

"Everything is arranged! The information for the phones has been sent out, it won't be long before we can use phones!"

Calyrex's eyes sparkled with anticipation: "I await that day's arrival!"

"Mm-hmm!" Back at the ranch, D felt excited and couldn't sit still: "You keep watching TV! I'll go check the fence!"

With that, D walked out the door, crossed the small river, and headed to the opposite bank to inspect the nearby wooden fences around the ranch.

Sure enough, he found several places damaged.

Since the ranch is in the wild, events of wild Pokemon damaging farmland fences occur frequently.

D wasn't surprised; he returned to the storeroom to fetch tools and some planks to repair the damaged spots.

After everything was taken care of, it was already five in the evening.

Zack arrived at the ranch to collect the goods on time, and when he saw D, he was slightly surprised and then delighted: "You're back?"

"Yeah!" D nodded.

Zack loaded today's milk and vegetables onto the truck, praised: "Your Pokemon are impressive! Those two Indeedee keep everything here well-organized! Truly enviable, if they ever lay eggs, remember to let me take care of one! I'd love an Indeedee housekeeper as well."

D's face darkened: "They are siblings!"

"Nani?! I thought they were a couple…" Zack was shocked, then awkwardly scratched his head: "Sorry, it's my mistake!"

D glanced back at sister Indeedee watering flowers, relieved they hadn't heard the conversation.

Zack didn't stay long; after loading the goods onto the truck, he bid D farewell and drove away from the ranch with a rumble.

But sometimes, what you say just happens.

D was about to start cooking when he saw two working Dragonites preparing to leave for Dragonite Island suddenly burst forth with a brilliant light.

---

This magical phenomenon immediately caught the attention of a large group of Pokemon.

D thought that Dragonite was about to undergo Mega Evolution and quickly ran over to check the situation.

But when he arrived, he saw that the light from the two working Dragonites was gradually dimming.

No, to be precise, the two lights separated from the two Dragonites, merged into one on the grass in front of them, and turned into an egg-shaped energy form.

Once the light fully disappeared, a Pokemon Egg with orange patterns appeared in front of all the Pokemon.

D: "!!!"

Whoa, a Dragonite laid an egg! What's going on here? Do Pokemon lay eggs by combining their energy into one rather than mating?

This is so amazing...

D was left standing there, dumbfounded at the scene before him.

No! How did these two working Dragonites suddenly become a couple?

He remembered that they just had a normal relationship. Did they suddenly fall in love while delivering goods?

D didn't know what to say for a moment.

Until one of the Dragonites picked up the egg.

That Dragonite gazed at the egg in its arms, then looked at its partner, and then both of them turned their eyes back to the egg.

What's going on? Are they planning to take the egg back to Dragonite Island? wondered D.

However, in the next second, he saw the Dragonite carrying the egg walking towards him, fluttering its big eyes and placing the egg in his arms.

D: "???"

Holding the egg, he was at a loss for what to do. What should he do now?

But the female Dragonite that had just laid the egg wasn't worried about that.

To it, D was a trustworthy human who was very friendly to Pokemon, so entrusting the egg to him was much better than taking it back to Dragonite Island to hatch naturally.

After all, they came to the ranch every day to eat Poffins, so taking the egg back to Dragonite Island would be too inconvenient.

Instead of doing that, it was better to leave the egg with D. They felt relieved and, once it hatched, the baby could help deliver goods and eat Poffins with them.

So, after handing the egg to D, the Dragonites and the Dragonair waved their claws at him and flew away.

Leaving D and a group of Pokemon staring blankly at the freshly laid egg.

With no experience in egg hatching, D looked around, tentatively asking, "How do you hatch this...?"

However, no Pokemon in the ranch had ever laid an egg, so they had no clue about such matters.

D had no choice but to call over Dragonite, thinking its kin might know something.

Dragonite was also stunned upon learning that the egg was laid by the two working Dragonites.

D ate a piece of Translation Nuts Cake and asked, "Have you ever had experience with egg-laying?"

Dragonite shook its head vigorously: "Roar!" (No, I don't lay eggs!)

"Then what should we do?" D was troubled.

Dragonite carefully recalled what it had seen on Dragonite Island and said to D, "Roar roar!" (Just leave it on the grass and let it hatch on its own!)

D: "..."

Isn't that a bit too casual?

After thinking for a moment, he took the Dragonite Egg back to the living room.

Upon seeing the egg, his sister Indeedee looked puzzled: "Indeedee?" (What's this?)

D was speechless: "A Dragonite egg, they just laid it and handed it over to me. I don't know how to hatch it either!"

Hearing this, his sister Indeedee smiled: "Indeedee~" (I see, then let my brother and me take care of it~)

D was a bit surprised, eyes lighting up: "Huh? You can do it?"

Sister Indeedee nodded and gently explained: "Indeedee indeedee!" (Before coming to the ranch, mom and dad taught my brother and me. Not only can we take care of Pokemon babies, but if you ever have a baby, we can help you take care of it too!)

---

---

---

---

# 359: Chapter 356: Bug-Catching Contest

Taking care of a baby...

D was momentarily speechless, as he went to the kitchen to prepare dinner and said:

"There's no rush for this. Besides, the way humans and Pokémon lay eggs is quite different; there are many rituals to go through before having a baby, like confessing love, dating, and marriage. But most importantly, you need someone you like first."

The Indeedee Sibling placed the Dragonite Egg onto the small bed and then joined D in cooking:

"Indeedee~ Indeedee?" (I know~ My parents used to tell my brother and me about this, so Master, do you have someone you like now?)

D: "..."

It felt rather strange to discuss this kind of thing with a Pokémon.

He thought it over and still shook his head: "I've been at the ranch all the time, where would I find the time?!"

D's attitude towards such matters was to let fate decide; if there wasn't anyone suitable, he'd rather spend his life with Pokémon.

"Indeedee." The Indeedee Sibling nodded earnestly, thinking it over too, and then put away the gossipy look, focusing on preparing the food.

Before leaving, D prepared several days' worth of Berry Platters for Farfetch'd.

But it had eaten them all and still hadn't learned the move it wanted, so it could only keep at it and continue eating.

Watching Farfetch'd scarf down the Berry Platters, D couldn't help but sigh: "A long road ahead!"

Three days apart, and Farfetch'd was almost turning into a big fat duck with thick fat.

*

The next morning, D moved the berry blender into the kitchen.

However, he didn't immediately start researching Pokeblocks.

Instead, he planned to go to the vehicle management office in town, take a driving test, and then buy a pickup truck to haul berries back to the ranch.

Besides this, he also needed to purchase some ingredients and seasonings to restock supplies.

During his absence, the food at home had almost been eaten up by the Pokémon.

Riding Cyclizar, D headed straight to the vehicle management office in town.

After paying the exam fee to the staff, a person in a blue uniform led him to the testing area.

Compared to his previous life, the driving test in the Pokémon World wasn't as complicated.

As long as you demonstrated your ability to drive and answered some questions about the driving process, you could obtain a driver's license.

D passed smoothly without any surprises or dangers, and he carefully stored his newly printed driver's license with the fresh ink smell, then headed straight to the car sale area with Cyclizar.

The showroom displayed a variety of vehicles, from business vehicles and sedans to luxury limousines, of all kinds and sizes.

Due to the presence of Pokémon, cars in this world weren't very expensive, and a decent one could be bought for over a hundred thousand League Coins.

Luxury cars weren't a necessity for D, so he skipped them and finally spent 120,000 League Coins on a brand-new white pickup truck.

Having paid, the car was officially his.

D patted the cargo bed, smiling: "Hop on!"

Cyclizar looked curiously at the car in front of it and leaped into it.

"Sit tight, and don't move around randomly!"

D gave a reminder, then climbed into the driver's seat.

"Vroom vroom vroom!"

The small pickup successfully started, and D drove, with Cyclizar in tow, back to Porto Marinada Town.

This time, he was responding to the Indeedee Sibling's request to buy a crib for the Dragonite Egg.

D told Cyclizar to wait in the car, while he went into the furniture store and came out after a short while.

"Just put it on top!" D pointed to the truck bed, and the two strong men combined efforts to lift the crib onto it.

The truck bed was already loaded with the groceries and daily necessities he had previously purchased, along with Cyclizar. D looked around and thought to himself how convenient it was.

At least now he wouldn't have to worry about moving large items when going out.

Sitting on top, Cyclizar saw D happy and began to smile foolishly.

Seeing that he'd accomplished everything he set out to do on this trip, D clapped his hands and said to Cyclizar: "Let's go, time to head back!"

"Kyah oh~"

"Oh right!" D suddenly remembered there was still one thing he hadn't done.

He needed to buy some books about Pokémon egg-laying!

In the past, he only hatched Pokémon Eggs in the games, where they hatched on their own as he walked.

Reality probably wouldn't be that simple; the time required for Pokémon Egg hatching and how to care for newly hatched Pokémon were things he needed to understand in advance.

Even though the Indeedee Sibling could care for the young ones, he felt more at ease by learning about it himself.

What if someday another Pokémon at the ranch found each other and laid eggs?

Thus, D headed back to Porto Marinada Town and dove into a bookstore, and with the staff's guidance, quickly found two books suitable for him.

Pokémon Breeding Guide - by Professor Elm.

Pokemon Egg Hatching Care - by Professor Elm.

The young store clerk watched him and smiled as she introduced: "These two books document how to let two Pokémon come together to lay eggs and are very popular in the breeding houses!"

"Breeding house?" D was a bit surprised. Was it the kind of breeding house he knew?

The young store clerk nodded: "That's right. There's a Pokémon breeding house near Porto Marinada Town where Trainers can leave their Pokémon to be looked after by the owner."

---

"In addition to that, the breeding center over there will specialize in breeding Pokémon. If there's a Trainer who just turned ten and wants a Pokémon, they can give a little adoption fee and then choose one newly hatched Pokémon from there to take as their starter Pokémon to care for."

Sure enough... D understood.

He nodded, didn't ask more, and with two books, drove the pickup truck directly back to the ranch.

As soon as the vehicle stopped, it attracted a curious crowd of Pokémon.

D smiled and introduced to them, "This is the vehicle I told you about before. Now I can give you all a ride!"

"Bonnie!"

Ogerpon looked full of anticipation.

D smiled slightly, then together with Cyclizar, unloaded the cargo from the truck.

He patted the truck bed and said to the group of Pokémon, "Get on!"

Alcremie, Ogerpon, Pawmot, Butterfree, Koraidon, Dragonair, Dratini, and others swarmed up, filling the truck bed to the brim.

D took the opportunity to glance at the load capacity of the pickup truck, surprisingly finding it could fit quite a lot, with so many Pokémon able to fit in.

But still, there were some Pokémon who couldn't get on because there wasn't room for them.

The five big Garganacls looked enviously at everyone.

D: "..."

He pondered for a while, then said, "I'll take you in the next batch!"

So the Garganacls stayed in the center of the ranch, watching eagerly as D drove around the ranch with a group of Pokémon.

After driving around with a group of Pokémon for a few laps, D parked the car next to the house, got off, and spoke to the group of Pokémon:

"Alright, go play now! I have some things to do."

The Pokémon gladly agreed, jumping off the truck and continuing to play on the grass.

D proceeded to help his sister Indeedee move the baby crib upstairs into their room.

Sister Indeedee happily laid a soft blanket on the baby crib.

Once the bed was prepared, Brother Indeedee carefully placed the egg he was holding into it.

"Ai yeet!" It's done!

Sister Indeedee looked pleased with her masterpiece.

D then sat on a chair and started to flip through the two books he held.

Both books were authored by Professor Elm from the Johto Region.

Professor Elm, a junior to Professor Oak.

Unlike Professor Oak, this Professor Elm's research topics are "Pokémon Breeding and Pokémon Eggs."

Pikachu's pre-evolution form was first discovered by him, the egg groups were also classified by him, and Professor Elm researched Pokémon's characteristics and evolutions, discovering quite impressive findings.

Due to his exceptional skills, Professor Oak once praised Professor Elm as a leading expert in Pokémon evolution.

Thoughts of Professor Elm filled his mind as D flipped open the book in front of him.

"The so-called Pokémon breeding refers to the entire process where two Pokémon of the same egg group mate to create the crystallization of love."

"Additionally, there's a special exception: Ditto can mate with all Pokémon to produce eggs."

"Offspring Pokémon hatched from the eggs have a certain probability of inheriting the appearance, characteristics, moves, and abilities of the parent Pokémon, usually referred to as heredity."

"Research suggests that the stronger the parent Pokémon, the more outstanding the offspring produced."

"Indeed, exactly like what I know," D continued reading, only to soon discover Professor Elm's egg group classifications for different Pokémon.

They included Monster Group, Bug Group, Fairy Group, Flying Group, Ground Group, Human-like Group, and so forth.

Each group contained sample Pokémon.

For instance, Lopunny belongs to the Human-like Group, while Dragonite belongs to Water 1 Group and Dragon Group.

D continued reading further.

"The incubation time for eggs of Pokémon from different species varies greatly; for example, a Caterpie's egg typically requires only about a dozen days from laying to hatching."

"However, the eggs of some Dragon Type Pokémon have incubation times several tens of times longer than those of Caterpie, such as the long-lived, powerful Dragonite."

"The incubation time for this type of Pokémon's egg is approximately five to ten months, and it's speculated this occurs because Dragon Type Pokémon have relatively slower growth cycles."

"Five months to ten months, huh..."

That requires quite a wait.

No wonder Dragonite previously mentioned that after laying Dragon Eggs, Dragonite would leave them on the grass.

Then, D watched the book for a while longer, firmly memorizing some information.

*

Mid-April, with the arrival of summer, the outside temperature gradually became hot, and at night, the sound of cicadas could be heard rushing from behind the mountain.

People shed their heavy coats and sweaters, changing into more lightweight and cool short sleeves.

This morning, D was watering and fertilizing the World Tree, which had grown considerably larger, in the ranch.

A stranger visitor suddenly arrived at the ranch.

D turned his head and saw a girl with a rural flair standing nervously outside the ranch.

Seeing that someone noticed her, the girl quickly asked:

"Hello, is this D Ranch?"

D stood up and replied, "That's right!"

---

He looked the girl up and down, noticing she didn't have any Poke Balls at her waist, not resembling a trainer here to challenge the trial.

After thinking for a moment, D asked, "Is there anything I can help you with?"

The girl nodded, "Um, I wanted to ask if you have any berries for sale here?"

"Berries?" This surprised D, wanting berries could simply be satisfied by buying them in town, right?

The girl saw his confusion and quickly explained, "No, no, it's not that. I want to buy a large quantity of berries. I'm from Sunnytown next door, where they're preparing to hold a Bug-catching contest event, but the berries for making snacks were all eaten by a greedy Snorlax!"

"Without berries, we can't make snacks to attract Bug Type Pokemon, and the Bug-catching contest won't be able to proceed... I've already run to several other farms and didn't see any orchards over there."

Bug-catching contest? Sunnytown next door? D had heard from Mayor Thomas before. He heard their harvest wasn't very good last year.

D hesitated slightly, glancing back at his own orchard, where he'd stored the berries intending to use them for developing Pokeblocks...

But upon thinking it over, he wasn't in a hurry, especially with Calyrex around; even if these berries were sold, they'd grow back quickly.

Given that, he decided to lend a hand!

Thinking of this, D nodded and said, "Yes, a few days ago I happened to harvest a batch of berries that hasn't been sold yet, take a look at how many you need."

Hearing this, the girl immediately breathed a sigh of relief, "The more the better!"

D: "..."

He simply brought the girl to his warehouse, looking at the pile of berries inside, the girl waved her hand, "I want them all."

D exclaimed, "Can so many berries be used up at the Bug-catching contest?"

He knew about the Bug-catching contest; it was a competition comparing who could catch the rare and precious Bug Type Pokemon.

The girl nodded shyly, "Yes, they can! Because there have been many trainers coming into town these days, all eager to participate in this Bug-catching contest!"

"Alright then!" D roughly calculated the price of this batch of berries and asked, "Do you have a vehicle over there to transport them?"

The girl said, a bit awkwardly, "We don't have a vehicle."

D: "..."

He had to reluctantly compromise and then asked, "How long does it roughly take to drive from Sunnytown to Porto Marinada Town?"

The girl: "Driving takes about twenty minutes!"

Twenty minutes? That's quite close.

D nodded and simply said, "Then I'll drive to deliver them for you!"

The girl was full of gratitude, "Then I'll leave it to you!"

Because there were other matters in Sunnytown, the girl quickly paid the deposit and rode back on a Cyclizar.

D, on the other hand, began packing the berries with a group of Pokemon and loading boxes of berries onto the pickup truck.

Once everything was done, D sat in the driver's seat and honked the horn twice, "I'm heading out, does anyone want to accompany me?"

As soon as he finished talking, several Pokemon ran over.

Ogerpon happily took the passenger seat, while the little Alcremie jumped onto the central console, its little face pressed against the windshield, looking forward mischievously.

The fluffy Pawmot and Glaceon, along with a few Dragonair, nestled into the back seat, leaning on the chair to look ahead.

Cyclizar and Koraidon sat in the rear compartment.

Flutter Mane and Dragonite didn't follow, but after D greeted them, he started the car, taking a cab full of Pokemon out the door to deliver goods.

On the road, the Pokemon's growls and laughter intertwined into a song, making D feel like he was taking a group of kindergarten kids out for a spring outing.

D glanced at these Pokemon through the rearview mirror.

With them around, he might never find this trip boring again!

---

---

---

---

# 360: Chapter 357: Wooloo Ranch, Building the Apple Garden, and Koraidon's Dilemma

9:25 AM, the weather is sunny and bright, with a blue sky overhead dotted with clouds like cotton, gently drifting with the breeze.

A pickup truck loaded with truckloads of berries and Pokémon travels along a deserted country road.

A song from a car CD plays, said to be performed by a Jigglypuff who loves singing.

The Alcremies follow the beat, howling out of tune, filling the cabin with a lively atmosphere.

D focuses on driving, while recalling the situation in Sunnytown in his mind.

Sunnytown, located south of Porto Marinada Town, is a typical small countryside town.

The town's residents live off agriculture and compared to Porto Marinada Town, it seems a bit rustic. But it's said that the town produces abundant sunflowers, hence the name Sunnytown.

Not far from the ranch, after driving for a little over ten minutes, D spots a golden Sunflora Field appearing ahead in the meadows.

Some Pokémon shaped like sunflowers mix in, raising their heads alongside the sunflowers to face the sun.

"Sunflora."

The name of the Pokémon crosses D's mind.

As they approach the town, some vehicles and Trainers with Pokémon also appear on the road.

D glances at these young kids, guessing they've been drawn in by the bug-catching competition, reminiscent of the Sakura Blossom Wine Party held over in Porto Marinada Town.

The car enters the town, and D retracts his gaze from the outside, following the directions given by the girl, driving all the way to the town center.

The girl who previously ordered the berries from the ranch is standing there chatting with another girl.

Seeing him arrive, the girl immediately runs back, waving her hand: "This way! Over here!"

D drives the car over and shuts off the engine, then opens the door and hops out.

The Pawmot and Dragonairs also squeeze to the window, sticking their heads out curiously surveying the outside scene.

The girl is shocked by the large group of Pokémon: "So many Pokémon! Wow, are those the legendary Dragonairs?! So many rare Pokémon!"

D chuckles: "Yes, they're all mine."

"Truly enviable!"

D turns to the Pokémon in the car and says: "Alright, Koraidon, Cyclizar, start unloading the berries ordered by the guest!"

"Ogerpon, Pawmot, you can jump down and play too, but don't run off! Stay close to the car."

"Bonnie~"

"Bammo!"

"..."

The Pokémon nod excitedly, agreeing.

They also jump out of the car, gazing around the unfamiliar town with curiosity.

Meanwhile, D begins unloading the berries from the truck with Koraidon and Cyclizar.

Soon, all the berries are unloaded. The girl checks the quantity, quickly pays the balance, and expresses her gratitude:

"Thank you so much! Rancher, thanks to your help, the bug-catching competition can proceed smoothly!"

"Just a small task, it's nothing." D replies with a gentle smile.

"Oh! By the way!" The girl suddenly remembers something and invites: "Rancher, how about staying around for the bug-catching competition in the town?"

D is slightly taken aback: "Can I?"

The girl nods lightly: "Of course! Normally, there's a registration fee for the competition, but since you helped us so much, you can join the contest for free!"

"Hmm..." D hesitates a bit. Honestly, his interest in Bug Type Pokémon isn't as strong as the furry ones.

But the competition sounds fun.

After a brief thought, D makes a decision.

He wants to experience the life of an Insect-Catching Youth!

"Do I need to prepare anything?" D asks.

The girl shakes her head: "No need, the town will provide snacks to attract Bug Type Pokémon for competing Trainers."

"Oh, and one more thing: during the competition, you can't use your Pokémon to battle against those Bug Type Pokémon!"

D: "I can only use snacks to capture them?"

The girl nods lightly: "That's right! Besides snacks, it also depends on the Trainer's personal charm! Those Bug Type Pokémon are smart; they'll choose people they like to follow."

D: "Understood."

Girl: "The competition will be held tomorrow at 9 AM, at the Natural Park south of the town. You can head over there directly. Today, we'll make all these berries into Pokémon snacks."

"Okay, then I'll come back tomorrow." D nods, indicating he understands.

He calls the Pokémon back to the car and drives away from Sunnytown, heading back to the ranch.

*

In the afternoon, D officially starts planning the new area across the river.

He rides a Dragonite up into the sky, taking out paper and pen to sketch a map of the terrain.

Then, starting from a stone bridge, D draws several straight roads, quickly dividing the land into several main areas.

---

Looking at the largest piece of land in the upper left corner, D pondered and thought to himself:

"Hmm, this grassland can be used to raise Wooloo..."

The flatland, along with the slopes around it, was wide open with no obstructions, allowing sunlight to reach every corner, enabling the grass to overgrow.

This is an ideal place to establish a Wooloo Ranch.

As for why not raise Mareep, which can also produce wool, it's because Mareep loses its wool when it evolves into Ampharos.

"This piece of land here can be used to raise some other productive Pokemon."

After a brief thought, several figures flashed through D's mind.

Pokémon like Tropius, which produces bananas, Sawsbuck, and Deerling, which can produce tea leaves...

D always wanted to raise a Tropius, but unfortunately, he never encountered one.

The town's ranch shops didn't have this kind of Pokemon either because Tropius brings much less profit than other productive Pokemon.

Thinking of this, D wrote down the names Tropius and Sawsbuck on the other two plots of land.

The tea made from the leaves on Sawsbuck's head is quite delicious, perfect for the few Pokémon on the ranch who enjoy drinking tea.

Moreover, brewed tea can also be used to increase the bulk of Sinistea and Polteageist, making it a triple win item.

D planned to leave the remaining one or two plots as they were for now and see if there were any suitable Pokémon; if not, he would build a children's park there.

Equipped with seesaws, slides, sandboxes, and other toys, it would be a place for Pokémon to play.

As for the other side of the road, D planned to develop it into agricultural land for growing some fruits.

For example, red apples.

After planning the new area, D patted Dragonite on the back and said to it, "Alright, let's come down now!"

"Roar~" Dragonite obediently descended.

D immediately found Dugtrio, called it over to the other side of the river, and pointed to this piece of land, saying, "I'll leave this place to you!"

"Chirp chirp chirp!"

Dugtrio glanced at it and then began working diligently.

After evolution, Dugtrio's work efficiency had significantly improved, and its long experience in farming made it increasingly adept at plowing.

Thus, very soon, a complete, freshly plowed plot of land appeared in front of D.

"Well done!" D patted Dugtrio on the head, "I'll make you some new treats to eat later." Perhaps Dugtrio's favorite Pokeblock.

"Chirp chirp chirp!"

Hearing this, Dugtrio's eyes immediately brightened.

It was very excited!

Returning to the living room, D checked his small treasury.

After buying that pickup truck, he still had some savings left, just enough to purchase a batch of apple saplings to plant.

As for Tropius and Sawsbuck… later, he planned to explore around Casseroya Lake to see if he could encounter any wild ones, then bring them back to the ranch like he did with Gogoat.

"I'll make a trip to town and will be back in about half an hour; you all play at home."

D waved at the group of Pokémon and then drove off in his little pickup truck.

Upon arriving at Porto Marinada Town, he parked the car by the roadside and pushed open the door to Elo Seed Store.

After learning the purpose of D's visit, Elo immediately led him to the backyard, laughing heartily:

"You've come at the right time, the store just got a fresh batch of apple saplings. How many do you want?"

D glanced around and found there were about a hundred or so saplings here.

He roughly calculated the size of the plot that Dugtrio had prepared, and then said, "Thirty, please!"

"Alright." Elo then asked, "Do you want me to deliver them later?"

D shook his head, smiling, "This time it's no trouble; I'll do it myself."

Elo was slightly surprised, but upon seeing the pickup truck parked by the roadside, he understood what D meant.

Elo exclaimed, "You bought a car? When did you get it?"

D: "Just the other day, mainly because the ranch is getting bigger, it's more convenient with a car. We can't always rely on Koraidon and the others to transport goods."

Elo chuckled, "True, that makes sense. I'll load them up for you!"

Loading up the saplings on the truck and returning to the ranch, D, along with Koraidon and Cyclizar, spent the afternoon planting the apple saplings in the ground.

By the time the sun set, they had opened up a brand-new apple garden.

D washed his hands in the river, wiped the sweat off his forehead, and said contentedly:

"Alright, it's done! Now, let these apple trees slowly grow, and by next year, there will be a vast, red apple garden here!"

The only uncertainty left is whether the apple trees will attract bugs... Pokémon like Applin, which eats the apple's flesh and hides inside, disguising itself as an apple.

For fruit growers, this kind of Pokémon is entirely harmful, detrimental to the apple's growth.

"Oh well, I'll worry about it later!"

D didn't really mind if the apple garden attracted such Pokémon, considering it as enriching the Pokémon ecology on the ranch.

He glanced at the muddy Koraidon and Cyclizar and then waved a big hand, saying to them, "Let's go home. I'll give you a bath!"

Tonight's dinner was entirely prepared by his sister Indeedee, while D took Koraidon and Cyclizar to the bathroom, holding a showerhead to wash them.

---

Two Pokémon stood obediently under the showerhead, allowing D to wash their bodies.

Washing off the mud stuck to them in the field, then applying a layer of fragrant shower gel and scrubbing out foam with a bath ball.

Cyclizar accidentally got some foam up its nose, and it stopped after sneezing several times from the irritation.

Nearby, Koraidon looked on disdainfully.

"Arggh…"

The next second, while breathing, it also inhaled a mouthful of foam into its nasal cavity and started to sneeze following Cyclizar's lead.

"Pfft, hahahahaha!"

D laughed heartily and mercilessly, leisurely picking up the shower to rinse the foam off the two Pokémon: "Now, are you still disdainful of Cyclizar?"

Koraidon: "..."

Cyclizar showed a silly smile on its face: "Gaaoh~"

Koraidon glared at Cyclizar, flicking its large tail, and turned around, ignoring people.

It was throwing a little tantrum.

D wasn't in a hurry to soothe it, first helping Cyclizar rinse the foam off and then using a towel to dry it, sniffing and praising:

"Smells good, alright, go wait in the living room for dinner!"

"Gaaoh~" Cyclizar said, happily running off.

Koraidon couldn't help but look back, and D sat on a bench, calling out: "Alright, come on over, let me rinse the foam off you."

Koraidon walked over rather honestly.

D picked up the showerhead, rinsing while saying: "Why disdain Cyclizar? Cyclizar hasn't been born for two years yet, for us humans, that's still a baby."

"Arggh…" Koraidon didn't know what to say.

"Everyone starts off not knowing anything!" D thought he should help Cyclizar ease its standing with Koraidon: "Cyclizar is still young now, give it a few more years, and it will undoubtedly become smarter and more capable, I have a hunch, it will grow into a very powerful Pokémon!"

After all, the effects of the birthday cake take time to manifest.

But hearing this, Koraidon's heart grew even more complex.

D… really believes in it and trusts it, huh?

Looking now, it seems not to have let D's trust down, indeed becoming a very powerful Pokémon, one so capable it could protect D and its past self.

Memories of the past flooded its mind, and Koraidon allowed D to dry its body with deep reluctance.

"Alright, you also go downstairs to wait for dinner!" D smiled, patting Koraidon's head.

Koraidon responded somewhat numbly and walked downstairs, looking at the silly Cyclizar.

Its heart was infinitely conflicted because D can now understand it, meaning it could tell D about things that would happen.

But doing so… might lead to terrible consequences.

In Koraidon's mind surfaced the conversations it had overheard between D, Professor Sada, and Professor Turo.

Back then, they were discussing matters about time travel.

Koraidon clearly remembered Professor Sada saying: If someone from the future travels through time to prevent their grandmother and grandfather from marrying, then the future person will disappear, never having existed.

At that time, as a Cyclizar, it was confused by these words, and D had specially explained it all to it.

When you play with time, time plays with you too.

And now, that kind of thing was happening to it.

If it tells D about the future, then things will change, and D will definitely stop something from happening.

Then, if Cyclizar didn't fall into the Time Machine, it wouldn't be born.

So, the current it would disappear along with it.

Koraidon didn't want to disappear; it wanted to stay by D's side forever.

But looking at its past self, Koraidon's heart also filled with a trace of unbearable compassion.

If it doesn't speak up, then its past self will go through those terrifying and painful experiences just like it did.

In Koraidon's golden eyes, a trace of conflicted emotion surfaced as it silently gazed at its past self.

At least… let it accompany D a while longer! Koraidon thought sorrowfully.

---

---

---

---

# 361: Chapter 358: Snorlax

The next morning, D rode his Cyclizar, wore a straw hat, and put on a short-sleeve shirt, dressing up as a forest Insect-Catching Youth, heading off to Sunnytown to watch the bug-catching competition in a very low-key manner.

When he arrived at the Natural Park on the south side of Sunnytown, a large crowd of Trainers had already gathered there.

Among them were some boys and girls dressed just like him.

Looking at those youths holding insect nets, D exclaimed to himself: There they are! The Insect-Catching Youths and Shorts Kids!

D blended in effortlessly, standing in front of the entrance sign to the Natural Park and reading the detailed introduction written on it.

Natural Park, a paradise for Bug Type Pokemon and Grass Type Pokemon, covers a vast area and is home to countless wild Pokemon.

This place is somewhat similar to the nature reserves in other regions.

However, to protect the Pokemon's ecosystem in the Natural Park, it is not usually open to the public, nor are Trainers allowed to catch wild Pokemon inside.

But to prevent the overpopulation of Bug Type Pokemon, Sunnytown holds two bug-catching competitions every year, allowing passing Trainers to catch these Bug Type Pokemon.

Further down, there are descriptions of the types of Pokemon living in this Natural Park.

Basic ones like Caterpie, Venonat, Spinarak, Ariados, Volbeat, Illumise, Scatterbug, Cutiefly, Tarountula, Nymble, Rellor.

And some rather rare Bug Type Pokemon.

Such as Vibrava, Heracross, Scizor, Scyther, and Pinsir.

"Too bad there's no Shuckle." D felt a bit regretful; otherwise, he could have captured some Shuckle to take back to his farm to help ferment the juice.

Shuckle, the fermentation Pokemon, can break down berries with its shell to ferment them into sticky, delicious juice, very convenient.

But if D recalls correctly, there doesn't seem to be Shuckle in the Paldea Region.

The crowd buzzed around him, with determined expressions on the faces of those holding insect nets.

Some even placed bets, competing with companions on who could catch the rarer and more unusual Bug Type Pokemon.

Some mischievous kids intentionally used Bug Type Pokemon to scare the girls who accompanied their partners to the bug-catching competition, making the girls scream in fright.

D looked at the scene, speechless.

These childish kids...

"Hey, the bug-catching competition is about to start. Participants, please come to the entrance, we'll open for the Natural Park shortly."

A notification sounded over the event's broadcast.

"Additionally, please prepare before entering the Natural Park, make sure you have enough Safari Balls and snacks to attract Pokemon."

The crowd started moving toward the entrance of the Natural Park.

As the entrance opened, those enthusiastic Insect-Catching Youths immediately rushed in, scattering to various places in search of rare Bug Type Pokemon.

D, however, calmly walked to the counter by the entrance, intending to buy a few packs of Pokemon snacks from there.

The staff selling the snacks was the girl who ordered berries from his farm yesterday.

The girl recognized him, looking pleasantly surprised: "You're here?"

D smiled and nodded, looked down at the empty counter before him, and asked, "Are there any snacks and Safari Balls left?"

"There are some left! Please wait a moment, I'll get them for you now!"

With that said, the girl turned and went into the tent behind her.

D patiently waited outside, reaching out to pat the head of his Cyclizar beside him.

Koraidon didn't come along on this trip.

He didn't know what had happened; since last night, it had been looking listless, and it didn't say anything when he asked what was wrong.

This morning, D wanted to bring it here to play, but didn't expect to be refused by Koraidon.

D pondered if it was because he mentioned Cyclizar during its bath time yesterday, which made Koraidon angry?

No, Koraidon isn't the type to get angry over trivial matters.

It didn't look angry, but it seemed to be contemplating something.

D had never seen Koraidon like this before; influenced by Koraidon, he too wasn't in a very enthusiastic mood today.

Remembering Koraidon's occasional dazed looks at Cyclizar last night, D felt thoroughly puzzled.

What could it be contemplating?

Suddenly, an exclamation came from inside the tent.

"Ah!"

Cyclizar instantly became alert: "Gaw?"

D snapped back to attention, thinking something terrible had happened, and quickly followed into the tent.

Inside the spacious tent lay a large-bodied Snorlax, sleeping soundly on the ground, its dreaming paws contentedly rubbing its plump belly.

Beside this Snorlax were several destroyed empty boxes.

D was slightly stunned: "This is..."

The girl was furious: "It's Snorlax! The Snorlax that stole all our berries the day before yesterday. Damn! Now all the Pokemon snacks we prepared have been eaten by it!"

Snorlax: zzzZ

The girl was somewhat frantic: "Ah! You glutton!"

She angrily slapped Snorlax's soft, round belly.

Snorlax: zzzZ

D: "..."

Faced with such a Pokemon, there was nothing the girl could do. She took a deep breath in resignation and turned to D, saying:

---

"I'm so sorry, Mr. Rancher, but we're all out of snacks for the event..."

D wasn't really interested in capturing Bug Type Pokemon; he just wanted to join in the fun. He didn't mind and instead looked at the Snorlax in front of him with curiosity.

"Is this Snorlax a Pokemon captured by a Trainer?" D asked.

"Eh?" The girl was momentarily stunned, then shook her head and said, "This little one doesn't have a Trainer... it's always lived in this town."

As the girl spoke, she recalled, "It was about three months ago, when it was still quite cold. That's when this Snorlax arrived in town."

"It was starving at the time, and the townspeople, seeing how pitiable it was, gave it lots of food. Since then, Snorlax has settled in the town, living alongside everyone, though its gluttonous nature often causes some trouble for the townsfolk almost every day."

"We even specifically asked Trainers to see if they could capture this Snorlax, but the results were less than satisfactory..."

"Some Trainers couldn't wake this Snorlax, even by attacking with full force; they couldn't get it into the Poke Ball."

"And even the Trainers who managed to capture it didn't have enough food to feed Snorlax, so the Trainer who ran out of food ended up bringing Snorlax back."

"Although some Trainers can provide enough food, this Snorlax sleeps for almost twenty-three hours a day and doesn't help in battles, so..."

So that Trainer also returned Snorlax, D mentally filled in the words the girl didn't finish saying.

He looked at the Snorlax in front of him, thinking it was perfect for him.

Just so happens, while learning about Pokeblocks earlier, he got a Gluttony Pokeblock recipe from Yu Hui and developed a special treat specifically for Munchlax using that recipe.

This special Pokeblock only required one piece to satisfy a Munchlax's hunger.

Although Snorlax is the evolved form of Munchlax, feeding it a few more should definitely fill its stomach.

Plus, he's not a professional Trainer, just a Rancher, and he doesn't need Pokemon for battles on the ranch.

Of course, what really attracted D was Snorlax's stomach as a Pokemon.

As a famous 'big eater' Pokemon, Snorlax's stomach can digest anything.

It has a sturdy stomach that can eat moldy food without a problem and without getting sick, as the digestive juices in Snorlax's stomach can neutralize any poison, and eating things off the ground is no issue.

Even more incredibly, Muk's poison is just like a seasoning for Snorlax's tongue.

The ranch just happens to need a taste tester, and so, thinking about this, D asked, "Are you still looking for a Trainer for this Snorlax?"

The girl nodded, then realized, "Are you planning to capture this Snorlax?"

D nodded affirmatively, "I want to take it back to my ranch."

"Of course!" the girl quickly said, "Then please take good care of it!" clearly eager to have Snorlax gone.

D: "..."

It's understandable; after all, in ancient times, Pokemon like Snorlax were seen as natural disasters.

Because in the productivity-limited ancient times, Snorlax would suddenly appear in villages and clean out the granaries in no time.

Only now, as humans and Pokemon gradually blend and productivity has improved, there's no longer a fear of going hungry, allowing people to start accepting Pokemon like Snorlax.

"But... how do you plan to capture Snorlax?" the girl asked hesitantly, knowing well how tricky Snorlax could be.

D thought carefully before asking, "Did those Trainers battle this Snorlax before?"

The girl nodded, "Snorlax has thick skin, even if the attacks hit, it only tickles it. Those people used food to lure Snorlax into the Poke Ball."

"Got it." In that case, food temptation it is.

D looked around and noticed a Berry blender nearby.

Recalling the Gluttony Pokeblock recipe he developed, D asked:

"Do you have any Berries left here? I need two Pecha Berries, one Oran Berry, one Bluk Berry, two Tamato Berries, and one Nanab Berry."

"We do!" The girl nodded quickly and went to a nearby box, rummaging around until she found the needed ingredients: "These are leftover from making snacks."

D reached out to take them and then started making the necessary Pokeblocks.

About half an hour later, a steaming pot of fresh Pokeblocks was ready.

[Beginner's Gluttony Pokeblock (C+): Cooking Effect, Gluttony, a Pokeblock that gives Pokemon a sense of fullness. Just one is enough to fill the stomach of a Pokemon like Munchlax.]

It's done!

D took out six Pokeblocks.

As he was about to turn around, he suddenly felt a large shadow looming over him.

D turned to see that Snorlax had woken up at some point and was looking at the Pokeblocks in his hand with squinted eyes.

"Want some?" D asked while offering a Pokeblock.

Snorlax looked down, took it with its paw, and popped it directly into its mouth.

Chomp, chomp.

The Pokeblock was crunched and swallowed, its compressed energy quickly expanding, giving Snorlax a satisfying feeling of fullness.

Its eyes lit up, looking at the human's hand for more food.

D, however, didn't immediately give it more but reached his hand toward Snorlax intending to pat it.

Though lazy and gluttonous, Snorlax isn't dumb.

Seeing this, it extended an arm and hugged the human, letting him sit on its arm.

D was startled.

But soon, he realized Snorlax meant no harm.

The girl nearby laughed, "Don't worry. This Snorlax is very gentle, even when town kids use its big belly like a playground, it doesn't get angry."

"Open your mouth." D demonstrated, "Ah!"

Snorlax opened wide.

D tossed another Pokeblock in.

Snorlax continued to chew.

After a few more Pokeblocks, Snorlax felt rather full, yawned lazily, rubbed its eyes, then plopped down, lying on its back. It casually placed D on its belly and drifted back to sleep.

D: "..."

While Snorlax was still drowsy, D knelt on its belly and asked, "Hey, Snorlax, do you want to come with me? Stay at my place, I'll make this food for you every day!"

Snorlax: zzZ

D teased, "If you don't say anything, I'll take that as a yes, okay?"

Snorlax: zzZ

The girl, recalling Snorlax's reaction, chuckled, "Looks like it really likes you! Snorlax has never been like this with the previous Trainers."

Looking at the peacefully sleeping Snorlax, D felt a mix of emotions.

He took an empty red and white ball from his pocket and aimed it at Snorlax's forehead.

The next moment, Snorlax's mountain-like body was transformed into red light and drawn into the Poke Ball.

The Poke Ball wobbled slightly, then settled.

This indicated a successful capture.

Seeing the Poke Ball quick to stop moving, D was momentarily taken aback.

Did I really just capture this Snorlax so easily?

Well, anyhow, a successful capture is what matters.

D stood up, holding Snorlax's Poke Ball.

The girl looked at the Poke Ball, a bit worried, "Um, Mr. Rancher..."

"What's wrong?" D noticed her expression.

The girl pursed her lips then said, "This Snorlax has a huge appetite, it might surprise you, and if you regret it, please don't send Snorlax back to us."

"Though it eats a lot and often causes trouble for the townspeople, even breaking into stores and eating everything, no one wants to hurt it..."

Hearing this, D paused, then smiled and nodded, "Don't worry, I'll take good care of Snorlax!"

---

---

---

---

# 362: Chapter 359: Koraidon's Resolve, Amusement Park, Water City Encounter

After putting away the Snorlax's Poke Ball, D remembered the purpose of his visit to Sunnytown.

The girl looked at the empty snack box that Snorlax had devoured and apologized:

"I'm really sorry, the snacks for the bug-catching contest were all eaten by Snorlax. If we remake them with the remaining Berries, it might take an hour to finish..."

By this time, the first batch of Insect-Catching Youth might have already returned from the Natural Park with their captured Bug Type Pokemon.

D naturally saw the situation. He originally came to join the fun and experience the atmosphere of the bug-catching contest. It wasn't necessary for him to capture a Bug Type Pokemon.

"In that case, let's forget about making extra Pokemon snacks," D said.

The girl still wore an apologetic expression. She had invited others to participate in the bug-catching contest, yet things went awry on their part.

To make amends, she said, "I'm very sorry, but we hold two bug-catching contests a year. Besides today's, there's another one in the fall. I will write to your ranch to invite you to join."

D nodded with a smile: "Alright, I'll come over then."

With thoughts in his mind, D did not linger here long.

He glanced at the empty entrance of the Natural Park, then said to the Cyclizar beside him, "Let's go, time to head back!"

"Gaaro," the Cyclizar nodded obediently and dutifully carried D away from Sunnytown back to the ranch.

The ranch was still as lively as always. As D got off the Cyclizar, he saw Koraidon basking in the sun on the grass, sleeping.

He approached and tried calling it, "Koraidon?"

Upon hearing his voice, Koraidon immediately lifted its head and looked towards him.

"Feeling better?" D sat beside it and softly asked.

Koraidon was momentarily stunned, not expecting D to notice its mood. Immediately, the whole dragon seemed a bit forlorn: "Agasi..."

D specially ate a piece of Translation Nuts Cake, then patted Koraidon's head, saying:

"If there's anything bothering you, you can tell me. Are you upset because I favored the Cyclizar when bathing it last night?"

Koraidon widened its eyes, slightly dazed, and after a moment shook its head repeatedly: "Agasi!" (No, no!)

"Then what's the reason?" D gently probed, "Did I do something wrong?"

Koraidon: "Agasi..." (Not that either...)

It lowered its head onto its paws, with a melancholy look on its face.

How should it say such things?

Koraidon, feeling down, said: "Agasi." (I want to stay with D all the time.)

Images of the past flashed through its mind.

Little Cyclizar quietly observed it from afar, looking at it with envious eyes.

Even though Little Cyclizar was very afraid of it, it still tried hard to get closer and become good friends.

Little Cyclizar shared the gifts D bought outside with it.
