Little Cyclizar, when hearing it didn't have a mom, was willing to share its mom, though she was also its former mom.

Back then, it was so weak, so naive...

Koraidon thought it couldn't let Little Cyclizar fall into the same machine and end up in that dangerous world, just like it did.

Although it always disdained Little Cyclizar, Koraidon didn't want to see it encounter such things. It wanted to protect its past self.

Koraidon made a decision in its heart; it planned to tell D about this.

But not now.

If it talks about what's going to happen in the future, it will have to disappear. It can't say it yet because it wants to stay with D a little longer.

D didn't expect Koraidon to be troubled by this matter.

He was stunned, his thoughts slightly derailed, then he speculated:

"Did you see on TV news about humans passing away, leaving Pokemon alone and lonely in this world, and that made you sad?"

Koraidon looked at him, blinked, and then started to lie: "Agasi." (Yes.)

"Silly, but you aren't an ordinary Pokemon!" D relaxed, smiling as he patted Koraidon's head: "I haven't told you this before, right?"

"After humans pass away, they don't immediately vanish from this world. Their souls leave their bodies and linger in places they often stayed."

"Until Dusknoir, the guardian of the Spirit Realm, arrives, leading souls into the Spirit Realm for reincarnation."

"Then, if Dusknoir comes for me, it'll be up to you to protect me. I will achieve immortality in spirit form and be with you forever," D said.

Koraidon listened intently to these words.

D indeed never told it about these things before.

But soon, it remembered that it couldn't stay by D's side forever. When it spoke of those things, it would have to leave.

By then, there would be no one to protect D.

"Agasi!"

Realizing this, Koraidon suddenly got up, anxiously pacing in circles.

This won't do!

But what should it do?

Soon, a good idea popped into Koraidon's mind.

Got it! Should it teach its past self some skills so it can protect D?

Seeing Koraidon's anxious look, D said helplessly, "…Calm down. It's still too early to talk about that. I can still live for a very long time."

---

Saying death now is too inauspicious!

D quickly stopped the conversation: "Stop stop stop!"

Koraidon came to a halt.

D took a deep breath and said, "Now you get it, right? There's nothing to worry about with such things."

Koraidon obediently nodded: "Aga ssss!" (Got it!)

Seeing Koraidon's spirits lift a bit, D breathed a sigh of relief, feeling very happy, and playfully blocked Koraidon's nostrils, saying to it:

"Alright, tonight I'm setting up a floor bed and sleeping with you!"

This was the only way he could reassure Koraidon's heart.

As Alder might reflect, the future remains unknown. For them, what's most important is to seize the present and enjoy every moment spent with the Pokemon.

Hearing this, Koraidon's eyes lit up, and it pounced on D with a paw, helping to wash his face with its large tongue.

D, with his face smeared in saliva, thought: "..."

Oh well, as long as Koraidon is happy.

After Koraidon finished licking, D finally got up. "Alright, go play with everyone! I just brought back a new Pokemon from outside, and I need to arrange a place for it to stay."

Koraidon nodded.

D said goodbye to it and prepared to find a suitable spot for Snorlax in the ranch.

Koraidon then went over to Cyclizar and exchanged a look with it.

*

A suitable place for Snorlax to live in the ranch...

In fact, Snorlax as a Pokemon is very laid-back, with no particular requirements for food and shelter.

As long as it's not in extreme places like icy volcanoes, it can just lie down anywhere and sleep peacefully.

D surveyed the ranch, looking at the empty space across the river, and considering Snorlax's characteristics, he quickly had an idea.

Slides, seesaws, sandpits, tree swings, Snorlax's belly, trampolines...

D immediately went to the other side of the river, looked at the vacant land before him, and then took out Snorlax's Poke Ball to release it.

A white light flashed, and Snorlax appeared on the grass.

It continued to sleep soundly on the grass, not waking up.

"Gentle temperament... belly can be used as a trampoline..."

D thought for a moment, then carefully began to climb onto Snorlax's large belly.

Soon, he was completely lying on Snorlax's soft, bouncy belly, which felt like a waterbed.

Snorlax: zzZ

"Still sound asleep, huh!" D tried bouncing a couple of times, but Snorlax didn't mind at all, showing no reaction.

Under the blue sky and white clouds, with the gentle breeze, the view extended to the vast expanses of wide open fields.

D lay on Snorlax's belly with a face full of wonder, treating it as an outdoor bed.

Comfortable!

Not just children; even adults love Snorlax!

After lying there for a while, D reluctantly got off Snorlax's belly.

He looked at the grassy area around, and his mind automatically started planning this area.

First, a big tree should be planted beside Snorlax, with a wooden swing set under it, where he and the Pokemon could swing in the breeze.

Next, a large pit could be dug into the grass, filled with a thick layer of golden sand, for building sandcastles or sunbathing.

Finally, a slide and seesaw should be installed on the other side.

This would create a relaxing park centered around Snorlax.

The sandpit and swing set could be easily solved with money, and Calyrex could help accelerate the tree's growth.

Without delay, that afternoon, D drove to Keri to order a batch of wood and bought a tall tree sapling from Elo's seed shop.

Next, D went to the department store to buy a slide and a seesaw.

To set up the sandpit, he drove to the beach and brought back a truckload of sand to the ranch.

Once all the materials were ready, D led a group of Pokemon to start building.

Using a hammer and screws to install the slide and seesaw on the grass, and using a shovel to dig a large pit to fill it with sand.

Then, D planted the sapling on the grass and invited Calyrex over.

A burst of intense light flashed, and the sapling disappeared, replaced by a sturdy tree nearly eight meters tall.

The final step was to assemble the swing set and secure it under the big tree.

By evening, with the sky filled with twilight, D gathered around with a group of Pokemon in the small park centered around Snorlax.

"Alright, from now on you can play here!" D waved his hand emphatically and announced loudly.

But before he could finish, a bunch of excited Pokemon had already rushed to the playground.

Ogerpon was very interested in the swing, and, after a bit of exploration, it figured out how to sit on it and started swinging.

Dragonair were very interested in the children's slide. They flew up and slid down the slide, letting out "woo woo woo" sounds, sounding quite thrilled.

Pawmo and Alcremie gathered around Snorlax, and after some cautious exploration and a nod from D, they climbed up and treated Snorlax's belly like a trampoline, bouncing and playing.

---

Just watching this group of Pokemon play in the playground he built filled D with immense joy.

To foster a bond between the Pokemon and Snorlax, he specifically went back to the house, found some Berries, and made two large boxes of Gluttony Pokeblock, then handed them to Pawmot and the others.

"As a thank you for letting you play on its tummy, give this gift to Snorlax!" D said with a smile, "Don't forget to tell it 'thank you'."

"Paomo!"

Pawmot nodded its small head, reached out to take the two boxes of Pokeblock, and flew back to Snorlax's side.

Smelling the aroma, Snorlax opened its sleepy eyes.

Following that, it saw a group of small and adorable Pokemon standing in front of it, saying thank you while handing over delicious food.

Snorlax opened its mouth wide, visibly stunned by the sudden scene.

But soon, it came to its senses, took those two boxes of Pokeblock, and swallowed them whole.

Satisfied after filling its stomach, Snorlax lay back on the grass, sleeping peacefully.

Before falling asleep, it stretched out its body, revealing its big belly for the few small Pokemon to play on.

D stood at a distance watching this scene, a smile unconsciously forming on his face.

Great, the playground construction is complete.

Next, it's time to build the Wooloo Ranch and start raising those fluffy Wooloo.

But before that, he needs to have a good sleep.

After a day of busy work, D returned home, ready to enjoy the delicious dinner his sister Indeedee prepared for him.

Meanwhile, in the Johto Region, at a seaside city called Alto Mare.

After a long flight over the sea, the Heroic Expedition Team has arrived here.

This is a stunning Water City, with complex small canals running through the town, as the morning sun rises, enveloping the entire city in a gentle dawn.

The people living in this place, along with Pokemon, paddle flat-bottomed boats through the town's canals, starting a new day of life.

The bustling calls of the marketplace, the sound of water, and the ringing of bells from the city center's Great Cathedral all resound together, weaving a rich atmosphere of life.

Unnoticed at the top of the Great Cathedral, a sharp-eyed, yellow-feathered Bird-type Pokemon sits, with a satchel tied to its side.

Beside this Bird-type Pokemon stand two adorable, small-sized Pokemon.

Teddiursa stands at the top of the Great Cathedral, listening to the mellow yet slightly dull sound of the bells below, overlooking the entire big city, its eyes full of a strange sparkling light.

"Ima!" (It's so pretty!)

Mew's blue eyes subtly flicker as its gaze falls on the Pokemon running on the streets and the shops on the side selling delicacies, then it speaks:

"miu!" (Let's get something to eat in exchange!)

Teddiursa naturally has no objections.

So the two Pokemon turn their eyes to Zapdos.

Zapdos remains silent for a moment, then shows a helpless expression: "Chirp." (Go ahead, I'll wait for you to come back here.)

Its size is too large, so to avoid drawing attention, it's better to wait here.

"Ima~"

"miu~"

Mew and Teddiursa happily nod, then take a few Berries they picked in the wild from Zapdos's satchel and quietly fly down.

Just before descending, Mew silently transforms, becoming a small Totodile, and blends into the crowd with Teddiursa, running on the cobblestone road.

They arrive in front of a shop selling food, raising their heads with eager looks towards the delicious items on the counter.

The shop owner is an elderly woman with graying hair, seeing the appearance of these two Pokemon, a smile forms on her face:

"Two adorable little ones, are you hungry? Would you like to taste the candied hawthorn made by grandma? Here, have some!"

She hands out two skewers of candied hawthorn.

Teddiursa and Mew reach out to take them, then place the Berries they prepared and a few small pearls on the counter.

D said, this is an exchange.

If there's something wanted in a human town, you mustn't steal; you should use precious items to exchange with the owner.

Seeing the actions of these two Pokemon, the elderly woman is slightly startled, then her face lights up with an even happier smile:

"Ah~ Is this the payment for the candied hawthorn? What adorable little guys! Here, I'll give you these extra skewers as well!"

Feeling deep affection for such cute and well-behaved Pokemon, the elderly woman lovingly wraps up two more skewers of candied hawthorn and hands them to Teddiursa.

Having acquired the items, Mew and Teddiursa joyfully bid farewell to this human and turned to run off.

They run into an empty alley, ready to return to find Zapdos, when Teddiursa suddenly alerts, turning around to gaze behind.

"miu?"

Mew, having just returned to its original form, looks at Teddiursa's action with some confusion, turning its head, it sees a girl in a skirt standing not far away, curiously looking at them.

The two Pokemon are slightly stunned.

But that girl unexpectedly runs over to them, suddenly coming close to Mew, observing it closely.

"miu!"

Mew jumps in fright.

Beside, Teddiursa murmurs: "Ima..."

However, in the next second, the girl in front of them reveals a smile.

Soon after, she starts changing as well.

From a human girl, transforming into a mysterious Pokemon with a streamlined body!

This Pokemon has a red-colored rear half body, with wings resembling a jet engine, fins like fish, and a blue triangle mark on its chest.

Its upper body is white, with a red face, triangular ears, and a white pentagonal pattern on its forehead.

It blinks its eyes, then beams with an innocent and joyful smile.

Witnessing this, Mew and Teddiursa freeze on the spot.

---

---

---

# 363: Chapter 360: Latias and Latios? The Heroic Expedition Team's Invitation!

Alto Mare, Mysterious Garden.

Mew and Teddiursa rode on Zapdos, following a Pokemon that could transform into a human form, through a hidden passage to this tranquil garden deep within the city.

The fountain in the garden gurgled, and sunlight pouring through the dense canopy of trees cast a dreamlike, misty hue over the area.

Awed by this ethereal sight, the three Pokemon of the Heroic Expedition Team were completely taken aback.

Flying ahead, Latias squinted in delight, letting out a gentle, melodious cry.

"Woo~" (Beautiful, isn't it? This is my brother's and my secret base~)

"Ima!" Teddiursa nodded repeatedly, admiring the beauty of the place.

And at that moment, another mysterious Pokemon suddenly flew out from deep within the garden.

Mew and its companions immediately turned their gaze towards it.

Upon seeing the figure clearly, they froze in place.

Because that Pokemon looked so much like Latias!

Latios, who sensed a strange presence intruding into the Mysterious Garden, wore a cautious expression, but his vigilance vanished once he saw the Pokemon accompanying his sister.

Latias quickly introduced them to her brother: "Woo!" (Brother, this is the Heroic Expedition Team!)

Mew nodded immediately: "Miu!" (That's right! We are the Heroic Expedition Team, traveling the world, helping other Pokemon in need!)

Teddiursa followed up: "Ima Ima!" (That's right!)

The proud Zapdos lifted its head high: "Chirp!"

After their introductions, Latias turned back to introduce Mew and the others: "Woo!" (Heroic Expedition Team, this is my brother, his name is Latios!)

Latios, who had witnessed everything, looked curiously at these three unfamiliar Pokemon.

As the Guardian God of Alto Mare, he had never seen such Pokemon here before.

The way Pokemon interacted with each other was simple and straightforward.

After introducing themselves, Latias, as the younger sister, began to curiously ask what the Heroic Expedition Team was.

Thus, on this sunny morning, Mew and Teddiursa sat on the grass, happily recounting to Latias and Latios the good deeds the Heroic Expedition Team had done in the past.

They helped a massive Dragonite find its partner.

They helped a Ninetales fulfill its wish.

They met a confused Mewtwo.

They helped a Poliwag find its mother.

Latias's eyes sparkled with curiosity, undoubtedly fascinated by these tales.

Meanwhile, the more mature Latios also showed a look of amazement on his face.

He looked uncertainly at the three Pokemon in front of him and asked, "Woo?" (Heroic Expedition Team? Can you really accomplish these things with just Pokemon power?)

Upon hearing this, Mew blinked without saying a word.

Teddiursa spoke with determination in its eyes: "Ima, Ima Ima!" (The power of one Pokemon may be small, but as long as everyone works together, any difficulty can be overcome!)

Mew nodded happily: "Miu!" (That's right!)

Its azure eyes reflected the images of Latias and Latios, continuing: "Miu." (And, it's not just us! D has been helping us all along!)

Latias and Latios showed curiosity.

"Woo?" (D?)

Teddiursa nodded, believing that they had already become good friends with Latias and Latios, so it said:

"Ima Ima!" (D is the best person in the world! He even planted a World Tree where many Pokemon can reside!)

Latias curiously asked: "Woo?" (World Tree? What is that?)

Teddiursa dramatically extended its arms: "Ima!" (It's a huge tree, big enough to house the Pokemon friends we meet on our journey!)

"Ima!" (D said if we meet companions on our travels that we can't bear to part with, we can invite them to live on the World Tree, and that way, everyone can meet anytime!)

Latias listened with great interest: "Woo!" (Amazing! We're good friends now, right? Can my brother and I go there to play?)

Next to her, Mew also added cheerfully: "Miu~" (Of course~ But we still have to continue traveling, and we can't go back to the World Tree yet since it hasn't grown enough to house Pokemon.)

Latias turned to her brother with excitement, pleadingly: "Woo!" (Brother, when the World Tree grows, let's go there to play with Mew and the others!)

Latios: "..."

He earnestly told his sister: "Woo." (We cannot leave Alto Mare lightly.)

Because they are the guardians here, they must protect this island.

Upon hearing this, Latias looked disappointed.

Seeing this, Latios was silent for a moment before adding: "Woo." (However, when the time comes, you can go there to play with Mew and the others.)

Latias quickly asked: "Woo?" (What about you, brother?)

Latios: "Woo." (I must stay and protect the Soul Dew, to prevent it from being taken by villains.)

Understanding her brother's meaning, Latias let out a cheer, affectionately snuggling up to her brother.

Brother is the best!

Latios also affectionately licked his sister's forehead.

Then, Latias transformed back into the short-haired girl form she had been earlier.

She looked happily at Mew and Teddiursa and said to them: "Woo!" (This is your first time in Alto Mare, right? Let's go play! I'll take you to see this beautiful town!)

Mew and Teddiursa looked eager.

Zapdos was not interested in going out to play, as its appearance would always attract a crowd of bothersome humans.

So, before Teddiursa and Mew could speak, it made known its intention to stay here and wait for their return.

Mew and Teddiursa were already accustomed to Zapdos being this way, so they nodded and promised to bring it something delicious later.

Then they transformed into Pokemon of Latias and followed her through the tunnel connecting the Mysterious Garden to the outside, heading to the town to have fun.

To avoid drawing attention, this time Mew transformed into a small Pichu, standing on Latias's shoulder.

Meanwhile, Teddiursa was held in Latias's arms.

The three Pokemon, in this manner, happily wandered through the streets and alleys.

At this moment, the town was hosting the annual Pokemon Water Race, with participants from other places and the town's athletes racing intensely on Water Type Pokemon along the waterways.

Latias led Mew and Teddiursa into the crowd to watch.

They didn't notice a flying surveillance camera hovering above, closely following the human-shaped Latias.

"Found it." The female thief Li'an looked at the figure in the surveillance, her lips curving slightly: "The legendary infinite Pokemon, Latias."

The other female thief, Sanna, was full of excitement: "As expected! The temperature of Latias, transformed into a human, is much lower than that of a normal human. Wow, Li'an, we're going to make a fortune this time!"

"Hmph, even the legendary Pokemon are nothing special, no matter how great, they'll become part of my spoils as the number one thief in the thief world, Li'an."

The thief named Li'an put away the detector, boarded a speedboat, and said to her accomplice Sanna: "Prepare for action!"

---

---

---

# 364: Chapter 361: World Tree Growth Progress: 1/5

Early the next morning, the energy consumed by Calyrex to accelerate the growth of the World Tree had completely recovered.

He found D, who was pruning the garden, and said to him, "My power has been restored; we can continue to cultivate the World Tree."

"Oh?" D was surprised, "So fast?"

He remembered that it seemed like less than a month had passed since the last acceleration.

Calyrex nodded slightly, "That's because I have an ever-growing number of followers on this land, and the Power of Faith they provide is becoming more and more vast."

Saying this, Calyrex turned around, gazing towards the back mountain.

"I can sense that the shrine dedicated to me is also providing me with a continuous flow of faith... This situation has already far exceeded my expectations, and it truly amazes me!"

Upon hearing these words, D immediately understood.

Unbeknownst to him, the shrine on the hill had already become a landmark for Porto Marinada Town.

Almost every day, groups of Trainers passing by would come to visit the shrine, to learn about the deeds of Calyrex.

Is this situation like those popular check-in spots?

After all, anything related to the Legendary Pokemon tends to pique the curiosity of Trainers.

"Then let's begin!"

When it came to cultivating the World Tree, D was also full of anticipation.

He put down the gardening scissors in his hand, called a group of Pokemon from the pasture, and led them beside the World Tree to watch Calyrex cultivate it.

This process was familiar to every Pokemon in the pasture.

Psychic energy surged around Calyrex, and he floated into the air, facing the World Tree as energy surged all over his body.

Then, Calyrex lifted his arms and activated his ability.

In the next second, an unprecedented surge of energy poured out like a tide, all infused into the World Tree.

Soon after, every part of the World Tree—trunk, branches, leaves—began to emit a faint green light.

In this light, the trunk of the World Tree began to rise upward at a speed visible to the naked eye.

"Rumble!"

The ground beneath shook in response, and a strange humming sound resonated from all directions.

Not only the parts above ground, but even the roots deeply buried in the soil were continuously growing!

D watched the World Tree in front of him, observing its changes.

[World Tree (Sapling Stage): A seed sapling that has absorbed a massive amount of the Power of Grass, which mutated during its growth due to Gogoat, brewing a majestic Life Force, finally breaking through the earth. It is said it can support an entire world, nurturing all things with Natural Energy, still requires 84,054 years to fully grow.]

[83,612 years.]

[82,560 years.]

[...]

[80,010 years.]

The number finally settled at 80,010.

As Calyrex's energy was completely expended, the light enveloping the World Tree completely disappeared as well.

The World Tree's appearance before D and many Pokemon had once again changed.

More robust, more luxuriant, more magnificent, more lively...

No, these words are already inadequate to describe it.

Because the height of this World Tree already exceeds thirty meters!

Its scale is already comparable to that of the great tree on the Dyna Tree Hill in the northern part of the Crown Tundra in the Galar Region!

All the surrounding Pokemon were also amazed by the appearance of the World Tree.

A few flying Pokemon looked at this scene incredulously, flew around the World Tree, dived into the dense foliage, climbed onto the thick branches, and observed this giant tree from top to bottom.

D was not too surprised because he had foreseen it.

He looked at the shorter growth period of the World Tree this time and murmured:

"The growth period shortened by four thousand years at once, it seems all that additional faith didn't go to waste, directly doubling Calyrex's power..."

D remembered that originally, Calyrex could only shorten the World Tree's growth time by two thousand years at a time.

But now, with a reduction of four thousand years at once, at this speed, the World Tree could fully grow in less than two years.

One-fifth of the hundred thousand years of growth time have already passed, and there's still a path of eighty thousand years to go.

In the past, D couldn't even imagine.

But a problem followed closely.

The World Tree is so large, a few more boosts would crowd the pasture.

If he forces maturation without considering the surrounding environment, then the pasture he and the Pokemon painstakingly built will be destroyed to make way for the World Tree's expansion.

D fell into contemplation.

He turned and looked at the somewhat weakened Calyrex, who had consumed a large amount of energy, and asked:

"By the way, Calyrex, I remember you mentioned before that in the future you saw, the World Tree leaves the ground, flies to the sky, and finally floats in the air?"

Calyrex nodded slightly, "Indeed, the future I envisioned looked just like that."

"That's a tricky one..." D felt a headache.

Could it be that in the future, he installed some black technology in the World Tree, making it technologically ascend?

"No, that's impossible, I have no such technology." D had a very clear self-awareness.

Then, could it be Professor Sada and Professor Turo, who created the Time Machine?

This idea seemed unlikely too, because as far as D knew, these two professors' research did not involve anti-gravity systems.

Then, there's only one possibility left.

Relying on the power of Pokemon!

And the Pokemon capable of achieving this, Giratina immediately came to D's mind.

Three minutes later, above the Reversed World, Flower Sky Island.

Lying among the flowers, smelling their fragrance, basking in the sun, occasionally eating a few berries, Giratina looked at the suddenly arrived D, feeling quite puzzled.

"Why did you suddenly come here today?" Its temperament had become carefree due to its long retirement life.

D stared at Giratina in front of him, sizing up its big head.

Giratina: "?"

With a furious expression and a gruff voice, it shouted out loud, "What are you doing?!"

D wiped the saliva sprayed from Giratina off his face, "Great, this is the Giratina I know."

Giratina instantly had black lines all over its face.

D then started to discuss business, "By the way, Giratina, is it possible for you to make something fly? For instance, a stone in the real world, could you use your Reversing Power to make it defy gravity and soar into the sky..."

"Just that?" Giratina's gaze was disdainful, thinking it was something important.

D: "..."

Seeing this, D's mind raced with thoughts; could it be that making the World Tree levitate was really Giratina's doing?

But in the next second, Giratina showed regret, "I cannot help you."

D was dumbfounded, "Why not?"

"Because I cannot interfere with the world on the opposite side, which was the warning issued when that existence banished me to this world," Giratina's tone was somber, but D could hear the underlying anger and unwillingness.

It was Arceus...

D pondered for a moment, then sincerely asked, "Then why did you cooperate with Volo to do something like that centuries ago?"

Giratina revealed a malicious smile, "At that time, I didn't interfere with the world over there at all, I only used my power to drive Dialga and Palkia into a frenzy."

D instantly understood.

Giratina was exacting revenge, revenge against the real world and Arceus.

Secretly influencing the Space-Time Gods, causing them to riot.

The consequence of this incident was the incredible instability of space-time in the Hisuin Region, generating terrifying temporal rifts, resulting in the destruction of the Hisuin Region.

---

---

---

---

# 365: Chapter 362: Teaming Up with Giratina? Calyrex Enchants Mjolnir!

As an extraterrestrial visitor, D can detach himself from all events and view them from a third-person perspective.

Arceus banished Giratina to the Reversed World, and the enraged Giratina kept causing trouble in any way possible.

This included, but was not limited to, using the Reversing Power to influence the Space-Time Gods, driving them into a frenzy, thus affecting the real world.

In the face of this situation, what did Arceus do?

He shook someone from another space-time, invoking them to come, bestowing them with an Arceus Phone, so they could resolve the space-time rift crisis.

But those things the troublemaker Giratina did, Arceus must have known about them…

So why didn't He just directly deal with Giratina?

Just as D was lost in thought, Giratina suddenly asked:

"Have you decided? If you want my help, it means you will team up with me. If that one finds out, you will have to face Him alongside me."

"To you humans, that guy should be the Sinnoh Venerable who created the world, right? Do you have the guts to face God directly?"

D was bewildered by Giratina: "Wait, wait! I'm not trying to team up with you to destroy the world, so what if Arceus finds out?"

After all, Arceus didn't care about the bad things Giratina did before.

Now, he just wants to create a one-of-a-kind flying giant tree, how could Arceus possibly summon someone like Rei from another space-time to stop him?

Giratina, however, didn't care and roared angrily: "If I help you, how do you plan to thank me?"

D thought about the time Giratina made off with a few of his fish, and said: "...catch a few more tuna for you?"

"No!" Giratina eagerly watched D: "I want you to team up with me to get revenge on Him!"

D: "..."

He originally wanted to just say goodbye to Giratina.

But on second thought, seeking revenge on Arceus might not be impossible.

Because in a few years, when Arceus, who has fallen asleep due to human greed, awakens, He will go after those humans to punish them.

This is the plot in the movie "Arceus and the Jewel of Life."

At that time, Dialga, Palkia, and Giratina's dragon trio will join forces to prevent Giratina from destroying the world.

If he takes that chance to secretly team up with Giratina to give Arceus a beating, wouldn't he be fulfilling Giratina's wish of "getting revenge on Arceus"?

Moreover, at that time, Arceus will be missing some plates.

The five plates of Water, Grass, Thunder, Dragon, and Ground that form the Life Orb are in a missing state.

Dragon Type can be immune to Fairy Type, which means that moves of Water Type, Grass Type, Thunder Type, and Ground Type can harm Arceus.

Grass Type includes Calyrex, Gogoat, and Ogerpon, Electric Type includes Pawmot and Dragonite, Water Type can use Ogerpon's water mask to fill in the gap.

Adding in the assistance of the Space-Time Dragon and Giratina, and restoring vitality with Life Sheep Milk and other fortunate delicacies, there might really be a chance, it's just a matter of scale.

Of course, it's not that they absolutely have to beat up Arceus; thinking about such a thing seems impossible, after all, Arceus is a level 100 top existence in this world.

They just need to put on a show for Giratina to see.

In popular terms, it's like throwing a fixed match.

With this in mind, D stayed, asking: "How do you plan to get revenge on Him?"

Giratina hadn't thought it through yet.

D made the decision for him: "Then it's settled. I have a feeling that the opportunity for revenge on Arceus is coming soon, at most, in two years. Before that, you should prepare well and strengthen yourself!"

"Alright!" Giratina agreed readily.

Just two years, for it who has lived for millions of years in this world, it just needed a nap.

D was speechless, then turned and left the Reversed World.

When he returned to the living room, Calyrex casually asked: "Did you find a solution?"

"Umm, sort of?" D was also a bit troubled, he explained in detail the events that had just occurred to Calyrex, in the end, he asked: "Am I being too naive?"

After all, that's the God who created the world.

Calyrex had never seen or heard of Arceus.

But to protect the World Tree and the pasture, it spoke up: "A God who created the world? I wonder how He fares against the Darkest Day, I could give it a try."

The Darkest Day seems to refer to Eternatus in the Galar Region.

D said: "Eternatus probably isn't His match because He carries the Creation Plate; any attribute attack in this world cannot harm Him."

Hearing this, Calyrex fell into deep thought.

A moment later, He finally spoke: "Since this is the case, then we must prepare well."

D was slightly taken aback, prepare?

Calyrex cast His gaze outside: "Now is the time, my power has long been restored to its peak, now I can bestow [King's Power] on Pawmot."

Just like the coronation ceremony He held for Zacian and Zamazenta in the Crown Shrine back in the day.

At that time, He infused King's Power into the Sword and Shield.

The two Hundred Battle Heroes carrying Sword and Shield were able to transform into the King of Swords and the King of the Shield.

Hearing Calyrex's words, D was even more confused.

Calyrex is going to enchant Mjolnir for Pawmot?!

As if sensing D's inner shock at that moment, Calyrex explained:

"That child possesses a brave and pure heart, and with [King's Power], it will become a true hero."

"But before that, I must first put it through a test, and when it passes, it means it is qualified to possess the King's Power."

"Test?" D asked incredulously.

Calyrex nodded slightly: "Leave this to me; you don't need to worry."

D had calmed down, "How do you plan to do it?"

Calyrex briefly replied: "I will use my power to create an Illusion Realm."

D understood in a flash, Calyrex is, after all, Psychic Type Pokemon, interfering with the mind, and creating illusions is indeed an easy task for Him.

"I understand," D sincerely said, "Then I'll leave it to you."

Calyrex showed a loving smile, floated off the couch, out of the living room, and into the pasture.

D followed, then called over Pawmot, who was playing with Glaceon and the others.

Upon hearing D's voice, Pawmot immediately ran over: "Pawmo?"

D asked it seriously: "Pawmot, do you want to become a true superhero?"

"Pawmo!" Pawmot unhesitatingly stood up and nodded.

"Then show Calyrex your courage and heart!" D said.

Calyrex gently looked at Pawmot, and conveyed through Telepathy:

"You needn't be nervous, next I will put you through a test; when you pass, I will grant you the King's Power worthy of your courage."

"Pawmo?" Pawmot seemed to understand and not understand at the same time.

It looked at D, and D gave it an encouraging nod.

Thus Pawmot's eyes began to fill with fighting spirit: "Pawmo!"

Calyrex nodded in satisfaction: "Then, the test begins!"

---

---

---

---

# 366: Chapter 363: God-Making Project? Hero's Trial, Challenges in the Illusion Realm! King of Hammers Born!

As Calyrex's words fell, a flood of seemingly tangible psychic power erupted from its being.

The dazzling light instantly engulfed the Pawmot standing on the grass.

The next second, Pawmot was surrounded by a faint blue aura. It closed its eyes, and its body slowly began to levitate.

Its calm demeanor made it appear as if it were asleep.

Watching this scene, D realized that the illusion realm trial of Calyrex had officially begun.

"I wonder what kind of experiences Pawmot will encounter..." D pondered to himself.

But that was Pawmot's trial, one he couldn't see or participate in, so he could only wait quietly outside.

D was unsure what the King's Power entailed.

But he knew that this power could transform Zacian and Zamazenta, both Hundred Battle Heroes, into the invincible King of Sword and Shield.

Their species strength would even rise from the original 670 to 720.

Bear in mind, even the avatars of Arceus possess a species strength of only 720.

Clearly, this power represents an extraordinary enhancement for Pokemon.

With the Thunder God effect of the special dish [Lightning Steamed Bun], combined with Calyrex's [King's Power], just how far could Pawmot break through...

A Quasi-God? A Mythical Beast? Or reach the realm of Legendary Pokemon?

An excited glint flashed in D's eyes. Together with Calyrex, they were creating a deity!

*

The trees were lush, the soil fertile, and the verdant canopy blocked out the sky and sun. Green moss climbed the trunks deep within the forest, mingling with the dense undergrowth to form a vibrant woodland.

The air was filled with the earthy scent of soil, sunlight filtering through the leaves and casting a distinct beam upon a rock.

A small Pawmi lying on the rock slowly opened its eyes.

Once it saw the surroundings clearly, a look of confusion and bewilderment appeared on Pawmi's face.

"Bamo?"

Wasn't this where it used to live?

Hmm? Why say used to?

Its head was a muddle. Pawmi raised its head to gaze at the sky, feeling as though it had had a very, very long dream.

In the dream, a Bird-type Pokemon with a big beak carried it far, far away, eventually bringing it to a human's ranch, where it was taken in.

The human helped it train, taught it to fly, and even crafted a small hammer for it.

Recalling this, Pawmi rubbed the electrical pouch on its face with the paw pad, wanting to unleash a powerful electric current like in the dream.

"Bamo——"

"Sizzle..."

After much rubbing, a weak electric current emerged from its paw.

So weak...

It really must have been a dream?

For some reason, Pawmi felt a twinge of disappointment.

"Bamo Bamo?"

At this moment, two familiar calls came from the distant tall grass.

Soon, two Normal Pawmots emerged from the rustling grass and looked toward their child.

"Bamo? Bamo!" (There you are? You scared us! Time to come back and eat!)

Pawmi hesitated, suddenly feeling tears welling up in its eyes, and called out, "Bamo!" (Papa, Mama!)

Pawmot's mother gazed lovingly at her child, "Bamo～" (We're here, let's go home together～)

Pawmi nodded, jumping off the large rock, and followed its parents home.

The family lived beneath a withered tree.

A warm and dry hollow was dug out at the base of the trunk, with a passageway leading underground.

Pawmi remembered that its father and mother had painstakingly dug that burrow.

The burrow had two corridors inside, leading to the food storage room and the bedroom.

The food storage room was where they kept food; Pawmot's parents stored plenty of Berries and grains there to get through the winter.

The bedroom was a more spacious burrow where the three of them snuggled together and slept every night.

That night, Pawmi slept soundly nestled in its parents' arms.

The next morning, it bade farewell to its parents and went to play in the forest.

In this forest, there was a very tall towering tree, which some elder Pokemon in the forest claimed had been there for a really long time.

Pawmi liked climbing it, sitting at the top of the tall tree to gaze at the blue sky and white clouds, observing the world outside the forest.

Today, Pawmi visited once more, gazing at the world beyond the forest and wondering what it was like.

Suddenly, a Pokemon's terrified scream sounded from below.

Accompanied by a piercing call, Pawmi quickly looked down to find the source of the sound.

It saw a diminutive Cubone frantically fleeing among the dense woods.

Chasing closely behind it was a frightening and fierce-looking Pokemon.

Pawmi recognized that Pokemon—it had heard its parents mention it countless times.

That was a Mandibuzz! A very bad Pokemon!

It flew circles in the sky every day, observing the ground. The moment it spotted weak prey, it would attack, grabbing its target with its claws to bring back to its bone nest.

This cruel Pokemon not only ate Pokemon but also fashioned nests out of the bones of its prey, decorating itself. Its nature was extremely violent, making it the notorious bully of the forest!

---

Watching the Mandibuzz eagerly chasing the Cubone, Pawmi's heart was filled with fear.

What should it do? Should it go help that Cubone?

But now it is so weak, and the electricity it releases is so feeble...

Moreover, if that Mandibuzz finds it, it won't be able to escape either, and will be taken back to the nest along with the Cubone as prey, leaving only bones.

Thinking of this, Pawmi's face involuntarily showed a fearful expression.

Fear occupied its heart, and Mandibuzz's intimidation froze it in place, not daring to move.

While it was dazed, the Mandibuzz had already caught up with Cubone, its powerful claws pinning Cubone to the ground, pecking at it with a sharp beak.

Cubone cried out in pain.

This sound startled Pawmi from its stupor.

Seeing Cubone about to be taken away, Pawmi took a deep breath, grabbed a small stone nearby, and hurled it at the Mandibuzz.

"Pawmi!"

The provoked Mandibuzz immediately locked its dark pupils onto the tiny Pawmi.

It angrily abandoned Cubone and flew straight toward Pawmi in the tree.

Filled with fear, Pawmi instinctively turned and began to swiftly weave through the forest.

Mandibuzz followed closely, chasing relentlessly.

Not knowing how long had passed, just as Pawmi's stamina was about to be exhausted, a cliff suddenly appeared ahead.

No way forward!

Pawmi quickly halted, turning to look at the approaching Mandibuzz.

Mandibuzz squinted its eyes, letting out a piercing screech, seemingly mocking the little creature's arrogance.

Such weak Pokemon should be devoured by them!

Mandibuzz accelerated immediately, diving toward Pawmi.

Pawmi's heart raced with tension; a bead of cold sweat appeared on its forehead as it desperately rubbed its electric sacs, trying to release a current to protect itself.

But it was just too weak...

Mandibuzz's speed was too fast, and before Pawmi could generate any electricity, it was sent flying by Mandibuzz.

At this moment, the fear within broke through to the limit, and the sensation of weightlessness caused Pawmi to scream uncontrollably.

"Pawmi——"

The sharp pain spread throughout its body, and Pawmi felt like it was dead.

Yet strangely, it could still think.

All around suddenly went dark, as if time and space had stood still.

A voice filled with authority echoed from all directions.

"Are you not afraid?"

Hearing this voice, Pawmi cautiously looked around but saw nothing.

It shouted loudly to bolster its courage: "Pawmi!" (Afraid!)

The voice continued, seemingly with a hint of confusion: "If you are afraid, then why did you attract the attention of that Pokemon?"

Pawmi shook its head repeatedly, trying to wake itself up: "Pawmi, Pawmi Pawmi!" (That Cubone will be eaten, I can't just watch it be eaten!)

"Even if it requires sacrificing yourself?" the voice continued to ask.

Pawmi was somewhat puzzled, it extended its paw and looked at its pink pads, feeling like something was missing.

In a daze, some fragments appeared in Pawmi's mind.

It seemed like someone handed it a hammer, saying: "From now on, you will be a righteous superhero! Go, take this hammer and protect the weak Pokemon!"

Thinking of this, Pawmi's heart surged with courage: "Pawmi!" (I am a righteous superhero!)

The voice was silent for a moment, then spoke again: "But do you know that the Mandibuzz capturing you is also to feed its child, to it, your actions are not just."

Hearing this, Pawmi was momentarily dumbfounded.

"Pawmi..."

Its heart was at a loss—was that mean Pokemon catching it and Cubone to feed its child?

If it prevents Mandibuzz from hunting, would the chick waiting in the nest for its mother to return go hungry?

Pawmi didn't know what to do for a moment.

"What will you do?" the voice asked again, seemingly very curious.

Pawmi didn't know, it involuntarily fell into deep thought.

Pokemon must eat to grow healthily...but they don't want to be eaten as food either.

The voice didn't hurry, patiently waiting for its answer.

Pawmi thought about itself, thought about its parents, thought about the distant dream it had yesterday.

After a long time, it found an answer that belonged to itself.

Pawmi lifted its head, its young face full of seriousness: "Pawmi!" (I will share my Berry with it, let it take it back to feed its child so they won't starve!)

Hearing this answer, the voice seemed very surprised.

"Is this your answer?"

Pawmi nodded seriously: "Pawmi!" (That's right!)

"If so, then show me!"

As the words fell, everything around began to rewind.

Pawmi opened its eyes and found itself back to that very moment.

In the forest below, the Mandibuzz was chasing a Cubone.

Watching this scene, Pawmi's heart gradually steadied.

It was no longer confused because it had already found the answer to the current predicament.

---

At this moment, a powerful force suddenly surged from within its heart.

Feeling the electric energy coursing through its body, Pawmi was astonished.

But it quickly adapted to this power, leaping forward to stand in front of Mandibuzz, shielding Cubone behind it.

"Pawmot!" (Don't harm it!)

Seeing this weak Pokemon blocking its path, Mandibuzz screeched irritably.

Pawmi didn't back down an inch.

Seeing this, Mandibuzz spread its wings wide and launched a Sky Attack towards Pawmi.

Faced with this situation, Pawmi held fast to the answer it had found.

A dazzling Spark erupted from its body, and then it dashed towards Mandibuzz.

The two Pokemon collided fiercely in mid-air.

The aggressive Mandibuzz was ultimately defeated and fell to the ground.

It looked at the seemingly weak Pokemon with a face full of fear.

Just when Mandibuzz thought it was going to be finished off, it saw the Pokemon pulling out a handful of Berries from somewhere and piling them up in front of its face.

Pawmi: "Pawmot!" (Take them back and feed them to your children!)

Mandibuzz froze for a moment, then looked incredulously at the Pokemon before it.

After a moment of silence, it abandoned the hunt for Cubone, got up, and flew back to its nest with the Berries.

Watching Mandibuzz's departing figure, Pawmi finally breathed a sigh of relief.

At this time, the mysterious voice sounded again: "It seems you have found your path."

"Pawmot?!" Pawmi looked around in surprise and doubt.

Yet, everything around it gradually faded away.

The voice continued: "Even if you lose everything and revert to being the weak Pawmi you were at the beginning, you still possess the courage to face strong foes and the kind heart to help them."

"I acknowledge your true self; steadfastly follow your path and find your own answers. I feel your conviction."

"You are truly a brave one. Come now, Pawmot, accept this King's Power, and from now on, use it well to help Pokemon in need!"

With these words, Pawmot emerged from the Illusion Realm and consciousness returned to reality.

It stared blankly at everything around.

D's smile, Calyrex's majesty, the worry etched on its companions' faces...

"Looks like you've passed the trial." D said softly.

"Pawmot..."

Pawmot buried its head in his arms, hugging D tightly, crying sorrowfully.

D, puzzled, asked: "What's going on?"

Pawmot wiped away its tears and said with grievance: "Pawmot." It thought it was all a dream...

It's not a dream, that's wonderful! Meeting D is so wonderful!

Calyrex, smiling nearby, said: "It thought everything it went through with you was just a dream."

D understood immediately.

He smiled and patted Pawmot on the head: "It's not a dream, how could it be a dream?"

Pawmot, recovering its emotions, awkwardly wiped its tears: "Pawmot."

D gently patted its head and said: "You are amazing, you've passed Calyrex's trial. As far as I know, only two Pokemon have passed Calyrex's trial, and they have now become legends in a region."

Speaking of which, Zacian and Zamazenta are like your mentors, Pawmot!

Upon hearing this, Pawmot's eyes lit up, almost unable to believe it all, am I really this amazing?

D smiled and said: "Alright, go ahead now, the coronation ceremony is next."

Pawmot nodded, its little face full of seriousness.

It landed before Calyrex, looking up at this majestic Pokemon.

Calyrex slightly bowed its head and met its gaze.

"Raise your hammer."

Pawmot held the small silver hammer in both hands and offered it before Calyrex.

Calyrex gazed solemnly at the hammer and then extended its right hand to cover it.

Immediately, a golden light burst from Calyrex's body and poured entirely into the hammer.

The small silver hammer was quickly dyed in a sacred and dignified golden hue, with noble King's patterns appearing upon it.

"Pawmot!"

Witnessing this scene, Pawmot gasped.

The next second, the golden light spread over its entire body.

Pawmot felt enveloped in a tide of scorching energy, golden light bursting forth from its body.

In the light, Pawmot's appearance rapidly transformed.

The golden energy automatically condensed into a King's Helmet atop its head, with metallic armor emerging on its body.

The armor connected in one piece with the red cloak tied behind Pawmot.

The mighty energy blew the cloak flapping in the wind, and Pawmot, wearing the golden King's Helmet, looked so majestic and awe-inspiring.

Holding the golden hammer tightly in its hand, an authoritative aura emanated from Pawmot, spreading outward.

D and the fellow Pokemon watched this scene in shock.

Pawmot's King of Hammers form had been born!

---

---

---

---

# 367: Chapter 364: The Third King, Hammer of Fearlessness! Gigaton Hammer!

Is this the "King's Power"?

D looked incredulously at the changes happening to Pawmot.

Like Zacian's King of Swords form and Zamazenta's King of the Shield form, Pawmot, empowered by the King's Power, was also covered with a metallic crown resembling a helmet.

This was the appearance of being fully armed.

D suddenly thought of something interesting.

When Zacian and Zamazenta transform into their kingly forms, their type changes from purely Fairy or Fighting types to dual types of Fairy/Steel and Fighting/Steel.

So the question is, has Pawmot's type changed as well?

Has it become Electric + Steel, or Fighting + Steel?

Besides that, there is another thing that D was concerned about.

And that's Pawmot's ability.

Zacian's ability is "Intrepid Sword", which boosts attack power.

Zamazenta's ability is "Dauntless Shield", which boosts defense.

Would the ability of Pawmot's King of Hammers form change accordingly?

If it did change, it might be an ability that has never appeared before in the Pokemon World.

Four big words immediately appeared in D's mind—Unyielding Hammer!

However, D couldn't confirm these speculations.

After all, reality isn't a game, and he couldn't open a backpack to check Pokemon's detailed stats whenever he wanted.

"How do you feel?" D asked.

Unexpectedly, Pawmot, having gained new power, did not show the usual excited enthusiasm.

Instead, it exuded a calm and mature aura, vaguely revealing an air of kingly majesty.

Pawmot looked determined, and answered, "Pawmo!"

Calyrex, nearby, smiled slightly: "It seems you've grown from that trial in the Illusion Realm."

With those words, D became even more curious about the specifics of that Illusion Realm trial.

So, he took Pawmot, ate a few Translation Nuts Cakes, and listened to its story about what happened in the Illusion Realm.

Turned back into Pawmi, returned to its previous home, saw its parents, encountered Mandibuzz, protected Cubone...

Towards the end, the mature expression on Pawmot's face couldn't hold.

"Pawmo..." (Luckily, it's not a dream now...)

D smiled slightly and asked again, "Are you happy now?"

Pawmot looked at him and nodded happily: "Pawmo Pawmo!" (Happy!)

After that, Pawmot felt a pang of sadness: "Pawmo." (I wonder how my parents are doing now.)

Everything in the Illusion Realm felt real.

At first, it truly believed it had returned home, to the den its family had dug together.

Sensing Pawmot's low mood, D reached out and patted its head, comforting it:

"Don't worry, they'll be fine. I've asked the inn's guests for help, and maybe soon, we'll have news."

"Then, I'll go with you back to the forest, and we'll bring your parents to the ranch to live with us, how about that?"

"Pawmo..." Pawmot was moved, jumping into D's arms in a pout: "Pawmo~"

"Alright, alright," D laughed, hugging Pawmot.

After all, Pawmot was raised by him from a young age. Outside, it's the King of Hammers, but on the ranch, it's just his Pawmot, his chicken coop manager.

"So, can you show me the power you've gained now?" D asked.

"Pawmo!" Pawmot's eyes lit up, and it nodded.

They came to the ranch, where Pawmot flew into the air, glanced at its hammer, then called out twice to D and the other Pokemon like Alcremie, signaling them to back away.

Seeing this, D was a bit surprised. It looks like the move Pawmot is about to use is quite powerful!

He picked up Glaceon with one arm and cradled Alcremie with the other, backing away a bit with Ogerpon and the Dragonairs.

Once it was enough, Pawmot nodded.

It gripped the small hammer tightly, a firm determination flashing in its eyes.

The next moment, a miraculous scene unfolded.

The small, exquisite golden hammer suddenly became immense!

Pawmot swung it with all its might.

"Pawmo—"

Then, it smashed down suddenly.

"Boom!"

Amidst a loud crash, the earth seemed to tremble.

Alcremie and the others were utterly shocked.

D, on the other hand, had an intense gaze.

It appeared! A move just like Zacian's and Zamazenta's! Evidently, this was the power of the King's Power!

The third giant beast series move after Behemoth Blade and Behemoth Bash!

What should it be called? Gigaton Hammer?

D found it perfectly fitting.

After the demonstration, Pawmot flew back.

Seeing everyone's shocked and admiring eyes, it looked a bit embarrassed.

The next second, a light flashed over its body, and it returned to its normal form from the King of Hammers.

"Can you freely control this power to change forms?" D curiously asked.

Pawmot gave a definite answer: "Pawmo!" (That's right!)

"Amazing!" D praised, quite excitedly.

Very well, in the future, when your senior brothers and sisters battle Eternatus, I'll send you to assist!

At that time, Zacian and Zamazenta will definitely be astonished.

*

Meanwhile, in the Johto Region, in the Water City Alto Mare.

---

The owner of the Secret Garden, Ben Grey, is handing over the female thieves Sauna and Li'an, who attempted to capture Latias and Latios and steal the Soul Dew, to Officer Jenny.

"Officer Jenny, I'll leave these two thieves in your hands!"

Last night, these two broke into the Secret Garden and nearly took the Soul Dew. Luckily, the three Pokemon friends Latias recently made were there to thwart their evil plan in time.

Early this morning, Ben Grey reported them to the police, and when Officer Jenny arrived, she instantly recognized the identity of those two female thieves.

They are the top-ranking thieves in the world of thievery, Sauna and Li'an!

"I'll make sure they receive the punishment they deserve!" Officer Jenny saluted Ben Grey, then led the two thieves, who were covered in injuries inflicted by Teddiursa, into the police car.

The police car drove away.

Only then did Ben Grey breathe a sigh of relief and turn to look at Latias, who had transformed into a young girl, and the two very special Pokemon beside him.

"Alright, you've all worked hard! Teddiursa and... oh right, I haven't asked your name yet." Ben Grey looked at Mew and asked.

Mew happily covered its mouth: "miu!"

"miu?" Ben Grey laughed and said, "Your name is miu! Well, as a thank you for your help, please join me in the Secret Garden for a sumptuous lunch!"

Upon hearing this, Teddiursa and Mew happily jumped with joy.

"miu!"

"One!"

In the corner, Team Rocket's trio were stealthily observing the situation over there.

Jessie muttered in disbelief: "Those Pokemon are really amazing, managing to capture the top-ranking thieves Sauna and Li'an!"

"Hey, Meowth, quickly translate, what were those two Pokemon just saying?" James asked Meowth beside him.

Meowth muttered: "They said they're the Heroic Expedition Team, meow."

Ben Grey led the Pokemon through the tunnel back to the Secret Garden.

Latios, the brother, and Zapdos were waiting there.

Mew and Teddiursa rushed forward happily and high-fived with Zapdos.

The Heroic Expedition Team once again successfully helped a Pokemon in need, it's wonderful!

Zapdos was delighted, too.

Unbeknownst to it, it had grown accustomed to this life of traveling far and wide, much more interesting than staying at the power plant.

"One!"

At this moment, Teddiursa suddenly recalled something.

"Uh?" Latias curiously leaned in.

Teddiursa jumped onto Zapdos, reaching into the backpack it was carrying, and pulled out a camera.

Next, it's photography time!

Ben Grey was amazed to see these Pokemon equipped so well.

Could it be that they have a human helping them prepare these things?

That person must be their Trainer, right? But Ben Grey wondered, how could a Trainer feel comfortable letting their Pokemon travel alone?

However, due to the language barrier, he couldn't ask directly.

So he only stepped forward, took the camera, and helped the Pokemon take a picture.

Latias and Latios also curiously got into the frame.

With a click, the scene was frozen.

Teddiursa satisfiedly looked at the photo, then found some mud, and along with Mew and Zapdos, pressed their paw prints on the back of it.

"One one!" (Next, let's send this photo to D!)

"Uh?" Latias was puzzled by this and found it quite interesting, so it also left its handprint on it.

Zapdos nodded: "Chirp." (Since that's the case, you stay here, I'll take this photo to the ranch and give it to D, then come back to you.)

"miu～" Mew nodded happily.

And upon hearing the conversation between the two, Latias couldn't help but become very curious about this human named D.

Latias: "Uh?" (D? Is he your Trainer?)

This question left Mew and Teddiursa unsure how to answer.

Because D is a bit different from other Trainers..to be precise, it seems he is not their Trainer.

After thinking for a moment, Teddiursa answered: "One!" (D is also a member of the Heroic Expedition Team! The only human!)

Latias's curiosity about D grew stronger, so it asked for more information about D.

For example, what kind of person he is, how you encountered him, whether he made the delicious Poffin, and that it would love to meet such an amazing person.

Teddiursa and Mew picked what they knew to answer one by one, but some details they couldn't fully explain.

Therefore, both Pokemon felt a bit embarrassed.

Teddiursa thought carefully, then suddenly came up with a great idea:

"One, one one!" (Since you want to see D, why not take this opportunity to go over there with Zapdos, and after seeing D, you can come back together!)

Upon hearing this, Latias's eyes lit up: "Uh!"

That's a great idea!

It promptly turned to look at its brother, wanting to get its consent.

Latios: "..."

With things having gotten to this point, could it say a reason to refuse?

So, it spoke up: "Uh." (Go ahead, I'll wait for you here with Mew and the others.)

Latias was thrilled and affectionately rubbed its brother's neck: "Uh!"

---

After enjoying the Pokemon feast prepared by Ben Grey, Zapdos and Latias started their journey to the Paldea Region.

*

The next afternoon, D was pouring black tea into Sinistea and Polteageist when he suddenly received a phone call.

Sinistea and Polteageist, drenched in black tea and absolutely delighted, lifted their heads only to see D putting down the tea and going to answer the call.

A stranger's number appeared on the screen.

D was quite surprised. Who could be calling?

He pressed connect, and the next second, Nemona's face popped up on the screen.

"Hey there, Mr. D, remember me? I'm Nemona!" Nemona's face was adorned with a bright smile.

"Of course I remember." D replied with a smile.

Nikar, behind her, looked helpless: "Nemona, move aside a bit, let me talk to D!"

"Alright, Mr. D, I'm going to your ranch for a challenge soon, so make sure you're ready before I get there!" Nemona said enthusiastically and then stepped aside.

Nikar finally had the chance to speak with D.

He looked quite helpless: "That girl Nemona, as soon as she heard I was reaching out to you, she rushed over immediately, just like a Mankey."

Hearing that description, D couldn't help but burst into laughter.

From a distance, Nemona's voice came: "Hey, hey! Dad, I heard that!"

Nikar coughed twice, quickly changing the topic: "So, the reason I'm reaching out is about the Rotom Phone."

Rotom Phone? D's interest was piqued: "With your technology, can you develop such a device?"

Nikar shook his head: "At the moment, no, there's a crucial technology missing."

D felt a bit disappointed; these technologies should be the ones later researched by Clemont, right?

Seeing his expression, Nikar smiled and said: "But don't worry, I've already gathered all our company's top engineers to study this technology, I'm confident progress will be made soon. We plan to make the Rotom Phone our company's main focus."

D nodded, outside of trusting the engineers, there wasn't much else he could do: "In that case, I wish you all the best!"

"Thanks for the kind wishes." Nikar smiled and nodded: "As soon as there's progress in this research, I'll let you know right away."

"Okay." D nodded.

The two then hung up the call.

D returned to the sofa to lie down and watch TV with Calyrex.

It seems it'll be a long time before he can play with a smartphone!

Oh well, he's not in a hurry anyway.

D calmed down and began thinking about when to set out to Casseroya Lake to find Tropius to bring back to the ranch.

And just then, a familiar cry suddenly came from outside.

It's Zapdos!

D was a bit surprised but quickly calmed down.

By the timeframe, the Heroic Expedition Team should be sending postcards back to the ranch.

Last time, they invited Farfetch'd to the ranch. Wonder what kind of Pokemon they'll invite this time.

With that thought, D, full of anticipation, got up from the sofa and stepped outside.

As he looked closely at the figure following beside Zapdos, his face immediately turned to one of astonishment.

A streamlined body, wings like a jet plane...

Could that Pokemon be Latias?!

---

---

---
