# 368: Chapter 365: World Tree and Shadow Pokemon

D looked on in amazement at the Latias following beside Zapdos.

What's going on? Is this Latias a new friend invited back by the Heroic Expedition Team?

As everyone knows, the legendary Pokemon Latias and Latios have tribes, they are not unique.

The Johto Region has them, and so does the Hoenn Region.

So the question is, where is this Latias from?

As D looked at Latias, Latias was also curiously observing this human.

Is this the D that Mew and Teddiursa mentioned?

At that moment, Zapdos, who landed in the pasture, lowered its head and used its beak to take a photo from the bag and handed it to D.

D reached out, took it, and upon glancing at it, immediately understood the origin of this Latias.

It is the Latias from the movie "Guardians of the Water City, Latias and Latios"!

"Are Mew and Teddiursa still traveling in the Johto Region?" This thought popped into D's mind.

Because the setting of this movie is in the Johto Region, in a water town called Alto Mare.

D remembered the plot of that movie.

Two female thieves captured Latios to steal the precious Soul Dew of Water City.

In the end, their dirty hearts contaminated the pure Soul Dew, triggering a terrifying disaster that nearly submerged the entire city.

At the critical moment, Latios, as the elder brother, sacrificed himself, transforming into a new Soul Dew to protect the island.

D looked at the photo in his hand.

The background in the photo exudes a mysterious aura, with trees, grass, and a beautiful fountain.

Mew and Teddiursa stood in the center of the photo, with the taller Zapdos behind them.

Latias and Latios poked their heads from the left and right sides, joining in for the shot.

The Heroic Expedition Team participated in this movie, though it's unclear what role they played in it, whether they stopped the disaster or not…

Out of curiosity, D invited the two Pokemon into the living room to let them rest for a while and offered them juice and snacks.

Meanwhile, D ate a piece of Translation Nuts Cake.

He looked at Latias, whose eyes were filled with curiosity, and then asked, "Is your brother… Latios okay?"

Upon hearing this, Latias was momentarily dazed, then showed a look of surprise: "Huh?" (How do you know about my brother?)

D smiled and said, "I not only know, I also know that you are the Guardians of Alto Mare, always protecting the Soul Dew in the secret garden."

The sense of wonder in Latias's eyes was now impossible to conceal: "Huh!" (Amazing!)

So this is D? Just as Mew and Teddiursa said, a human who knows a lot!

"Is Latios alright?" D smiled and continued to ask.

Latias was full of confusion, seemingly not understanding D's meaning, but after thinking, it said: "Huh? Huh!" (Of course my brother is alright. He should be sleeping in the big tree in the secret garden now!)

I see… D wondered if the plot had happened or not, so he asked again: "Were the bad guys caught?"

Bad guys, Latias immediately recalled the two humans who wanted to steal the Soul Dew.

It nodded repeatedly, very curious: "Huh! Huh?" (They were caught! Ben Grey handed them over to Officer Jenny, but how do you know about it, D?)

"That's my secret!" D maintained the mystery.

Latias instantly felt this human was even more formidable!

D, looking at the photo, realized that the Heroic Expedition Team must have changed the plot by accident.

The Latios that was supposed to sacrifice itself to protect the island did not die and survived.

Heroic… truly worthy of being the Heroic Expedition Team!

D looked at the yet unaware Latias, a smile appearing on his face.

Latias didn't understand D's intention, but seeing how he asked so many questions about its brother, it thought D really liked its brother.

Latias, understandingly, said: "Huh!" (Brother is guarding the city! He can't leave there, but if you want to see him, D, you can go to Alto Mare!)

"No need." D shook his head with a smile: "I'm relieved just knowing he's safe, you are worthy Guardians."

Latias blinked in confusion, feeling it started to not quite understand the meaning of D's words.

D didn't say much either, he stood up, placed this commemorative photo into a frame and put it away, then turned and said:

"Zapdos, the World Tree should be habitable now, do you want to take a look?"

"Chirp!" (I saw it!)

Zapdos, when it was in the sky, saw the World Tree, which had grown quite a bit.

Though it hadn't reached the level Teddiursa and Mew mentioned, the current World Tree could already accommodate it.

"Do you want to stay?" D asked.

Zapdos shook its head: "Chirp!" (Not for now, I want to be with Teddiursa and the others!)

"Alright." D respected Zapdos's choice.

Meanwhile, Latias was filled with intense curiosity about the World Tree.

---

So, D led Latias outside and introduced the big tree to him.

"This is the World Tree. A few months ago, it was just a small sapling. Thanks to the cultivation of Calyrex and the Gogoats, it has grown to this size."

Latias had already heard about the World Tree from Mew and Teddiursa.

It knew the tree would grow very large and that in the future, the Heroic Expedition Team would establish a secret base on it, inviting friends met during their travels to live on the tree.

Everyone living on the tree wouldn't have to worry about never seeing each other again after parting...

Thinking of this, Latias's eyes sparkled.

So dreamy!

It quickly asked, "Woo?" (Can my brother and I come play on the World Tree too?)

D was slightly taken aback, then smiled and said, "Of course you can."

If he remembered correctly, Pokemon like Latias and Latios were very good at flying, capable of reaching speeds of Mach 4, surpassing jet planes.

Traveling between Alto Mare and the World Tree would be a breeze for them!

However, as Guardian Gods of the Water City, they probably wouldn't move their home over directly like Zapdos did.

With D's permission, Latias felt extremely happy.

It let out a cheer and couldn't wait to go back and share everything it had seen on the ranch with its brother.

Coincidentally, Zapdos had also recovered its strength and was eager to return to Mew and Teddiursa to continue traveling.

So, the two Pokemon didn't linger long at the ranch.

D, of course, understood what they were thinking. After replenishing the supplies for the Heroic Expedition Team, he bid them farewell.

"Safe travels, be careful!"

"Cheep!" Zapdos nodded slightly.

Latias happily responded, "Woo!" (D, we'll see each other again!)

D smiled softly, "Yes, I'm looking forward to it."

Watching the silhouettes of the two Pokemon disappear over the horizon, D finally withdrew his gaze.

Thinking of Latias and Latios, D suddenly had a very peculiar feeling.

Is this a chain reaction?

He had initially left behind Teddiursa, which led to Teddiursa meeting Mew.

Afterwards, the two Pokemon began traveling together, forming the Heroic Expedition Team.

Until today, even the fate of Latios was changed by them.

One has to admit, the influence of the Heroic Expedition Team is really growing!

Who would have thought that the Teddiursa, who used its cute appearance to scam food and drink, would become like this?

Just like Pawmot.

After obtaining the King's Power, it's become more mature and stable than before.

Now, Pawmot has proactively started helping D by taking on the role of foreman.

Managing Pokemon like Glaceon, Alcremie, Psyduck, and Miltank.

Playing with them, smoothing out conflicts over Berries, just like Ash's village leader Bulbasaur.

These were things that Koraidon wouldn't do, neither would Calyrex, and the Dragonites spend all day eating, working, and sleeping, much less interested in such things.

Though Ogerpon is strong, its temperament is not much different from Alcremie. It plays around the ranch all day like a child.

Looking at the current Pawmot, who would have thought it was once the small Pawmi so weak that it only dared to sneak out for chicken feed while everyone was asleep?

Normally, D didn't feel much staying with them all day, but now, upon reflection, D suddenly realized that the Pokemon around him are also constantly growing!

*

The fact that the World Tree is getting bigger and bigger has become hard to hide in Porto Marinada Town.

That evening, when Zack came to the ranch to pick up goods, he immediately noticed the extremely conspicuous big tree that had suddenly appeared there.

Zack was taken aback, staring up at the giant tree from afar, and exclaimed:

"When did that tree appear?! I remember it wasn't there yesterday, was it?"

D: "…Well, it's complex to explain, but in short, it's all because of the Pokemon's power."

Zack, knowing D had caught Calyrex, immediately realized: "It's Calyrex's power?"

"That's right," D said. "I wanted to make a giant treehouse for the Pokemon to live and play, so I asked Calyrex to help cultivate this tree."

"I see…" Zack understood but was still shocked: "But it grew so exaggeratedly! Will it continue to grow? Shouldn't the ranch be too small for it by then?"

"Yes, that's indeed a problem." D remembered his agreement with Giratina and said, "But I'll find a way to move it when the time comes."

However, where to relocate it also remains an issue...

Furthermore, if the tree is moved, what about the Gogoats and Arboliva who spend all day under the tree?

At that moment, D really wanted to borrow Doraemon's magical Shrinking Lamp, shrink the World Tree, and then when it matured, restore it to its original form and move it elsewhere.

"Alright." Zack was still a bit dazed, mumbling about how huge it was while loading the milk onto the truck.

---

Not only the townspeople, but some passing trainers were also attracted by this giant tree and came over to investigate.

D finally managed to send off a batch of trainers, only to welcome a group of students from Paldea Academy.

D's vision darkened, as he seemed to envision a continuous stream of visitors being drawn to the ranch, turning the World Tree into a horrifying tourist attraction.

No, it can't go on like this!

That afternoon, D found Giratina, asking if it could help move the World Tree into the Reversed World for cultivation.

Giratina didn't have a clue what the World Tree was.

So, D took it to the ranch and pointed to the tall tree, saying: "See that? That's it."

With just one glance, Giratina was stunned.

For it felt a natural force and life aura as vast as a mountain, or an ocean from that tree.

The life level of that tree far surpassed all the flowers and trees in this world!

"What is that?" Giratina stared with wide eyes, its tone quite shocked.

"The World Tree." D answered honestly: "I wanted to ask you to help it fly up into the sky earlier."

Giratina fell silent. It gazed at the tall tree, nearly able to foresee what changes it would bring to this world once fully grown.

No, not just this world... Perhaps its Reversed World would also be influenced, evolving in a positive direction.

Giratina unusually fell into quiet contemplation, not saying a word, looking quite intimidating.

D: "?"

After a long time, Giratina finally spoke: "Although I do not know whence it came, this tree will alter the rules of this world; you will be watched over by Him."

D was momentarily dumbfounded: "Who?"

He's just planting a tree, who would watch over him?

Soon, D realized who Giratina was referring to with Him.

It's the Creator God, Arceus!

Giratina: "This world and all of us were created by Him."

Although it is reluctant to acknowledge, this is the truth.

Time, space, emotion, wisdom, will, and anti-matter that supports the real world it represents...

All and everything, their origin is that existence.

D was stunned, hardly believing: "Arceus wouldn't come to punish me just because I planted a tree, right?"

That would make the Creator God quite petty, D thought sarcastically.

From Giratina's words, it seems this World Tree, upon maturity, would trigger evolution across the planet.

Such as the birth of more natural resources, emergence of more Pokemon, etc.

Giratina didn't know what Arceus would say about this matter, but the thought of being able to cause Him trouble made Giratina quite happy to help D with this task.

It transformed into a pitch-black shadow beneath the World Tree.

The next second, a magical scene unfolded.

The shadow seemed like a lake, within which the World Tree gradually sank.

The Gogoats were startled upon seeing this, all stepping forward to attempt to stop it.

Seeing this, D quickly restrained them: "Don't worry, the World Tree hasn't disappeared, it's just been moved to another place."

Only then did the Gogoats pause.

Soon, the World Tree was completely swallowed by darkness, leaving behind an empty grassy land as if nothing had ever happened.

D blinked, finding Giratina's ability quite impressive.

"Come on, I'll take you over there to have a look!"

D led the three Gogoats into the Reversed World.

As soon as they arrived, they saw an enormous tree standing in a void space.

The World Tree at this moment was rooted to a giant Floating Island, where an upstream waterfall provided water for the World Tree.

The Gogoats affectionately ran forward, surrounding the World Tree, using Grass Energy to cultivate it.

D thoughtfully observed the World Tree until it showed no signs of wilting or decay, then he felt relieved.

Upon reflection, it makes sense. If the World Tree needs to absorb nutrients from the soil to grow, then its scale might lead to the entire ranch being insufficient to nourish it.

The Gogoat's Grass Energy, Calyrex's Power of Fertility, just relying on these two points would be enough to provide sufficient energy and nutrients for the World Tree.

But there was still one thing that concerned D.

That is, whether the Reversing Power in the Reversed World would cause the life-filled World Tree to mutate into a Tree of Destruction?

Perhaps sensing his thoughts, Giratina suddenly spoke from not far away:

"The life level of this tree far surpasses my understanding; even with my power, I cannot change its nature."

D understood immediately upon hearing this.

He fully relaxed, saying to Giratina:

"In that case, the World Tree will be fostered here with you, for up to two years. Right, usually keep the gate connecting the two worlds open, Gogoat and Calyrex will enter regularly to cultivate the World Tree, helping it grow."

The Reversed World has soil and water to offer basic nutrients for the World Tree, sunlight is provided by the special crop Sunflower mutated from sunflower.

It is a special crop that can regularly produce sunlight to illuminate the surrounding area, just suitable for this situation.

Giratina felt quite complex inside, though it had an irritable temper, it fully committed to promises made.

Thus, it nodded, indicating it understood.

However, D was still a little uneasy.

In order to provide a better growth environment for the World Tree, he returned to the ranch to find Dugtrio, having it cultivate the enormous Floating Island where the World Tree stood, to aid its growth.

In addition, D transplanted a dozen sunflowers in, and fed Gogoat the Lucky Pudding to enhance their luck.

D: "Alright, now use your Grass Energy to cultivate these sunflowers, see if we can mutate them all into Sunflower!"

Upon learning that the sunlight produced by Sunflower could help provide nutrients for the World Tree, the Gogoats were instantly filled with vigor.

"Bleat!"

They released Grassy Terrain directly, a dazzling green glow enveloping the entire Floating Island, the World Tree and sunflowers were all covered within.

Perhaps it was heaven's favor upon the Gogoats.

Soon, one of the sunflowers began to mutate, becoming a special sunflower emitting warm light.

[Sunflower: Special crop, originally a normal sunflower, underwent some magical changes after being cultivated by Calyrex, able to periodically produce sunlight, illuminating a certain area, providing temperature and light, the dishes made from its fruit will have the effect of "Sun Stone."]

# 369: Chapter 366: Eevee Eevee Eevee!

The issue with the World Tree was resolved, and D couldn't help but sigh in relief.

Next, it's just a matter of letting the World Tree grow slowly in the Reversed World.

As for what Giratina said about changing the world's rules and being noticed by Arceus...

D felt that Arceus, being the Creator God, couldn't be so petty, and besides, the World Tree is beneficial and harmless to this world, so there was really nothing to worry about.

With the tree gone, fewer Trainers came to visit the ranch, and D's life returned to its original state.

The development of the ranch still needed to continue.

This morning, after finishing the daily work at the ranch, D set off for the ranch shop in town to buy a batch of production Wooloo.

However, when D arrived, he found only Xiaomei there.

This surprised D, and he casually asked, "Where's your grandpa?"

Xiaomei replied, "Grandpa went to the Kalos region to attend a funeral. A friend of his passed away a few days ago, and now I'm taking care of the shop."

"I see..." D nodded and didn't say much more.

Xiaomei smiled and said, "So, Mr. D, is there any Pokemon you want to bring back to the ranch today? I can help you with that."

"Wooloo, do you have Wooloo?" D asked.

"Yes, we do!" Xiaomei nodded and turned to push open the back door. "Please follow me!"

The shop's back door connected to a spacious ranch where a large number of production Pokemon lived.

D had visited a few times before and had an impression of the setup here.

Noibat resting in the woods, Miltank on the grassland, Nacli in the caves, Smoliv in the bushes, and so on.

Along the way, besides these production Pokemon, D also saw many regular Pokemon that weren't for production.

For example, Fighting Type Machop, Rock Type Sudowoodo, and Floette holding a flower.

This caught D by surprise.

"What are these Pokemon?" he asked.

Xiaomei glanced back and softly introduced, "Those are Pokemon that Grandpa rescued from the wild. Mr. D, do you remember the wildfire on the hillside last year?"

D suddenly understood.

Xiaomei continued, "Of course, many people from the town went over to help! Grandpa and I went too and rescued quite a few Pokemon."

"Grandpa originally planned to return them to the wild once they recovered, but these Pokemon seemed curious about the human world."

"So, Grandpa decided to keep them to see if he could find Trainers for them, or send them to farms to assist the farmers."

"I see!" said D.

He was very clear about the nature of this ranch shop.

It's definitely not a black market Pokemon Hunter shop, so the Pokemon sold here are very affordable, more like an adoption fee.

If placed on the black market, the price of a Miltank would probably not be less than five figures.

Xiaomei asked again, "Would Mr. D like to take a look at these Pokemon?"

Gazing at the Pokemon, D paused.

Xiaomei waved with a smile, and the Pokemon that had been on the grass came running over.

Machop, Sudowoodo, Floette, Oddish, Poliwag, Eevee...

D's eyes were drawn to the fluffy Eevee.

Feeling his gaze, the Eevee looked up and cheerfully greeted him.

"Eevee!"

"It's a very outgoing Eevee!" D thought, completely different from the Glaceon at home.

Xiaomei then stood before the Pokemon and introduced them one by one:

"This is Machop. It's very strong and can help move things with ease."

As if to prove its strength, Machop flexed its muscles.

Xiaomei moved to the next Pokemon.

"This is Sudowoodo... well, it can't help move things like Machamp, but it can serve as a decorative bonsai in the ranch! Haha, just this Pokemon is very afraid of water and hides away when it encounters it."

Sudowoodo immediately froze in place, looking just like a real tree.

Bonsai... for a moment, D didn't know what to say, but he remembered that some people really liked Sudowoodo.

There's even a magazine dedicated to Sudowoodo, with enthusiasts very particular about the length and angle of its arms.

It's said that the larger the green parts on Sudowoodo, the more popular it is among those people.

"This is Floette, don't be fooled by its small size; it's very good at taking care of flowers!" Xiaomei said, patting Floette on the head.

D looked at the Floette and immediately thought of AZ's Eternal Flower Floette.

A special variant of Floette with formidable powers, the only one of its kind in the world, with no way to obtain it even in the games.

Xiaomei continued, "And this is Oddish, it's a weed Pokemon, but its evolved form, Gloom, is very beautiful!"

Oddish, Gloom, Bellossom.

D felt a bit tempted, but thinking about the stinky nature of Gloom, he dismissed the idea.

---

Because the secretion from Gloom is incredibly stinky, it can drift up to two kilometers away and is strong enough to make people faint.

"Poliwag, a Water Type Pokémon, is very adept at using Water Gun, making it useful for irrigating fields. However, this Pokémon has already been reserved by a farm owner, so we can't let Mr. D have it!" Mei apologized.

D wasn't bothered at all: "No problem, I have Pokémon in my ranch specifically for watering."

He was referring to his own Dragonite.

"Well, there's only one left!" Mei squatted down and patted the head of the Eevee: "This little one also came from over the hill. It's very lively and cheerful, easy to get along with!"

"I could tell." D squatted down, greeting the Eevee while observing it closely.

In the real world, an Eevee looks similar to a fox.

Eevee's fur is mostly brown, with fluffy cream-colored fur around its neck and on the tip of its tail, making it look very cute.

Underneath are four fluffy legs, each with three toes and pink pads, and its long ears resemble a rabbit's. A pair of brown eyes are very lively, with a small black nose beneath them.

"Vee~"

At this moment, the Eevee was sitting on the grass, gently swaying its fluffy, big tail, tilting its head to look over, with big eyes full of innocence.

"Boom!"

D felt as if his heart had been struck by Cupid's arrow of love, pounding wildly.

He decided he wanted this Eevee!

Mei noticed his intent and chuckled lightly: "It seems Mr. D also likes Eevee, this Pokémon!"

"Hello, Eevee, my name is D, nice to meet you." D started greeting Eevee.

Eevee: (◦˙▽˙◦)

Mei looked down and asked: "Eevee, Mr. D is a very kind person. Would you like to go live with him at his ranch?"

Eevee nodded happily: "Vee!"

"It seems Eevee also likes you!" Mei smiled: "So Mr. D, please take care of this Eevee from now on."

"Okay!" D nodded firmly.

He squatted down, patted his shoulder, and said to Eevee: "Come, get up here!"

The little Eevee looked at the spot and then happily jumped up, standing on D's shoulder, much like Ash's Pikachu.

"Good, I still have a little task to do, and once it's done, I'll take you to the ranch." D turned his head to say.

His neck brushed against Eevee's soft fur, feeling very happy.

Eevee naturally had no objection, obediently standing on D's shoulder as they went to the Wooloo's habitat.

In a grass field surrounded by fences, a large group of fluffy white Wooloo were leisurely basking in the sun.

The Wooloo had black bodies wrapped in white wool, looking like large round white balls, very cute in appearance.

Mei: "Mr. D, how many Wooloo do you plan to purchase?"

D thought briefly and then said: "Six!"

If there were more, the field in the ranch would become a bit crowded.

"Got it." Mei stepped forward, opened the gate, and let D in to select six.

Wooloo bred and raised by humans are very docile and already accustomed to human presence, so they weren't alarmed by the arrival of a stranger like D.

Looking at the Wooloo, which all seemed like carbon copies, D felt they were exactly the same.

So he didn't bother choosing, and simply picked the first six in front.

After all, once they reached the ranch, he'd raise them healthy and round, it's fine if they're skinny now.

Mei nodded, then called out the six Wooloo: "Do you need my help to take them to the ranch?"

D smiled and shook his head: "No need, I drove over."

"Alright." Mei nodded, leading the six Wooloo outside the shop and setting up a wooden board to link the ground and the truck bed.

Then, the six Wooloo automatically walked up the plank.

The six Wooloo completely filled the pickup truck's truck bed.

D paid the money.

Before leaving, Mei asked again: "By the way, Mr. D, do you know how to raise Wooloo?"

D: "Feed them with grass and berries, let them bask in the sun on the grass? And regularly shear their wool?"

Mei nodded: "That's right, Wooloo's white wool continues to grow for life. Even if you shear it clean, it takes only three months to grow back."

"But be careful, if their wool grows too long, they won't be able to move. So, Mr. D, keep an eye on the wool's growth condition, and once it's grown, shear it."

D knew this, he nodded and said: "I'll remember that."

Zack over there also buys wool, and Wooloo's wool is high-quality; clothes and carpets made from it are durable, and they're popular everywhere.

Bidding goodbye to Mei, D drove off with a truck full of Wooloo, heading back to the ranch.

This time out, neither Koraidon nor Cyclizar came along.

---

Because Koraidon was teaching Cyclizar to practice moves.

When he first saw this scene, D was quite surprised, but he quickly felt happy for the friendship between these two Pokémon.

Koraidon must have been moved by Cyclizar's guts and recognized Cyclizar, which is why it is teaching it moves!

Phew... In that case, he can't fall behind either.

D was driving the car and glanced at the little Eevee sitting in the passenger seat with its seatbelt on.

What should this Eevee evolve into in the future?

There's already a Glaceon at home, so Glaceon is ruled out first.

Then...

Flareon? Vaporeon? Jolteon? Or Leafeon?

These forms of Eevee can all evolve directly, as there is a pile of Evolution Stones at home which Teddiursa brought back through Pickup.

Fire Stone, Water Stone, Thunder Stone, and Leaf Stone.

Besides these four evolutionary forms, there are also Espeon, Umbreon, and Sylveon.

There are so many types, D was also a bit indecisive for a moment.

He looked at this Eevee and found that the pattern on its tail was heart-shaped.

This meant that this Eevee was female, whereas the male Eevee's tail pattern was jagged.

"It's a pity it's not male." D felt a bit regretful.

If it were male, it might fall in love with the Glaceon at home and produce a bunch of Eevee Eggs.

Just like the working Dragonite.

Due to the imbalance of Eevee as a Pokémon, plus the instability of genetic inheritance, wild Eevee populations are extremely rare.

D planned to form an Eevee army, collecting all types of Eevee, and only two Eevee wouldn't suffice.

"Whatever, let's just live with this Eevee for a while and see!" D planned to respect Eevee's choice and see what kind of Eevee it wants to evolve into.

The pickup truck drove into the ranch, D honked the horn twice to signal the Pokémon on the road to make way, and then drove the car to the land across the river.

The fence of the Wooloo Ranch had been set up in advance, enclosing that small hillside, and now he just needed to put the Wooloo inside.

As for why the fence was set up...

It's because Wooloo, as a kind of Pokémon, would use Rollout against the fence, and it wouldn't be good if they ran outside.

Just like the Wooloo of Hop that ran into the Slumbering Weald at the beginning of the Sword and Shield storyline.

Opposite the Wooloo Ranch is the Apple Garden, but the apple trees have not grown yet.

D wasn't in a hurry, so he didn't ask Calyrex to speed them up.

After all, the important thing is the process of planting fruit trees with the Pokémon, not the result.

Not to mention that the ranch isn't lacking in funds right now.

After settling Wooloo, D then took little Eevee back across the river.

He found Alcremie and Glaceon, and introduced with a smile:

"This Pokémon is Eevee, starting today, it will be living with us on the ranch!"

Alcremie, Ogerpon, and Pawmot immediately expressed their welcome with joy.

Faced with everyone's actions, the little Eevee seemed very happy.

Its personality was originally very innocent and lively, so before long, it was playing together with the equally vibrant Alcremie, Ogerpon, and the others.

Seeing this scene, D's face showed a smile: "So you all must get along well!"

Not far away, Glaceon looked at the Eevee that had quickly become popular with everyone, and suddenly had a feeling that couldn't be described.

Moreover, more importantly, it even proactively showed Charm to D and asked D to hold it!

This kind of thing... it had never done before!

Will D prefer that Eevee? The thoughts couldn't help but arise in Glaceon's heart.

Not far away, Koraidon, basking in the sun on the grass, watched this scene and let out a helpless sigh.

"Ah gahs..."

Finally, a Pokémon understands the feelings it had back then.

---

---

---

---

# 370: Chapter 367: Eevee's Evolution Goal! Pokeblock for Snorlax, Born!

The year was 199 in the League calendar, early May, and the weather was sunny.

The summer heat was accompanied by a gentle breeze sweeping over the bursting-with-life farmlands.

The ranch ushered in the harvest season, with the fragrance of Berries bathing in the scent of fresh grass.

In the morning, D donned his straw hat, picked up the scissors, and headed out to help the Wooloo shear their wool.

It had been slightly over half a month since their arrival, and the Wooloo's wool had grown at an astonishingly fast rate, now forming a thick layer.

Coupled with the sweltering weather, this group of Wooloo appeared to be worn out and lethargic.

D opened the gate to the Wooloo pen and set about shearing the thick, dense fleece from each Wooloo.

Initially, D's skills were quite raw, prompting him to specifically seek advice from the neighboring Rancher who raises Wooloo Pokémon.

After dedicated practice, he finally mastered the technique of wool shearing.

First, prepare the specially-made electric clipper in advance.

Then position the Wooloo in a sitting posture, starting from the heel of the foreleg, continuing along the thigh to the belly, and then all the way down to the back heel.

After trimming the belly, move upwards from the lower back, shearing the Wooloo's neck.

With that, a complete fleece of Wooloo wool is trimmed.

D remembers the elderly Rancher next door telling him that the more complete the sheared wool, the more valuable it is; if it breaks midway, it won't fetch a good price.

Therefore, throughout the process, full concentration is essential to avoid breaking the wool.

Though seemingly simple, the shearing work is actually quite intricate and demanding.

After fully concentrating on shearing the wool of eight Wooloo, D felt as if his waist was about to break from exhaustion.

He glanced at the time; the entire process had already lasted nearly two hours.

A few more Wooloo, and he feared he might really ascend to heaven.

However, upon finishing the shearing and seeing the refreshed Wooloo in front of him, D felt genuinely happy.

With the thick wool shorn off, the Wooloo felt lighter and cooler, rejuvenating their spirits.

"All done with the shearing, go ahead and have fun!" D said with a smile.

The eight Wooloo lined up, marching in perfect unison up the hill like a squadron of Falinks.

This is a characteristic of Wooloo Pokémon.

They often move in groups, mimicking the behavior of their owner or the leader of the group.

The green pasture was teeming with life.

The lush grass, blooming wildflowers, Wooloo in the wooden pen, butterflies drawn by the scent of flowers...

Watching this scene, D softly uttered, "What a beautiful picture!"

He smiled, then gathered the wool on the ground and headed back.

In the big cities of the Paldea Region, handmade wool products like these are quite expensive, fetching a good price in the market.

Unfortunately, D didn't have the skills to make handmade wool carpets and scarves, so he could only sell the shorn wool.

Though the price didn't match those handmade items, it's still a significant source of income for the ranch.

Putting the wool in the warehouse, D returned to the living room to rest, have a drink, and then proceed with other tasks.

As soon as he entered the house, a few Pokémon dashed towards him.

D patted each of their heads, then moved to one corner of the living room, opened the fridge, and took out a bottle of chilled soda, gulping it down.

As D settled onto the sofa, the little Eevee skillfully jumped onto his shoulder, snuggling affectionately.

D was utterly charmed, promptly holding Eevee's front paws and playing with it.

In one corner of the living room, the Glaceon hid behind a wall, only revealing half of its face to observe stealthily.

Previously, when there was no other Pokémon of its kind, it didn't mind; but now, with another Eevee suddenly appearing on the ranch, Glaceon sensed a different atmosphere.

So D treated Eevee like that!

But why hasn't he treated it the same way? It had never received such treatment...

Glaceon, too, wished D would play with it the same way, but it felt it had already grown into a mature Eevee, and doing such things would be too embarrassing.

D didn't notice Glaceon in the corner.

Usually, his Pokémon would venture to various places to play, leaving traces even in some nooks and crannies.

For instance, the dim basement, under the bed on the second floor, behind the TV, and the roof of the house.

Thus, even if one suddenly disappeared, D was used to it, unless they hadn't returned by mealtime.

D retrieved the "Eevee Encyclopedia" he previously bought from the bookstore, a book documenting various types of Eevee.

Its author, named Eevee Fan, is an enthusiast of Eevee.

Holding Eevee in his arms, D opened the book and looked at its contents together with it.

"Look, what kind of Eevee would you like to become in the future?" D asked.

Eevee seemed puzzled: "Eevee?"

After a moment of thought, D explained, "Consider it a way to pick your direction of growth early! Once you choose, I'll help you advance toward that goal. Or do you want to remain as you are without evolving?"

---

Upon hearing this, Eevee immediately shook its head: "Vee!" It still wanted to evolve into a more powerful Eevee.

D chuckled, "Then let's have a look! So far, you can choose from eight evolution paths."

Eevee lowered its head to look at the book.

D pointed to the eight evolutionary forms of Eevee above, introducing each one:

"Flareon, a Fire Type Pokemon, can spew scorching flames from its mouth to burn its opponent."

"Vee?" Eevee showed a look of interest, appearing somewhat curious about this Flareon.

D continued, "Vaporeon, a Water Type Pokemon, can merge with water and become invisible to others. However, after evolution, it will have no fur and will be smooth like a fish in water."

Eevee shivered and immediately shook its head; it didn't want to become like that!

"This is Jolteon, capable of projecting powerful electric currents from its mouth. The stimulus makes its fur stand on end. The book says that because of the electric currents, Jolteon can become a bit neurotic and difficult to get along with."

Eevee didn't seem very interested in Jolteon.

"Then take a look at this Leafeon, a Grass Type Pokemon. Once evolved, it can use Synthesis to absorb nutrients and no longer needs to forage for food."

Eevee's eyes lit up, but thinking of delicious food, it still shook its head; it didn't want to become like that.

D smiled slightly and looked to the next one: "This is Glaceon, an Ice Type Pokemon, with a cold body capable of freezing opponents with ice and snow."

"Speaking of which, we also have a Glaceon at home. You've probably met it before, right?" D asked.

Eevee immediately thought of that cold-looking, hard-to-approach Glaceon: "Vee..."

D saw Eevee's thoughts, "Actually, it's a very kind Eevee at heart, it just doesn't show it. It lives in the little Ice House out back."

At this point, D suddenly had a flash of insight, looking up to meet the eyes of the Glaceon secretly observing from the corner.

Glaceon looked flustered and quickly drew back its head.

However, D was not ready to let it get away.

He got up and brought Glaceon over, letting it sit together with Eevee on his lap.

The two Eevees exchanged glances.

Little Eevee blinked and then greeted it warmly: "Vee!"

Glaceon's expression didn't change much; it appeared cold, turning away from Eevee.

"Vee..." Eevee's tone was very disappointed.

Hearing Eevee's sad growl, Glaceon's heart was moved, and it couldn't help but think:

Would treating the little Eevee this way hurt its feelings? It looks so young... Being treated so coldly, it must feel sad, right?

Thinking of this, Glaceon hesitated and turned back, responding softly, "Vee."

Little Eevee's eyes immediately sparkled with joy and eagerly nuzzled against Glaceon.

Being treated this way so suddenly, Glaceon was slightly taken aback.

Such an enthusiastic little Eevee, a bit like Alcremie... Looking at it this way, it wasn't so annoying after all.

The interaction between the two Pokemon was observed by D.

He introduced the little Eevee's background to Glaceon: "This Eevee survived last year's forest fire. It lost its home and can only take refuge in the town."

Glaceon knew of that fire; it had been involved at the time and had seen many Pokemon perish.

Thinking of this, Glaceon was deeply moved, looking at Eevee with a sympathetic expression, feeling this little guy was quite pitiful.

Little Eevee seemed unaware, tilting its head to look at the Glaceon in front of it, sensing it wasn't disliked as much anymore, it immediately called out happily.

"Vee!"

D petted both Eevee's heads, smiling as he said, "Alright, let's continue watching!"

"Next, I'll introduce you to Espeon."

"Unlike the previous Eevee evolutions, Espeon can use Psychic in battles. The red gem on its forehead absorbs sunlight and converts it into spiritual power, but Espeon is not adept at fighting at night."

Eevee and Glaceon looked down at the picture of their kin in the book.

They had never heard of Espeon before!

"The black one below, on the other hand, is the opposite of Espeon. Its name is Umbreon, an Eevee evolved at night capable of harnessing the power of Moonlight."

"Vee..." Eevee murmured, it didn't like the black fur and didn't want to become like that.

Glaceon didn't really feel much, as it had already evolved.

At this point, Eevee suddenly noticed the special Eevee below.

It patted the photo with its paw pad, asking, "Vee Vee?"

D glanced over and laughed, "That's Sylveon. It's cute, right? There was even a fairy tale once written by humans based on Sylveon."

Hearing this, both Eevee and Glaceon immediately became curious about this fairy tale.

"Vee?" Both Eeveelutions asked in unison.

D raised an eyebrow, "Do you want to hear it?"

"Vee!" They nodded in unison.

---

"Alright, let me tell you a story," D cleared his throat, leaned back on the sofa, and began narrating a fairy tale he had heard from the Galar Region to the two Eevees.

"Once upon a time, there was a small town named Apple Town where people lived peacefully and happily."

"Until one day, a scary Dragon-type Pokemon came to the town. It kidnapped the mayor's daughter and destroyed the apple trees, terrifying the townsfolk."

"In the midst of all this, a beautiful and kind Sylveon came to the town and, after hearing about the people's troubles, bravely set out on a journey to defeat the evil dragon."

"After some effort, the beautiful Sylveon successfully rescued the mayor's daughter and helped the people drive away the scary Dragon Type Pokemon."

"Afterward, Sylveon and the people lived happily ever after together."

Having heard this fairy tale, Eevee and Glaceon couldn't help but look on with a longing expression.

"Eevee!"

However, the tale was overheard by several nearby Dragon Type Pokemon.

Dragonite loudly retorted, "Roar!" Dragon Type Pokemon are not villains!

It is also a Dragon Type Pokemon and has never done such things!

"Roar..." Cyclizar hesitated a bit but agreed that Dragonite wasn't wrong.

Koraidon joined in the fun, "Aaarrgh!"

The three Dragon Type Pokemon stood united, demanding an explanation from D.

D: "..."

"It's just a fairy tale. If you'd like, I can tell a story tonight about brave Dragon Type Pokemon defeating villains and saving the world," he said.

"Aaarrgh!" Koraidon immediately expressed its interest; they wanted to hear the story that night!

D resigned himself and agreed, "Alright, alright, I'll tell you tonight."

Only then were the wronged Dragon Type Pokemon satisfied.

D looked at the eagerly-eyed Eevee and asked, "Have you decided? What kind of Eevee do you want to become in the future?"

Eevee resolutely extended its paw and patted the picture of Sylveon: "Eevee Eevee!"

D: "..." Looks like little Eevee has a heart for dragon-slaying!

"Have you decided?" D asked.

Eevee nodded repeatedly, "Eevee!"

"Alright, then I'll help you evolve in the direction of becoming a Sylveon,"

D said while recalling the evolution method for Sylveon in games.

It seems like it involves reaching a certain level of friendship with the trainer and learning a Fairy-type move to evolve?

In that case, it'll take some effort.

But for D, that didn't matter.

Time, after all, is something they have plenty of.

After enough rest, D let the two Eevees go off to play on their own while he took Koraidon and Cyclizar to the Berry Garden.

In about a dozen days, a new batch of berries had grown fully.

Looking at the plump, round berries on the trees, D planned to use them all to make Pokeblocks.

The amount of berries was quite large, but thanks to the help from the Pokemon, the harvesting went smoothly.

The berries that were picked were placed into baskets, and the whole garden's harvest filled up six large baskets.

D was delighted to find two Dye Berries among the batch!

[Dye Berry: A special ingredient that, when consumed, changes the Pokemon's color. It can be eaten directly or cooked.]

Last time, Pawmot turned shiny after eating one of these berries.

And now it meant that besides Pawmot, two more shiny Pokemon could appear in the ranch!

Who should it be given to?

Koraidon?

He remembers that a shiny Koraidon is black, giving a very cool and handsome vibe.

Or Eevee?

If given to Eevee, it would evolve into a shiny Sylveon, with its color changing from pink to blue, making it look even more adorable.

Decided! One of them would be given to Eevee then!

Then the remaining one...

As D was thinking about whom to give it to, he suddenly saw the Dragonairs playing and frolicking in the Berry Garden.

D's gaze fell on one peculiar pink Dragonair among them.

Everyone knows that shiny Dragonair will transform into a somewhat frightening green when it evolves into Dragonite.

Those Dragonairs are undoubtedly going to evolve.

A green Dragonite would be terrifying.

Thinking for a bit, D beckoned to those Dragonairs.

"Hmm?"

The Dragonairs curiously flew over.

D handed the remaining Dye Berry to the pink Dragonair:

"This berry can help you become like everyone else. Do you want to eat it?"

The shiny Dragonair blinked, then opened its mouth and ate the Dye Berry in one bite.

In the next moment, a radiant light burst forth from its body.

Amidst the light, Dragonair's color swiftly changed from its original pink to match the others.

The surrounding Dragonairs watched this scene in amazement, chirping excitedly, circling around their now similar-looking companion.

The Dragonair that had transformed like everyone else wore a look of disbelief.

But the expression on its face conveyed immense happiness and excitement; it showed no signs of sadness for becoming like an ordinary Dragonair.

---

"Indeed!" Watching this scene, D knew he hadn't made a mistake.

He reached out to pat the heads of the few Dragonair and said, "Alright, you continue playing, I have work to do!"

The Dragonair surrounded him, and excitedly expressed that they wanted to help out, just like the Dragonite who assisted D!

"Woo!"

Seeing the eager faces of the Dragonair, D fell silent.

He turned to glance at the baskets of Berry next to him.

Dragonair didn't have hands and wouldn't be able to help him carry the Berries, right?

Though they could hold Berries in their mouths, that would be too inconvenient.

As he hesitated, Koraidon and Cyclizar had already moved the Berries, leaving only the last basket.

Watching Koraidon come over to pick up the last basket of Berries, D could only say to the Dragonair:

"I feel your enthusiasm, but you're still too young now. Wait until you become big dragons to help out!"

"Woo..."

The Dragonair immediately showed a look of disappointment.

Becoming big dragons... does that mean evolving into Dragonite?

That's right! Once they evolve into Dragonite, they can help D, just like the Dragonite!

The Dragonair soon regained their vigor, full of determination.

It's decided!

They will work hard to become stronger to help D!

D didn't yet know the Dragonair's plan, but seeing them cheer up, he smiled and said:

"You go ahead and play, I need to get these Berries into the house."

Koraidon and Cyclizar had already run to the truck, D got in the driver's seat and drove the berry-laden pickup to the house.

Then, he began, along with the two Pokemon, to carry the Berries into the living room and the kitchen.

Some were kept for making Poffins and Berry Juice, while the rest were intended for his Pokeblock research.

Researching Pokeblocks is a highly complex task.

Some rare Pokeblock recipes aren't available to the public, so D had to research them himself.

Currently, the number of Pokeblock recipes he has mastered is limited.

Those for attracting Poipole, for feeding Munchlax...

But these aren't enough.

Therefore, D planned to spend today developing some new types of Pokeblocks.

After all, with the gluttonous Snorlax on the farm, there's no worry that the Pokeblocks made will go uneaten and wasted.

First, he aimed to improve the Munchlax snacks that Yu Hui had given him.

Munchlax is Snorlax's pre-evolution, and after evolving into Snorlax, not only does its size increase, but Munchlax's appetite grows significantly.

This has made the previous Pokeblocks insufficient.

Recalling the Berry recipe for Munchlax snacks, D picked out several Berries from the basket.

"After evolution, Snorlax's appetite grows, the previous Pokeblocks can't satisfy it. Hmm... to improve, it's necessary not only to increase Berries but also to replace some special Berries."

"Replacing Razz Berry with Rabuta Berry, compared to Razz Berry, Rabuta Berry contains more energy and heat, only then can it satisfy Snorlax's needs."

"And the flavoring with Rawst Berry..." D thought about Snorlax's unique taste, even Muk's Toxic Curry could be eaten.

This Berry's flavor is sweet, effective for humans and other Pokemon, but somewhat useless for Snorlax.

Thus, D replaced the Rawst Berry with the more caloric Wiki Berry.

Additionally, the quantity of Berries must be increased...

Thinking this, D started working.

While handcrafting Pokeblocks isn't as convenient as using a Berry Blender, it offers higher operability and potential.

Peeling, mixing, heating, stirring the Berries...

Until finally, a batch of special Pokeblocks, colored black, was fresh out of the oven.

D took a look and found it was a failed product with no effect.

"Did it fail?" D wasn't too surprised and began pondering where the problem might have occurred.

After a moment of contemplation, he attempted a second time.

Then a third, fourth time...

On the fifth attempt, the produced Pokeblocks didn't turn black, but instead showed a peculiar shade of indigo.

D realized the unusual nature of these Pokeblocks and eagerly checked their details.

[Snorlax Snack: Special Pokeblock designed for Snorlax, made using advanced Berry compression technology, compressing dozens of high-energy Berries into a special Pokeblock. Just one can satisfy Snorlax's hunger, note: ordinary Pokemon are strictly prohibited from consumption!]

"Success!"

Gazing at the Pokeblocks before him, D was delighted that his research direction was indeed correct!

This simplifies the feeding problem for Snorlax significantly.

D quickly washed his hands and recorded the Pokeblock recipe with pen and paper.

D has two notebooks.

One is the "Cookbook," which detailed the ingredients and methods needed for those special meals, along with their specific effects.

The other is the "Pokeblock and Poffin Encyclopedia," which documented the recipes and methods for Poffins and Pokeblocks, along with the special effects of Pokeblocks.

Now, there was one more recipe added to this notebook.

After recording, D embarked on the next research task with great enthusiasm.

This time, he wasn't guided by any particular research direction and worked spontaneously, combining various Berries to make Pokeblocks.

Time passed by the minute as the sun rose high and then sank into the distant horizon.

After a day of research and hard work, although he couldn't match the prowess of skilled Breeders, D did manage to figure out some basic Pokeblock recipes through his own exploration.

For example:

[Grass Pokeblock: Pokeblock to attract Grass Type Pokemon. Placed outdoors, it is guaranteed to attract Grass Type Pokemon.]

[Bug Snacks: Pokeblock to attract Bug Type Pokemon. It is highly favored by Bug Type Pokemon, placed outdoors it is sure to attract Bug Type Pokemon.]

...

"Barely usable." Looking at these Pokeblocks, D felt a bit regretful as they didn't possess any magical effects or groundbreaking features.

After a brief thought, he shared them with Butterfree, Gogoat, Arboliva, and Combee, these Bug Type and Grass Type Pokemon.

The result was unanimous praise from everyone.

D was very pleased, at least it showed that his day's work hadn't gone to waste.

After nightfall, D planned to go to bed early after dinner.

But before he could go to bed, he was stopped by several Dragon Type Pokemon from the pasture.

D: "..."

Oh well.

"Follow me!" D said.

Calyrex was watching TV in the living room, so D took a few Pokemon outside and sat down by the river where fireflies were dancing.

Koraidon, Cyclizar, and Dragonite looked over expectantly.

D had promised to tell them stories of heroic Dragon-type Pokemon defeating evil and saving the world.

Stories about Dragon Type Pokemon...

D didn't know where to start for a moment.

After all, in the Galar Region, there really is a fairy tale about Sylveon defeating Dragon-type Pokemon.

After a moment's thought, D suddenly had a great idea.

Got it! Since the Dragonite want to listen, he'd tell them the tale of Rayquaza stopping the war between Groudon and Kyogre and saving the world!

After all, Rayquaza is also a Dragon Type Pokemon.

With this in mind, D began speaking slowly:

"A long, long time ago..."

---

---

---

---

# 371: Chapter 368: Rayquaza, the Great Hero

"A long, long time ago, there were two Pokemon in this world called Groudon and Kyogre."

"One represented the earth, and the other represented the ocean. The two Pokemon constantly battled over the centuries to expand their territories."

"Their immense strength brought about torrential rains worldwide, the sky was covered in thick dark clouds."

"People living in the world saw this situation and thought the apocalypse was near, so they prayed to heaven for someone to come and save them."

"Perhaps heaven heard their prayers, suddenly, the clouds parted, and bright light shone through the layers of clouds."

"A powerful and heroic Dragon Type Pokemon descended."

"Its strength was immense, and it engaged in a great battle with Groudon and Kyogre, successfully stopping their conflict."

"In the end, Groudon, heavily chastised by the Dragon Pokemon, returned to the volcano to sleep, while Kyogre went to a deep sea cave to slumber."

"That Dragon Pokemon's name is Rayquaza."

"Witnessing this epic battle, humans built a Sky Pillar dedicated to Rayquaza to commemorate it, and within the tower, they recorded its heroic world-saving moments with murals."

The story concludes, with D seeing the three Dragon Type Pokemon before him all revealing looks of admiration.

Cyclizar: "Gao!" (Amazing!)

Koraidon looked deeply moved.

Dragonite had fully immersed itself in the story, imagining itself as the hero who saved the world.

D glanced at Dragonite, suddenly recalling that Dragonite had seen two of the Hoenn legends.

But seeing Dragonite couldn't remember, D reminded, "Dragonite, you actually met Rayquaza and Kyogre before."

"Ow?" Dragonite quickly asked.

D: "It was last year when you said you encountered a black Dragon-type Pokemon in the eye of the typhoon at sea, wasn't it?"

Hearing this, a mysterious, pitch-black figure immediately surfaced in Dragonite's mind.

A light bulb lit up on its head.

"Ow!" It remembered!

"That Pokemon is Rayquaza," D paused before continuing, "And the blue Pokemon that fought you and defeated you at sea was the Sea God Kyogre."

"Ow..." Dragonite seemed a bit incredulous, as the Pokemon hadn't revealed its name back then, causing Dragonite to always assume it was merely a rather strong Fish-type Pokemon.

Seeing Dragonite's reaction, D smiled and said, "Speaking of which, you're really lucky! You know, some humans and Pokemon may never see a Legendary Pokemon in their lifetime, yet you've seen two."

Not just two.

There were also Zapdos, Koraidon, Giratina, Calyrex, and its two steeds.

Dragonite felt incredibly excited.

So those two Pokemon were Rayquaza and Kyogre!

"Alright, the story's finished, now it's time to go to sleep."

Dragonite found itself too excited to sleep, so after D went to sleep, it wanted to take Koraidon and Cyclizar to find the great hero Rayquaza who saved the world from Groudon and Kyogre.

Koraidon thought of something, his expression turned a bit odd and he spoke: "Ah gasi..." It seemed he found the matter rather dull.

Koraidon didn't want to go, so Dragonite turned its gaze to Cyclizar, asking: "Ow?"

Cyclizar looked troubled: "Gao..."

Though it really wanted to go, it had no wings and couldn't fly!

"Ow." That indeed was a problem.

Dragonite thought carefully about a solution for Cyclizar.

Very soon, it came up with a good idea!

That was to let Cyclizar enter its Poke Ball, and then it would carry a small backpack with the Poke Ball inside and set off!

Dragonite immediately shared this suggestion with Cyclizar.

"Gao..." Cyclizar was tempted, but thinking about leaving the ranch for the outside world, D would be worried about them.

Regarding this, Dragonite vowed sincerely: "Ow ow!" (Don't worry, I'm a fast flyer. We'll be back before dawn! When D wakes up, we'll already be back!)

Seeing Dragonite's earnest demeanor, Cyclizar's eyes lit up, thinking this was indeed a good idea.

So it ran back to the living room, where Calyrex was still watching TV. It glanced at Cyclizar's sneaky figure with some surprise but said nothing.

Cyclizar found its Poke Ball on the rack and quickly darted out the door.

On its first time doing something behind D's back, Cyclizar felt nervous yet excited.

There were still Pokemon awake on the ranch.

Ogerpon was chatting with Butterfree on the grass and seeing Cyclizar and Dragonite seemed about to do something, she immediately called out:

"Boni!"

Cyclizar and Dragonite turned around, and Dragonite excitedly shared the story D had just told them with Ogerpon and Butterfree.

Learning that they were planning to find the great hero Rayquaza, Ogerpon, already filled with adventure desire and curiosity, was instantly intrigued.

---

"Poni!" (I want to go too!) Ogerpon's starry eyes were extremely bright.

Butterfree beside it was also very tempted; it had always lived in the pasture and had never seen such an existence before.

Dragonite patted its chest, speaking with a boss-like tone: "Roar roar!" (You can, but on the way, you all must listen to me!)

Ogerpon and Butterfree had no objections.

So, the two of them followed closely behind Cyclizar's steps, sneaking out their own Poke Balls from the living room, then ran into their Poke Balls.

Dragonite properly secured its milk-delivery satchel, put the three Poke Balls inside, then bid farewell to Koraidon, setting off alone on a journey to find the great hero Rayquaza.

Watching their figures disappear into the night sky, Koraidon sighed softly.

"Ahh hiss…"

*

Dragonite's journey was not smooth.

The first place it went to was the sea where it first saw Rayquaza.

Clouds floated in the night sky, a bright moon hanging high above. The sea was calm, with occasional ripples breaking the night's silence, only to quickly return to tranquility.

There was no typhoon here now, nor was there Rayquaza.

Dragonite blinked, recalling the scene of Rayquaza soaring into the sky back then, then flew towards a higher place.

They passed through the clouds and arrived beneath a majestic, grand dome.

Dragonite increased its speed, trying to find the traces of Rayquaza in this place.

However, Dragonite felt as if it had circled half of the planet, still without seeing Rayquaza.

This left Dragonite feeling a bit disappointed inside.

"Roar…" Where exactly did Rayquaza go…

Just as Dragonite was about to give up searching and return to the pasture, a sudden gust of wind sweeping past in the high sky gave it a brand-new inspiration.

Dragonite remembered, after eating the Storm Steamed Bun given by D, it could feel everything around through the flow of air!

So now, could it use this method to search for the whereabouts of Rayquaza?

Thinking of this, Dragonite immediately put it into action.

As it flew, it began to feel its surroundings through the flow of the air.

As time passed by, when the morning light faintly appeared, Dragonite suddenly opened its eyes.

The breeze brought a message: in the northeast of this place, there was a strong and familiar aura!

Dragonite's eyes lit up, and it immediately flew in that direction.

The surrounding sky gradually began to whiten, a hint of red appeared at the distant horizon, while the other side of the sky remained shrouded in night.

When Dragonite passed through the thick clouds, reaching beneath the deep blue dome, all sounds around seemed to disappear along with it.

Suddenly, everything around quieted down; looking around, there was a vast expanse of pristine white clouds ahead.

And atop a not-so-distant white cloud, a pitch-black dragon-shaped Pokemon was curled up, eyes closed, seemingly sleeping.

It was Rayquaza, the great hero who saved the world!

Dragonite's eyes lit up, feeling an immense excitement and thrill.

It immediately flew over there.

Seemingly sensing someone approaching, Rayquaza, who was originally sleeping, suddenly opened its eyes.

Golden pupils locked onto the Pokemon that intruded into its territory.

The aura surrounding Rayquaza suddenly rose, an overwhelming pressure capable of suppressing the heavens and the earth rapidly swept in all directions.

Dragonite felt this pressure, and couldn't help but show a hint of retreat, cautiously probing: "Roar?" (Rayquaza senior? Don't you remember me?)

At this moment, Rayquaza was fully awake. It gazed at that Pokemon, and upon hearing its words, it showed a look of confusion.

Dragonite proactively explained: "Roar roar!" (I'm Dragonite, we met last year! In the typhoon, I saw you!)

Rayquaza pondered for a moment, and finally remembered an image of a figure persistently breaking through the typhoon's eye, not giving up despite being cut by violent airflow.

Back then, it was moved by the Guts of that Dragonite, so it stayed to watch it for a while.

After thinking for a moment, Rayquaza asked: "Roar?" (Why have you come to seek me?)

Seeing Rayquaza recognize it, Dragonite was very happy. It then landed on that magical cloud below Rayquaza, which one could stand on, and started telling Rayquaza why it had come.

"Roar roar!" (D told us the story of how you, Rayquaza senior, defeated Groudon and Kyogre, saving this world! So we wanted to come and meet you!)

Dragonite's tone was full of admiration.

"Roar!" (Rayquaza senior is the great hero who saved the world!)

In response to this, Rayquaza felt both surprised and amazed.

But it was sure of one thing: the Pokemon in front of it had no intention of invading its territory, it seemed like they just wanted to meet it.

Just as Rayquaza wanted to say something, it saw that the Pokemon named Dragonite took out three Poke Balls from the satchel on its body.

Accompanied by flashes of white light, three ordinary Pokemon appeared on the clouds.

Ogerpon, Butterfree, and Cyclizar looked at the towering figure before them, recalling the Divine Dragon that saved the world from the story, and couldn't help but show admiration.

"Poni?" (Is this the Divine Dragon Rayquaza that saved the world? Amazing!) Ogerpon's starry eyes burst with a flash of light, filled with admiration: "Poni!" (You're amazing!)

Cyclizar also raised its head to look at the figure of Rayquaza, its heart was shocked: "Gao..." (So amazing...)

Butterfree was so overwhelmed by that terrifying pressure it couldn't speak at all.

The power of this Pokemon is simply too formidable!

Dragonite followed alongside the three Pokemon, nodding excitedly: "Ooh!" (That's right, truly impressive!)

Listening to these words of praise and gazing at these admiring eyes, Rayquaza could clearly feel that these Pokemon genuinely thought it was incredible.

Rayquaza was very happy with the flattery, it had never felt like this before, nor had any Pokemon treated it this way.

Rayquaza was completely rid of its morning grumpiness, looking at the few Pokemon in front of it, and remarked joyfully:

"Roar." (All those are past events, I only intervened back then because I sensed the pleas of humans, and thus stopped those two fellows.)

"Wow!" The group of Pokemon exclaimed in awe, so impressive!

Rayquaza burst into laughter, as if opening a box of stories, narrating its past to these adoring Pokemon.

Dragonite and Cyclizar listened eagerly.

Dragonite thought enthusiastically: No wonder Rayquaza is such a senior, neither Groudon nor Kyogre together could be its match. In the future, it too wants to become a divine dragon like Rayquaza!

In Cyclizar's eyes shone a light of admiration: Rayquaza truly is amazing, so awesome!

Butterfree, being the weakest Pokemon, didn't dare to speak with such a great being, and thus stayed quietly on the side, listening.

Ogerpon, with its outgoing personality and courage, occasionally asked Rayquaza about the details back then, such as what moves Rayquaza used to defeat those two Pokemon.

Rayquaza answered one by one, introducing its most skilled move called Dragon Ascent.

When it unleashes this move, its body turns into a green line piercing the sky.

Apparently feeling that speaking wasn't enough, Rayquaza even demonstrated to a few Pokemon.

The sky gradually brightened, and the few Pokemon who happily chatted with Rayquaza senior completely forgot about returning to the ranch before dawn.

Paldea Region, Porto Marinada Town, D Ranch.

When the sun rose from the east, D got up, ready to start his new day's work.

But he quickly discovered something amazing, several Pokemon in the ranch were missing!

D hurriedly counted the Pokemon in the ranch and finally found the missing ones were Dragonite, Cyclizar, Butterfree, and Ogerpon.

Oh no!

D was greatly shocked, countless possibilities flashed through his mind.

Could it be that some terrifying being broke into the ranch in the middle of the night and took away these Pokemon?

Beside him, Koraidon looked speechless: "Aghiss..."

Seeing that Koraidon seemed to know where they went, D quickly ate a piece of Translation Nuts Cake.

Then, Koraidon spoke: "Aghiss." (They went to find Rayquaza.)

"???"

D froze for a moment, then asked incredulously: "Went to find Rayquaza?"

Koraidon nodded its big head helplessly.

D immediately thought it might be because of the story he told last night about Rayquaza.

These Pokemon...

D suddenly felt a headache, asking: "Who led them?"

How could even the most honest Cyclizar be coaxed into this?

Koraidon hesitated and said nothing, wanting to help conceal the truth.

However, D saw through it at a glance: "It must be Dragonite!"

Back then, the only ones listening to the story were Cyclizar, Koraidon, and Dragonite. Given Cyclizar's nature, it couldn't have done such a thing, and Koraidon didn't go, it must have been Dragonite who suggested it.

Remembering this, D turned to check the Poke Ball rack at home, sure enough finding three Poke Balls missing from it.

"They sure are smart." Knowing to use Poke Balls to take the non-flying Pokemon out.

*

Above the clouds.

When the noon sunlight shone on Cyclizar's face, Cyclizar finally came back from its excited state.

It suddenly remembered something, then opened its mouth and said: "Gao?" (We should be heading back, right?)

With its words, the few Pokemon beside it also suddenly realized it was getting late.

So Dragonite stood up, reluctantly bidding farewell to Rayquaza senior.

"Ooh!" (We should be heading back, otherwise D will worry!)

Rayquaza looked at it, asking: "Roar?" (D?)

Dragonite nodded happily: "Ooh!" (That's right! That's our trainer, we live together on the ranch with him! He's the one who told us about Rayquaza senior's story!)

Rayquaza nodded, thinking that human indeed portrayed it as the great hero who saved the world in the story.

Thinking of this, looking at the few Pokemon ready to leave, Rayquaza without hesitation took out something from its body.

It was a pitch-black Dragon Scale, shimmering with a peculiar luster, appearing extremely mystical.

Rayquaza used its Dragon Claw to hand the scale to Dragonite.

Dragonite reached out to receive it, looking down at the scale, a bit puzzled: "Ooh?" (What is this?)

Rayquaza replied: "Roar." (This is a scale shed from my body, you can use it to call upon me, at that time, I will come to meet with you.)

---

---

# 372: Chapter 369: Farfetch'd, Scallion Garden, and Sky Scales

The scorching sunlight spread across the pasture, with a wave of summer heat lingering in the air.

D stood beside Glastrier's stable, cooling off, and his gaze was fixed on Farfetch'd, weaving back and forth between the wooden dummy stakes.

Farfetch'd focused intently, holding the stalk of a scallion in its right hand, its eyes slightly narrowed.

Sheathing the Sword, Iaijutsu, Iaido, Dragon Ascending...

The next second, its silhouette turned into a white light and dashed out swiftly.

"Bang, bang, bang, bang, bang!"

Several rapid strikes sounded one after another. Under Farfetch'd's superb Scallion Technique, a few already dilapidated dummies were sliced in half.

The white light faded, and Farfetch'd appeared with its back to the dummies, sheathing its sword, exuding the demeanor of a true swordsman in its every move.

"Clap, clap, clap, clap!"

Witnessing the entire process, D couldn't help but applaud: "It seems your sword skills are getting more and more advanced!"

Could it be that in the process of forgetting and relearning, Farfetch'd had gained deeper insights and understanding of its original moves?

D suddenly felt a sense of "so it can be like this?"

Farfetch'd turned its head and nodded slightly, "Quack!"

It looked down at the scallion in its hand with a troubled expression on its face.

Long-term practice had worn down this weapon it was so proud of. Now, it seemed that this scallion was not quite fitting anymore.

D noticed Farfetch'd's troubled look and curiously raised an eyebrow: "Is there something wrong with the scallion?"

As everyone knows, the scallion is a very important weapon for Farfetch'd.

If they don't have a scallion, they won't be able to survive in the wild.

So when faced with Pokémon trying to snatch their scallions, Farfetch'd will fight them desperately until they knock them out.

However, some scholars have indicated that the quality of the scallions different Farfetch'd hold seems to vary.

Good scallions can be used for a long time, while bad scallions will break after only a short period.

For this reason, wild Farfetch'd often fight each other to compete for good scallions.

Hearing D's words, Farfetch'd waved the scallion in its hand, "Quack."

D quickly saw the problem.

That scallion was not quite fitting for the current Farfetch'd.

He looked at Farfetch'd; compared to when it first came to the farm, the Farfetch'd had undergone a significant transformation.

The combination of nutritious food and continuous training had made Farfetch'd's physique stronger and its stature bigger.

This made the scallion that was once suitable now seem a bit small, like an adult holding a child's toy.

In such a situation, what would Farfetch'd do?

The answer soon became apparent.

After lunch, Farfetch'd found him, seemingly wanting to say something.

D ate a piece of Translation Nuts Cake and heard Farfetch'd say goodbye seriously:

"Quack!" (D, I'm leaving!)

D was slightly taken aback, then thought of a possibility.

Sure enough, Farfetch'd's next words confirmed his guess.

Farfetch'd: "Quack!" (This scallion doesn't suit me anymore; I need to go outside to find a fitting scallion. Thank you for your help during this time!)

D curiously asked, "Don't you want to learn those powerful sword skills?"

Farfetch'd shook its head, sincerely said: "Quack." (I have tried many times but never succeeded; maybe that kind of power doesn't belong to me.)

"Quack." (However, I've gained a lot during this time. The original techniques, I've mastered them to perfection, and their power has increased.)

"I see..." These changes were indeed within D's expectations.

He looked at Farfetch'd, who was about to say goodbye, and suddenly felt a bit reluctant.

Because the personality of this Farfetch'd really suited his taste.

After some thought, D asked, "Where do you plan to search for a scallion?"

As far as he knew, there were no Farfetch'd Pokémon in Paldea, and he wasn't sure if such stalks existed in this land.

Farfetch'd: "Quack." (I'll go out and look; I'll find one sooner or later.)

Searching for scallions is a skill every Farfetch'd is born with.

D nodded.

Speaking of scallions, he remembered that Porto Marinada Town had scallion seeds for sale...

If he helped Farfetch'd plant scallions, would Farfetch'd stay?

Thinking of this, D asked, "How about this: I'll build a Scallion Garden on the farm, fill it with scallions, and let you choose a fitting weapon. That way, you won't have to leave the farm."

Farfetch'd was taken aback: "Quack?"

Can it really be like this?

D nodded affirmatively: "Of course, maybe I can even cultivate a better variety of scallion, with superior durability and power."

Hearing this, Farfetch'd was somewhat tempted.

But it was also puzzled as to why D was helping it so much and treating it so well?

"Quack?" Farfetch'd asked, confused.

Understanding its meaning, D smiled and replied:

"Of course, it's because I want you to stay at the farm and live with us. I thought if I help you grow scallions, you wouldn't have to go into the forest to find them."

Hearing these words, Farfetch'd couldn't help but feel moved.

Previously, it was just a wild Farfetch'd with no friends in the wild, practicing swordsmanship under a waterfall all day long.

---

Back then, Farfetch'd didn't feel much, but now if it were to return to that lifestyle, it would find it incredibly dull and boring.

And staying at the ranch...

Farfetch'd turned its head and looked at the Indeedee Sibling who delivered Berry Platters to it every day.

Coincidentally, those two Pokemon were also looking this way.

Brother Indeedee's face was calm, like an adult, nodding at it.

Sister Indeedee revealed a slight smile, "Ai yi~"

Farfetch'd lowered its head, falling into deep thought.

Moments later, it nodded seriously, "Quack!"

"That's more like it!" D reached out to pet Farfetch'd's head and asked, "Then let's set off now to town to buy scallion seeds for you."

Farfetch'd nodded gratefully, "Quack!"

D smiled slightly; just as well, he didn't have much to do this afternoon, so he could build a Scallion Garden at the ranch for Farfetch'd.

The scallions grown not only could serve as weapons for Farfetch'd but also be used for cooking.

Like pancakes wrapped with scallions and such.

D went outside, brought out his pickup truck, preparing to take Farfetch'd to town to buy seeds.

Just as D was starting the car, Dragonite returned.

"What time do you think it is? You finally know how to come back." D looked seriously at Dragonite.

Dragonite was feeling very guilty under the gaze, "Aowoo..."

D: "Where are Ogerpon and Butterfree?"

Dragonite quickly took out several Pokemon, releasing Ogerpon, Butterfree, and Cyclizar.

D glanced over, seeing all of them were safe and sound, finally felt relieved.

But he didn't get out of the car, instead glancing at Dragonite and said, "We'll talk when I get back."

With that, he drove off from the ranch.

Only leaving several Pokemon standing there looking at each other in confusion.

Butterfree and Cyclizar appeared worried, and Ogerpon had lost its previous cheerfulness.

Could D be angry?

The Pokemon, like children who had done something wrong, didn't know what to do.

Ogerpon, Cyclizar, and Butterfree stayed there waiting for D to return.

Having not seen them all morning, Pawmo, Glaceon, and Alcremie, among other Pokemon, came running over to inquire about the situation.

Dragonite, feeling guilty, found Koraidon to ask for news, "Aowoo aowoo?"

Koraidon looked speechless, "Aga hiss..."

*

D drove all the way to Porto Marinada Town, buying a batch of scallion seeds from Elo Seed Store.

On the way back, D looked at Farfetch'd beside him and thought:

Farfetch'd's improved swordsmanship is also a good thing for the ranch.

Shearing Wooloo is quite a tedious task, maybe he could ask Farfetch'd to help trim Wooloo's wool.

With Farfetch'd's current skills...

A scene appeared in D's mind.

At Wooloo Ranch, Farfetch'd wielding a scallion, calmly facing a flock of Wooloo.

The next second, it raised its hand slightly; a white light flashed, and a Wooloo stood bare, shyly bleating.

The shorn wool gently fell onto the grass beside.

"Pfft!"

D couldn't help but burst into laughter.

Farfetch'd turned to look over, "Quack?"

D asked, "Farfetch'd, do you know how to trim wool?"

Trim wool? Farfetch'd looked puzzled.

D explained, "It's about helping Wooloo trim their wool; when their wool grows too long, it's uncomfortable, so it needs regular trimming, like using your swordsmanship."

Farfetch'd understood.

It imagined the image of Wooloo in its mind, then nodded, "Quack."

This sort of thing was quite easy for it.

"Really? You can?" D was surprised.

To repay D, it patted its chest assuredly, "Quack! Leave it to me."

D hadn't seen Farfetch'd trim wool before; he originally planned to let Farfetch'd try once they got back to the ranch.

But then he remembered that he had just trimmed Wooloo's wool these past days, and the new wool hadn't grown out yet.

"Then let's let you try next time," D said.

Farfetch'd indicated it was no problem.

Quickly, the truck drove into the ranch.

D turned off the engine and got out with Farfetch'd.

He turned his head to see those Pokemon lined up, obediently admitting their mistakes.

D's heart softened, and he took out a piece of Translation Nuts Cake, ate it, and asked, "Do you know what you did wrong?"

Dragonite lowered its head, pitifully responding, "Aowoo." (Shouldn't have run out.)

D: "..."

He looked toward Cyclizar, "And Cyclizar?"

Cyclizar: "Gaoow." (Shouldn't have done dangerous things.)

D then looked at Ogerpon and Butterfree.

Butterfree: "Fy ee." (I was wrong.)
