Ogerpon cautiously observed D's expression, "Ponny... Ponny ponny!" (We know we were wrong... D, don't be mad! We'll never do such things again!)

D helplessly rubbed his forehead.

"I'm not angry because you went out to play," he said.

Several Pokemon looked over.

D continued, "But the premise is you can't sneak out; before setting off, you need to tell me."

"For example, going to Dragonite Island to play with the Dragonites, if you tell me, I would certainly let you go, not only fully support but also give you some snacks to share with the Dragonites."

---

"If you all just sneak out without saying anything, you'll only make people worry, you understand?"

Seeing D didn't get angry, several Pokémon nodded repeatedly, indicating they understood.

D waved his hand: "Alright, you haven't eaten all night, right? Go find my sister to get some food."

"Rawr!" Dragonite hugged D and happily ran off.

But soon, as if it just suddenly remembered something, it turned back and took something out from its small satchel and handed it over.

D instinctively reached out to take it: "What's this?"

Looking down, he saw a pitch-black scale lying in his palm.

The scale shimmered with a strange luster, cold and hard to the touch.

Dragonite explained excitedly: "Rawr rawr!" (It's a scale given to us by Rayquaza seniors! They said we can use this scale to summon them, and then they'll come play with us!)

D: "???"

"Wait, what did you say?" D was momentarily stunned.

Dragonite looked puzzled but still repeated itself.

D's emotions suddenly surged like a tidal wave as he looked at the scale in his hand with disbelief and asked:

"Rayquaza's Dragon Scale? You guys found it?"

Ogerpon: "Bonnie bonnie!" (Rayquaza is a very nice Pokémon! It chatted with us for a long time, and even showed us its skill in defeating Groudon and Kyogre!)

Cyclizar looked hopeful: "Gaarow!" (So awesome!)

Butterfree nodded repeatedly: "Fwi fwi!" (That's right!)

D: "..."

His first reaction was to think how bored Rayquaza must have been to chat with these Pokémon for an entire night and morning.

But soon, D was amazed by Dragonite's unbelievable luck.

First, it went to an underwater cave and found Kyogre, then went searching for Rayquaza after hearing about its exploits, and even made connections with it.

So the question arose, what if he introduced other Legendary Pokémon to Dragonite?

With this thought, D suddenly became interested and said to Dragonite:

"Let me tell you the myth of Arceus. Next time, see if you can find Arceus and bring it back."

Dragonite: "..."

"No, no way, Arceus is probably still asleep in the Alternate Space now." D shook his head.

Then what about Ho-oh and Lugia?

If possible, he could really tell Dragonite about the legend of Ho-oh and the Rainbow Hero!

But obviously, these are somewhat unrealistic.

Looking at the scale in his hand, connecting it with the previous feathers of Zapdos and Lugia, D started to think about making a dish for Pokémon out of Rayquaza's Dragon Scale.

But looking at Dragonite, he felt this thing might be quite important to Dragonite.

After all, it could be considered a testament to the friendship between Rayquaza and Dragonite.

And since Rayquaza said Dragonite could use it to summon them, it meant Rayquaza could sense this Dragon Scale.

In this situation, if he ground the scale into powder to make dishes, he couldn't tell how Rayquaza would regard them.

It might think Dragonite destroyed their token of friendship, and then just cut ties with Dragonite.

The more severe possibility would be that it angrily came over to fight.

After some thought, D abandoned the idea of making it into a dish and handed the Dragon Scale back to Dragonite.

"Keep it safe!"

Dragonite extended its claw to receive: "Rawr?"

D suddenly thought of something quite interesting.

He said to Dragonite: "If you run into Kyogre again, and it attacks you, you can use this scale to summon Rayquaza for help."

---

---

---

---

# 373: Chapter 370: Dragonair's Resolve: Growing Stronger to Help on the Ranch!

"Alright, go play!"

D waved at a few Pokemon, then grabbed some scallion seeds and, together with Farfetch'd, arrived at a plot of land.

This land was originally planted with a crop of spinach, which he had just harvested the other day, and hadn't yet had time to sow the next batch of crops.

Now, it was just right to be used for building the Scallion Garden for Farfetch'd.

He called Dugtrio over and asked it to help plow the land.

Dugtrio chirped once and then quickly started its work.

It plowed the land in front while D followed behind planting scallions.

Soon, the entire plot of farmland was filled with scallions.

D patted the dirt off his hands, turned back to Farfetch'd, and asked, "Are you eager to change your weapon?"

Farfetch'd looked at the scallion in its hand and shook its head, "Quack!"

It could still use this old scallion for a while longer.

"Well, let's leave it at that for now," D nodded. "Next, we just need to water and fertilize these scallions regularly. Before long, they'll grow."

Farfetch'd looked expectantly, "Quack!"

Seeing this, D smiled, then called Gogoat over, asking them to use Grass Type Energy to help Farfetch'd cultivate the scallions.

Gogoat agreed happily and surrounded the Scallion Garden, releasing green Grass Energy from their bodies to nurture the scallions buried in the soil.

Now with its mind set on scallions, Farfetch'd even stopped practicing its Scallion Techniques.

Growing its own scallions was something it had never thought of doing.

Previously, when Farfetch'd lived in that forest, there was a food crisis.

To cope with the crisis, it ate the emergency scallion in its hand and had to run far away to find another scallion.

Wild Farfetch'd were like this; when their scallions went bad, they found new ones, never thinking of growing them themselves.

Now, it had the chance to see so many scallions growing simultaneously and, once they all matured, it could pick the strongest scallion to serve as its new weapon.

Just the thought of it filled Farfetch'd with immense joy.

"Quack!"

It's decided! It wanted to ask D how to plant scallions and cultivate them personally!

So, as soon as D returned home, he saw Farfetch'd coming up to him.

Upon hearing its thoughts, D pondered briefly and then said:

"Dragonite is responsible for watering, and Gogoat will also help cultivate the scallions."

Farfetch'd felt a bit disappointed, "Quack." Was there nothing it could do?

"Hmm..." D thought carefully and suggested, "You could use your Scallion Techniques to watch over the Scallion Garden, preventing any wild Pokemon from causing damage."

Previously, there were wild bird Pokemon on the ranch that secretly entered the fields to eat the corn and wheat.

Initially, D even placed a scarecrow in the fields.

But it turned out the scarecrow had no effect on those bird Pokemon, as they were much smarter than ordinary birds.

Later on, it was Koraidon who drove away the wild Pokemon.

It didn't even need to take action—just lounging on the grass next to the field and releasing its Dragon Type Pokemon pressure was enough to keep the bird-like Pokemon away.

And for Koraidon, it was a chance to bask in the sun.

Hearing that there was something it could do, Farfetch'd immediately puffed up its chest with a promise, "Quack!"

This task was left to it! It would certainly keep the fields secure!

To express its gratitude to D, Farfetch'd included the other fields in its patrol range.

"Thanks for your hard work!" D chuckled.

Farfetch'd marched off with its scallion.

From that day on, Farfetch'd began its role as a guard in the fields.

To protect its beloved scallions and ensure their healthy growth, Farfetch'd diligently patrolled between the furrows from morning till night with its scallion in hand.

Life on the ranch continued.

With Farfetch'd's situation resolved, a few Dragonair playing around the ranch found D and asked for help.

The next morning, D was repairing a fence knocked over by Wooloo at Wooloo Ranch, and a few Dragonair chattered around him in a language he couldn't understand.

"Hold on!" D paused his work, ate a slice of Translation Nuts Cake that allowed him to understand Pokemon speak, and then continued, "Alright, go ahead!"

Dragonair's eyes glittered, "Roo!" (D, we want special training too!)

"Hmm?" Hearing this, D felt quite surprised.

The weather was a bit hot, so as he sipped iced water, he asked, "Why's that? Aren't you all happy with life now? Why suddenly want special training? It's quite exhausting!"

The Dragonair shook their heads unanimously, their faces filled with determination.

One of them said, "Roo!" (We're not afraid!)

Another nodded and said, "Roo!" (Indeed! We want to evolve into Dragonite to grow arms so we can help D!)

"Pfft—" D nearly spat out his water.

He looked incredulously at the Dragonair.

Though he secretly hoped that once Dragonair evolved, they could assist with deliveries along with the working Dragonites, he never thought they'd suggest it themselves.

What was this? The Dragonair had such high awareness?

The Dragonair exchanged glances, seemingly puzzled by D's reaction.

D wiped his mouth, set down the water bottle, and asked, "Why are you suddenly thinking of helping me work?"

The Dragonair blinked, talking joyfully and innocently:

"Ro-ro-roo!" (Because D always makes delicious Poffins and pastries for us. D is so good; we want to help him too!)

D understood, realizing the Dragonair were expressing their gratitude.

Hearing such sincere words, D felt deeply touched.

Pokemon...this is what Pokemon are all about!

Inherently simple and grateful, if you treat them well, they reciprocate.

D took a deep breath, patting the heads of each Dragonair affectionately, moved:

"Good kids, I understand—I appreciate your sentiments."

"Roo~"

The Dragonair affectionately nuzzled against him.

D couldn't help but smile.

Decided! From now on, the Dragonair family is his favorite Pseudo-legendary Pokemon!

To reciprocate the sentiments of these five Dragonair, D immediately enrolled them in the training class Koraidon held for Cyclizar.

"Koraidon, please take care of the Dragonair! Teach them some Dragon Type moves and help them grow!"

"Cyclizar, from now on, Dragonair are your classmates. Koraidon's strength is far greater than yours, so you can spar with the Dragonair to gain experience through battles."

"I'll often come by to oversee your progress!"

Koraidon sighed helplessly, "Ah-gas..."

But Cyclizar seemed delighted, looking at the Dragonair and welcoming them, "Gao!"

Meanwhile, across the river at the Snorlax Park.

Dragonite, Ogerpon, Butterfree, Glaceon, Eevee, Pawmot, Alcremie, and other Pokemon gathered there.

They surrounded a napping Snorlax, forming a circle, looking at the black scale on Dragonite's claw.

"Pawma...?"

Pawmot had a puzzled expression. Through this scale, could they really summon that world-saving hero Pokemon?

Yesterday afternoon, Dragonite and Ogerpon recounted to it and Glaceon their extraordinary encounter that night and the tale of the great hero Rayquaza.

Pawmot and Glaceon were extremely curious.

To prove it to them, after watering the fields, Dragonite called several Pokemon over to the Snorlax Park.

It planned to summon Rayquaza and invite it to the ranch for a visit to rekindle their bonds.

---

---

---

---

# 374: Chapter 371: Dragonite’s Call, Rayquaza’s Visit!

That day they left in a hurry, and they still had many things they hadn't had time to say to Rayquaza.

Not only that, but Rayquaza also told them it always lived in the sky.

At that time, several Pokemon felt that Rayquaza was truly too pitiful.

A single Pokemon living in the sky, without food, without a soft bed to sleep in, exposed to the elements, even in the cold winter staying outside, only able to gnaw on stones floating among the stars when hungry.

Thus, after some discussion among several Pokemon, Dragonite and Ogerpon decided to invite Rayquaza to the ranch as a guest.

They had already prepared a sumptuous spread to entertain Rayquaza.

On the grass of Snorlax Park, a blue checkered picnic cloth was laid out, covered with a variety of colorful Berries and an assortment of dishes made by D.

There were sweet Pokeblocks, crisp Poffins, small biscuits filled with a creamy aroma, and cakes baked with eggs and MooMoo Milk.

The picnic cloth was requested by Ogerpon from his brother Indeedee.

The Berries were picked by Dragonite from the Berry Garden.

The Pokeblocks and Poffins were taken by Pawmo from the refrigerator.

The small biscuits and cakes were brought by Alcremie with the help of Garganacl.

Now, everything was ready except calling Rayquaza to come over.

Thinking of this, Ogerpon and Pawmo turned their gaze to Dragonite.

Dragonite held a scale, staring intently, focusing its thoughts to sense the scale, and earnestly called out to Rayquaza.

One second, two seconds, three seconds...

Half the day passed, and there was still no response on the scale.

"Aowoo..."

Dragonite was very disappointed.

Because when Rayquaza gave it the scale, it didn't explain how to use it.

Seeing this, the other Pokemon also didn't know what to do.

They looked up at the sky, murmuring, "Mamak?" (Will Rayquaza come?)

"Boni..." (I don't know...)

Pawmo commented regretfully, "Bomo." (It's a hero that saved the world; it would be great if we could just see it once.)

Meanwhile, in the Hoenn Region, Mossdeep City.

The satellite at the Mossdeep Cosmic Center suddenly detected a strong magnetic field fluctuation.

To prevent satellites from being damaged and to explore the mysterious cosmos, each satellite launched into space is equipped with an energy camera, capable of capturing real-time images around it.

As soon as the magnetic field fluctuation appeared, the monitor sitting at the console immediately brought up the real-time satellite images.

The next second, a scene appeared on the electronic screen in front of the monitor.

The scene was of outer space, the vast and boundless mysterious cosmos in the distance, and a beautiful blue planet below.

Suddenly, a pitch-black figure appeared in the satellite images.

They saw the creature flying to a meteorite and starting to eat.

Staring at the screen, the monitor exclaimed, "That Pokemon is..."

Senior staff at the Mossdeep Cosmic Center appeared one after another.

They stared at the pitch-black Pokemon, and soon someone recognized the mysterious Pokemon.

"That silhouette... isn't it the Legendary Pokemon, the Sky God, Rayquaza?!"

"Living in the ozone layer, able to freely travel to outer space, that's it, no doubt about it!"

"It's truly astounding! Such a majestic and magnificent figure!"

"What's it doing? Is it feeding?"

In the cosmos, Rayquaza, while absorbing energy from the meteorite, looked up at the distant floating machine.

It knew what that thing was.

The thing was man-made, flying from Earth into space.

At first, Rayquaza was attracted to this thing, thinking it was an enemy invading the sky.

But it quickly realized the thing posed no threat to it, so it simply left it alone, letting these machines ascend into the cosmos.

Just then, Rayquaza's eating suddenly paused.

As if sensing something, it turned its body to look at the beautiful blue planet below.

The scale vibrated, seemingly responding to something, someone on the ground was calling out to it.

After a brief hesitation, Rayquaza stopped eating, turned around, and flew in that direction.

At the Mossdeep Cosmic Center, astronauts watched the reaction of the Legendary Pokemon, looking puzzled and shocked.

"It stopped eating!"

"By the way it looks, did it discover something?"

"What could make Rayquaza stop eating be?"

A senior officer contemplated for a moment, then ordered, "Track Rayquaza's movements!"

The Mossdeep Cosmic Center not only undertakes the exploration of the cosmos but also acts as the Hoenn Region's safety alert center.

Unlike other regions, the Hoenn Region has always faced threats.\p>

To deal with these potential disasters, the Hoenn League established a dedicated department within the Mossdeep Cosmic Center, continuously monitoring the Hoenn Region's conditions.

Such as earthquakes, tsunamis...

Should the legends prove true, and the Earth God Groudon and the Sea God Kyogre awaken and start their battles, they can detect it immediately and formulate response strategies, sending Champions to intervene.

The satellite tracked Rayquaza's magnetic field fluctuations back to the planet.

On the radar detection map on the screen, the small red dot representing Rayquaza moved rapidly.

It crossed the Hoenn Region, passed through the Unova Region, yet Rayquaza did not stop.

"Report, we've reached the limit, Rayquaza has exceeded the radar's maximum detection range!" the monitor reported.

On the screen, the small red dot representing Rayquaza disappeared.

The senior officials were shocked and puzzled.

According to legend, Rayquaza is not one to casually visit the land.

It lives high above in the sky, rarely appearing.

Only when the Earth God Groudon and the Sea God Kyogre start their conflicts does it descend to stop them.

Yet now, Rayquaza has left space.

"What is it that's attracting Rayquaza?"

*

In the Paldea Region, Porto Marinada Town, D Ranch.

On the grass, Dragonair was in a tense Pokemon battle with Cyclizar.

At this moment, the battle had reached a fever pitch.

Dragonair opened its mouth, purple energy gathering inside, attempting to defeat Cyclizar with Dragon Rage.

But Cyclizar had clearly more battle experience than Dragonair.

It executed the Divine Technique "Dodge," then swung its Dragon Tail, hitting Dragonair.

"Great, Cyclizar, that Dragon Tail was beautifully executed!"

"Dragonair, don't give up! Hang in there!"

D watched as Dragonair and Cyclizar sparred, cheering them on.

But just then, D suddenly saw Calyrex appear in front of him in a flash.

Calyrex looked serious.

"Huh?" D looked over, "What's wrong?"

Calyrex looked up at the sky, saying, "I sense a powerful aura approaching."

D was stunned, looking bewildered, "What's happening?"

The sparring Pokemon stopped in confusion.

Koraidon walked up to D and looked up at the sky with him.

"Ahh, hiss..."

The next second, a massive pitch-black figure descended here, enveloped in a whirlwind, as the clouds roiled and stormed in the sky.

---

---

---

---

# 375: Chapter 372: Rayquaza Reaches for the Stars

Rayquaza!

It's Rayquaza!

D recognized the suddenly descending Flying Dragon Pokemon instantly, and it was shiny too!

Rayquaza's size was extremely large, and its gaze swiftly scanned through the ranch.

D clearly felt Rayquaza's gaze cast this way, and the palpable fear it induced made his spirit instantly tense.

What on earth is happening? Why did Rayquaza come?

He clearly didn't make that scale into a dish!

But regarding this question, D soon figured out the answer.

Because he saw Dragonite flying over from the other side of the river, barking and calling out to Rayquaza.

Hearing Dragonite's voice, Rayquaza shifted its gaze away from them and then turned its head to look at Dragonite.

Afterward, it twisted its pitch-black body, flew towards Dragonite, and then landed together in Snorlax Park, not sure what for.

D: "..."

Well, now he knew why Rayquaza came to the ranch.

D felt speechless about Dragonite's behavior.

It really called Rayquaza over, and it's been only half a day and a night, barely making up twenty-four hours.

Seeing Rayquaza, Cyclizar was immediately attracted.

It looked towards Snorlax Park with a face full of longing, seemingly wanting to go play too.

Cyclizar looked eagerly at D: "Gaoh..."

D helplessly waved: "Go, go!"

Cyclizar happily ran off.

The Dragonairs followed curiously.

Suddenly, the grassland was left with only D, Koraidon, and Calyrex.

Koraidon didn't show any surprised expression, nor did it have any desire to go join the fun.

Calyrex was the same, gazing at the phenomenon, its mind inevitably drifting to recall the future it once saw.

On the towering World Tree, a completely black Flying Dragon Pokemon spiraled.

"Didn't expect it to come now?" Calyrex felt somewhat taken aback.

It thought the other party would come a long, long time later.

"Really surprised me for a moment." D said helplessly.

He didn't expect Rayquaza in this world to be so trustworthy, so idle...

But thinking carefully, Rayquaza usually doesn't have much to do other than eat and sleep, living a very dull life.

And under such circumstances, when some admiring Pokemon suddenly find it, praising and wanting to hear more of its stories, Rayquaza having such a reaction is quite natural.

Seeing that there wasn't much going on here, Calyrex floated back to the living room to continue watching TV.

D stood in place, looking across the river at Snorlax Park, somewhat unsure about what he should do next.

Rayquaza's visit was unexpected, disrupting D's original plans.

Rayquaza, that legendary Rayquaza, the Legendary Pokemon most humans never see once in a lifetime.

D was very curious about what the Pokemon group would talk about with Rayquaza.

He thoughtfully ate a few pieces of Translation Nuts Cake, took Koraidon, and pretended to have a reason to clear weeds in the Apple Garden, intending to secretly observe Rayquaza and the Pokemon gathering.

D went across the river with a straw hat.

Meanwhile, the group of Pokemon, including Alcremie and Pawmo who hadn't seen Rayquaza, were flattering Rayquaza.

"Marma!" (So amazing!)

"Pamo pamo!" (I also want to work hard to become a great hero!)

"Bui!"

The little Eevee's eyes shone brightly, looking at the Dragon-type Pokemon before it, feeling that Dragon-type Pokemon wasn't as evil as D's stories suggested.

Moreover, Rayquaza senior saved the world, which was truly impressive!

It also wanted to be so impressive, but could Eevee evolve into a Dragon-type Pokemon?

The little Eevee didn't know, so it quickly turned to ask Glaceon: "Bui bui?"

"Bui..." Glaceon shook its head, indicating it hadn't heard of it; after brief speculation, it suggested, "Bui." (You can ask D later.)

The little Eevee's eyes lit up: "Bui!"

A group of Pokemon sat around the blue striped picnic cloth; Dragonite pointed at the food atop it, hinting Rayquaza senior to eat.

"Owu!"

So when D walked over, he saw Rayquaza lowering its head and biting a Pokeblock off the picnic cloth to eat.

Looking at this scene, D's heart didn't know what to feel for a moment.

Rayquaza actually ate the food he made...

Are Dragonite and the others entertaining Rayquaza?

D suddenly noticed the picnic cloth and food surrounded by the Pokemon group in the middle.

Seeing Rayquaza swallow the Pokeblock down, the other Pokemon also picked up the Berries and Poffins in front of them, happily enjoying them.

With Dragonite and Ogerpon's enthusiastic entertainment, Rayquaza ate a lot, and soon the food in front was eaten up.

At this point, finally, a Pokemon noticed D and Koraidon.

Alcremie joyfully ran over, saying, "Marma!" (D, you're here!)

"Yes, I'm here to tend to the apple saplings." D said, his expression unchanged, his face maintaining a smile: "Are you entertaining Rayquaza?"

"Marma!" Alcremie nodded and then requested: "Marma?" (D, can you make some more delicious food? Rayquaza senior is too big! It seems it didn't get enough to eat.)

---

D looked at Rayquaza with a complicated expression.

Seemingly afraid of being overheard by Rayquaza, Alcremie jumped onto D's shoulder and began to whisper to him.

"Mawa! Mawa!" (Rayquaza is so unfortunate! It always lives up in the sky and only eats hard stones when it's hungry. D, let's make a delicious cream cake for Rayquaza together!)

D: "..."

He wanted to explain that Rayquaza's food wasn't stones, but rather the energy from meteorites.

But looking at Alcremie's eager eyes, D simply couldn't bring himself to refuse.

His Alcremie was really just too kind.

"Alright, you all wait here, I'll make it for you," D said.

"Mawa!" Alcremie cheered excitedly.

They had plenty of cream left at home, so Alcremie didn't need to help.

D let Alcremie continue to play while he returned to the room to start making the cream cake.

Eevee sneaked over as well.

D was cracking eggs when Eevee ran to his leg, calling out expectantly:

"Bui! Bui?" (D! Can I evolve into a Dragon-type Pokemon?)

D was taken aback for a second, somewhat unsure he had heard correctly: "What did you say? A Dragon-type Pokemon?"

Eevee nodded vigorously: "Bui!" (Yes! Like the great hero Rayquaza!)

D understood. Eevee was captivated by the story of Rayquaza saving the world? It didn't want to evolve into a Fairy Eevee that fights off evil dragons, but rather a more powerful Dragon Eevee.

But the problem was, a Dragon Eevee didn't even exist!

As of the time he had crossed over, GF still hadn't released a Dragon-type Eevee.

Even if Eevee could evolve into a Dragon Eevee, he didn't know how it would be done.

After briefly thinking it over, D shook his head and said: "I'm afraid not, because as of now, this world doesn't have a Dragon Eevee."

"Bui..." Eevee looked disappointed.

D consoled it: "Don't be sad."

Eevee lifted its head to look at him.

D smiled and said: "As long as you have the heart of a hero, no matter what you evolve into, you can save the world and protect others."

"Fairy Eevee can, Leafeon can, Espeon can, even if you don't evolve, you can still protect others."

"Bui..." Eevee looked adorably dumbfounded.

After a moment, it seemed to understand, nodding excitedly: "Bui!" (I understand!)

With that, Eevee dashed out the door.

"This kid..."

D smiled helplessly, then focused on baking the dessert for Rayquaza.

Time passed second by second. He didn't know how long he was at it, but with a light ding, the fragrant cream cake was freshly baked.

The cream, as pure white as mutton-fat jade, was adorned with a few bright red cherries. Cutting it open revealed the soft golden cake inside. The cake had two layers, filled with fruit in the middle to enhance its flavor and texture.

D cut the cream cake into several pieces, then grabbed some poffins, juice, milk, and other foods from the refrigerator to bring to Snorlax Park.

Upon his arrival, the Pokemon erupted in joyous calls.

D sensed Rayquaza's gaze on him.

He also heard Ogerpon happily introducing him to Rayquaza.

"Bonnie bonnie!" (Rayquaza, this is D! He's the one who told us about you saving the world!)

D: "..."

He swore he was only telling a story about a Dragon-type Pokemon to share with Dragonite and the others.

Upon hearing Ogerpon's words, Rayquaza looked over again and then, opening its mouth, let out a deep growl.

D heard it say, "Not bad!"

D was a bit surprised, was Rayquaza complimenting him?

He placed the freshly made food on the picnic cloth and said to the gathered Pokemon: "Enjoy!"

The Pokemon cheered joyously.

D stood nearby for a moment, hearing Dragonite and the others ask Rayquaza a series of questions.

Questions like, "Rayquaza, do you always live in the sky?", "Are there lots of stars up there?", "Do you eat the moon, Rayquaza?"

Rayquaza answered them all simply.

"Yes, I always live in the sky.", "Stars? There are many, they are outside this planet.", "I don't eat the moon, it's too large."

Every time Rayquaza answered, a Pokemon would gaze at it dreamily and say, "Rayquaza, you're amazing!"

Getting such praise, Rayquaza was visibly pleased, relaxing entirely while savoring food it had never tasted and chatting with these adoring Pokemon.

D was beginning to feel drowsy from listening; the Pokemon's questions were way too innocent.

Eventually, he decided to head home, leaving the impromptu party in peace.

The gathering lasted until the afternoon.

Just before dusk, Rayquaza, amidst farewells from Dragonite and the others, left the ranch and soared into the sky.

It was over.

D withdrew his gaze from the sky, feeling a sigh of relief, turning to Dragonite to ask:

"What made you think of calling Rayquaza over?"

Dragonite looked innocent: "Awoo." (Because everyone wanted to meet Rayquaza!)

D: "..."

Oh well, Pokemon aren't human, after all, and when Rayquaza entrusted its Dragon Scale to Dragonite, it probably wasn't just being polite.

Dragonite really can go to it when needed; now it seems even when it's not needed, it can still go.

"Alright, then," D took a deep breath, just about to return indoors for dinner when he felt a familiar gust of wind.

He looked up to see Rayquaza returning.

In its Dragon Claw was a "star" emitting a yellow glow.

Rayquaza landed in the middle of the ranch, giving the "star" to a few Pokemon before turning around to shoot into the clouds.

This time, it had really left.

Seeing Dragonite holding the star in its Dragon Claw, D felt bewildered.

Dragonite and the few Pokemon shared his confusion.

But quickly, D realized.

This was Rayquaza's return gift for the gathering!

Since a Pokemon had earlier asked about stars, Rayquaza had plucked a star for them.

Yet D couldn't help but be at a loss for words — that wasn't an actual star!

That damn thing was a Minior, a Yellow Core Minior!

Minior, the Meteor Pokemon.

Shaped like a shining star, born in the stratosphere, residing in the ozone layer, a Pokemon born from mutations of nano particles, it survives by absorbing surrounding dust particles, serving as food for stronger Pokemon.

And that stronger Pokemon was probably Rayquaza, living in the ozone layer...

Thinking about this, D felt a turmoil of emotions.

But soon, he had no time for such thoughts.

The glow on the Yellow Minior was starting to fade.

This was a trait of these Pokemon; if their core remained exposed for too long, they would vanish, disappearing completely from this world.

Watching this Minior about to dissipate, D hurriedly returned to his room to grab a new Poke Ball and caught it inside.

The Poke Ball shook a few times, then fell silent.

"Awoo?" Seeing what D did, the few Pokemon in front of him looked puzzled.

D explained to them: "That's not a star, it's a Pokemon called Minior."

"Without its shell, it can't be exposed to the air for long, or it will disappear and lose its life. Only in a Poke Ball can it survive."

Hearing this, Dragonite was shocked.

The stars in the sky were Pokemon?!

Just then, the sound of a vehicle approaching reached them.

D turned to see Zack driving onto the ranch.

He had just parked when he jumped out, wide-eyed and staring in shock at the sky above the ranch.

"Did something just fly out of the ranch?!"

"...Yes," D replied succinctly, "It was a Pokemon, visiting the ranch."

"I see..." Zack recalled the dark silhouette he had seen on the road, murmuring, "It scared me. I've never seen a Pokemon like that before. It's fine, as long as it's fine."

---

---

---

---

# 376: Chapter 373: Tomb Raider and Ancient Gold Coins

At night, moonlight streams into the room through the curtains.

Above the dimly lit room, a "star" emanating a faint glow is floating.

D is sitting on the bed, looking at the Minior together with Pawmot.

This is the first time he has seen a Pokemon like Minior.

Unlike other Pokemon living on land, Minior seems unable to understand human speech.

Though it spins its body gently in response to being touched or called, D doesn't know if that's its conscious response or an unconscious action.

Because of this, the matter of how to handle this Minior has become a problem.

As a gift from Rayquaza to the Dragonite clan, if the Minior is placed in the ranch, it might just end up staying in a Poke Ball all the time.

After all, the ecological habits of this Pokemon prevent it from being exposed to air for long periods.

There were instances before where Minior would fall to the ground after streaking across the sky, only to vanish in the end.

Moreover, Minior's diet is vastly different from other Pokemon.

As a Pokemon born from nanoscale particle mutations, Minior lives by absorbing the dust floating in the ozone layer.

In summary, Minior is not suited for living on land.

Thinking of this, D said to Pawmot and Koraidon: "Let's see if we can send this Minior back to the sky tomorrow!"

Koraidon had no objections and let out an "Arargh" as a response.

Pawmot also nodded, having heard from D about this Pokemon's situation, knowing that it couldn't stay on the ground for too long, or else it would die.

So they needed to send this Minior to the sky.

Early the next day, D found Dragonite, handed it the Poke Ball containing Minior, and let it accompany Pawmot to send Minior home, advising:

"Minior can't live in the ranch, so I'm entrusting it to you guys, please send it to the sky!"

Dragonite and Pawmot nodded.

"Roar!"

"Bawmo!"

The two Pokemon flew away as D began a new day's work.

Milking the Gogoat, collecting Rock Salt from Garganacl, weeding the fields, gathering honey, feeding Snorlax...

Dragonite and Pawmot didn't return until ten o'clock.

Once D confirmed with them that Minior had returned to the ozone layer, he felt relieved.

"Then it's fine." D sighed in relief and said to the two Pokemon: "Thank you for your hard work, go play for a while, and then you can have lunch."

"Bawmo～"

"Roar～"

Just as the two Pokemon were about to run off, D suddenly remembered there was something he hadn't said.

He quickly called Dragonite back and said: "One more thing!"

"Roar?" Dragonite looked puzzled.

D: "Next time you see Rayquaza, remember to tell it not to send Pokemon like Minior again."

Dragonite understood D's words right away, nodded, and promised: "Roar!"

"Alright, that's all for now, I'm heading to town to buy some groceries, you guys go have fun!"

D patted Dragonite on the head and bid it farewell.

Koraidon was instructing Cyclizar and Dragonair on practicing Moves; D didn't disturb them and instead called a Gogoat, hung two bags on its back, and rode it towards Porto Marinada Town.

Dragonite kept D's words in mind.

So it immediately used a Dragon Scale to call over the esteemed Rayquaza.

Soon, Rayquaza's massive figure reappeared on the ranch.

A few Pokemon gathered around Rayquaza, explaining that Minior is not a star, but a Pokemon.

Dragonite said: "Roar, roar roar roar!" (D said, if Minior stays on land all the time, it will die, which is so pitiful, so we just sent it back up to the sky!)

Hearing this, a hint of embarrassment flashed across Rayquaza's face.

Because it sometimes catches Minior and eats the shells on their bodies.

The shells contain energy similar to meteorites, though it doesn't eat Minior itself.

However, even so, those Minior still flee frantically when they see it.

But Rayquaza thought it couldn't tell Dragonite and the others about this, or it might ruin its image as a great hero in their eyes.

In the past, it wasn't a big deal, but now, with these Pokemons admiring it, Rayquaza felt a burden.

"Roar."

Hearing Dragonite and the others chatter about Minior's situation, Rayquaza nodded, indicating that it wouldn't catch them anymore.

In its heart, it started thinking about what to bring to these Pokemon next time.

Yet Rayquaza thought for a long time without finding a good answer, so it prepared to go see what was out in space.

Thinking of this, Rayquaza bid farewell to the Pokemon in front of it and turned to fly away.

D just happened to return from town, riding the Gogoat; he was still some distance from the ranch. As he looked up, he saw Rayquaza flying out of the ranch.

Seeing this, a question mark appeared above D's head.

?

What's happening? Why did Rayquaza come over again?

He quickly urged Gogoat to speed up, and upon arriving back at the ranch, he saw Dragonite and the others happily looking up at the sky.

D approached and asked: "Did you call Rayquaza over again?"

Dragonite happily nodded, patting its chest and assuring: "Roar!"

---

It's already told Rayquaza everything that D had instructed, from now on Rayquaza will no longer catch Minior!

"..."

D was speechless, his mind in a daze as he followed Dragonite and looked up at the sky.

Rayquaza! Are you really that idle?

After a while, D let out a sigh.

"Sigh."

Forget it, this is up to the Pokemon, as a human he shouldn't interfere.

D turned to the kitchen to start preparing lunch.

In the afternoon, D originally planned to go into the mountains to see if he could gather some mushrooms and hazelnuts, and also to check on the wild Pokemon he had previously rescued.

But a phone call disrupted his plans.

"Hello?" When the call connected, there was no video from the other side, leading D to guess that they might be using a landline instead of a video phone.

"Is this D?" A deep male voice came from the other end: "I'm the owner of the town's antique shop, we've got a new shipment of ancient Paldea gold coins, do you need them?"

Hearing this, D was slightly stunned, then he remembered the treasure chest Gimmighoul in the tavern.

He nodded quickly: "Yes, yes, I'll come over now!"

"Alright, I'll be waiting for you at the shop!" the antique shop owner said with a smile.

After hanging up, D temporarily enlisted a working Dragonite to rush to Porto Marinada Town.

Dragonite landed in front of the antique shop, and D went inside with it.

The moment he entered, the antique shop owner took out a treasure chest and grinned, "This time I've been lucky, I received twenty-eight ancient coins in one go, it's a big harvest!"

The chest opened to reveal a pile of shimmering gold coins imprinted with the symbol of the Paldea Ancient Kingdom.

28 coins? D was shocked by this number: "How can there be so many?"

The antique shop owner chuckled, lowering his voice: "The person who sold me these coins is a professional tomb raider, all of these ancient coins were stolen from the same ruin."

D paused for a moment, then asked: "Isn't this kind of behavior illegal?"

He wasn't very knowledgeable about the laws in the Pokemon World.

The antique shop owner explained: "It's hard to say, generally speaking, it's a gray industry, and robbing graves is indeed very unethical."

D: "..."

"But don't worry! Besides cemeteries, the Paldea Region has numerous ancient ruins left from the kingdom era, such as castle ruins, which contain many treasures!" the antique shop owner added, "You don't have to worry about such ancient castle ruins!"

Indeed, D thought.

With that, he felt relieved.

"I'll take all these coins." D paid decisively.

Gimmighoul had already collected 3 coins, and with these 28, the total number of gold coins reached 31.

Stepping closer to the goal of 1000.

But at this pace, it's still too slow... D couldn't help but fall into contemplation.

He counted the number of coins in the chest and then packed them up, ready to take the treasure chest with him.

Before leaving, D asked: "Logically speaking, these kinds of coins were circulation currency in the Paldea Ancient Kingdom, so there should be many of them, right?"

The antique shop owner nodded: "Of course, from what I've heard, adventurers and tomb raiders say that these coins are the most abundant thing in tombs and ruins."

D suddenly became interested: "What do people do with the coins once they get them? Sell them all?"

"Not necessarily!" The antique shop owner thought for a moment, then said: "Some people collect them specifically, or sell them to antique collectors willing to pay a high price."

"I see..."

"Oh, by the way, you don't seem to be short on cash." The antique shop owner looked D up and down, "Let me suggest a forum, lots of people sell relics there, maybe if you're willing to offer high prices, they'll sell to you."

"Forum?" D was taken aback.

"You've never heard of it?" The antique shop owner looked amazed: "You look younger than me, I thought you'd know more about new things than I do!"

D: "..."

The owner laughed: "You're out of the loop, forums are places where users post discussions. Just buy a computer, log onto the forum website, and you can join in the discussions! Isn't the internet amazing? People from all over the world can communicate through it!"

This is so outdated, D thought to himself.

The internet in this world is indeed still in its infancy.

"I see, I'll buy a computer and check it out sometime." D replied.

"Alright, then good luck to you." The owner handed him a piece of paper with the forum's address.

D glanced at it, then put the paper away.

He left with Dragonite, walking down the streets of Porto Marinada Town, observing the shops along the road.

As for the computer, D never really considered buying one, at least not yet.

Because the computers of this era all have bulky monitors, their performance is poor, and they lack games, people in this world mostly use them for sending emails or office work.

D didn't have anyone to email, nor was he interested in Solitaire games, so he never bought one.

But it seems necessary for him to get one for the farm now.

---

Thinking about this, D went to the department store that day and bought a computer.

The tower and monitor were installed by staff who visited his home, and finally, he connected it to the internet, enabling basic online functionality.

The computer was set up in the study on the second floor, and D pressed the power button, adjusted the settings, and seeing no issues, temporarily placed the computer there.

Then he carried the treasure chest to the Pokemon Tavern.

In its treasure chest form, Gimmighoul looked exactly like a treasure chest from the outside. It was quietly placed on a beverage rack used for drinks, serving as the tavern's decoration.

D tapped on the treasure chest and said, "Gimmighoul, I've collected more gold coins here. Do you want to come out and have a look?"

The Gimmighoul inside the treasure chest clearly heard this statement.

Its heart was stirred, and then it lifted the lid of the treasure chest with its head, peering through the gap to observe the situation outside.

D turned his head and met the gaze of Gimmighoul.

Just when he thought Gimmighoul would hide away as it did at first, the creature flew without hiding in fright, instead showing an excited and anticipatory expression.

"Well then!" D asked with a smile, "You're not afraid of me anymore?"

Gimmighoul let out a growl, sounding like the clinking of gold coins, very peculiar.

During this period, it stayed inside the tavern, listening to the sounds of Pokemon guests outside each night.

Gradually, its bravery was polished stronger.

Moreover, the human named D not only did not harm it but also helped it collect gold coins and provided it with food.

This made Gimmighoul very grateful, and over time, it wasn't so afraid of this human. Seeing him no longer made it want to flee or hide.

"Alright then, come check out these gold coins!" D stepped forward and placed the treasure chest form of Gimmighoul on the bar.

Seeing that box full of shiny gold coins, Gimmighoul's eyes instantly turned starry-eyed.

Its appearance was just like a little money-lover.

"There are a total of twenty-eight gold coins here, plus the three you have, bringing the total to thirty-one,"

D calculated slightly and then said:

"You need 969 more gold coins to evolve into Gholdengo. Although it's a lot, don't worry, I'll help you collect them together!"

"Jingle~!"

Gimmighoul let out a crisp and pleasant growl, expressing its inner joy.

D chuckled, then began packing those gold coins into the treasure chest where Gimmighoul resided.

After all were packed, D waved goodbye to Gimmighoul: "Then I won't disturb you, see you later!"

"Jingle~!" Gimmighoul returned to the shelf, serving as the tavern's treasure chest decoration.

Meanwhile, D went to the study on the second floor, turned on the computer, and entered the website given by the antique shop owner.

Soon, a forum named "Secret Garden" loaded.

Although it sounds like Secret Garden, the content has nothing to do with flowers.

D glanced briefly; most posts on the forum were about tomb treasure hunting, exploring ruins, and selling antiques.

For example:

"King Henry III of the Paldea Ancient Kingdom used this King's Crown, currently seeking a sincere buyer!"

D clicked in and found that it wasn't a crown but rather resembled a cooking pot.

Looking through the comments, several users indeed pointed out the poster's mistake.

Really, what a crown! D couldn't help but grumble internally.

He felt this person and Spectrier, who mistook a trophy for a crown and wore it on its head, refusing to take it off, must have lots to discuss.

Glancing simply, he registered an account named "Wing King," and imitated those posts to make one himself.

"Wing King: Seeking high-priced, large quantities of ancient Paldea gold coins, knowledgeable ones come!"

---

---

---

---

# 377: Chapter 374: Ancient Investigator and Treasures of Ruin

The post didn't receive any replies immediately when it was sent out.

After all, the internet in this world is not very developed yet, and with mobile phones yet to be updated, surfing forums can only be done through computers.

D wasn't in a hurry and left the computer on the forum while he went downstairs to tidy up the house.

The weather was great today, so D took the blankets from his bed, clothes from the wardrobe, and the plush nests of his Pokémon outside, hanging them all on the clothesline to dry.

Eevee and Alcremie chased each other around the clothesline stand, running back and forth by D's feet.

Butterfree landed on the clothes hanger, watching D's actions with an innocent look.

"Flutter?"

"I'm drying the blankets," D answered with a smile. "At night, it's more comfortable to cover with a sun-dried blanket, and it even smells a bit like sunshine."

"Flutter～"

"Alright, Alcremie, take Eevee to play somewhere else. If you keep running around here, I might accidentally step on you later."

D looked down at the two Pokémon who started circling around his right leg, and said with a face full of helplessness.

"Maa maa!"

In response, Alcremie had complete trust in D, not believing he would step on them!

So they continued playing here.

"You two little troublemakers!"

D picked up the two Pokémon, poking their foreheads with his finger, and then walked about twenty meters, handing them over to a Garganacl.

The Garganacl scratched its head in confusion but still reached out to take Alcremie and Eevee.

After hanging the blankets, D went to the underground wine cellar to carefully check the fruit wine's brewing progress.

Just then, his sister Indeedee came to tell D that the fruit wine at the Pokemon Tavern had sold out.

So D moved a barrel of freshly brewed Pecha Berry Wine to the Pokemon Tavern to restock.

Once everything was done, D stopped his activities and went back to the living room to rest for a bit.

Sipping the red tea brewed by his sister Indeedee and looking at the Polteageist and Sinistea floating in midair in front of him, D felt his life was incredibly fulfilling now.

This is what life is!

Awesome!

D drank the tea in one go, nestled on the sofa, watching TV with Calyrex.

Time passed by slowly, soon the sky outside began to darken.

Dusk arrived, and smoke started rising from the chimney, that was Indeedee using Slugma's flames to prepare dinner for everyone.

After watching TV all afternoon, D stretched and realized it was already five-twenty in the evening.

Considering the time, Zack would soon arrive at the ranch.

So, D stood up and began to organize the goods to be shipped today.

Miltank's milk, Garganacl's rock salt, Gogoat's goat milk, more than twenty barrels of fruit wine, a truckload of pumpkins.

By the time Zack arrived, D had just moved these goods out of the warehouse.

Zack parked and jumped down to help D load the goods onto the truck.

Looking at those fresh vegetables and fruits, Zack grinned and said, "Another harvest season, huh!"

"Yeah!" D nodded, the power of Pokémon was truly amazing.

Cultivating crops with Grass Energy, accelerating growth...

These effects were incredible; even the best fertilizers from D's previous world couldn't make such effects.

"Alright, here is today's money, take care of it!" After loading the goods, Zack wiped his sweat with a towel hanging around his neck and handed over today's money.

Receiving hundreds of thousands of League currency in one go, D felt at ease as he had become accustomed to it.

The Wooloo Ranch was already built, an Apple Garden was also opened, the trucks for transporting goods were purchased, and there wasn't much else needing expenditure at the ranch.

Therefore, D planned to save this money, waiting to see if there was anything needed later.

Oh wait!

D suddenly remembered a rather costly matter—buying ancient gold coins to evolve Gimmighoul into Gholdengo!

After sending Zack off, he immediately went to the second floor and sat down at the computer.

Refreshing the forum webpage with the mouse, several more replies had appeared under that post.

「Winged King: Purchasing large amounts of Paldea's ancient gold coins at a high price, knowledgeable people come!」

「Treasure Seeker (Floor One): No support, just bumping.」

「Gardevoir's Owner (Floor Two): What does the poster want to do?」

「Ancient Investigator (Floor Three): How high is the price? How many are needed?」

D's gaze swept over each one, finally stopping at the reply from "Ancient Investigator" on the third floor.

He typed a few keys, replying: 「Price is negotiable, not lower than market rate, I want as many as you have.」

Next, it was a waiting game for the other party's reply.

D glanced at the reply's timestamp, realizing it was posted at three in the afternoon, and wondered if this person was still online.

But it seemed like the wait was going to be a bit longer.

Thinking briefly, D decided to go downstairs for dinner first, but just as he was about to get up, a private message from "Ancient Investigator" suddenly popped up.

[Ancient Investigator: Oh, quite the confidence! Can you handle a quantity of several hundred?]

Several hundred? Does this person have that many?

If this is true, then the evolution of Gimmighoul could make a significant leap forward!

Suppressing his excitement, D began typing a reply on the keyboard.

---

[Lord Wing: Of course I can eat that much, but do you really have that much over there?]

[Ancient Investigator: I never lie! Plus, I have quite a reputation throughout the tomb raider realm.]

[Lord Wing: Never heard of it, when can we exchange? Tell me the time and place.]

D waited for a while, but there was no immediate reply from the other side.

After a long while, a message finally came through.

[Ancient Investigator: Where are you?]

[Lord Wing: Paldea Region, Porto Marinada Town.]

[Ancient Investigator: Porto Marinada Town... I'm currently in Mesagoza City, and can't leave at the moment. If you want it, you'll have to come to Mesagoza City yourself.]

Mesagoza City...

D was slightly taken aback.

That is the largest city in the Paldea Region, and Paldea Academy is located there.

He thought for a moment, then went downstairs to fetch a map of the Paldea Region to locate Mesagoza.

"Hmm, Mesagoza is right in the center of the Paldea Region. If Dragonite flies at full speed, it shouldn't take more than an hour to get there," D murmured.

Since it wouldn't take too long, he planned to go there after finishing work on the ranch tomorrow, and if he was quick, he could even return before noon.

Thinking of this, D replied: [Alright, I have time tomorrow.]

The other side responded quickly.

[Ancient Investigator: Great, but make sure to bring enough money. As for the location... let's meet at the Welcome Coffee House! At eleven in the morning.]

D replied with an "okay."

Just when he thought the conversation was over, another private message came through from the other side.

[Ancient Investigator: Lord Wing? Have you also seen Heath's adventure notes?]

Looking at the message on the screen, D couldn't help but be a little surprised.

Heath's adventure notes... Lord Wing... This person knows a lot!

He thought for a moment and replied conservatively: [Yes.]

There were no further replies, so D shut down the computer and went downstairs to eat.

The next morning, after finishing work on the ranch, D informed his Pokémon that he was going out.

He initially planned to ride Dragonite, but surprisingly, Koraidon cuddled up to him.

D looked at it, then at the nearby Cyclizar, and asked with a smile, "Don't want to stay on the ranch this time?"

He still remembered how Koraidon had thrown a tantrum and refused to go out to the Bug-Catching Contest because of a bath last time.

Koraidon drooped its head, looking quite aggrieved, "Agh, geez..."

"Alright." D reached out to pet Koraidon's head and couldn't resist giving it a kiss on the forehead. "Then let's go together!"

Koraidon's eyes lit up instantly, "Agh, geez!"

D mounted up and told the Pokémon on the ranch, "I'm heading to Mesagoza, so I should be back around one in the afternoon."

Saying that, D turned to Sister Indeedee, saying, "Sister, I'll leave today's lunch to you."

Sister Indeedee nodded happily, "Indeedee~"

"Alright, we're off!"

With that, D gently patted Koraidon's neck and flew into the sky under the watchful eyes of the Pokémon on the ranch.

At 10:30 a.m., Koraidon arrived at Mesagoza.

After landing on the street, D suddenly remembered something very important.

Oops, he forgot to ask what the other person looks like!

After leaving the computer, he had no way to contact the other person, so even if they met, they might not recognize each other.

What to do? Should he hold up a sign to find someone?

D felt a bit awkward but had no choice but to muster up the courage to stop an older man wearing a Naranja Academy uniform on the street and ask for directions to the Welcome Coffee House.

The old man was over fifty, his hair almost completely white, and the Naranja Academy uniform gave a strange sense of discord.

"The Welcome Coffee House? Just keep heading straight along this road, and you'll see a crossroads. Turn right, and the Welcome Coffee House is on that street."

The old man replied while casting his eyes on Koraidon.

His eyes gleamed with excitement, "Wow, a Pokémon I've never seen before! You're a Trainer, right? How about a Pokémon battle with me?"

D: "..."

He immediately shook his head, "No, no, I'm in a hurry, already have an appointment."

The old man looked regretful, "Then can you tell me the name of this Pokémon?"

"Of course." D nodded. "It's called Koraidon."

*

"So, Naranja Academy really has no age limit for admission..."

Walking along the streets of Mesagoza, D couldn't help but feel a bit emotional.

It's true what they say: live and learn. Even the old man could attend this ancient academy.

Following the directions given by the old man, D found the Welcome Coffee House after crossing an intersection and turning left.

He entered with Koraidon.

It was already lunchtime, and there were quite a few customers inside the café.

Most of them were students from Paldea Academy, and they curiously eyed the unfamiliar Pokémon that had entered with D.

D found an empty seat and sat down, occasionally glancing at the entrance of the café, hoping to spot the netizen named Ancient Investigator this way.

But after waiting for what felt like ages, there was still no match.

Just as D was contemplating finding a computer to log onto the forum for messages, he suddenly heard several voices greeting someone.

"Oh? Teacher Raifort, you're dining here too?"

"Hello, Teacher Raifort!"

Hearing the voices, D looked up and saw a purple-haired woman with brown-red skin, in a blue top, wearing yellow-framed glasses, walking in.

The woman wore a gentle expression, smiling and responding to the students one by one.

Her gaze swept around the café and finally settled on D and Koraidon in the corner.

Next, D watched as she walked over to him openly and asked, "You're Lord Wing, right? Nice to meet you, I'm Raifort, currently teaching history at Paldea Academy."

D: "..."

He noticed Raifort's eyes linger on Koraidon, a look of surprise crossing her face.

"Hello, Raifort, I'm... D." D ultimately told her his real name.

Raifort smiled slightly and then said, "Come on, let's talk in a private room."

D nodded and followed with Koraidon, recalling some information about this woman.

Raifort, a history teacher at Paldea Academy, maintains a passionate curiosity about the legends passed down through history.

In the game storyline, she investigates the four "Treasures of Ruin" and commissions players to help capture the Pokémon that once caused calamities in the Paldea Region.

Wo-Chien, Chien-Pao, Ting-Lu, Chi-Yu.

Before becoming a history teacher at Paldea Academy, Raifort was involved in tomb raiding activities.

In layman's terms, she's a tomb raider or a dealer.

So, the gold coins Raifort mentioned on the forum were her spoils from tomb raiding?

...I should have guessed earlier.

D suddenly didn't know what to say.

He followed Raifort into a private room and sat down, asking curiously, "How did you recognize me?"

Raifort adjusted her glasses and smiled, "It's simple. I'm very familiar with this café. During lunch, most of the customers here are my students; you're the only exception."

D: "..."

He conceded.

Raifort turned her attention to Koraidon beside her, asking, "Lord Wing, is this your Pokémon? It looks very wild! It has a primitive beauty, just like those ancient mysteries and legends, prompting exploration."

"...Yes." D introduced, "It's called Koraidon. Did you bring the gold coins?"

Raifort temporarily shifted her attention from Koraidon, tapping her bag. She opened the zipper, revealing piles of glittering ancient coins.

The space in the bag was packed tightly.

D was startled, "So many?!"

Raifort smiled, "Of course."

D looked up, incredulous, "Were they all looted from tombs?"

He was actually more curious about how a tomb raider like Raifort ended up as a history teacher—it seemed so... unconventional to him.

"Looted from tombs?" Raifort shook her head and corrected him, "No, it's all for necessary historical research!"

---

---

---

---

# 378: Chapter 375: Ruinous Pokemon Reversal Plan

D: "..."

What the hell is historical research!

D's gaze fell on the pile of gold coins.

The ancient gold coins gleamed, exuding the scent of money.

So, your research involves taking away other people's burial items?

Noticing his gaze, Raifort smiled slightly and said:

"In professional terms, my actions are called archaeology. To be honest, I'm very interested in the legends passed down from the past."

"The reason I took these ancient coins is to study the monetary system of the Paldea Ancient Kingdom thousands of years ago. You know, I'm a history teacher."

D was at a loss for words. Although nothing was wrong with her words, something felt off about it.

"...So what are you doing now?" he asked.

Raifort shrugged and said matter-of-factly, "I finished my research, and these coins no longer hold any significance for me, so I'm planning to give them to someone who needs them."

"Like you," Raifort said with a smile.

Alright then! D didn't ask more because he really needed those coins.

So he changed the subject and got down to business.

"How many coins are there in total?" D asked.

"Hmm," Raifort shook her head, "I'm not really sure because I didn't get these coins all at once. I collected them from more than a dozen tombs and ruins."

D: "...So how much are you planning to sell each coin for?"

Raifort thought for a moment and then smiled:

"According to the market price of antiquities, the price of one ancient gold coin is 50,000 League coins. But considering you're buying so many at once, I can give you a discount! But the condition is you have to tell me what you plan to do with them."

D countered, "Have you heard of a Pokemon called Gholdengo?"

"Gholdengo?" Raifort was slightly taken aback and then surprised, "Are you talking about the Pokemon that is said to evolve from a Gimmighoul?"

D nodded: "Someone told me that to evolve a Gimmighoul into Gholdengo, you need to collect a certain amount of coins."

Raifort understood and pondered slightly, mumbling, "So you plan to try it out..."

She had heard of Gimmighoul before.

It's said that it was born from the passion of explorers for treasure thousands of years ago.

And being a history teacher, coupled with her interest in ancient legends and history, Raifort knew a lot about these things.

"That's right." D nodded.

"I understand," Raifort said softly. "In that case, I'll give you a 30% discount, as a way to verify the legend of the Gimmighoul. So if your Gimmighoul evolves, you must show it to me!"

"No problem." D readily agreed, making him believe that Raifort wasn't tomb raiding for money.

If it were for money, she could have set a higher price.

And from Raifort's background in the game, she's inherently interested in historical legends. Though she's in school, she often wants to go out tomb raiding.

If it weren't for Principal Clavell watching over her, she'd probably use any means necessary to solve legendary mysteries.

"Now you believe I'm not tomb raiding for money, right?" Raifort insightfully asked with a smile.

"I believe it." D nodded.

Raifort smiled slightly and continued, "And I wasn't lying about what I said on the forum."

"Even in the whole tomb raider realm, I'm quite famous because I've once reached the deepest part of the renowned underwater ruins in the Unova Region in one go!"

"Even though I've retired now, my legend still circulates in the tomb raider realm!"

D nodded, turning his gaze to Raifort's attire, and then noticed something particularly interesting.

The plaque on Raifort's chest closely resembled the legendary Pokemon Articuno, and the headpiece she wore was very much like the emissary of Ho-oh, Marshadow.

The bracelet on her hand evidently had elements of Tapu Koko.

"Come on! Let's count them together!" Raifort poured the coins from the bag onto the table.

Immediately, a small shimmering golden hill appeared in front of D.

His first reaction was that if Gimmighoul saw this mountain of coins, it would be overjoyed, right?

Then the two began counting the ancient gold coins in front of them together.

As time ticked by, after about twenty minutes, D finally had the exact number of coins.

142.

A total of 142 coins.

Adding the 31 coins in the Gimmighoul's treasure chest, it totaled 173 coins.

"Still not enough!" D couldn't help but ponder.

He wondered what other ways he could acquire more coins.

"Altogether, it's 142 League coins, the original price being 7,100,000, hmm... After the discount, it's 4,970,000," Geeta calculated using a pocket calculator.

D snapped back to reality and nodded, having already gotten a considerable bargain and couldn't haggle any further.

"Okay, I didn't bring that much money with me. I'll go to the bank in a bit, and I'll transfer it to you," he said.

Good thing he had sold the milk and crops from his warehouse before coming here, plus his family's savings, which barely amounted to that much.

"Alright." Raifort nodded with a gentle smile.

The two left the café and went straight to the bank, where D transferred the money to Raifort.

---

Raifort handed the bag of gold coins to him, "I'll give you the bag too."

D glanced at it. It was just a normal hiking bag, so he didn't pay it much mind. "Thanks a lot."

Raifort yawned. She had stayed up late in the Paldea Academy library reading ancient texts, so she was feeling a bit tired now.

"Alright, I'm going to catch up on some sleep. If you have any questions, you can contact me directly on the forum."

"Okay." D looked down at the bag in his hand and couldn't help but fall into deep thought.

One gold coin is fifty thousand, a thousand coins would make fifty million. He'd have to save up for many years to earn that much money.

Truly fitting for a Gholdengo...

But thinking about such a rare Pokemon like Gholdengo, D felt that all these efforts were worth it.

Especially since once Gimmighoul evolves into Gholdengo, it can even create gold coins on its own. Then, he could slowly sell some to recoup the costs.

Make It Rain, Gholdengo's exclusive move.

It's a bit like Meowth's Pay Day, both are moves that can earn money.

After saying goodbye to Raifort, it was already one in the afternoon.

D and Koraidon hadn't had lunch yet, so they simply bought two sandwiches by the roadside and, after eating, headed straight back to the ranch.

D, carrying the hiking bag, pushed open the wooden door of the Pokemon Tavern, then unzipped the bag and tilted it upside down.

"Clatter!"

A pile of gold coins fell onto the bar like rain.

Hearing the sound, Gimmighoul immediately peeked its head out from the treasure chest.

Seeing that D had brought so many gold coins at once, Gimmighoul was completely shocked.

What followed was a surge of ecstasy.

"Ding ding!"

"Take it!" D smiled.

Gimmighoul was overwhelmed with excitement and joy, so much so that it forgot all its fears, leaping straight out of the treasure chest.

Transforming into its walking mode, Gimmighoul jumped forcefully onto D's shoulder and gave his cheek a firm kiss.

"Hmm? Ah!"

D quickly realized this, and a smile appeared on his face. Gimmighoul was indeed overjoyed!

"Happy?" D asked.

Gimmighoul: ٩(๑^o^๑)۶

"Alright, alright, let's start putting these coins back in the treasure chest together."

Saying this, D lifted the treasure chest, where Gimmighoul usually stayed, onto the bar and opened it, then started putting the pile of coins inside.

Gimmighoul watched excitedly from the side.

When all the coins were in place, Gimmighoul hopped in with a thump, diving into the pile of gold coins with only its head exposed.

In this state, Gimmighoul looked as if it were swimming in a sea of coins.

D stood by, watching the scene, and couldn't help but smile.

"I'm a bit tired. I'll go rest for a bit. See you later!"

Gimmighoul turned to look at him, happily saying, "Ding ding!"

D put Gimmighoul's treasure chest back in its place and then got up to leave the Pokemon Tavern.

He went back to his room, drank a glass of water, then went to the second floor to check on the progress of his purchase post on the computer.

Currently, he had only collected one-tenth of the ancient gold coins, and D planned to keep the post up, hoping that some cash-strapped tomb raider might see it and contact him in the future.

After roughly going through the forum posts, D opened the private message interface.

Looking at the [Ancient Investigator] chat column, he couldn't help but fall into contemplation.

Raifort, Treasures of Ruin...

D still remembered the legend and origins of those four Treasures of Ruin.

Legend had it that during the distant Paldea Kingdom era, a merchant brought four treasures from the Eastern Lands: a cauldron, a sword, a tablet, and a bead, and sold them to the King.

However, that very night, the four treasures suddenly transformed into Pokemon and caused havoc, ultimately destroying the entire royal city.

Later, the surviving King investigated the merchant and the four treasures.

He eventually discovered that the four items weren't real treasures; their true form was Pokemon, having absorbed many desires and curses after being handled and transferred for so long.

And when they reached the King, those desires reached a critical point. With the added desire absorbed from the King, the power surpassed the critical point, causing the four Ruinous Pokemon to awaken and destroy everything.

That King eventually summoned a Pokemon Tamer to suppress the four Ruinous Pokemon, sealing them within four shrines located in four places in the Paldea Region.

It is said that only those with clear hearts can break the seal to summon the four Pokemon.

Namely Ting-Lu, Chi-Yu, Wo-Chien, and Chien-Pao.

In the game, Raifort has been investigating this matter tirelessly.

She looked up numerous ancient texts, historical disaster records, personal diaries... and eventually confirmed the authenticity of this legend.

She conveyed the location of the shrines and the method of breaking the seal to the game's protagonist, asking him to help release those four Pokemon.

Regarding those four Pokemon, D had some thoughts long ago, but he abandoned them as they would bring calamity to the ranch.

However, now he's concocted reverse recipes using water from Giratina's home, which might invert the calamity-bringing traits of those four Pokemon.

Reversing them into beasts that bring blessings and abundance.

Wo-Chien, which used to absorb the energy of vegetation, causing forests to wither, and fields to produce poor harvests, would transform into a Pokemon that nourishes forests and makes fields bountiful.

Ting-Lu, which used to cause earthquakes, cracking the ground, would become a Pokemon adept at manipulating terrestrial energy, plowing the land for ideal growth.

Chi-Yu, which used to manipulate 3000-degree flames to create lava seas and swim leisurely within them, would become a fire Pokemon that cooks and keeps the ranch's Pokemon warm.

Chien-Pao, which used to trigger avalanches and control ice, would transform into a guardian Pokemon that wards off evil and protects the ranch.

If he wished to capture these four Pokemon, he would first need a good relationship with Raifort.

Because only Raifort knows the exact location of the four shrines and how to interact with the seals.

To Raifort, the four Treasures of Ruin are dreams come true; her goal is to investigate them and reclaim them for herself.

But...

Deliberately pursuing someone to capture a Pokemon is something D couldn't do.

Besides, it feels too false, creating a guilty feeling of deception within him.

After a brief thought, D discarded that idea.

"Forget it, I'll just let things take their course!"

With that in mind, D was about to turn off the computer and head downstairs for a nap.

But just then, his chat window flickered.

D was slightly startled, then clicked to see.

He then saw a few messages from Raifort.

[Ancient Investigator: !!!]

[Ancient Investigator: I just went to the library to look up some materials and found an old notebook documenting the legend of the Winged King. Based on the descriptions, I discovered that the Pokemon named Koraidon that was with you today shares very similar characteristics in some areas!]

[Ancient Investigator: Where did you capture it?]

The stream of messages left D a bit dazed.

After some thought, he replied: [It wandered into my ranch. I helped heal its injuries, and since then, it has stayed at my ranch.]

[Ancient Investigator: ...What do you mean?]

D was unsure whether he could talk about the research conducted by Professor Sada and Professor Turo.

After a moment's pause, he cautiously replied: [Sorry, this involves some private and confidential information that I can't reveal.]

*

Mesagoza City, Paldea Academy, Teacher Dormitories.

In a dimly lit room, Raifort furrowed her brows, eyes wide open, mouth slightly ajar, staring in shock at the reply on her computer screen.

[Winged King: But what I can tell you is that Koraidon is not a Pokemon of this era. Just as you thought, it comes from a distant past.]

"Not a Pokemon of this era?!" Raifort muttered to herself, "What does that mean?"

Had that Koraidon traveled through time?

Or has it been sleeping in an ancient ruin, only awakening now in modern times?

Or perhaps, it was revived from a fossil as an ancient Pokemon?

In this moment, many thoughts flooded through Raifort's mind.

But what astonished her even more was the fact that the contents of the old notebook found in the second-hand market turned out to be true!

---

---

---

---

# 379: Chapter 376: Treasure Vault, Mung Bean Soup, and the Season of Green Onion Harvest

League Calendar Year 199, June 23rd.

The Paldea Region welcomed the hottest summer season.

The air shimmered with heat, and it was so scorching that a group of Pokemon, including Pawmot and Eevee, all rushed to Glaceon's Ice Cave to escape the heat.

Because their fur is thick and dense, running outside in this weather could easily cause them to become drenched in sweat.

In such weather, even traveling Trainers stayed obediently in towns, only venturing to the next town when the evening brought cooler temperatures.

The ranch was also temporarily devoid of Trainers coming to challenge the trial.

After all, with the weather this hot, no one wanted to run around aimlessly in the wilderness...

D was also happy to enjoy the leisure. He took advantage of the cooler morning to finish the ranch chores and then stayed at home.

Outside, the sun was shining brightly, and he could faintly hear the chirping of cicadas from the back mountain.

The air conditioner was on inside the house. Calyrex watched TV on the first floor, while Pawmot and Eevee had just been invited to the Ice Cave by Glaceon.

His younger sibling Indeedee and older sibling Indeedee were tidying up the living room, which had been messed up by other Pokemon.

D, meanwhile, sat in front of his computer on the second floor, checking the post he had previously published, to see if anyone wanted to sell ancient Paldean gold coins.

Over the past month, he had gradually acquired some scattered ancient gold coins from others on the forum.

Including the ones he had saved before, the current collection progress of Gimmighoul's coins had reached 230/1000.

"Just 50 more coins, and I'll reach a quarter of the way there. If I can keep it up, by this time next year, I should have enough saved," D calculated in his head.

But he couldn't guarantee it, because the process of acquiring coins was not smooth.

In addition, there were quite a few antique enthusiasts lurking on the forum. Both relic crowns and ancient gold coins fell within their collection scope.

Those people became formidable competitors for D; just the other day, they even outbid him for those gold coins with the sellers he was in contact with.

This gave D a lot of headaches.

Although the current income from the ranch was decent, it wasn't enough to support him in spending money recklessly.

At that moment, a private message popped up on his screen.

D opened it and found it was from Raifort.

[Ancient Investigator: How's it going? Is the acquisition process going smoothly?]

Raifort had just finished lunch at this time.

She had no classes in the afternoon, so she went back to her dorm, turned on the air conditioning, browsed the forum for any information on ancient legends and ruins, and chatted with D.

[King of Wings: Not very smoothly. A few days ago, someone offered a higher price and snatched the sellers I was in contact with.]

[Ancient Investigator: That's quite common. After all, if these antiques hit the market, they are mostly sold through auctions, and only the highest bidder can purchase them.]

D naturally understood this principle; he couldn't help but sigh. Just as he was about to reply, Raifort's message came through.

[Ancient Investigator: Are you thinking about other ways to acquire more coins? If so, I might be able to help you!]

D was slightly stunned, then an unbelievable thought flashed through his mind.

His fingers typed on the keyboard for a moment and replied: [...You're not suggesting I go grave robbing with you, are you?]

[Ancient Investigator: Exactly! Want to go? If you're lucky, you might get a lot of coins at once!]

Honestly, D was tempted, but his reason told him that such behavior was disrespectful.

After a moment of hesitation, he decided against following Raifort's idea of grave robbing: [Forget it, robbing someone's grave is just too unethical.]

However, a quick response came from the other side.

[Ancient Investigator: Grave? It's not a grave this time! My target is an Ancient Ruins deep underground.]

Within the teacher's dormitory of Paldea Academy.
