Raifort pressed the send key and then turned her gaze to the ancient book on the computer desk.

Her eyes flickered with a hint of excitement.

This ancient book was something she found deep in the library.

As one of the few old schools in the world, Paldea Academy had a very long history and rich background.

The library here housed many books, including numerous ancient texts passed down through the ages.

One reason Raifort came to this academy to become a history teacher was to utilize this library.

And now, she had finally found information and data on the Treasures of Ruin.

This ancient text detailed the great Ruination that occurred thousands of years ago.

Though written entirely in ancient script, this posed no difficulty for Raifort, as she specialized in deciphering ancient languages.

The book stated that four disaster-causing Pokemon destroyed the royal city, and the King, to protect his country, summoned the renowned Pokemon Envoy.

The Pokemon Envoy engaged in fierce battles with those four Treasures of Ruin Pokemon and successfully imprisoned them.

However, just as they were about to be imprisoned, the "Vessel" among the Treasures of Ruin used all its strength to trigger a terrifying earthquake.

---

The ground cracked open, and the destroyed royal city was buried deep within the earth due to the earthquake, its whereabouts unknown.

After that, the king abandoned the old royal city that was mostly destroyed, choosing instead to build a new royal city elsewhere.

This time, Raifort's goal was the ruins of that royal city, as she planned to go there to investigate the Treasures of Ruin.

As for why she sought D's help, through conversations over the past month, Raifort learned that he was an excellent trainer.

The royal city ruins located deep underground might harbor some powerful Pokémon. In such situations, having a strong trainer by her side would make her investigation go more smoothly.

Hmm? Not a graveyard?

Seeing the message from Raifort, D became interested.

[King of Wings: Tell me more.]

[Ancient Investigator: It's the ruins of an ancient royal city, which were buried deep underground for some reason before the disaster. The people there didn't have time to gather their belongings and evacuate.

When the time comes, as long as we find the castle's treasure vault, we'll surely be able to collect a lot of gold and silver treasures there. We might even get hundreds or thousands of gold coins in one go!]

A treasure vault of the kingdom's castle?

Hundreds or thousands of gold coins?

D's heart was deeply moved. If this was true, wouldn't that mean he could evolve Gimmighoul into Gholdengo in one go?

Raifort was also straightforward, directly stating her reason for seeking D's assistance.

[Ancient Investigator: Places like those might harbor some strong Pokémon. Having a Champion Level trainer present would make my investigation much more convenient.]

Seeing this message, D was slightly taken aback.

After a moment, he replied: [How do you know?]

[Ancient Investigator: D, D Ranch, Porto Marinada Town, that magical ranch. It has a bit of a reputation even at Paldea Academy. I've heard my students talk about it.

Although I didn't realize at first that you were D from D Ranch, later on, you told me you run a ranch in Porto Marinada Town. Plus, there's that mysterious Koraidon, so I figured it out!]

[King of Wings: Alright then, when do we set out?]

[Ancient Investigator: Next month. July 20th. Paldea Academy will have a two-month summer break, so I won't have to stay at school as a teacher. It's a perfect opportunity to go out and investigate the ruins. How about it?]

July 20th... that's 27 days from today.

He'll do it for the birth of Gholdengo!

Thinking of this, D agreed right away.

Raifort also expressed her gratitude to him.

[Ancient Investigator: Thank you very much. If we can't find gold coins in the treasure vault, I'll try my best to collect coins from other tomb raiders when we return.]

[King of Wings: With those words, I'm relieved.]

Double insurance, either way, he doesn't lose out.

Besides, agreeing to help Raifort might earn her trust and lead to information about the Treasures of Ruin.

Seeing that no one else was contacting him to sell ancient Paldean gold coins, D simply turned off the computer and went downstairs.

In the living room downstairs, Calyrex was elegantly sipping tea while watching TV.

Seeing D come down, Calyrex glanced over at him.

D, watching the TV-absorbed Calyrex, wondered if Calyrex would eventually become nearsighted.

But then again, can Pokémon become nearsighted?

Briefly pondering, D asked, "Calyrex, do you ever feel any discomfort in your eyes?"

Calyrex was puzzled, "What do you mean?"

D gestured, "Like your eyes feel sore, you can't see things in the distance clearly, only a blur."

Hearing this, Calyrex contemplated for a moment as if perceiving, then gently shook his head, "I have never experienced such a sensation."

D: "..." It seems Pokémon don't become nearsighted.

D glanced at the clock on the wall and realized it was already eleven-thirty in the morning.

Time to start preparing lunch.

D stretched, then went to the kitchen, took out ingredients from the refrigerator, and began preparing lunch for the Pokémon.

"Indeedee～"

Seeing this, his sister Indeedee also came up to help D out.

The weather was quite hot today, so D decided to cook a refreshing mung bean soup to cool down the Pokémon.

He soaked the mung beans for ten minutes, then took them out, washed them clean, and placed them in a pot to simmer.

There were no mung beans planted on the ranch; he had bought them previously from the town.

Under the stove, seeing D cooking, the Slugma that lived in the heating duct had already crawled to the spot beneath the stove.

Seeing this, D smiled slightly and then took out a few berries to feed Slugma as he squatted down.

Slugma happily ate the berries and then focused on releasing heat to help cook the soup.

Thanks to daily practice, this Slugma had become quite skilled at controlling its temperature.

Now, it had become a good helper in the kitchen, helping Indeedee and D a lot.

While the mung bean soup was cooking, D began frying steaks for the three carnivorous Pokémon: Koraidon, Cyclizar, and Flutter Mane.

---

D had long been skilled at this technique, handling it with ease.

Once the steaks were seared to a golden brown, he lifted his hand with force, and the pieces of meat—crispy on the outside and tender on the inside, with a golden and enticing color—landed on a nearby white porcelain plate.

Then, D started steaming bass for Dragonite and making sandwiches for Ogerpon...

When all the food for the Pokemon was ready, the mung bean soup was also done.

D carried the pot to the side, then crouched down and began using an iron hook to clean the chunks of charcoal out of Slugma's body.

These charcoal chunks were made from the Berries Slugma consumed, where the pulp was digested by Slugma, and the seeds were transformed into charcoal by the magma-like high temperature and expelled from its body.

Slugma doesn't have hands, so it's not very good at cleaning itself.

So every time D helped clean it, Slugma would make a happy growl, indicating it was feeling comfortable.

After cleaning, D asked Cyclizar to go to the Ice Cave to call over the group of Pokemon for lunch.

Cyclizar obediently ran out the door.

In no time, accompanied by a lively chorus of growls, a large group of Pokemon rushed into the house.

D brought the dishes to the table and glanced down at the effect of the mung bean soup, only to regretfully discover that its effect truly was refreshing and cooling.

Yes, just as the words implied.

[Mung Bean Soup (C): Cooking effect, refreshing and cooling. When consumed by humans and Pokemon, they feel a burst of coolness, preventing heatstroke, making it very suitable for drinking during the hot summer.]

At the table, the Pokemon had already happily started eating the food D had prepared for them.

D ate while looking apologetically at the group of Pokemon before him.

In his past life, he wasn't a top chef, just an office worker who worked overtime until very late every day.

Due to his limited culinary skills, the food he made had always been the same few dishes.

Sandwiches, seared steaks, steamed bass, and occasionally some home-cooked dishes like chicken stew with mushrooms, braised pork, eggplant with garlic sauce, rib soup, and vermicelli with pork...

At that moment, D deeply regretted not having gone to New Oriental to further his studies in cooking during his past life.

But Koraidon and the others didn't feel that way at all.

Because they would always happily devour any food D made, even if the dishes were repeated, they never got tired of it.

After all, their current life was incomparably better than their time in the wild.

In the wild, nobody would make delicious meals for them with a variety of flavors every day—just Berries, again and again.

And every day, D would rotate their menu; besides food, there were also delightful Poffins and sweet Pokeblocks to eat.

By four in the afternoon, the temperature had gradually dropped, not as hot as at noon.

Taking advantage of this time, D went to the ranch to begin inspecting the crops in the fields.

Nearly a month had passed, and all the scallions he planted for Farfetch'd last time had sprouted.

Whether it was due to absorbing Gogoat's Grass Energy or some mutation, these scallions had shot up directly to two meters, several times the size of normal scallions.

This inevitably reminded D of the scallions in Shandong from his past life, which grew taller than people...

When he arrived at the Scallion Garden, Farfetch'd was holding its original little scallion, patrolling the furrows with passionate zeal.

Its gaze turned to those scallions taller than itself, filled with unprecedented excitement and thrill.

If it used these scallions as weapons... then its moves' power would certainly be greatly enhanced!

[Super Scallions: Special crop, scallions mutated from being nurtured with Gogoat's Grass Energy, grown to two meters in height, with greatly increased durability. It's truly a super scallion, which a certain Pokemon can use as a weapon, or it can be used in soup, where it tastes exceptionally delicious, and will fully mature in 1 day.]

"I knew it..." Gazing at these scallions, D didn't quite know how to express his feelings.

With scallions this large, can Farfetch'd really still use them as ordinary swords?

Or is Farfetch'd planning to switch weapons, using these super scallions as a spear or a greatsword?

---

---

---

---

# 380: Chapter 377: Evolve, Eevee! Change Weapons, Farfetch'd!

"Quack quack!"

In the Scallion Garden, Farfetch'd excitedly darted through the "scallion forest" that towered over two meters high.

Even as calm as it usually was, it couldn't contain its excitement at this moment.

So many scallions! This was practically the dreamland Farfetch'd had always longed for!

It couldn't imagine how happy a Farfetch'd born here would be!

No, it couldn't let its guard down so easily.

Farfetch'd's gaze instantly became alert as it swiftly turned its head, glancing left and right, worried that other Farfetch'd might be attracted to these perfect scallions and compete with it.

Because in the wild, this sort of thing happens; Farfetch'd often fight over better scallions.

Yet no other Farfetch'd were seen, but D had somehow arrived at the Scallion Garden.

"Quack!" Farfetch'd exclaimed excitedly, tapping its leek against the nearby scallions, as if showing D how perfect they were.

D: "…Calm down, they'll be mature by tomorrow; wait until then to harvest."

Farfetch'd had waited nearly a month, so a little more time didn't matter. It curbed its emotions and calmed down.

"Quack!" Got it!

Just then, Farfetch'd noticed a few yellow-feathered, vacant-eyed Psyducks waddling over.

The Scallion Garden was built near the river, and the opposite side was where Psyducks lived; they often roamed nearby.

Since Farfetch'd had been practicing sword skills in the dummy area, it wasn't familiar with these Psyducks.

Thus, seeing those Psyducks approach, Farfetch'd became cautious.

D helped introduce, "Don't worry, these Pokemon are water type Psyducks. They don't eat scallions or use them; they're probably just here to play."

Upon hearing this, Farfetch'd relaxed a bit internally.

It watched the Psyducks wander into the Scallion Garden, weaving among the sturdy scallions, looking up through gaps, but it wasn't sure what they were gazing at.

Soon, one Psyduck was drawn over by Farfetch'd.

It paddled its duck feet over to Farfetch'd, holding its head and staring vacantly at Farfetch'd.

Farfetch'd: ?!

Farfetch'd was startled, "Quack quack!"

Psyduck: "Quack quack?"

Then came a burst of encrypted quacking, a chorus of duck sounds.

D: "…"

Oh well, these scallions won't mature until tomorrow. Whatever happens next, it'll have to wait until then to tell Farfetch'd.

D waved goodbye to the group of Pokemon in front of him, then turned to the warehouse to start sorting the farm produce and fresh milk they planned to sell today.

Once everything was prepared, Zack arrived at the ranch just on time.

D helped Zack load the goods into the car, then heard Zack say, "The fireworks festival is coming up soon; don't forget to attend!"

"Sure, I'll be there." D replied with a smile.

That would be a great time to take Ogerpon to experience the local culture of Porto Marinada Town.

Besides Ogerpon, there are the Pokemon that arrived at the ranch this year.

Like Eevee, Farfetch'd, and Slugma.

All the goods were loaded onto the truck, and Zack closed the door, returning to the driver's seat, and shouted to D through the window, "I'm off!"

D nodded, watching Zack drive away.

Zack's life is actually quite busy, commuting daily between various rural ranches, transporting purchased fruits and vegetables to the town and other cities for sale.

He comes and goes in a hurry, mostly just greets and leaves.

After dinner, D sat on the sofa as usual, watching tomorrow's weather forecast.

On the TV screen, a female host in uniform was reporting the weather forecast for the next few days.

"Expected from tonight, the Paldea region will experience a thunderstorm, primarily in the northwest. Residents are advised to prepare in advance."

"The southeastern region remains sunny, but high temperatures are expected in contracted areas; those venturing out are urged to prepare to prevent heat stroke."

D looked at the screen featuring the soon-to-rain town area, surprisingly finding Porto Marinada Town listed.

"Thunderstorm, huh..."

Handing the remote to Calyrex, D got up to collect the clothes outside.

Sure enough, it started raining at night.

Raindrops splattered against the windows, accompanied by the incessant sound of rain, followed by the deafening roar of thunder.

Pawmot and Eevee were pressed against the window, looking out at the dim, oppressive world through the hazy glass.

"Pawmo..."

"Eee..."

Compared to the frightening thunderstorm outside, the inside of the house exuded a warm and peaceful atmosphere.

Soft carpet on the wooden floor, plush beds placed beside the bed for Pokemon to sleep in, under the bright warm lighting, D was trimming Koraidon's claws.

Koraidon closed its eyes comfortably, looking completely content.

D glanced back and said to Pawmot and Eevee, "If you're scared, you can draw the curtains, so you won't see outside."

"Pawmo!"

Pawmot wasn't scared, but seeing the little Eevee beside it looking so terrified, it flew up to pull the curtains closed.

Little Eevee came back running, jumped onto the bed, and shouted loudly, "Eee!"

---

The voice seemed to be for self-reassurance.

D sharpened Koraidon's claws, gave the dragon's head a stroke, then got up and went to bed.

"Eevee, did you ever encounter such a heavy rain in the mountains before?" D asked while petting Eevee.

"Vee…Vee!" Little Eevee nodded repeatedly.

Back then, as a lone Eevee living in the mountains, every time it rained, it would run into a tree hollow to hide because rainy nights were truly frightening.

Dark and cold, occasionally seeing fierce Pokemon running in the darkness.

D sensed Eevee was somewhat afraid now, so he gently stroked it to comfort, saying, "It's alright, the ranch is very safe. You can live here without worrying about rainy days anymore."

"Vee~" Eevee endearingly snuggled up to D.

Lately, it's been very happy every day, and has made many good friends.

Not only that, D also makes various delicious Poffins and juices for it.

It really likes D and everyone at the ranch.

D is the best person in the world!

It really likes D and everyone at the ranch!

At this moment, Eevee's affection for D reached the highest level, as it completely trusted the human who would hold it to sleep on a rainy night.

And then, suddenly a dazzling light burst from Eevee's body.

Intense emotional power intertwined with the night, transforming into a powerful energy flowing into little Eevee's body.

In the midst of this light, Eevee's appearance began to change at a visible speed.

Watching this scene, D was slightly stunned.

Is this…evolution?!

When the light dissipated, a Pokemon, pitch black with yellow eyes, blue ring patterns on its forehead, tail center, and ears, appeared before D.

A shiny Umbreon was born!

D was quite shocked looking at the Umbreon before him, unable to react for a moment.

After evolution, the Umbreon had a slight change in temperament, its yellow eyes gazing calmly at D, exuding a unique cool aura.

Just like a hunter hidden in the shadows.

"Umbreon?" D tentatively asked.

In front of him, Umbreon showed a slight smile, its growl deep yet with an evident endearing tone.

"Vee~"

Umbreon looked down at its own form, suddenly realizing it hadn't evolved into a Sylveon as originally intended, but instead into an Umbreon.

This left Umbreon feeling a bit disappointed.

On the contrary, D quickly snapped out of it and consoled, "No matter, Umbreon is also very powerful!"

Indeed…Eevee's genes are just too unstable.

Although not a Sylveon, a shiny Umbreon is also very beautiful.

Especially those blue ring patterns, now emitting a strange glow, giving a mysterious feeling.

D reached out, touched those patterns, and said to Umbreon, "Tomorrow Glaceon will definitely be surprised to see you."

Just as D anticipated.

Early the next morning, Glaceon ran from the Ice Cave and froze on the spot seeing Umbreon at first sight.

Umbreon lightly jumped in front of it, "Vee."

It was only then that Glaceon reacted, looking at D incredulously: "Vee?"

D laughed and said, "Don't recognize it? It's Eevee! It just evolved into Umbreon last night."

Umbreon lightly nodded, confirming the truth of D's words.

"Vee…" Glaceon was stunned before finally accepting the reality.

It circled around Umbreon, apparently very curious about Umbreon's form.

Umbreon just stood there, letting it observe.

Watching the two Eevees playing on the floor, D felt very satisfied.

Great, already collected two Eevees. Including the Normal form Eevee, seven more to go to collect all Eevee forms!

In the morning, the rain outside lessened, still hadn't stopped.

But Farfetch'd was impatiently eager to harvest some scallions and get a new weapon.

With no other option, D had to let Koraidon unleash its strength.

With its Scarlet Pulse activated, the next second, the rain clouds vanished completely, revealing a clear blue sky and golden sun above.

It was bright and sunny.

D and Farfetch'd arrived at the Scallion Garden.

Before officially harvesting scallions, D unsurely asked, "Can you really handle scallions of this size well?"

"Quack!" The Scallion Garden nodded confidently.

These scallions are the dream scallions for every Farfetch'd. With these, it wouldn't fear any enemy!

D: "…Alright."

Just after the rain, the scallions were still covered in water, making them feel damp.

D went forward and dug out a scallion taller than himself, cleared the roots of soil, and handed it to Farfetch'd.

Meanwhile, he was curious how Farfetch'd would use this super scallion.

Farfetch'd moved.

It quickly opened its mouth, swallowed the original small scallion, then bravely approached the super scallion.

It extended a wing, grasped the thick white stem of the super scallion firmly.

With the weapon in hand, Farfetch'd was like an armored mecha, swinging the scallion forward, seemingly adjusting to the new weapon's feel.

After a while, seemingly accustomed to the weapon, Farfetch'd paused, confidently carrying it on its shoulder.

D was greatly taken aback.

Oh dear, Farfetch'd is directly changing regional forms, becoming a Galar Region Farfetch'd!

"How does it feel?" D pondered aloud, "Can you still use the original sword moves?"

"Quack!"

Farfetch'd nodded affirmatively.

With the bulky super scallion, it left the Scallion Garden and approached the area with dummy wooden stakes.

Farfetch'd focused intently, wings gripping the scallion tightly, then unleashed its most skilled Cut.

A flash of white light, the dummy stakes were cleanly sliced in half.

This scene was enough to demonstrate the power of Farfetch'd's Cut!

"Indeed, swapping scallions has enhanced Farfetch'd's moves' power." D murmured.

But soon, he noticed something.

Wielding this super scallion seemed to consume more of Farfetch'd's stamina.

After one slash, Farfetch'd was already panting heavily.

The scallion was indeed too heavy.

Seeing this, D suggested, "Why not try holding the scallion with both hands? It might be better."

Hearing D's suggestion, Farfetch'd pondered briefly, then started gripping the super scallion with both wings.

Watching this familiar scene, some thoughts suddenly filled D's mind.

Perhaps he should try teaching Farfetch'd some great sword skills?

Moves like Horizontal Slash, Vertical Slash, Upper Hook Slash, Charged Slash, True Power Slash…

Transforming from Tachi Hero to Great Sword Hero, Farfetch'd seemed unfamiliar with this sensation.

It held the scallion, felt it carefully, then awkwardly swung the scallion.

There's a noticeable difference between holding the scallion with one wing and both wings.

After observing Farfetch'd for a while, D quickly dismissed teaching it great sword skills.

Because Farfetch'd hadn't fully adapted to this new weapon yet.

More importantly, its strength and stamina weren't enough to sustain prolonged use of this super scallion.

"Your strength isn't enough, and stamina inadequate to wield it," D pointed out Farfetch'd's current condition.

Farfetch'd felt the same. Looking at the scallion, it felt unsure of what to do.

This scallion was perfect, yet very different from the scallions it was familiar with.

Farfetch'd felt confused.

Seeing this, D guided Farfetch'd: "Feel it?"

"Quack." Farfetch'd nodded.

"So, from today start training your strength until you can fully adapt to the weight of this scallion!"

Work out and transform into a great sword macho!

---

---

---

---

# 381: Chapter 378: Boy or Girl?

To help Farfetch'd train its muscles, D found two very professional gym instructors for it that night.

7:00pm, the brightly lit Pokemon Tavern.

A group of Pokemon gathered here, raising their glasses and celebrating with drinks.

Among them were Pokemon living in the town, as well as wild Pokemon who came specifically after hearing rumors about the Pokemon Tavern.

The two gym instructors D wanted to find for Farfetch'd were among these Pokemon.

He brought Farfetch'd into the tavern, his gaze sweeping over the crowd of Pokemon, finally resting on two Machamp with tall figures, bulging muscles, and strong bodies.

These two Machamp belonged to Keri's family, and they would come to the Pokemon Tavern every evening to drink and relax.

D walked up to them and greeted, "Hello, Machamp."

The two Machamp, holding wooden mugs, turned their heads over.

They had worked on this ranch and therefore recognized D, so they casually asked, "Ni-ke?"

D glanced at the Farfetch'd by his side and explained to the two Machamp:

"Here's the thing, Farfetch'd also wants to build strong muscles like you, so I'd like to ask you about your workout methods."

The Machamp understood.

Such a small matter was easy for them.

The simple and kind Machamp gladly agreed.

So the next evening, when they came to the Pokemon Tavern for drinks again, they brought along some dumbbells.

The two Machamp found D and Farfetch'd, then began to demonstrate their training secrets to Farfetch'd.

Only to see Machamp's four stout arms raise high, each arm tightly gripping a dumbbell, lifting them high and then putting them down, repeating the process.

This workout method could greatly enhance arm strength.

Farfetch'd watched intently from the side.

Soon, Machamp stopped, placing the dumbbell in front of Farfetch'd, signaling it to follow along.

Farfetch'd looked at the pair of distinctive black dumbbells in front of it, something it had never seen before.

Plus, seeing how easily Machamp had lifted it just now made Farfetch'd instinctively think it could do the same.

So, Farfetch'd extended its wings, tightly grasping the dumbbell's handlebar, attempting to lift it.

But the dumbbell on the ground didn't budge at all.

"Gah!"

Farfetch'd exerted all its might but eventually became breathless and exhausted, unable to lift it, and instead collapsed onto the grass, panting heavily.

"Gah..."

Seeing this, D stepped up to check the dumbbell's specifications.

Unbelievable—this was shockingly a 300kg dumbbell, almost as heavy as a Koraidon!

And if each of Machamp's arms holds one, doesn't that mean the weight held by all four arms reaches 1200kg?

1200 kg, which is 2400 pounds...

Indeed, Machamp's strength is terrifying!

D gazed dumbfoundedly at the two Machamp in front of him.

The Machamp, however, looked puzzled and bewildered.

They scratched the back of their heads as they stared at the dumbbells on the ground, realizing after a good while that the dumbbells they used were a bit too heavy for this duck Pokemon.

The Machamp fell into thought.

Soon, a light bulb lit up over their heads.

"Ni-ke!"

One of the Machamp thought of another workout method.

It bent over, supporting itself with its limbs on the ground, and then began demonstrating to Farfetch'd how to do push-ups.

Farfetch'd watched for a while and felt it could do this.

"Gah!"

Farfetch'd nodded, then mimicking Machamp's posture, used its wings to support itself on the ground, and began doing push-ups.

The two Machamp guided Farfetch'd's movements from the side, and once Farfetch'd mastered it, they nodded earnestly, "Ni-ke!"

"Thank you." D thanked the two Machamp on behalf of Farfetch'd: "Today's drinks are on the house, I won't charge you."

When the Pokemon Tavern first opened, D's plan was not to charge Pokemon, aiming to help Pokemon in need.

Moreover, Pokemon didn't have money...

But he overlooked one thing that most Pokemon in the town had families.

For example, Officer Jenny's Growlithe, Keri's Machamp, the firefighter's Quaquaval...

Whether it was Officer Jenny or Keri, after the initial shock of Pokemon heading to the Pokemon Tavern every day to drink, they quickly accepted this.

After all, Machamp and Growlithe were their Pokemon, and couldn't always eat and drink for free, so every day before the Pokemon set out, they would give them some spare change to hand to the Indeedee assistant.

And wild Pokemon were a different case altogether.

Without money, upon witnessing town Pokemon paying, they too brought things like berries and stones from the wild to give to the Indeedee assistant as payment.

Some self-absorbed Pokemon even left behind beautiful feathers that had fallen from their bodies...

Over time, these Pokemon formed a habit.

Upon hearing D's words, the two Machamp chuckled simply and went for drinks.

Farfetch'd officially embarked on its fitness journey.

To help Farfetch'd better train its strength, D specifically went to Porto Marinada Town the next morning to buy a suitable set of dumbbells to bring back to the ranch.

---

The Scallion Garden is practically Farfetch'd's gym.

While it lifts dumbbells and does push-ups there, a few Psyduck from across the river always come over to watch.

Their eyes still look very vacant, and after watching for a while, the other Psyducks leave.

Only one Psyduck stays behind.

D, with his sharp eyes, notices that Psyduck's gaze changes when it looks at Farfetch'd!

Just like the way Ash's Grovyle looked at Miss Joy's Meganium in the anime!

D is stunned.

What's going on here?

Could it be that this Psyduck has fallen for Farfetch'd?

This is serious!

Psyduck and Farfetch'd can't lay eggs, right?

What egg group are these two Pokemon in?

To be sure, D hurried back to his room to search on his computer for the egg groups of Psyduck and Farfetch'd.

The result revealed a troubling fact—they are both in the Field egg group and can lay eggs together!

But D wasn't sure if that Psyduck truly had feelings for Farfetch'd.

To confirm, he spent the following days specifically observing these two Pokemon.

Farfetch'd has a very regular daily routine.

After eating a few Berries in the morning, it runs to the Scallion Garden to inspect it, making sure no bird-type Pokemon will come to steal the crops, then it starts its daily workout.

Lifting dumbbells, practicing push-ups.

And when Farfetch'd is doing these activities, that Psyduck comes over to watch.

Occasionally, it even catches small fish from the river to give to Farfetch'd, but Farfetch'd doesn't like fish and doesn't like being disturbed while working out, so it rejects it all.

The Psyduck feels downcast.

All the while, Farfetch'd is completely absorbed in its fitness regimen, oblivious to Psyduck's unusual behavior.

Eventually, not receiving any response, the disappointed Psyduck leaves and returns to the riverbank, never going back to the Scallion Garden again.

D: "..."

"Roar?"

Suddenly, Dragonite's voice comes from behind.

It turned out that it saw D sneaking around near the Scallion Garden's fence and curiously came over.

Looking at the distant Farfetch'd working out, D felt extremely emotional inside.

The love life of Pokemon is indeed so pure!

He suddenly remembered something and turned around to ask: "By the way, Dragonite, didn't you have a crush on a Dragonite before?"

He recalled that Dragonite used to be too clumsy to learn moves, couldn't win any battles, and would run to Casseroya Lake every day, staring gloomily at the moon.

D remembered this clearly.

Hearing this, Dragonite felt a bit embarrassed.

It roared and gestured for a long time, implying that was all in the past and now it no longer likes that Dragonite.

D sensed Dragonite's meaning and suddenly became curious about something, so he asked:

"Does that mean you'll remain single for life, never finding another Dragonite to lay eggs with?"

Single for life... Dragonite blinked its eyes, then shook its head and roared, "Roar roar!"

No! It still has D and everyone else!

As for laying eggs... Dragonite felt it was irrelevant.

Although D hadn't eaten Translation Nuts and couldn't understand the exact meaning of Dragonite's words, from its reaction, he could roughly guess a little.

Unconcerned with egg-laying, living happily as it pleases, no mating seasons, but would lay eggs with a loved Pokemon because of love...

Pokemon really are magical creatures!

At noon, after lunch, D went to the second floor to check the Dragonite Egg placed in the crib.

The egg laid by Dragonite had light blue patterns like Dratini and was quite large, giving off a feeling of strong life force.

This Dragonite Egg was indeed the love crystal of the two working Dragonites on the ranch.

Now it was being cared for by him and the Indeedee Sibling.

Every time he thought about this, D couldn't help but silently wonder: how could those two Dragonites trust them so much, entrusting them to care for the egg?

Logically, wouldn't wild Pokemon parents want to keep watch over their child 24/7?

D felt the trust those Dragonites had in him.

He certainly wouldn't harm their child and would take good care of the egg.

The noon sun was bright, shining through the window into the room, with the sounds of Pokemon playing below.

D took off his shoes and sat quietly beside the crib, gently stroking the egg's shell.

It had been over two months since it was laid, yet studies said that Dragonite's egg takes nearly ten months to hatch.

Now it was the end of June, and by calculation, this egg wouldn't hatch until around January next year.

"Phew, time really passes slowly!" Hearing the cicadas chirping that filled the entire summer, D couldn't help but think.

He got up and went downstairs, hoping that by the time he reached the bottom, time would have caught up to the day of the Rotom Phone's birth.

However, that was impossible.

At five in the evening, Zack arrived at the ranch as usual to collect goods.

While he parked, D was calculating the day's sales.

Watching D tally the Berries, Zack wiped the sweat off his face with the towel around his neck and grinned:

---

"This ranch has really developed! It's hard to believe that it's been less than two years, and it looks completely different now!"

D smiled, "Thanks to the Pokemon's helping hand."

Upon hearing this, Koraidon and Cyclizar, who were helping D move things, showed happy smiles on their faces.

"Ar-ghiss~"

"Gao~"

"Haha! Koraidon and Cyclizar are really capable!" Zack laughed heartily and, looking around, continued, "You're not young anymore; it's time to find a wife and get married. Once married, you can manage the ranch together, and life will only get better and better."

D: "..." Is Zack urging me to get married?

He reluctantly said, "It's still too early."

"It's not early anymore!" Zack suddenly started gossiping, "Hehe, do you have someone you like?"

"No way..." D shook his head. Though people in the Pokemon World are good-looking, it's all about the feeling.

Since he crossed over, the number of women he chatted with hasn't even exceeded ten fingers.

Keri counts as one, Mrs. Dante counts as one, Geeta counts as one, Professor Sada counts as one.

But Keri's not suitable, the Chief's not suitable, Mrs. Dante and Professor Sada, even more so.

Oh no, almost forgot one person!

A figure of Lono flashed in D's mind.

That vibrant girl.

The girl he met by chance in Levincia City and enthusiastically took him to a Chinese restaurant to enjoy some delicious food.

But... Lono's not suitable either.

"Do you want me to introduce you to someone?" Upon hearing D didn't have anyone he liked, Zack's eyes lit up instantly, "A friend of mine has a daughter, she's also a trainer. If you feel it's okay, I can introduce you, and you can try a date."

"..."

D was shocked inside. Oh my, even in this world, there's such a thing as dating!

Feeling inexplicable fear over this, he waved his hands repeatedly, "No need, no need, I'm not in a rush, I'm not in a rush."

"Alright then." Zack could only drop the idea, "If you decide in the future, you can tell me. I'm sure your grandfather would love to see you start a family."

D: "…Okay."

Zack smiled, stepped forward to load the goods onto the truck, handed the money to D, and then drove away from the ranch.

Watching the truck go far into the distance, D turned around and went home.

He glanced at the calendar on the wall; today was already June 28, and the summer fireworks festival would be held the day after tomorrow.

While calculating in his mind to take Ogerpon and Umbreon to watch the fireworks then, D began preparing dinner for the Pokemon at home.

The next morning, the sky was bright.

D was milking the Gogoat when he saw his sister, Indeedee, rushing over in a panic.

"In-dee!"

D was slightly taken aback, thought there might be something urgent, promptly stopping his work, and asked, "What's going on? What happened?"

His sister, Indeedee, pointed to the entrance of the ranch; D got up and took her over to take a look.

Then, there, he saw a pretty girl with long blue hair and icy blue eyes who had somehow arrived at the ranch.

The "girl" was initially looking at the Dragonite on the grass, and upon hearing the commotion here, gently turned her head around.

In the next moment, D met those icy blue eyes.

He heard the "girl" begin to speak:

"Hello, I'm Grusha, the Gym Leader of Glaseado Gym, sent by the Chief for special training at this place."

---

---

---

---

# 382: Chapter 379: The Four Trials, Grusha's Shock!

Grusha, how beautiful...

D was momentarily stunned.

If he hadn't known the background of these Gym Leaders in advance, he would definitely mistake Grusha for a girl right now.

Not only him, but his sister Indeedee also looked surprised.

She tugged at D's sleeve and asked quietly, "Indee?"

Is this human really a boy?

Even though he hadn't eaten any Translation Nuts, D somehow understood what his sister Indeedee meant.

Because he had previously explained Grusha's gender to his sister Indeedee.

Considering how she had rushed over with a panicked look, D felt that his sister Indeedee was indeed shocked by Grusha's appearance and actual gender.

"Hello, Mr. Grusha, I'm D, the owner of this ranch. The Chief has already talked to me about the trial."

When D came back to his senses, he introduced himself to Grusha.

Grusha nodded lightly and didn't speak; he wasn't much of a talker.

The atmosphere in the air felt a bit awkward for a moment.

D didn't know what to say either.

He carefully recalled Grusha's background.

If he remembered correctly, Grusha used to be a famous snowboarder, with a passionate personality back then.

However, after a severe injury in a competition, he could no longer ski and chose to retire.

Since then, Grusha's once-fiery personality seemed frozen, becoming as silent and cold as an Ice Type Pokemon.

After a brief thought, D decided to introduce some trials in the ranch to Grusha first.

Just then, another visitor arrived at the ranch.

"Hello, is anyone there?!"

A lively girl's voice came from behind.

D and Grusha turned around and saw a girl in a beige short-sleeve shirt with a lightning mark on it running over.

Grusha was slightly stunned, and D couldn't help but widen his eyes.

What a coincidence! He had just mentioned Lono yesterday, and here Lono came.

But speaking of which, Lono's sudden appearance felt like a Bug Catcher suddenly popping up in a game, unexpected and abrupt.

Lono spotted Grusha and looked surprised: "Huh? Grusha, you're here to take the trial too? What a coincidence!"

Grusha nodded slightly: "...The Glaseado Gym is on break for the next couple of days, so I had time to come by."

"I see!" Lono said while glancing at D, waving at him: "Good morning, D, sorry to bother you again!"

A smile crept onto D's face: "That works out perfectly. Grusha just arrived, so I'll introduce you both to the ranch's current trials!"

Grusha nodded: "Thank you."

Lono chuckled from the side: "Last time I came to challenge, I had quite a hard time, so be careful later, Grusha!"

Hearing Lono's words, Grusha felt somewhat surprised. He had things he wanted to say but opened his mouth and didn't speak.

"Sister, please get the guests some tea," D said to his sister Indeedee.

"Indee~" His sister Indeedee nodded happily and turned to go back into the house.

D then took the two of them on a tour around the ranch:

"Up until now, there are four trials in the ranch, namely the Grass Trial, Thunder Trial, Hurricane Trial, and Ancient Double Battle Trial."

He didn't mention the Metronome Trial, as it's an entertainment-focused trial that can only be participated in by Pokemon that also have the Metronome move.

"In the Grass Trial, challengers need to battle my three Gogoats."

D turned his gaze to the three Gogoats on the grass.

Grusha looked over there.

Gogoats, Grass Type Pokemon, are countered by Ice Type Pokemon.

Being a Trainer highly skilled in Ice Type, battling under type advantage might not achieve the desired effect.

If he had a choice, he would prefer to opt for trials that counter him, to better hone his skills.

Hence, Grusha mentally brushed off this Grass Trial.

Lono seemed to have seen through Grusha's thoughts, and she giggled from the side: "These Gogoats aren't just ordinary Gogoats! It's said that even the Chief finds them tricky to handle!"

D: "..." Geeta had indeed witnessed the Gogoats using Grassy Terrain.

Upon hearing this, Grusha was slightly taken aback, then realized these three Gogoats might be Champion-Level Pokemon.

Sometimes, type advantage doesn't mean everything. He had been careless.

"I understand now," Grusha said.

D continued walking but couldn't find Pawmot, so he called towards the Chicken House.

The next second, Pawmot flew toward them.

D: "The next I want to introduce is the Thunder Trial, run by Pawmot."

Pawmot flew up to D, looking at him in confusion: "Pawmo?"

"This guest intends to challenge the trials at the ranch, and I'm introducing them," D explained.

At these words, a gleam lit up in Pawmot's eyes.

It immediately extended its little arms to show off its strength: "Pawmo Pawmo!"

---

As Grusha gazed at the special Pawmot before him, his mouth fell open in surprise.

A flying Pawmot? What's going on here?

And what's that hammer it's holding? Is it Pawmot's weapon?

But is a normal Pawmot supposed to look like this? No, right?

At this moment, countless questions arose in Grusha's mind.

He could no longer hold back and asked, "How does this Pawmot do it?"

"Of course, it's like the electromagnetic levitation of Magnemite." D spouted nonsense seriously, "It's the interaction between electromagnetic fields generated by electromagnetic induction, enabling the object to levitate. Electric Type Pokemon can naturally control electricity, and Pawmot has been receiving specialized training."

Grusha: "???"

"I lost to Pawmot last time!" Lono, who had already witnessed Pawmot's prowess, shifted her tone and said with determination, "But this time, I've professionally trained my Pokemon, and we'll definitely pass!"

D: "..."

He didn't reveal the fact that Pawmot had gained the King's Power, but instead introduced the next trial.

"Next is the Storm Trial led by Dragonite, where challengers must face special weather conditions during a storm and defeat Dragonite within it."

Upon hearing this, Grusha nodded, indicating he understood.

D smiled and then turned his gaze to Koraidon and Flutter Mane.

"Next, I'll introduce the ultimate hidden trial on the ranch! The ancient double battle!"

"Challengers need to face attacks from those two Pokemon simultaneously. This is the most challenging trial on the ranch, and only trainers who have reached Champion Level can have the qualifications to challenge it!"

Upon hearing this, Grusha and Lono both froze slightly.

Only trainers who have reached Champion Level can challenge? Sounds impressive! Lono's eyes lit up.

While Grusha looked at the two unfamiliar Pokemon, he was equally astonished.

No wonder the chief sent them to this small ranch for the trial...

What surprised Grusha even more was that such a powerful Champion Level trainer was hidden away in this place.

"Have you decided? Which trial do you want to challenge?" D asked.

Grusha fell into thought.

Meanwhile, Lono raised her hand first: "Thunder Trial, hehe, this time we'll definitely pass!"

"That's not necessarily guaranteed!" D took Pawmot to the battle ground.

Grusha hadn't made his decision yet, so he watched the match from the sidelines.

For this match, D didn't participate in directing, leaving Pawmot to utilize its abilities on its own.

Lono stood at the other end of the battle ground, pulling out a red-and-white ball. As a white light flashed, a Kilowattrel appeared on the battlefield.

Her brother Indeedee blew the whistle he held, officially starting the match.

Lono opened with Tailwind, establishing a favorable advantage for her side.

Pawmot swung its small hammer, charging at Kilowattrel, who flapped its wings and unleashed a Hurricane.

The fierce wind surged wildly, blade-like air currents slashing toward Pawmot.

Pawmot's forward steps were thwarted. Seeing this, Lono directly ordered Kilowattrel to pursue.

"Discharge!"

With the command, Kilowattrel erupted with a powerful current.

Its ability "Wind Power" was activated with the aid of Tailwind, putting Kilowattrel in a Charge mode.

From its center, it unleashed a vast spread of yellow lightning.

Pawmot's figure was instantly engulfed.

All the while, Lono took a quick glance at D, "Hehehe, see the real skills of Lono!"

"Being distracted during a battle isn't a good habit!" D reminded her helplessly.

At this point, Grusha, watching the match, realized something was wrong.

No, something's off! That Pawmot is absorbing Kilowattrel's electricity!

On the battlefield, the intense currents began to frantically pour into Pawmot's hammer and body.

Amid the spark, Pawmot's eyes emitted a blue glow.

Bright glimmers coursed over its body, and suddenly a golden helmet and armor appeared out of thin air!

Seeing this, Lono panicked, "Huh? What's that?"

Grusha was visibly shocked.

The Pawmot, in the form of King of Hammers, raised its hammer high.

The originally tiny hammer suddenly expanded at a visible rate.

Pawmot swung the giant hammer, charging forward, to strike Kilowattrel.

The hammer's coverage was too large for Kilowattrel to evade, ultimately smashing to the ground, it lost its ability to battle with its eyes rolled back.

"Ah... lost again..."

Lono's face was filled with despair, and sensing the trainer's mood, the two little Magnemite above her head began to spin in circles abnormally.

The light around Pawmot flickered, returning to its normal form.

D went forward and fed Life Sheep Milk to Kilowattrel, and it soon revived.

A sheepish Pawmot flew forward. The Electric Type Energy had been too overwhelming, stimulating it beyond control, instinctively using the King's Power to defend.

---

D Ranch

Until he saw Kilowattrel recover, Pawmot finally breathed a sigh of relief.

Looking at Lono's expression of absolute dejection, D couldn't help but laugh.

This side of Lono is really adorable!

Compared to her virtual persona in the game, the real-life Lono is truly lively and vibrant.

Though initially downcast from another battle loss, Lono didn't stay despondent for long, quickly bouncing back to her energetic self.

She then lifted her head and beamed at D, flashing a bright, vivacious smile.

In the sunlight, the girl's smile was radiant, full of vitality and energy.

D felt like something struck his heart, and for the first time in his life, he felt his heart skip a beat — simply beautiful!

At this moment, Grusha stepped forward.

She looked at Pawmot with eyes full of shock and bewilderment.

"D… what was going on with Pawmot just now?" Grusha couldn't help but ask.

D shifted his attention to Pawmot.

He thought for a moment, then explained, "Well, how should I put it? That power isn't its own."

Hearing this, both Grusha and Lono were momentarily stunned.

"What do you mean?" Grusha asked, confused.

Faced with this situation, D chose to enlighten, "That's the power of The King of Bountiful Harvests, Calyrex."

Lono had been to the Ranch before and had seen Calyrex, so she knew of its existence.

So this time, D aimed his enlightenment at Grusha.

He detailed the legend of Calyrex to Grusha and then added:

"The courage within Pawmot and its heroic heart to help other Pokemon earned Calyrex's recognition, and Calyrex granted it special power to grow stronger."

"The King of Bountiful Harvests…" Grusha muttered after listening, "I feel like I've heard that somewhere before."

Lono reminded her, "There was a broadcast on TV about the legend of Calyrex! It once helped a mountain restore itself! Truly amazing!"

Grusha then recalled where he had heard it.

He looked at Pawmot in disbelief, "It's incredible that such things exist in this world…"

D smiled faintly and asked, "So, have you decided? Which trial do you want to take part in?"

Grusha nodded, "I want to participate in Gogoat's Grass Trial, D. Let me see the strength of a Champion Level Gogoat!"

The Gogoat that even the top would find daunting intrigued him greatly.

Grusha's choice truly surprised D.

He thought Grusha would choose Pawmot or Dragonite…

"Alright." D nodded and then summoned three Gogoat.

Two of the Gogoat stopped behind him, while the other one stepped forward into the battle arena.

The next moment, a vast ocean-like grass energy surged from its body, instantly enveloping the entire battle area.

On the originally barren dirt ground, thousands of grass blades appeared, seemingly both real and illusory.

They swayed gently in the breeze, appearing just like a real meadow.

Watching this scene, Grusha's face showed a grave expression.

Grassy Terrain?

Releasing such immense energy from its body instantly, an ordinary Pokemon couldn't do that!

This time, it was Lono with Kilowattrel watching the battle from the side of the arena.

Her eyes were bright, studying with enthusiasm and excitement like a referee in a gym:

"Now facing off are the Gym Leader Grusha from the Glaseado Gym and the Rancher from D Ranch. Who will ultimately claim the trophy? Hehe, let's wait and see. Now, the battle begins!"

---

---

---

---

# 383: Chapter 380: Koraidon, Cyclizar, and Lono

Looking at the mysterious glow emanating from the grassy terrain in front of him, Grusha slowly took out a Poke Ball from his waist.

Pressing the button, the red and white ball, originally shrunk to the size of a marble, began to enlarge immediately.

A beam of white light shot out from it, and a large-sized Pokemon with a massive body, filled with strong muscles, appeared opposite Gogoat.

This was Cetitan, an Ice Type Pokemon that dwells atop snowy mountains.

The battle officially began.

"Cetitan, use Ice Spinner!"

At Grusha's command, Cetitan's snow-white body started to spin rapidly, covered in a layer of thin ice, and charged towards Gogoat.

Where Ice Spinner passed, the grassy terrain on the ground was destroyed by the cold hard ice.

Trying to use Ice Spinner to destroy the grassy terrain?

D saw through Grusha's plan, but it's not that easy!

"Gogoat, use Vine Whip!" D calmly issued the command.

"Baaah!"

With a sheep-like cry, two thick Vine Whips flew out from Gogoat's body, resembling giant pythons, and went straight towards the spinning Cetitan, aiming to strike.

Before the Vine Whip, Cetitan's body resembled a spinning top, starting to turn on the spot when struck.

Witnessing this, Grusha couldn't help but look shocked.

That move... is it Vine Whip?

No! How can there be such thick Vine Whips!

But suddenly, Grusha noticed something terrifying.

The half of the grassy terrain originally destroyed by Ice Spinner astonishingly gathered again!

"What on earth is happening?" Grusha exclaimed in disbelief, everything before him exceeded his comprehension.

Meanwhile, Cetitan, treated like a spinning top, stopped dizzying, eyes spinning like mosquito coils, and eventually couldn't handle the dizziness, falling to the ground.

"Hold on!" Grusha shouted, trying to suppress the strange feeling in his heart.

As soon as the words fell, Grusha sensed something amiss.

Because the grassy terrain didn't restore Cetitan's stamina!

Before he could figure it out, he heard D give another command:

"Gogoat, use your best move!"

"Baaah!"

Gogoat responded with full combat spirit.

Grusha was slightly stunned.

In the next moment, he saw Gogoat slightly bent over, green light swirling in its mouth, ultimately morphing into a giant grass blade.

Grusha's pupils contracted.

With the grass blade clenched between its teeth, Gogoat charged like an arrow released from a bowstring.

In a flash of white light, the still-dizzy Cetitan was hit in a critical spot and collapsed to the ground with a thud.

And Gogoat stopped not far behind Cetitan.

Its posture and expression looked incredibly suave and confident.

Cetitan lost the ability to battle...

Grusha stood frozen in disbelief.

Watching Lono, shocked, eyes wide and mouth agape: "Wow!"

"Unfortunately, you didn't pass the Grass Trial," D said.

Grusha remained silent, completely stunned by what had just occurred.

Indeedee brought Life Sheep Milk, treating Cetitan's injuries.

Grusha was unable to snap out of it for a long time.

He had too many questions lingering.

Wondering about the move used by Gogoat, why the grassy terrain didn't affect Cetitan, why the destroyed grassy terrain swiftly restored.

In the end, unable to contain his curiosity, Grusha asked.

D answered each question.

"That move is Leaf Blade, just a special version of it, inspired by a Pokemon named Zacian."

"As for the other two questions, it's because Gogoat's mastery over Grass Energy has reached a level that ordinary Pokemon cannot achieve."

"It can precisely control the surrounding Grass Type Energy, making it effective only for itself, and similarly, Gogoat can release endless Grass Energy to restore the grassy terrain destroyed by Ice Spinner."

After all, the three Gogoats in his home grew up eating leaves from the World Tree! Achieving all this is hardly surprising.

Hearing these words, Grusha gazed at the three Gogoats with fascination.

At this moment, he finally understood why the chief wanted Gym Leaders like them to come to this ranch for special training...

Those three Gogoats are true Champion Level Pokemon.

Battling and exchanging skills with Champion Level Pokemon would indeed be greatly beneficial for Gym Leaders like them.

"How amazing!" Lono's eyes glimmered with admiration. "D, how do you usually train these Pokemon?"

"Hmm? How do I train them..." D looked at Lono and chuckled: "Just integrate training into everyday life, since living itself is a form of practice."

"Living itself is a form of practice..." Grusha mumbled.

D nodded and explained: "Integrate special training into daily life, such as letting Dragonite use Rain Dance to water the plants every day."

"One time or two may not show results, but after a thousand times, ten thousand times, its proficiency in the move will reach a perfect level, capable of instantly using Rain Dance to change the weather, gaining an advantage in battles."

---

"The same goes for Gogoat; I usually let them use Grass Energy to cultivate the crops in the fields, which not only ensures the healthy growth of the crops but also trains their ability to control Grass Type Energy."

Hearing this, Grusha couldn't help but relate it to himself.

He fell into deep thought and couldn't snap out of it for a long time.

Just then, Koraidon, who was training on the other side, ran over with Cyclizar and Dragonair to find D.

Because one Dragonair didn't control its strength well during the sparring, the opposing Dragonair got injured and lost its ability to fight.

After understanding the situation, D immediately took out Life Sheep Milk to feed the injured Dragonair.

Dragonair soon woke up and slowly opened its eyes.

Upon seeing D, it immediately snuggled up to him affectionately.

D quickly turned his head to look around and was relieved to see his Dragonite wasn't paying attention.

He reached out to pat Dragonair's head and said, "Okay, okay, have a rest first. Go play in Snorlax Park for a while!"

"Woo~"

A few Dragonair flew away together.

Lono looked brightly at their backs; she was just about to say something when she suddenly noticed an unignorable gaze on her.

Lono turned her head and saw D's Koraidon squatting on the grass, looking back.

She was slightly stunned, then cheerfully greeted Koraidon, "Long time no see, Koraidon! How have you been lately?"

"Aga hiss~" Koraidon nodded its big head continuously.

D noticed this scene. He looked at Koraidon, then at Lono, and suddenly realized that his Koraidon seemed to like Lono quite a bit?

Lono then looked at Cyclizar nearby.

Then she noticed something.

"D, doesn't Koraidon look a bit like Cyclizar?"

Both look like motorcycles, with airbags on their chests resembling tires...

D smiled and nodded, "That's right. Some data say Koraidon is what Cyclizar looked like in the past, but whether it's true or not, who knows."

"However, the Paldea Region doesn't have Koraidon as a native Pokemon; it was a Pokemon discovered by Professor Sada in the Paldea Giant Pit, and though it looks fierce, it's quite gentle in nature!"

Hearing D praise it, Koraidon let out a joyful growl.

"Aga hiss!"

D smiled and reached out to pat its head.

Cyclizar watched this scene with eyes full of envy.

It also wanted to snuggle up to D like Koraidon...

D didn't notice Cyclizar's expression, but Lono did.

She squinted her eyes, feeling she had discovered something significant.

D has caught so many Pokemon that Cyclizar seems to be neglected!

Cyclizar keenly sensed a gaze on itself and turned its head, seeing a human looking at it.

"Gao?"

Lono waved and greeted, "Hi, Cyclizar!"

Cyclizar blinked its eyes, looking adorably clueless, "Gao..."

At this moment, Grusha snapped out of his thoughts, feeling enlightened, and solemnly thanked D.

"Thank you very much; I understand now why the head instructed us to come to you."

Grusha used formal language.

D immediately felt a bit embarrassed, "It's not that grand... just use informal language, you see, Lono calls me just by my name."

But Grusha was very insistent.

D: "..."

Before they knew it, it was noon, and D invited the two to stay for lunch at the ranch.

As lunch was almost ready, the Pokémon playing outside all ran back into the house.

Seeing this group of Pokemon, Grusha and Lono were once again amazed by the rare Pokemon in the ranch.

Even Calyrex The King of Bountiful Harvests and its two loyal steeds were in the ranch!

Grusha sat beside this Legendary Pokemon feeling an indescribable confusion.

Can Legendary Pokemon watch TV like humans do...

Especially since Calyrex greeted him with a friendly smile.

Grusha was stunned.

This made him more convinced that D was indeed a Champion-level Trainer with years of travel experience.

Lono, on the other hand, noticed something else.

She observed how these Pokemon interacted with D.

Pokemon like Koraidon, Pawmot, Alcremie, and Ogerpon expressed their fondness for D very directly.

They would actively run up to interact with D, letting him pet them.

Only one Pokemon didn't do this; it just stood at a reasonable distance, looking enviously at everything.

"Cyclizar is neglected..." Lono murmured in her heart.

Such situations occur from time to time among some Trainers.

When a Trainer catches too many Pokemon, some of them inevitably get neglected.

After all, there's only one Trainer, and they can't fairly allocate their affection to every Pokemon.

For this reason, major Leagues advise Trainers to carry only six Pokemon during their journeys.

Thinking this, Lono couldn't help but call out, "Cyclizar!"

Cyclizar turned its head upon hearing its name.

Lono encouraged it, "You can go up to D and seek him out, just like Koraidon! He will definitely notice you!"

---

"Gao?" Cyclizar was a bit taken aback. Could it also become Koraidon?

Lono nodded seriously: "That's right, believe in yourself!"

Cyclizar felt a bit troubled, but it ultimately couldn't resist the temptation and mustered up the courage to head to the kitchen.

D, who was cooking soup, was quite surprised to see Cyclizar coming over: "What's up?"

Cyclizar mimicked Koraidon and nudged close to D.

Noticing Cyclizar's actions, D was slightly stunned, then a smile appeared on his face.

Cyclizar was being affectionate with him!

D chuckled and reached out to rub Cyclizar's head: "Good boy!"

"Gao!"

Cyclizar's eyes lit up, and it gradually grew bolder.

It enthusiastically extended its tongue to lick D's palm.

And then, D's hand was covered in drool.

D: "..."

"You and Koraidon must both be like dogs," D couldn't help but tease.

These two Pokémon really loved using their tongues to lick people.

He had never seen a Gardevoir lick its Trainer with its tongue!

Detecting that D's tone wasn't scolding it, Cyclizar was instantly happier.

"Alright, go play in the living room for now, I'm cooking!" D helplessly patted Cyclizar's head.

Cyclizar obediently walked away.

Returning to the living room, it exchanged a delighted glance with Lono.

Lono grinned and encouragingly nodded towards Cyclizar.

Nearby, Grusha noticed Lono's small gesture.

He looked calmly at Cyclizar, saying nothing, maintaining his composed demeanor.

"D treats Pokémon so gently!" Lono exclaimed.

Grusha nodded slightly. Although they hadn't been together long, he could feel the warmth emanating from D.

Grusha's gaze swept across the living room, seeing Pokémon all over, chasing and playing with each other.

Would these Pokémon be living happily by his side?

Grusha thought about those companions beside him.

He used to be as lively and enthusiastic as Lono, but ever since that incident forced him into retirement, he couldn't laugh as joyfully as before.

Every day at the Glaseado Gym, he struggled to suppress his emotions, often teased by the gym's assistants for being as cold as ice.

His companions, Alolan Ninetales, Frosmoth, Beartic, Cetitan, Weavile, and Altaria, must be worried, right?

In the past, these Ice Type Pokémon would happily laugh and ski with him enthusiastically.

But since his retirement, unknowingly, their faces also lost smiles.

Those Pokémon just silently stayed by his side.

Thinking of this, Grusha looked at his right leg. In that accident, it was nearly broken. Though it was saved later, he couldn't ski anymore.

"What are you thinking about?"

D came into the living room with the dishes, seeing Grusha lost in thought, staring at his right leg, not knowing what he was thinking.

Grusha snapped back, calmly saying: "Nothing."

"Then come over to eat!" D roughly guessed the reason in his heart but didn't ask further, instead smilingly letting his sister Indeedee take the guest to wash up for dinner.

Grusha nodded, got up and followed his sister Indeedee out.

Left behind, Lono gave D a look as if wanting to speak but hesitating.

D raised an eyebrow, asking: "What's up?"

Lono explained: "Grusha wasn't like this before; he used to be a great skier, but he was forced to retire due to a severe injury, which also changed his personality... No one knows how to comfort him."

She had also encouraged Grusha before, hoping he could move on from the past and look forward, but to no avail.

That's why Lono hadn't spoken just now.

"A leg injury, huh..." D nodded thoughtfully.

He suddenly remembered something.

Was the Life Sheep Milk produced by Gogoat also effective on human injuries?

But seeing Grusha's serious condition, the accident must have hurt his bones.

In such a case, who knows if the Life Sheep Milk would work...

After all, sheep milk isn't a cure-all.

After a brief consideration, D said to Lono: "After we eat, I'll try to think of something."

If Life Sheep Milk doesn't work, then let's try the power of Pokémon.

His sister Indeedee's Lunar Dance, Calyrex's Life Dew, and Heal Pulse, combined with Life Sheep Milk and some special dishes, might be able to help Grusha.

---

---

---

---

# 384: Chapter 381: Geeta: Tell Me Your Wish

Think of a way? What way?

Hearing D's words, Lono was a bit taken aback.

She was just introducing Grusha's situation to D and didn't mean to ask for his help, right?

Moreover... is it even possible to do something like this?

D didn't think much about it; he returned to the kitchen to serve the prepared dishes on the dining table.

During the meal, D exchanged a glance with Lono, and as Lono hesitated to speak, D asked Grusha:

"I heard that your leg was injured not long ago, how is it feeling now?"

Grusha was momentarily stunned, not quite understanding why D brought this up.

After a brief silence, he replied, "I can walk normally, but I can't do any intense exercise anymore."

If I force myself, my right leg hurts a lot.

"I see..." D nodded. "After the meal, let me take a look at it for you!"

Grusha was slightly startled, "Mr. D, are you a doctor?"

"No," D shook his head, "I just want to see if I can help because I happen to have a very effective remedy here, useful for both humans and Pokemon."

Hearing these words of concern, a ripple stirred in Grusha's usually calm heart.

He knew D wanted to help him, but his injury had already been diagnosed by renowned orthopedic surgeons and was hard to return to its original state.

Though he held no hope, Grusha remained composed on the surface, "I understand, thank you very much."

D smiled and said nothing more.

After lunch, D found Calyrex and asked for His Psychic abilities to examine Grusha's right leg.

Calyrex, having nothing else to do, agreed.

He levitated in front of Grusha, eyes flashing, and soon a blue light crept up Grusha's right leg.

Calyrex closed His eyes, and all the traces perceived by His Psychic abilities fully formed in His mind.

"...Hmm, it is indeed a significant injury," Calyrex said. "I see fracture marks on thy bone."

Sitting on the sofa, Grusha's mouth slightly opened, showing amazement. He glanced at his right leg under Psychic control and then at the Legendary Pokemon emitting a blue aura in front of him, momentarily at a loss for words.

"Can it be healed?" D asked.

Calyrex nodded, "I can use Heal Pulse to mend the bone. D, fetch some sheep milk, and together, they can fully heal his leg's hidden injury."

"Got it," D replied, turning to the fridge to fetch a bottle of Life Sheep Milk and approached Grusha.

Grusha was still in disbelief.

Did Calyrex just say that his leg could recover?

How could that be possible...

But what if it was true?

Grusha's emotions suddenly became excited.

Nearby, Lono heard the conversation, and her face also showed an expression of disbelief.

"Can Grusha's injury really be healed?" Lono asked.

D nodded and placed the sheep milk on the table, "Calyrex said yes. Next, He's going to use Heal Pulse on your bone, and now drink this bottle of sheep milk, it will help you recover better."

Grusha recognized the sheep milk in front of him.

The bottle had a chibi Skiddo on it, and he recalled that in the morning, D had given this to the Pokemon who lost their ability to fight.

And the Pokemon that drank this milk almost instantly recovered.

Seeing Grusha lost in thought, D smiled and said, "D Ranch specialty, one-time offer; if you want more later, you'll have to pay for it!"

Grusha finally snapped out of it.

Thinking his leg injury could be healed, his long-frozen heart trembled after a long time.

Grusha picked up the sheep milk and gulped it down.

Seeing this, Calyrex began the treatment.

His spiritual power turned into magical Heal Pulse, and these pink Mind Waves rippled out like ripples.

Grusha's right leg was submerged within.

One second, two seconds, three seconds...

Calyrex stopped, showing a gentle smile, "It's all right now."

Grusha stared blankly at his right leg.

At this moment, he could clearly feel a warm energy traveling through his entire right leg.

It felt like a long-blind person regaining sight!

A spark of light appeared in Grusha's eyes.

He tentatively stood up, feeling the reborn right leg.

Watching this, D was also curious, "Do you want to go outside and run a bit?"

Grusha nodded heavily.

Then, the group headed to the ranch.

Under the gaze of many Pokemon, Grusha began running and jumping on the grass, performing various high-difficulty movements.

For the first time in a long time, his cold and pale face finally showed a long-lost smile.

Seeing this, D knew Grusha's leg had completely recovered.

He couldn't help but exclaim, "At this rate, I'm going to become Paldea's renowned physician!"

First helping Alder's Volcarona with an injury, then helping Grusha with his leg.

Hearing this, Lono turned her head to look over, from this angle, she could only see D's profile and the smile on his lips.

---

D's figure is tall, his eyebrows and eyes are deep, and his face is handsome.

Perhaps it is due to years of farming, his skin is a little tanned by the sun, but this only makes him appear more mature and stable, giving off a sense of reliability.

D who helps others, D who takes care of Pokemon, D when cooking, D being serious...

Lono's gaze was involuntarily attracted.

Seeing the smile on D's lips, she couldn't help but reveal a radiant smile as well.

Just then, D turned to look over, seeing Lono watching him, D was quite surprised: "What's the matter?"

Lono quickly withdrew her gaze, shaking her head: "It's nothing, nothing."

D: "..."

Lono changed the subject: "Have you ever helped someone heal before?"

D: "Not exactly, Grusha was the first, but I did help other Trainers heal their Pokemon before."

"That Pokemon was also seriously injured, and with the current medical technology, it was impossible to heal it. If things continued like that, death was inevitable."

Lono understood, she grinned and said: "That Trainer must have been very happy, right?"

D recalled Alder's appearance, nodded: "Yes, he helped me out a lot as a thank you!"

At this moment, Grusha ran back.

His face was rosy, his expression excited, he rushed up and gave D a big hug.

D: "???"

Grusha let go, gratefully saying: "Thank you, D!"

Only then did D realize why Grusha suddenly made such a move.

He smiled and said: "No need for thanks, it was just a lift of a hand."

But Grusha insisted, shaking his head: "No matter what, I have to thank you."

D was just about to say something, then saw Grusha's Pokemon also excitedly surrounding him.

D pointed at them and said: "Your Pokemon are worried about you too!"

Grusha turned around, looking at the group of Pokemon that had accompanied him for so long.

Alolan Ninetales, Frosmoth, Beartic, Cetitan, Weavile, Altaria.

Each Pokemon's eyes were full of joyful light, they looked at their Trainer brightly.

Grusha was moved, he wiped the fog from his eyes, opened his mouth: "Everyone... I'm sorry, I have made you worry during this period!"

"Oo~!"

The Alolan Ninetales at the front stood up, emitting a melodious call from its mouth.

Grusha reached out his hand, gently stroking Alolan Ninetales' head.

Seeing this, the other Pokemon also gathered around, all of them happy for their Trainer's restored health.

Grusha's face always wore a smile, reaching out to gently stroke each of his Pokemon.

A scene of great reunion.

D stood in the back watching this scene, couldn't help but ask: "Is this Grusha's true personality?"

Lono smiled and nodded: "Yes, that's right! Grusha is actually very lively and cheerful!"

"It really is surprising." D sighed.

After soothing the Pokemon that had been accompanying him, Grusha then came to the two of them.

Lono grinned at him: "Congratulations, Grusha!"

D asked: "Do you have any plans afterward? Are you planning to continue as the Glaseado Gym Leader, or go back to the ski resort as an athlete?"

This question no longer needed to be tangled with.

Grusha glanced at D, then at Lono, and laughed softly:

"Do you remember comforting me before, letting the past be past, people need to look forward."

Lono nodded and said: "That's right! So have you made up your mind now?"

Grusha gently nodded: "The past is already past, now I am just Grusha, the Glaseado Gym Leader, I want to live with everyone, and face the challenges of Trainers together!"

The few Pokemon standing in a row behind Grusha responded to him with voices.

"I understand." D joked: "If that's the case, I'm relieved. To be honest, I was just worried that you'd resign from being a Gym Leader to return to the arena, and the Glaseado Gym would be unmanaged, and the chief would come to scold me!"

Grusha burst into laughter.

Lono imagined the scene and chuckled: "The chief is not like that at all, she would drag you to the Glaseado Gym to let you work there for Grusha."

"Then forget it!" D shook his head repeatedly: "It's too cold at Glaseado Mountain, the Cyclizar can't get used to it."

Meanwhile, at the Paldea League Headquarters.

Geeta was busy dealing with the wild Pokemon wreaking havoc in the city, ruining the power grid system, suddenly sneezed several times.

Geeta had dark circles under her eyes but didn't pay attention.

Behind her, Rika leaned against the door frame, arms crossed, looking at the Glimmet entwined in Geeta's hair, couldn't help but say: "Chief, you should go take a rest."

Geeta didn't turn around: "There are still some things unfinished, by the way, did you see the power outage incident in Weimu City yesterday?"

Rika: "...You gave that matter to Larry this morning, by now, he should be there!"

"Oh." Only then did Geeta put down her work, embarrassedly smiled: "In that case, I really should go and rest a bit."

---

Rika sighed helplessly.

Their boss is really on a roll…

*

Grusha and Lono left the ranch and returned to their respective Gyms to continue working.

Life went on as if nothing had changed.

But D found that Cyclizar had become more clingy.

After Cyclizar ran over for the thirty-eighth time to lick him, D couldn't hold back any longer.

"Don't learn bad habits from Koraidon!"

"Gaa…", Cyclizar looked pitifully.

Koraidon, on the other hand, looked innocent: "Ah, gaa!"

It really hadn't done anything! Well… not completely nothing, since everything Cyclizar was doing, Koraidon had done before.

Thinking of this, Koraidon immediately turned its head guiltily, showed its back to D, closed its eyes, and pretended to nap.

D: "…"

He saw right through Koraidon's guilty conscience, thinking Cyclizar must have learned its licking move from Koraidon!

"Cyclizar, get ready to battle!" D said.

Cyclizar looked bewildered, battle what?

It quickly turned its head to look around but didn't seem to spot any enemies.

Koraidon was also awakened by the sound and raised its head to observe the surroundings.

D gave Cyclizar a command: "Use Lick on Koraidon!"

"Gaa?!" Cyclizar looked incredulous.

It looked at Koraidon, and Koraidon was looking at it.

The two Pokemon looked at each other, eyes full of disdain.

Cyclizar didn't want to lick Koraidon, so it cried out and turned to run away.

It ran to a shaded spot and lay there leisurely.

Being close to D was so nice!

Cyclizar felt immensely grateful to the human named Lono.

D watched Cyclizar run away and fell into deep thought.

Koraidon stretched lazily and seeing D staring at Cyclizar, it couldn't help but ask: "Ah, gaa?"

D pondered, "Have you noticed Cyclizar has gotten mischievous recently?"

Could Pokemon have a rebellious phase?

No, Cyclizar doesn't look like it's going through a rebellious phase.

Instead, it seems more like a human child growing from an innocent and cute young age into a mischievous adolescence.

Hearing this, Koraidon immediately understood the situation.

"Ah, gaa…"

At this time, it might just be trying to catch D's attention and play with him?

At that moment, the videophone in the corner of the living room rang.

D turned and walked over for a glance.

It was Geeta calling.

Pressing the answer button, Geeta's face appeared on the screen.

With a satisfied smile, she started with: "You did well, D!"

D was a bit puzzled: "What?"

Geeta: "I've already heard from Lono and Grusha. You had Calyrex help Grusha heal his leg injury, resolved his personal issues, and strengthened the Glaseado Gym."

"I'm very happy you're willing to help him. Tell me, what reward do you want? I can offer you a gift on behalf of the Paldea League, as a token for hosting these Gym Leaders."

D: "…"
