What? He hadn't even considered a reward for helping Grusha!

However, Geeta encouraged: "Come on, speak up about the reward you want, Pokemon, training tools, evolution stones, or anything else. If the Paldea League doesn't have it, I can also give you my personal items."

---

---

---

---

# 385: Chapter 382: Geeta's Grand Plan for Recruiting Talent

"Chief, did you know? What you said reminded me of a wish-granting Divine Dragon."

Geeta seemed slightly surprised, "Divine Dragon? Is it also a Pokemon?"

"Maybe!" D said, "It's just a legend."

On the screen, Geeta nodded slightly and continued, "So, what kind of reward would you like?"

D thought for a moment, then shook his head helplessly and said, "To be honest, there's nothing in particular that I need."

He wasn't short of money, and he didn't need Pokemon breeding tools, since Teddiursa had brought back a bunch of them before. The ranch had everything.

Hearing this, Geeta nodded gently, appearing to be in thought:

"How about having the Paldea League build a professional Gym for you at the ranch? I heard that you often host Trainers passing by for challenges, and your ranch is quite famous near Paldea Academy."

"If you had a Gym, it would make the work easier, just like Lono and Grusha."

D: "..."

He knew it.

But if this thing got formalized, he would need to take it seriously, whether it was hosting challengers or communicating with the Paldea League. It would mean he couldn't do things as he pleased.

So, D shook his head and declined Geeta's proposal, "Never mind, it's fine as it is now."

"Alright then." Geeta couldn't help but look a bit disappointed. She continued, "What about other rewards? If you can't decide, I'll prepare one for you according to the League's rules."

D: "..." So this reward must be given?

Geeta smiled slightly, "Please take good care of the next Gym Leaders."

D understood; Geeta meant that there would be many more Gym Leaders coming to his ranch to participate in trials!

This time, he didn't refuse. Considering the current situation of the ranch, D curiously asked, "Can I really ask for anything?"

Geeta nodded, "As long as it's something we can do."

D: "Then I want no less than 1,000 Paldea Gold Coins."

Hearing this, Geeta was momentarily stunned, "Paldea Gold Coins?"

"Because not long ago, I captured a Gimmighoul, and I want to see exactly how many Gold Coins it will take to evolve it into Gholdengo."

Gimmighoul and Gholdengo...

As the Champion of the Paldea League, Geeta naturally had heard of these two kinds of Pokemon, and she immediately understood D's intention.

"Okay." Geeta agreed, "We'll try our best to prepare them for you, but I can't guarantee that we'll be able to collect that many coins."

"It's okay, anything is better than nothing, I'm collecting too." Geeta was so generous that D couldn't help but feel a bit embarrassed.

Maybe he should agree to Geeta's Gym certification and do something for her as well?

Just as D was about to speak, Geeta had to hang up the call to attend to other matters.

D swallowed his words.

Oh well, he would find another way to thank the Paldea League!

Just as Geeta hoped, he would seriously host the remaining Gym Leaders at the ranch, helping them grow through trials to become stronger.

Apart from this, he could also gift them some fruit wines that could enhance Poison resistance, Paralysis resistance, and Burn resistance as a token of appreciation.

*

After hanging up, Geeta immediately contacted Larry, an employee in the business department far away in Medali Town.

In Medali Town, Larry, who was having lunch inside the Treasure Eatery, took out his communicator from the pocket of his suit, and glanced wearily at the incoming call display.

"Time to start work again."

Larry put down his chopsticks, picked up his briefcase, and left the Treasure Eatery.

On the way, he answered the call.

"Hello? Paldea Gold Coins... I see, I'll go help gather them."

On the other end, Geeta nodded in satisfaction, "Moreover, once you've completed this task, the League will give you a week's vacation. You should head over to D Ranch to experience the rural scenery and have a good rest!"

It would also be a good opportunity to deliver the collected Paldea Gold Coins.

A week's vacation...

Hearing these words, a spark of light flashed in Larry's eyes.

People like them always cherished their vacations the most.

Thinking of this, even Larry's tone began to fluctuate, "Got it, I'll start working on it right away."

Geeta: "Yes, go ahead, I'm counting on you!"

After hanging up, Geeta rubbed her temples while sitting in her chair.

A moment later, she dialed the phone of the Glaseado Gym.

Soon, Grusha's voice came over the phone.

Geeta asked, "When you visited the ranch, did you notice anything that D likes?"

Paldea Gold Coins were collected by D to evolve Gimmighoul, not exactly a reward he personally enjoyed.

Apart from those rewards, Geeta planned to send D a gift in her own name.

This was not only to thank D but also to gain his favor and prevent such talent from being poached by another region's Pokemon League.

Even though Geeta was busy, she'd heard about D's close ties with Galar's Champion Leon and Unova's Champion Alder.

If those two were to lure D away, that would be troublesome.

On the other end, Grusha was momentarily stunned, then realized what the Chief was saying.

"Things D likes... does Pokemon count?" Grusha pondered for a moment.

"Pokemon?" Geeta wasn't surprised at this response, as everyone loved Pokemon, especially rare ones.

Grusha agreed, and slowly spoke, "Eevee, D really likes Eevee. That day, both Lono and I saw two Eevees at his ranch."

"At the time, D told us his dream was to collect all types of Eevee and create an Eevee Paradise at the ranch."

"Eevee..." Getting the answer, Geeta smiled, "I see, thank you very much for the information, Grusha."

Grusha: "... You're welcome, Chief."

Once the call ended, Geeta stood up, preparing to visit the Pokemon Breeding Center of the Paldea League.

This place is specifically used by the Paldea League for Pokemon breeding.

Various Pokemon lived there, playing and laying eggs together.

Once hatched, these eggs would be taught specifically and then sent to ten-year-old children far and wide to be looked after.

This ensured a continuous cycle of Trainers in the Paldea League.

Coincidentally, the breeding center also had Eevee Pokemon.

However, upon arriving there via Squawkabilly Flying Taxi, Geeta learned from the Pokemon caretaker that the Eevee Eggs hadn't hatched yet.

Seeing the Chief standing and pondering over the Eevee Eggs, a nearby caretaker cautiously said:

"The hatching trial for these eggs is still over half a month away. If you're not in a hurry, you can wait a bit longer."

Geeta wasn't in a rush, but she thought of something.

It's that Pokemon hatched from eggs would become especially close to the first human they see.

Thinking of this, Geeta made up her mind, "Leave these eggs to me! I'll take them to a Trainer who can take good care of them."

"Y-yes!" the caretaker beside her instinctively nodded and started placing the eggs into special egg pouches.

Then, loading these eggs onto the Squawkabilly Flying Taxi.

Geeta initially thought of calling Larry and Rika over to deliver the eggs, but when she took out her communicator, she remembered she had dispatched them for other tasks.

So, Geeta opted to call someone else instead.

Soon, the call connected.

Geeta smiled and said, "Hello, Hassel, do you have time? I need your help with something at the League Headquarters."

---

---

---

---

# 386: Chapter 383: Friendship in the Forests and Mountains

League Calendar Year 199, mid-July, when the cool breeze from Casseroya Lake reached Porto Marinada Town, it officially announced the arrival of autumn.

The leaves on the hillside began to turn yellow, with heavy, full berries hanging on the branches. The farms around Porto Marinada Town were filled with endless golden wheat fields.

During this wonderful season, farmers' faces were filled with the joy of harvest.

That afternoon, D, having nothing to do on the ranch, carried a basket into the hills.

This season is the peak time for mountain goods.

Last year at this time, the hills were filled with various mushrooms and wild fruits. He picked many and made a mushroom and chicken stew for the Pokemon.

Unfortunately, the fire that followed consumed everything in the mountains; mushrooms, fruits, flowers, and grasses all turned to ashes.

Fortunately, Calyrex stepped in to help revive the forest.

Half a year has passed. I wonder if those mushrooms and berries have regrown.

Thinking so, D entered the hills.

He hadn't been to this side for a long time, but he could tell that compared to last year, the vegetation and flowers on the entire hillside were more lush and thriving.

This was due to the forest fire.

The plants and trees turned to ashes became nutrients for the earth, reintegrating into the cycle of nature.

Thinking of last year's events, D sighed for the wild Pokemon that had perished.

From time to time, the deep forest echoed with the cries of wild Pokemon.

As he walked along, D encountered significantly fewer wild Pokemon.

Probably because the Pokemon ecosystem here hasn't fully recovered from the damage.

Soon, D reached the forest he had planted with his Pokemon in the spring.

Here, D saw several "old friends."

Greedent, Tinkaton, Maushold...

Greedent was picking berries from the fruit trees, using its furry big tail to hold the berries it had picked. It swiftly leaped around the tree.

Only when it reached its nest did Greedent carefully store the berries it had picked.

Looking at the tree hollow full of berries, Greedent's little face lit up with a satisfied smile.

Tinkaton was sitting under a big tree, polishing its hammer with Steel Type energy, only stopping when the hammer shone brightly.

The small-sized Maushold stood before a pile of fallen leaves, as Mouse Dad and Mouse Mom lifted the leaves like a quilt and gently covered their two children.

Watching this beautiful scene, D couldn't help but smile.

Just then, as Greedent was about to take a rest, it keenly noticed him.

This Greedent immediately shrieked "Squeak squeak squeak" excitedly.

Then, it slid down the tree trunk to the grass and ran quickly toward him, wagging its big tail.

"Squeak squeak!"

"Long time no see, Greedent!" D greeted it with a smile.

"Squeak squeak!"

The other Pokemon heard the commotion and gathered around.

D's gaze swept over them one by one.

The Maushold family of four remained unchanged, with the two little mice holding their parents' hands and staying by their side.

Wattrel looked more robust; compared to last year, it had grown considerably, and it seemed it wouldn't be long before it evolved into Kilowattrel.

What surprised D the most was that Tinkaton had also evolved.

"Tinkaton, you've evolved, huh?" he asked.

"Wooah!" Tinkaton nodded happily, proudly swinging the hammer in its hand.

Now it was the guardian Pokemon of this forest!

The Pokemon rescued by D formed deep bonds at the ranch last year; even when they returned to the wild, they didn't separate and continued living here together.

Just like good friends.

"Amazing!" D praised, "You've become more dependable."

He chatted with the Pokemon here for a while and then planned to go into the mountains to pick mountain goods.

Unexpectedly, the Pokemon, upon hearing D's intention, immediately patted their chests and offered to help.

So, with the help from Greedent, who was very familiar with the forest, D quickly picked a large heap of fresh mountain mushrooms and wild berries.

In the end, D's basket was overflowing.

"Alright, alright, thank you for your hard work today. Now go back and have a good rest!" D said.

But the Pokemon didn't feel tired; they happily ran around D, picking berries and bringing mushrooms, repaying his past aid in their way.

D felt a slight warmth within.

At that moment, several new Trainers visiting Calyrex's Shrine in the hills noticed the situation.

They appeared surprised and exclaimed, "Pokemon are helping people pick mushrooms!"

Hearing the voices, D turned around and saw two boys and a girl group of Trainers standing not far away, watching.

They were quite young, appearing to be at most around twelve years old.

The girl in the group asked puzzled, "Are those Pokemon specifically trained to pick mushrooms?"

"No!" D replied with a smile, "They are wild Pokemon living in this mountain forest. I helped them before, and they came to help me pick mountain goods in gratitude."

"Wow!" Hearing this, the three youths expressed looks of surprise.

One robust boy, full of disbelief, exclaimed, "Wild Pokemon are so friendly to humans!"

"As long as you, as Trainers, treat Pokemon sincerely, whether they are wild or not, they can definitely sense your intentions," D said with a gentle laugh.

The three youngsters hesitated momentarily, showing thoughtful expressions.

D smiled and prepared to take these mountain goods back to the ranch.

But just as he was about to leave, the boy behind suddenly called out to him, "Hey, sir, please wait a minute!"

D turned around, "What's up?"

"Uh… Are you the Forest Ranger living nearby?" a boy asked.

"No." D shook his head, "I'm a Rancher."

"A Rancher…" The boy's eyes lit up, then asked, "Have you seen Calyrex? Did Calyrex really revive this forest? Was he truly present here?"

Hearing this question, D was slightly taken aback, then asked, "Do you really like Calyrex?"

The boy nodded seriously, "Yes! Calyrex is really impressive! Its powers are so great, and it helped so many Pokemon!"

"I see." D smiled, responding, "Calyrex indeed appeared here at that time, and many people witnessed its miraculous acts."

"Wow!" The three new Trainers were surprised to find the legend was true, their eyes lighting up excitedly.

"If you wish to see it, you can come to Porto Marinada Town on the last day of autumn. A harvest festival will be held there, and people will visit the Abundant Shrine to pay their respects. Perhaps Calyrex will appear then!"

D planned to invite Calyrex to come over at that time.

If Calyrex appears at the festival, it might make the townspeople and tourists think Calyrex is touched by their intentions, thus promoting The King of Bountiful Harvests more vigorously.

Moreover, [Calyrex appearing before believers, sensing human intentions], this sounds like a beautiful tale of humans and Pokemon mutually striving.

And then, this story will continue to be passed down here, eventually becoming a new legend.

---

---

---

---

# 387: Chapter 384: Oh No, I’m Surrounded by Eevee!

After bidding farewell to the three, D finally returned to the ranch with a basket.

He took out some of the mushrooms, planning to make a delicious chicken stew with mushrooms for dinner, while the rest were to be dried and reserved for the snowy winter.

Additionally, D shared the events he encountered in the back mountain with Calyrex.

D: "I met some human Trainers who admire you. All three specifically came to visit the Abundant Shrine."

Upon hearing this, a smile appeared on Calyrex's face.

Recalling the events of the past two years, even it felt filled with emotion.

Life now seems like a dream.

Humans who worship it, pursue its legend, and trust it, are continuously emerging on this land.

Who would have believed that a year ago, it was still enduring loneliness and sadness all alone in the Crown Tundra?

"I... I feel very happy." said Calyrex.

D eagerly commented: "Speaking of, this year is the first time the Harvest Festival is being held here. I wonder how it will be."

He attended last year's festival and there were already many participants then. Now with Calyrex's fame spreading throughout the Paldea Region, it's uncertain how many will be drawn here.

It's likely that the entire back mountain will be packed, with visitors crowded in front of the Abundant Shrine.

Calyrex nodded slightly: "I am looking forward to it as well."

This season, the weather is not as hot as summer, and Pokémon have left their air-conditioned rooms to play in the ranch.

After tidying up the mushrooms, D stepped out of the house, planning to pick some scallions from the field to cook.

But as soon as he stepped outside, he saw a Squawkabilly Flying Taxi slowly flying over.

Hmm? D was quite surprised.

Who is visiting the ranch now?

The Squawkabilly Flying Taxi landed on the grass, and then a blond man dressed in a brown coat jumped down from the vehicle.

His expression was serious, and he looked extremely meticulous.

D was slightly taken aback, is this Hassel?

Hassel, one of the Elite Four in the Paldea Region, is the Dragon Elite, an expert in Dragon Type Pokémon, and the young master of a Dragon Tamer family, who also works as an art teacher at the Paldea Academy.

"Hello, Mr. D, I'm Hassel, commissioned by the chairperson to bring you a gift." the man spoke.

D was puzzled, a gift? What kind of gift? Could it be that Geeta collected 1000 Palladian Gold Coins for him so quickly?

Confused internally, D replied politely, "Hello, Mr. Hassel."

Then he saw Hassel unloading seven eggs with brown markings from the Flying Taxi.

Seeing this, D felt utterly confused.

"…What are these?"

Hassel: "These are gifts the chairperson commissioned me to deliver to you, they are Eevee Eggs."

Wait! Eevee?

How did Geeta know he wanted to collect all the different types of Eevee?

D's brain was swiftly processing, quickly recalling that afternoon when he told Lono and Grusha about his plan to build an Eevee Paradise.

Geeta is too extravagant, isn't she?

Feeling grateful for her help in collecting Paldean Gold Coins, D was unexpectedly and suddenly gifted seven Eevee Eggs by her.

What is Geeta planning?

"The gift has been delivered, I still have matters at the Academy, so I won't disturb you further."

With that, Hassel prepared to leave on the Flying Taxi.

However, in the next second, his sharp eyes caught sight of many Dragonite and Dragonair in the ranch.

Hassel paused slightly, his eyes flickering.

Why are there so many Dragonites here? Hassel felt a little shocked, slightly lost in thought.

One, two, three… a total of nine.

As the young master of the Dragon Tamer family, their entire family had only five Dragonites!

And now, there are more Dragonites here than the entire family…

At that moment, Hassel couldn't help but wonder who truly is the Dragon Tamer family.

D didn't notice Hassel's reaction as his attention was on the Eevee Eggs. Now hearing Hassel's intent to leave, he quickly and politely urged him to stay:

"Leaving already? How about staying for a cup of tea?"

Hassel snapped back to reality, it was getting late and he had classes tomorrow.

"No need, I must return to the Academy as soon as possible, so I won't disturb you." Hassel replied.

Knowing Hassel was a teacher at the Paldea Academy, D didn't insist, just offered a "thank you for your efforts."

Hassel departed the ranch on the Flying Taxi.

Leaving D alone to stare at the pile of Eevee Eggs on the grass.

"Aii?!"

Coming out from the room, his sister Indeedee, who planned to clean ingredients at the Pokemon Tavern, was startled by the sudden appearance of so many eggs in the ranch.

Its growl attracted a large group of Pokémon.

Alcremie, Pawmot, Dragonite, Dragonair, Umbreon, and Glaceon gathered around, surprised at the eggs.

"Uh… these eggs were brought over by that gentleman for me to take care of." explained D, "The Pokémon inside are all Eevee."

Upon hearing this, the Pokémon had varying reactions.

Alcremie and Pawmot looked dumbfounded, one egg equating to an Eevee, they suddenly felt surrounded by Eevee staring at the pile of eggs.

Dragonair, on the other hand, felt curious about these unhatched eggs.

They flew up, circled the eggs, occasionally sniffing them with their big noses, listening for sounds inside the eggs.

Glaceon appeared shocked, unable to believe what was happening.

What's going on... Why are there so many Eevee?

Doesn't this mean seven more Eevee will come and share D's affection?

At that moment, Glaceon felt the sky collapse, the world turned dark.

Beside it, Umbreon didn't think too much, its ears twitched, and it quietly stepped forward to observe the eggs of its kin.

"Alright, let's get these Eevee Eggs inside now!" D waved his hand, as the Pokémon began to hustle.

Everyone together moved the eggs back into the house, placing them on the bed in the second-floor incubation room.

Looking at so many Eevee Eggs, D felt overwhelmed.

While happy about collecting enough Eevee, he was worried about caring for so many at once!

D almost foresaw the scene where the seven newly hatched Eevee clung to him, wanting milk, wanting cuddles.

Is he about to become an Eevee caretaker?

No, he must find Pokémon to help care for them!

D turned to look at Glaceon and Umbreon, who followed him inside, speaking seriously:

"From now on, you two will be the leaders of these Eevee, Glaceon you're the big sister, Umbreon you're the second sister, and you'll be responsible for helping me take care of them!"

"Bui?!"

The two adult Eevee: =͟͟͞͞=͟͟͞͞(●⁰ꈊ⁰●|||)

---

---

---

---

# 388: Chapter 385: Emergency Help from Elif Cherry Garden

Glaceon and Umbreon never thought they would have the day when they became mothers.

After D entrusted them with the task of taking care of the Eevee Eggs, he went downstairs to answer the phone.

Suddenly, only the two evolved Eevees and a bunch of unhatched Eevee Eggs were left on the second floor.

Umbreon stepped forward and gently tapped one of the Eevee Eggs with her paw, her yellow eyes full of curiosity.

"Eevee?" (Can an Eevee really hatch from here?)

Glaceon sighed and nodded reluctantly: "Eevee." (That's right.)

Umbreon imagined the scene and couldn't help but cheerfully ask: "Eevee?" (Will the hatched Eevee think of us as its moms?)

Hearing this, Glaceon was slightly taken aback and asked in disbelief: "Eevee?" (You want to be a mom?)

Umbreon nodded while looking at the egg in front of her and replied: "Eevee!" (I never had a mom, and these little Eevees don't have moms either. I'm going to be their mom and take care of them!)

Glaceon was stunned and didn't know what to say.

After a long pause, she could only whisper a meaningless murmur: "Eevee..."

Downstairs, D was having a phone conversation with Geeta.

Geeta on the other end was all smiles: "Have you received the gift?"

D: "…Yes."

"Oh, that's good." Geeta smiled faintly: "I've heard everything from Grusha; you're planning to create an Eevee Paradise, and it just so happens there are Eevee Eggs at the League's Pokemon Breeding Center, so I sent them to you."

D had a hesitating expression: "Chief, this is too extravagant..."

Geeta nearly embarrassed him, first helping to find ancient Gold Coins and now sending Eevee Eggs.

D felt that if needed, Geeta could arrange even a marriage for him.

"Not extravagant at all." Geeta said sincerely: "A person like you with Champion Level abilities is a real asset for Paldea. Even though you don't wish to join the Paldea League, I sincerely hope you can always stay in the Paldea Region."

D was somewhat confused: "I never planned on moving to another region, and my home is right here. Where would I even go?"

Geeta thought that although you hadn't intended this, other regions' leagues might differ.

She had heard that the neighboring Galar Region had already launched a special talent incentive policy.

Though these thoughts crossed her mind, Geeta did not voice them. She just softly laughed: "Think of it as a personal gift I'm giving you as a friend."

Friend… Hearing this word, D felt a slight stir in his heart.

Since coming to this world, he had always interacted with Pokemon and hadn't made many proper human friends.

Thinking of this, D revealed a smile: "In that case, I'll accept it."

"Good." Geeta nodded, then advised: "By the way, those Eevee Eggs were laid two months ago; they'll hatch in about half a month, so you'll need to pay extra attention soon."

"Half a month?" D nodded: "Alright, I understand."

"That's settled then; I have other things to attend to, I'll hang up now." Geeta cheerfully waved her hand and then ended the call.

D stood in place, pondering for a long time.

He appreciated Geeta's goodwill, but as a friend, he couldn't only receive without giving.

After a brief thought, D decided to send Geeta some of his specially crafted coffee as a gift.

The coffee's effect is [Sleepless], preventing the drinker from falling asleep after consuming it. For humans, it can also enhance concentration and energize someone while completing tasks.

This coffee would definitely be very useful to Geeta, who works long hours in the office.

D initially planned to have Dragonite help deliver the gift, but then he thought Dragonite probably wouldn't know the location of the Paldea League Headquarters.

So, D went to town and handed the packaged coffee to the delivery worker Allen, asking him to send these gifts to the Paldea League Headquarters.

After taking care of matters, when D returned to the ranch, the sky was already beginning to darken.

D washed his hands and went to the kitchen to prepare dinner for the Pokemon.

He cleaned the mountain mushrooms he had picked from behind the mountain, then put them into a pot and stewed them with prepared chicken.

During this process, D saw Glaceon and Umbreon come running down from upstairs.

D called the two Eevees over and asked with a smile: "How about helping me take care of those Eevees together?"

"Eevee!"

Umbreon happily nodded, and Glaceon hesitated briefly before nodding along.

D: "That's great. There are still half a month before those Eevee Eggs can hatch, and then I'll rely on you both."

"Eevee?" Umbreon was a bit confused; how long is half a month?

D saw her meaning and explained: "Once the apples in the Apple Garden across the river are ripe, the Eevee Eggs will hatch."

He had checked the field this morning, and after Gogoat's nurturing, those apple trees were growing quickly. In just a few months, they had grown taller and stronger, with clusters of immature apples on the trees.

As it stands now, it is expected to take half a month for the apples to mature.

---

The Umbreon ran off after receiving the answer.

It was planning to check out the apples on the trees in the Apple Garden across the river.

Meanwhile, the Glaceon stayed indoors, feeling a bit hungry, and went to the small bowl D had prepared for them, starting to eat the Pokeblock and Poffin inside.

As night fell, other Pokemon playing in the ranch gradually returned to the house, lying around in the living room, waiting for dinner.

Watching this scene, D smiled slightly and began planning his next actions in his mind.

Today is already July 15th, and there are only five days left until his agreed date with Raifort, which is July 20th.

That day, Paldea Academy will start its summer vacation, and Raifort, being a teacher, will also be on holiday.

The two agreed to explore an underground ruin together, which is estimated to take a day or two to complete.

Thinking about this, D said to the Indeedee Sibling: "I'll be leaving soon, and I'll be back in about two days. I'll leave everyone in the ranch to you then."

The Indeedee Sibling nodded willingly.

"Indeedee~"

D can leave without worry! They will take care of everything at home!

*

The next day, it was another crisp autumn day.

Early in the morning, D got up and went to work in the ranch.

Milking the Gogoat, collecting the Honey brewed by Combee, watching Dragonite water the fields, harvesting ripe crops from the ground with Psyduck, picking Berries in the Berry Garden with Dragonite, and feeding Snorlax with Pokeblock.

After several days, the Wooloo's wool had grown back.

This time, D did not trim it himself with electric clippers but found the Farfetch'd who was working out.

Seeing D needed help, the much bulkier Farfetch'd immediately stopped and quickly went to the Wooloo Ranch.

Facing the group of fluffy Wooloo, Farfetch'd focused, and with a swift movement, sliced off the wool from a Wooloo, allowing it to gently fall onto the grass.

"Baa!"

The startled Wooloo calmed down only after realizing it wasn't hurt.

Soon, the second one, third one…

In just five minutes, all the Wooloo's wool had been neatly trimmed.

With a confident look, Farfetch'd put away the scallion and returned to the Scallion Garden to continue working out.

Standing by, D was dumbfounded.

Wow!

Using electric clippers to shear the wool would not only take several hours, but it would also leave him exhausted with a sore back.

Yet now, with just a single Pokemon like Farfetch'd, the shearing work was done in five minutes!

The power of Pokemon is truly remarkable!

After recovering from his shock, D picked up the fallen wool, planning to sell it to Zack in the afternoon.

Once all the chores in the ranch were done, it was already half-past nine.

D planned to go back into the house to drink some water and rest for a while.

But before he'd sat down for even a few minutes, a phone call came in.

Hmm? Who could it be?

D got up to check, and upon seeing the caller's name, he was quite surprised.

It was Uncle Elif from the neighboring Cherry Garden!

What business could he have?

Curious, D answered the call.

Elif's side didn't have video call capabilities, only a landline, so his face didn't appear on the screen.

As soon as it connected, Uncle Elif's anxious voice came through.

"D! Are you at the ranch? Could you come over to the Cherry Garden? A wild Pokemon has invaded my Cherry Garden and injured my Pokemon!"

Upon hearing this, D was momentarily stunned, then replied: "I'm here. I'll be right over. Avoid approaching that Pokemon for now!"

In this world, it's common for wild Pokemon to invade farms and cause trouble.

Not all farmers possess the strength to battle wild Pokemon with bad temperaments like he does.

So D immediately realized Uncle Elif was seeking his help.

After hanging up, D quickly found Koraidon, who was training Cyclizar and Dragonair, briefly explained the situation, then rode Koraidon to fly to Elif Cherry Garden.

When they arrived, Uncle Elif was already waiting there in advance.

Seeing D and Koraidon approach, Elif breathed a sigh of relief.

"Thank heavens, a Trainer finally came!"

"Where's the Pokemon?" D asked.

"In the Cherry Garden!" Elif quickly led the way ahead.

On the way, Uncle Elif recounted the whole incident to D.

Just yesterday morning, when he got up to tend the fruit trees in the Cherry Garden, he saw an aggressive wild Pokemon in there.

The Pokemon had taken over his Cherry Garden and chopped down a few cherry trees, and what's more, it wouldn't let them get close. Anyone who approached would be attacked.

"That's how it is, I really have no way out, and couldn't find a Trainer to help drive away that Pokemon, so I had to ask you." Elif said.

Understanding the whole story, D shook his head and reassured: "No worries, we're all neighbors after all."

While speaking, the two arrived deep in the Cherry Garden.

---

Here, D saw the wild Pokemon that occupied this land.

It was a Pokemon that resembled a mantis.

It had three distinct horns on its head, and on its arms were two large scythes that glinted with a cold, cruel light, with two pairs of small wings on its back.

That is... Scyther?

At this moment, the Scyther, who was originally resting with its eyes closed, noticed their arrival.

It suddenly opened its eyes, looking this way with vigilance.

Scyther's gaze swept over the two humans, finally settling on Koraidon.

The next second, Scyther staggered to stand up from the ground, the pair of small wings on its back started vibrating at high frequency, lifting its body from the ground.

But the Scyther did not attack, it seemed more like a warning not to approach.

Elif was somewhat frightened.

D immediately noticed the Scyther's bluff, and also the way its right leg drooped weakly.

Clearly, this Scyther was injured.

"Is it being so alert because of its injury?" D thought to himself.

Normally, wild Scythers wouldn't break into human territory at random.

They live in groups deep in the mountains, using their scythes to cut trees as part of their training.

The Trainer's guide clearly states: if you find a large number of trees chopped down in the forest during your travels, it indicates Scythers' territory.

D pondered for a moment, then began to negotiate with the Scyther.

"Scyther, we mean no harm, and we won't hurt you. If I'm not mistaken, you're staying here because of your injury, right?"

The Scyther's expression was still full of alertness and tension, its sharp eyes fixed unwaveringly on D.

"But this won't do, you are causing quite a bit of trouble for others here." Looking at the trees chopped down, D spoke.

"Next, I will make some medicine for your injuries. Once you eat it, your wounds will recover, and you will be able to walk normally."

This trip was very rushed, D hadn't brought any healing items.

He pondered for a moment, preparing to concoct one on site.

"Uncle Elif, do you have any Oran Berries or Leppa Berries?" D asked.

Elif was stunned for a moment, then nodded, "Yes!"

"I need two, and also a bottle of MooMoo Milk."

Before D finished speaking, he heard a tender voice from behind.

"Big brother, are you going to help heal that Pokemon?"

D was stunned for a moment, turned around to see a cute little girl about five or six years old hiding behind a cherry tree, watching.

Seeing the girl, Elif couldn't help but frown, "Anna, it's dangerous here, you can't come over here!"

The little girl named Anna, being scolded like this, immediately teared up, "I was just worried about dad..."

Elif: "..."

He sighed helplessly, introducing to D, "Sorry D, this is my younger daughter, Anna."

Elif had two daughters, his elder daughter Sina was already ten years old and went traveling last year, leaving only his younger daughter Anna at home with his wife and him.

"It's okay." D smiled, looking at the little girl, answering her previous question, "That's right, the Scyther is injured. I thought, maybe if we heal it, it will leave."

Anna blinked, repeating D's words, "You need MooMoo Milk and Oran Berries?"

D nodded.

Anna's eyes lit up, cheerful, "Anna knows berries, Anna will get Oran Berries and MooMoo Milk for big brother!"

After saying that, the little girl turned and ran off.

Elif was full of helplessness, "This child..."

"She really cares about you!" D smiled.

"Yes, Anna has always been so understanding!" Talking about his daughter, Elif's face beamed with joy, radiating happiness.

On the opposite side, Scyther remained vigilant, but because of its injury, it couldn't leave.

Soon, Anna ran back with berries and MooMoo Milk.

D reached out and took them, then began concocting onsite.

Squeezing the juice of Oran Berries into the MooMoo Milk, this created a Berry Milk with healing properties.

Although its healing effects couldn't match Life Sheep Milk, it was still better than regular potions on the market.

Elif held Anna in his arms, Anna obediently sat in dad's arms, her bright eyes watching D's actions, curiously asking, "Big brother is going to feed the milk to that Pokemon?"

D smiled and nodded, "That's right, milk helps Pokemon heal injuries."

Saying so, he took the Berry Milk and negotiated with the Scyther.

"Scyther, this milk can help heal your wounds. After you drink it, your injuries will recover and you'll be able to leave this place."

Scyther was tensed up with nervousness.

D took a few steps forward, then placed the Berry Milk on the ground.

"Rest assured, I won't come closer, you can come over and drink it yourself."

Scyther paused, seeing the human no longer approaching, its tense spirit relaxed slightly.

Now, Anna spoke out to encourage the Scyther, "Scyther, don't be afraid, mama says drinking milk will make you grow tall!"

Listening to her innocent words, the Scyther's rapidly vibrating wings gradually slowed down.

It slowly descended back to the ground, its steps wobbling due to the injured right leg, moving forward slowly.

Seeing Scyther start to approach the milk, Anna's eyes brightened, appearing very happy.

"Go on, Scyther!" Anna shouted out.

Seemingly infected by her simple words, Scyther's steps seemed to become much lighter.

One step, two steps, three steps...

Finally, it reached the milk.

Using its large scythes, it scooped up the milk and gulped it down.

The next second, the wounds on Scyther's right leg began to heal visible to the naked eye.

Seeing this scene, Elif finally breathed a sigh of relief.

---

---

---

---

# 389: Chapter 386: Scyther at Work

"Clap clap clap!"

Seeing that the Scyther was safe, Anna immediately began clapping for it.

Elif, as a father, looked helpless and couldn't be happy because this Scyther had chopped down several grown cherry trees he had painstakingly planted.

But his daughter was happy, so Elif didn't say much and just hoped this healthy Scyther would leave soon.

After calming down, the Scyther saw the surrounding fruit trees it had damaged.

It was due to its previous injury and wary state that it had made a false alarm to warn others not to come close.

Looking back now, it must have caused quite a disturbance to these two humans.

"Chah..."

The Scyther felt very apologetic for its actions, glanced at the humans in front of it, and then turned around and flew away.

Anna waved her little hand to bid farewell to the Scyther: "Goodbye, Scyther!"

D and Koraidon stood aside watching this scene.

Seeing the Scyther leave, Elif felt a sense of relief and invited:

"Thanks to you this time, D. Come, have a seat at my place. I'll give you a few bags of cherries we picked a few days ago to take back."

D, thinking he had nothing else to do, went with Koraidon to Elif's place to sit for a while, chat, and relax.

Passing through the Cherry Garden, a two-story mansion on the lawn appeared in front of them.

Without a doubt, this was Uncle Elif's home.

The house was built entirely of wood, with a few logs stacked on the ground outside the door, and an ax stuck in a nearby stump, seemingly for chopping firewood.

Entering the house, Uncle Elif's wife Mana gratefully poured D a cup of tea and served a plate of bright red cherries, while also giving Koraidon a Pokemon treat.

"Sorry for interrupting!" responded D.

Mana smiled graciously: "Make yourself at home; don't be polite. You probably feel lonely living alone on the farm?"

"Not really, since I have many Pokemon to take care of. With them around, I never feel bored." D replied with a smile.

Mana gently nodded and then retreated to the upstairs to organize things.

Elif sat with Anna on the sofa.

Little Anna seemed very curious about the Pokemon.

She squatted obediently on the wooden floor, watching Koraidon eat cherries, and asked:

"Big brother, this Pokemon is so strong! What's its name?"

D replied with a smile: "It? It's called Koraidon, a Dragon Type and Fighting Type Pokemon."

"Wow! Amazing!" Anna asked again, "Big brother, are you also a trainer?"

"Barely, but my main job is running the ranch."

Upon hearing this, Anna turned and pounced into her father's arms, charmingly declaring: "Daddy, I also want to become a trainer as amazing as big brother in the future!"

Uncle Elif teased his daughter: "Who was it before who said they'd stay in the Cherry Garden with mom and dad forever?"

Little Anna quickly covered her mouth and shook her head: "It wasn't Anna!"

"Hahahaha!" Uncle Elif immediately burst into laughter.

D, with a smile on his face, watched this scene from the sofa.

The Elif family also raised several Pokemon.

In the stairway, a Minccino diligently cleaned the floor with its tail.

The Dachsbun napping beside the sofa looked like it was quite old, and seemed rather lethargic.

Also, the Squawkabilly perched on the lamp stand, and Tarountula nestled in a Pokemon den.

The Elif family lived with these Pokemon in the Cherry Garden.

D's attention was drawn to a photo hanging on the living room wall.

It was a group photo of Uncle Elif's family of four with Pokemon, showing happiness.

Thinking of Zack and Mayor Thomas' words encouraging him to find a wife to run the ranch together, take care of the Pokemon, and have a child to pass it to, D felt a bit envious.

Family... In the previous life, he was a corporate slave, busy every day in the steel jungle made of concrete and steel, earning a meager salary, unable to afford a house, let alone get married.

Back then, he often thought this life would just end like that, single, alone, was fine too.

But now...

The old ideologies seemed to be wavering again, watching Little Anna charmingly with her father, D suddenly thought, what would it be like if he had a child?

If that child didn't want to travel, he'd teach her to run the ranch.

If she wanted to travel, he'd pass down a child birthed by Dragonite to her, letting her travel with a hatched Dratini.

Elif noticed D's gaze.

Following his line of sight to the photograph, he said happily:

"Those two in the photo are my daughters. The eldest is named Sina, she just turned ten last year and went traveling with her Pokemon."

D had some recollection of that name since Zack mentioned it to him last year.

"You guys had a big argument then, right?" D asked with a smile.

"Word got to you too, huh?" Uncle Elif was startled, then sighed helplessly, "Originally, I didn't plan to let Sina travel so early, because it's so dangerous outside, but that child locked herself in her room and cried a lot...."

"Why not send her to Paldea Academy?" D asked curiously.

Elif chuckled bitterly, "Sina didn't want to go to school. She believes that instead of learning boring knowledge at school, it's better to experience it herself."

D understood, "No wonder."

Just as Elif wanted to continue speaking, there was a sudden knock at the door.

Little Anna, who was sitting on the sofa, immediately ran to open it.

However, it wasn't a human who came to visit, but a Scyther holding a Berry, with an apologetic face.

Seeing this, D and Elif were both slightly taken aback.

The Scyther gave the Berry to Anna.

Little Anna asked excitedly, "Are these fruits for Anna?"

"Cha!"

The Scyther nodded.

Little Anna cheered and hugged the Scyther's waist.

"Anna!"

Elif's heart was in his throat.

The Scyther was startled but then felt a bit flustered, yet it didn't show any signs of aggression.

Anna liked Scyther as a Pokemon because she thought Scyther was cool.

Maybe from watching her sister capture Pokemon before, Anna asked expectantly, "Scyther, would you like to become my Pokemon?"

Hearing this, D and Elif paused.

And the Scyther looked down at this little human girl.

Feeling guilty for damaging the farmland and touched by the encouragement from the girl named Anna, the Scyther nodded happily.

"Cha!"

It decided to stay and work to make up for the trees it had cut down.

"Yay!" Little Anna cheered, turning to her dad, "Dad, I've caught a Pokemon. From now on, I'm a Trainer like sister!"

Elif had a ghost-seeing expression, still not fully processing what was happening.

D, however, accepted such things well, considering he grew up watching Pokemon; they are such amazing creatures.

"Congratulations, Anna, Scyther is a really strong Pokemon. It's great at cutting and gathering firewood, it will definitely help you a lot."

"Yes! From now on, the task of chopping wood will be left to me and Scyther!" Anna patted her chest, her little face full of seriousness, "This way, Dad can rest!"

Scyther nodded, "Cha!"

Elif clutched his chest, looking confused, "D... is this real?"

With clearer insight, D nodded, "That Scyther probably feels guilty about cutting down the trees, it might be trying to atone in this way."

"Phew..." Elif chuckled bitterly, "I've never encountered something like this."

Perhaps to prove its ability, Scyther followed Anna to the outside land and began chopping firewood.

With a couple of white flashes, a log was cut into four pieces, turning into firewood ready for burning.

Elif watched in surprise, his mouth wide open, "A-amazing!"

Beside the pile of firewood, Scyther tirelessly chopped wood while Little Anna watched.

When all the logs turned into firewood, Scyther finally stopped, its forehead glistening with sweat, panting heavily.

Seeing this, Little Anna hurried back inside, brought out tissues and a cup of water, stood on her tiptoes to give the water to Scyther, and used tissues to wipe its sweat.

Scyther had never experienced such treatment before, and its affection for this little human girl soared.

"Looks like it's all good now." D said with a smile.

And so, to make up for the trouble it caused, Scyther stayed in the Cherry Garden to help out with work.

D, meanwhile, returned to the ranch with Koraidon, and before departing, Uncle Elif gave D several bottles of Cherry Wine and a big bag of fresh cherries as thanks.
