# 390: Chapter 387: Intense Pursuit Beneath the Yellow Sands, Within the Desert!

League Calendar Year 199, July 23, there are still two days before D sets out to explore the ancient ruins.

That morning, D began checking the results of Cyclizar and Dragonair training with Koraidon during this period.

The Dragonair are doing fine, they were training through play, and each learned one or two new moves in more than half a month.

But Cyclizar is different, it has been working very hard.

At first, when Koraidon proposed the teaching, Cyclizar was very surprised.

Because Koraidon always displayed an attitude neither actively approaching nor distancing itself from Cyclizar.

Cyclizar initially thought this was a joke, but upon seeing that Koraidon was serious about teaching, it settled down and followed Koraidon diligently to learn.

And now, a long time has passed since its special training.

Standing on the meadow of the ranch, Cyclizar confidently demonstrated to D the moves it learned from Koraidon.

Rushing towards the opponent, jumping high, using its body to attack the opponent.

"Seems like a Normal Type Body Slam," D observed Cyclizar's movements, guessing in his mind.

He watched Cyclizar use Body Slam and suddenly felt that this move's actions were somewhat similar to Koraidon's Collision Course.

Both involve jumping high and crashing towards the opponent.

"Great, continue," D noted Cyclizar's newly mastered move in his notebook.

Cyclizar continued to demonstrate the next move.

Time passed by second by second, the whole process lasted about ten minutes.

By the time Cyclizar finished demonstrating the last move, the page recording its data gained a few more lines.

[Name: Cyclizar

Temperament: Cute, naive, inexperienced, a bit dumb, seems to want to become stronger like Dragonite and Pawmi recently

Hobbies: Sunbathing, chasing butterflies in the grass, happy when working with me

Favorite Food: Pan-Fried Pork Chops, D's Secret Sandwich, Berry Milk

Mastered Moves: Tackle, Growl, Rapid Spin, Dragon Tail, Agility, Breaking Swipe, Quick Attack, Acrobatics, Aerial Ace, Body Slam, Shed Tail, Shift Gear, Dragon Pulse, Dragon Rush, Outrage, Iron Head, Giga Impact]

"Shift Gear... Agility..."

Looking at these two speed-enhancing moves, D suddenly wanted to know how fast Cyclizar could get if it got serious.

At this thought, D said to Cyclizar: "Use Shift Gear for me to see."

"Gaaow!"

Cyclizar nodded its head.

The next moment, the tire-like part in front of its chest began to emit a white glow, spinning rapidly like a speeding motorcycle.

"Run!" D said.

Cyclizar immediately started sprinting across the grass.

"Sure enough, speed has increased quite a bit."

D timely called to stop, then had Cyclizar use Shift Gear + Agility to maximize speed.

Cyclizar diligently applied buffs to itself.

Once the buffs were fully stacked, it started running again according to D's request.

A blur flashed, and Cyclizar's figure instantly vanished from D's sight.

D was slightly surprised.

Such speed!

It seems the record in the Cyclizar Pokédex stating its maximum speed can reach 120km per hour is no joke!

In that case, Cyclizar can utilize its speed advantage more in battles.

For example, evade opponent's attacks, close the gap with the opponent, and attack using moves like Dragon Tail and Iron Head.

Seeing things were almost clear, D once again called Cyclizar to stop, then explained his thoughts carefully.

"Speed is your advantage. In battle, you must learn to leverage your speed and high mobility for maneuvering, using the professional term, it's called kiting the opponent."

"Gaaow?" Cyclizar listened with a vague understanding.

Just as D wanted to explain, he saw Koraidon beside him lazily yawning.

A good idea suddenly popped into his mind.

Instead of explaining word by word, it's better to do a practical demonstration.

Thinking of this, D said, "Koraidon, next I'll have you spar with Cyclizar. I'll be directing Cyclizar to battle against you."

"Ah-gah," Koraidon blinked, seeming a bit speechless, but still agreed to D's request, getting up to stand on the grass, ready to face the challenge from its formerly taught self.

Looking at Koraidon, Cyclizar's face was filled with determination.

The battle officially begins!

Cyclizar continuously used Shift Gear to maximize its speed against Koraidon!

D had Cyclizar start with the Dragon Type move Breaking Swipe.

In the next moment, Cyclizar's figure lunged out, sweeping its resilient tail towards Koraidon.

Koraidon remained motionless, cooperatively playing the role of a live target.

Once the move hit, D immediately said, "Open the distance, don't let the opponent have a chance to catch you!"

At the command, Cyclizar immediately withdrew, using its speed advantage to maneuver around Koraidon.

"Do it again, this time use Dragon Claw, then withdraw quickly!"

Cyclizar rushed at Koraidon again, executing Dragon Claw before leaping back.

These two brief encounters gave it some enlightenment.

Just then, D spoke: "Understand? This is kiting tactics! Speed is your advantage, you should learn to use this advantage in battle to maximize it."

---

"Screee!"

Cyclizar is not stupid; it quickly figured this out.

"Want to try by yourself?" D said.

Cyclizar nodded, then seized the moment, its figure flashed and it arrived in front of Koraidon.

The glowing Dragon Claw was raised high and then swung toward Koraidon.

However, this time, Koraidon moved.

Cyclizar's speed was fast, but Koraidon's speed was evidently faster.

Before the Dragon Claw could land on it, Koraidon grabbed Cyclizar's arm and threw it flying.

Koraidon's speed was really fast!

D couldn't help but be amazed; he had never seen this Koraidon exert itself fully before.

But thinking carefully, Cyclizar's speed species strength is 121; though this number surpasses many Pokemon, it still doesn't exceed the Legendary Pokemon, Koraidon.

If he remembered correctly, Koraidon's speed species strength was as high as 135.

"That's enough, stop here!"

D called to stop the battle and watched Cyclizar come over looking all dusty.

"Screee...," it still couldn't beat Koraidon...

D smiled and patted Cyclizar's head: "No worries, you're only one and a half years old now, not even two, there's still plenty of time to gradually become stronger."

"Screee~" Cyclizar felt comforted. It happily nuzzled D's palm and then looked at Koraidon with a full fighting spirit.

"Graaah..." Koraidon felt a complicated surge internally.

Moments later, it spoke to its past self: "Graaah!"

Cyclizar froze for a moment.

D stood between the two Pokemon, glanced at one, then the other, and said with a smile:

"That's more like it! You two need to get along well in the future!"

Reflecting on Koraidon's earnest guidance during this time, Cyclizar happily nodded: "Screee!"

Though Koraidon might appear intimidating, Cyclizar could sense that this Pokemon's heart was just as kind as its own!

Cyclizar had wanted to be friends with Koraidon for a long time, but back then Koraidon was always fierce to it, only recently had it gotten better.

Koraidon looked at D, then at Cyclizar, and with a bit of resignation nodded: "Graaah..."

*

D had never robbed a tomb before nor explored underground ruins.

So, that night, when he logged onto the forum, he specifically sought advice from Raifort, who had extensive tomb-robbing experience.

[Wing King: Only two days left until our scheduled time, is there anything I need to prepare on my end?]

Raifort happened to be online, so D quickly received her reply.

[Ancient Investigator: No need; I've got everything ready on my end. You just need to bring your ace Pokemon with you when the time comes.]

[Wing King: Alright, where should we meet then?]

[Ancient Investigator: Let's meet in Cascarrafa City, as I found in ancient documents that the capital of the Paldea Empire is buried beneath the sands of Asado Desert.]

Asado Desert?

Isn't that just south of Porto Marinada Town?

In the game, it's a vast, extensive desert where The Quaking Earth Titan Great Tusk and Iron Treads ran to after breaking through the Paldea Giant Pit's defense line.

But presently, those two Paradox Pokemon probably haven't run out of Paldea Giant Pit yet.

[Wing King: Got it. I'll be waiting for you in Cascarrafa City then.]

Once the two made their arrangements, D logged off.

The desert is different from the outside; although Raifort said she would prepare, D, being cautious, still went to the town beforehand to procure some necessary adventure supplies.

Items like dust-proof goggles to prevent sand, enough drinking water and food, a compass for direction, and clothes for warmth in cold weather.

Deserts have vast temperature differences between day and night; it's scorching hot during the day and temperatures plummet at night.

D, always being careful, after thorough consideration, besides Koraidon, decided to also bring Slugma, Gogoat, and a Water Type Psyduck.

If they encounter a sandstorm and lose their luggage, they can use Slugma for warmth, drink goat's milk to regain strength, and have Psyduck use Water Gun to replenish their water supply.

Once everything was prepared, the time had come for the appointed date.

On July 25th, in the 199th year of the League calendar, D, after arranging the work at the ranch, rode Koraidon towards Cascarrafa City, known as the "Water City."

When D arrived at the destination, Raifort was already there waiting.

She stood at the topmost level of Cascarrafa City, leaning against a scenic fountain, holding a telescope and gazing towards the Asado Desert.

Following Raifort's gaze, D saw a vast expanse of endless yellow sands creating a desert landscape.

Raifort was dressed in a brown-yellow exploration outfit, wearing dust-proof goggles, looking fully equipped.

"You've come!" Upon seeing D and Koraidon, Raifort smiled slightly, put away the telescope, and asked, "Are you ready?"

D: "Do we set off now?"

Raifort looked him up and down, then took an exploration outfit from her bag, "Change into this first; we'll set off once you're ready."

D didn't say a word, took the clothes, and went to a roadside changing room.

When he came back out, he saw Raifort with eyes gleaming, examining Koraidon.

"Living relics of the past...I never thought I'd have a chance to see such a Pokemon in my lifetime!"

"Maybe you'll see more ancient Pokemon in the future!" D smiled.

After all, when the defenses of the Paldea Giant Pit eventually collapse, those ancient and future Paradox Pokemon will emerge from the pit and invade the Paldea Region.

But Raifort didn't understand: "What do you mean?"

D: "It's nothing, I was just thinking that if Koraidon could travel through time to the Paldea Region, then perhaps other Pokemon might do the same."

"Is that so?" Raifort nodded slightly.

However, she didn't dwell on this topic for long, as she was more concerned with what lay ahead.

Finding the lost royal city of the Ancient Paldea Empire might lead to more information about the four Treasures of Ruin.

She could then determine the locations where the Treasures of Ruin were imprisoned and collect them.

"Let's head out!" Raifort said.

Then D watched as she drove out a black off-road buggy resembling a buffalo from a nearby alley.

D was almost speechless: "T-this is way too extravagant!"

Raifort laughed, casually patting the seat behind her: "Hurry, both of you, get on!"

D nervously climbed on with Koraidon.

Raifort stepped on the gas, and the vehicle charged out of Cascarrafa City.

After passing a stretch of asphalt road, they officially entered the desert area.

The four-wheel-drive buggy sped quickly across the sand, the engine roaring, and the four large tires spun, leaving an exaggerated trail of yellow sand behind.

"It's so fast!"

D shouted, feeling uneasy, fearing the vehicle might flip at any moment.

Raifort, however, smirked: "Relax, trust my driving skills!"

"Moo!"

Suddenly, in the distance, a cloud of yellow sand rose as if something was rushing their way.

D took up Raifort's telescope to check and saw a herd of robust, wild Pokemon with coarse black fur charging towards them.

Tauros! Those are Tauros! And the fiery-tempered Blaze Breed Tauros!

The Tauros ran incredibly fast over the desert, quickly closing the distance to the vehicle.

This herd of unruly bull-like Pokemon seemed to have mistaken the roaring buggy for an intruder in their territory, showing a determination to overturn the vehicle.

"Tauros are catching up—!" D shouted.

Raifort glanced in the rearview mirror with a smirk: "Put on your goggles!"

D hurriedly put on dust-proof goggles for both himself and Koraidon.

Raifort slammed the gas pedal and turned the handlebars.

A monstrous roar echoed from the engine, and in the next instant, the off-road buggy accelerated forward, immediately widening the gap from the charging Tauros behind them.

---

---

---

---

# 391: Chapter 388: The Lost Country Beneath the Earth, Mysterious Fossils? Are They Pokemon?

The setting sun's last rays cast over the deserted desert, Raifort drove the off-road vehicle forward, raising a cloud of dust behind.

The dry, hot wind accompanied by yellow sand greeted them head-on. Although it was already evening, the temperature hadn't completely dropped, and there was still a trace of heat lingering in the air.

It had been nearly ten hours since they entered the desert, with only one stop for a meal, with the rest spent traveling.

Along the way, D witnessed a scene vastly different from the outside world in this place.

The red sun on the horizon painted the entire desert in a crimson hue.

The Pokemon species residing here were predominantly Ground Type, unlike the outside where Bird-type Pokemon and Grass Type Pokemon were commonly seen, here they were nowhere to be found.

A family of Donphan wandered aimlessly across the desert, with a mischievous Phanpy running around its parents.

Several dozen Hippopotas huddled together on the sand, digging sand pits to hide in, planning to sleep deep in the sand as the desert night would bring a drop in temperature.

The towering Stonjourner stood motionless in the desert, with several Stonjourner naturally arranged in a circle.

If one didn't look carefully, passersby might mistake them for ancient ruins left by a long-lost civilization.

"Magnificent!"

Gazing at the vibrant and unique ecology of the desert, D couldn't help but murmur.

From the front, Raifort nodded and said, "Indeed! I felt an overwhelming sense of awe when I first saw such a scene; nature is truly vast, and we are so small within it."

She had once witnessed a terrifying sandstorm.

The natural disaster arrived abruptly, as sand tornadoes connected the sky and earth, seemingly engulfing the entire desert in fury.

"How much longer until we reach our destination?" D asked.

Raifort glanced at the radar in the car and replied, "Five more minutes!"

Five minutes later, the off-road vehicle stopped behind a sand dune.

Raifort jumped out of the car and deftly took out a set of highly professional detection equipment from the backpack.

Curious, D leaned in to take a look.

The screen seemed to show a radar map, with the radar flashing as if probing.

"Is this the machine you use for grave robbing?" D asked.

"It's for archaeology," Raifort corrected.

D: "..."

At this moment, the radar map seemed to have detected a target, as a small red dot appeared.

"Found it!"

Raifort took out a Poke Ball from her waist, and with a flash of white light, a lithe Zoroark appeared beside them.

"Zoroark, go scout ahead!" Raifort instructed.

Zoroark nodded silently and turned, skillfully heading forward, vanishing within a few leaps.

"Next, we'll wait here for Zoroark to return!" Raifort said.

D nodded in agreement and took the opportunity to start preparing dinner.

He released Slugma and took a frying pan from his backpack, placing it on Slugma.

Then he poured oil into the pan, waited until it was ready, and added eggs.

Soon, an enticing aroma wafted through the desert.

The tired and hungry Koraidon couldn't help but swallow.

"Huh? Your cooking skills are pretty impressive!" Raifort exclaimed in surprise.

"It's all from practice over time." D placed the cooked eggs and meat into the bread slices.

Thus, the sandwiches were completed.

D distributed several sandwiches to the Pokemon in front of him.

Koraidon reached out to take one, happily devouring it.

Koraidon had a great appetite; its way of eating the sandwich seemed like it was enjoying a gourmet meal, making onlookers' mouths water.

Raifort also took a bite and immediately gave a thumbs up, praising, "Indeed, I didn't choose the wrong person this time!"

D, speechless: "I'm not here to be your professional chef."

Raifort laughed and said, "I know, I know. Once Zoroark returns, you'll need to help with Koraidon."

As they spoke, Zoroark returned to the campfire.

Raifort handed it a sandwich and asked, "How is it?"

Zoroark nodded.

This indicated that it had successfully found the path forward, and they could proceed.

Upon seeing this, Raifort smiled with anticipation, "Great, once you finish eating, we can set out immediately!"

D noticed Raifort was so excited she was losing herself, and he looked at the darkening sky, reminding her, "It's getting dark."

Raifort, excitedly: "It's okay; my Grumpig can use Flash, illuminating even the darkest environment. Besides, don't you think exploring ruins at night has a certain feel to it?"

D: "...Alright."

After all, this was a temporary job for him, and his only goal was the ancient Paldea Gold Coins scattered within the ruins; everything else was up to Raifort.

After dinner, the sky had completely darkened.

The desert had cooled down at night, so both D and Raifort put on warm clothes.

D worried about Koraidon being cold, so he had specially brought it a warm vest when they came.

The vest was something he had bought and modified himself, cutting off the sleeves and removing extra buttons, making it fit Koraidon perfectly.

---

D put a warm coat on Koraidon, while Raifort released her Grumpig from the Poke Ball.

"Grumpig, use Flash!"

With a command, an intense light burst forth from Grumpig, as if someone had suddenly turned on a powerful flashlight, illuminating everything around them.

Raifort clapped her hands in satisfaction: "Now we can set off with peace of mind!"

D felt exhausted inside.

Raifort appeared scholarly, like a learned person.

Yet in truth, she was undeniably a formidable woman.

She was someone who could dive into the depths of ocean ruins in one go.

Zorua led the way ahead, Grumpig provided illumination, and Raifort and D followed with Koraidon in tow.

After about fifteen minutes of trekking on the sand, Zoroark came to a halt.

The surroundings were vast and empty, with nothing in sight.

Raifort looked around in confusion: "Strange... isn't there an entrance to go underground?"

Zoroark gazed at a depression in the ground ahead.

Upon closer inspection, Raifort quickly noticed something unusual: "It's a quicksand terrain! Could the entrance be beneath here?!"

Zoroark confirmed with a nod.

Raifort immediately became excited: "Great, put on your goggles and dust mask, we're going down!"

D's eyes widened; was Raifort really intending to jump into the quicksand?

But, could this really lead directly to the underground ruins? Is it not dangerous?

"Trust me!" Raifort had already put on her gear, she recalled Zoroark and Grumpig into their Poke Balls, "I'll jump first, follow me!"

With that, she leaped into the quicksand terrain.

Raifort's body was quickly engulfed by the quicksand, as if swallowed by a desert monster.

D looked on in shock.

Raifort couldn't have perished, could she? After all, the game's storyline never indicated that Raifort had explored such a place before.

And now, because of his involvement, Raifort had boldly ventured over...

Just as D was worried, a shout suddenly echoed from below.

Listening carefully, it was Raifort.

"Hey! Come down now!"

D breathed a sigh of relief upon hearing Raifort was fine, and he didn't hesitate anymore. He helped Koraidon cover its nose and mouth and jumped down with it.

They were slowly engulfed by the quicksand. Before long, D felt a sense of falling and, together with Koraidon, landed on a soft pile of sand.

When D opened his eyes, he saw Raifort standing before him with Zoroark and the glowing Grumpig, smiling.

"Grumpig!"

D had just ingested quite a bit of sand and hurriedly spat it out.

"The first time is always like this." Raifort laughed, "You'll get used to it over time."

D was filled with frustration, and after confirming that he had spat out all the sand from his mouth, he spoke:

"This is the first and the last time. Once we gather Gimmighoul's Gold Coins, I won't be tomb raiding again!"

Upon hearing this, Raifort gazed at his Nanjing with interest: "Oh? Really?"

D stood up from the ground and helped Koraidon brush off the sand: "Of course."

"Even if raiding a tomb could yield a treasure trove of valuable gold and silver?"

"I don't lack money; I don't see any use for it."

Perhaps he'd buy a few more villas, but given the situation now, he probably wouldn't leave the Pokemon on the ranch to vacation in a villa, unless he brought them all along.

"I understand." Raifort smiled and stopped questioning, then turned to look forward: "Let's continue!"

Beneath the quicksand lay another world, with a scene vastly different.

Ancient buildings buried in sand, dilapidated ruins of watchtowers...

D observed more closely, but Raifort seemed uninterested in these.

She followed the path downward without stopping; eventually, only the light from Grumpig illuminated their way, and the surrounding air began to thin.

This meant they had descended very deep.

Suddenly, Raifort stopped in front of him.

D was slightly puzzled and asked: "What's wrong?"

"Come and see." Raifort beckoned.

D stepped forward and saw where the previously narrow and chaotic tunnel suddenly ended.

And before them was a vast, endless space swallowed by darkness.

"This is..." D murmured.

Raifort explained: "If I'm not mistaken, this would be an underground cavern formed by tectonic movements two million years ago."

"I read some related materials in the Paldea Academy library; it said that this land was struck by an unprecedented earthquake long ago, creating this large cavern, and it's said there is more than one in the Paldea Region."

"Large cavern..." D repeated softly.

Raifort's face bore a thoughtful expression: "During the Paldea Empire era, a Ruinous Pokemon named Ting-Lu triggered an earthquake in the capital, causing the ground to split and leading the prosperous capital into this very place."

D recalled another matter.

Could the tectonic movements two million years ago have been caused by Regigigas shifting the continental plates?

D remembered that Regigigas's Pokédex entry described it as follows: Regigigas, a colossal Pokemon, is said to have once pulled continents with ropes, forming today's various regions.

---

It's just that the timing of Regigigas dragging the continental plates is still uncertain, and D can't confirm if the earthquake in the Paldea Region at the time was affected by Regigigas.

"Right below us!" Raifort suddenly spoke: "Now we need to find a way to get down there."

After a brief contemplation, she took another Poke Ball from her waist.

With a flash of white light, a Braviary with sharp eyes appeared in the pit.

Raifort recalled Zoroark into its Poke Ball and rode on with Grumpig.

And D also climbed onto Koraidon.

The two of them descended along the dark and vast abyss; the terrain inside was incredibly complex, with all sorts of cliffs and deep ravines.

After an unknown amount of time, Koraidon and Braviary finally reached the deepest part.

Here, Grumpig's illumination alone was no longer enough to let them see the full picture of their surroundings.

Just as D was wondering how to find the ruins of the ancient castle, he saw Raifort pull out two helmets from her backpack.

"Put it on, it's equipped with night vision, allowing you to see the surroundings clearly!"

D reached his hand out to accept it, and as he put on the helmet, everything around them instantly became clear.

They were currently positioned on the top of a protruding cliff.

A dilapidated ancient castle lay quietly beneath the cliff, nestled within the endless darkness.

Perhaps it was damaged during its collapse in the past, the once-majestic castle now only had crumbling walls, full of marks eroded by time.

"Found it!" Staring at the ruins of the ancient castle in the distance, Raifort's eyes lit up: "It's right there!"

She mounted Braviary and flew towards it.

D had to chase after her on Koraidon, not as excited as Raifort; he wasn't very interested in the ruins of the ancient castle, his gaze turned to the surrounding terrain filled with an ancient aura.

This vast abyss seemed to have experienced multiple collapses.

Rainwater and groundwater eroded the soil and rock, causing numerous collapses over time.

Just then, D suddenly noticed a few oddly shaped stones on the ground.

He signaled Koraidon to stop and landed to investigate.

Koraidon obediently landed, and D jumped off, reaching down to pick up those stones.

After wiping off the thick layer of dirt and dust covering them, D was able to see these objects clearly.

Is this... a fossil?!

More than just one!

D's eyes wandered with surprise over these stones, quickly noticing something.

Other than the initial piece he excavated, the rest weren't intact fossils!

Either split in half or just a single corner remained.

What puzzled D even further was that these stones resembled gems.

"Strange, is this a Pokemon fossil?"

But D had never seen fossils like this before.

In his memory, neither Omanyte nor Aerodactyl fossils looked like this.

"Or could they be gems fallen from the ruins of that castle?" D speculated randomly.

Come to think of it though, can gems turn into fossils?

Koraidon beside him also looked puzzled as it eyed the stones ahead.

Meanwhile, Raifort up ahead noticed D hadn't caught up, so she called back to him: "D!"

"Coming!"

D answered, tossed away the broken fossils, kept only one intact stone in his pocket, and rode on Koraidon to catch up with Raifort.

---

---

---

---

# 392: Chapter 389: Harvesting Abundance, Gold Coins Piled High!

"What were you just doing?" Raifort asked in surprise as she stopped to wait.

D pulled a stone from his pocket: "I just found many stones here that look like gems."

Raifort glanced at them and guessed, "Perhaps they're some ancient creatures' fossils? There was a major crustal movement two million years ago that led to many ancient Pokemon's extinction."

"Ancient Pokemon..." D couldn't help but ponder.

As far as he knew, there were 15 kinds of Fossil Pokemon discovered so far.

Kanto's Omanyte, Kabuto, Aerodactyl; Hoenn's Lileep, Anorith; Sinnoh's Cranidos, Shieldon.

And in Unova, Kalos, and Galar, they also have corresponding Fossil Pokemon.

Only the Alola Region and Paldea Region lack Fossil Pokemon.

"These ancient stones are quite valuable in the eyes of some fossil enthusiasts!" Raifort laughed. "I suggest you keep it well. If you have the opportunity in the future, you can use it to trade with other collectors."

D thought about it carefully and realized she was right.

Moreover, since there's no facility to revive fossils in the Paldea Region, he'd have to go to the Kanto Region or Hoenn Region for revival.

But he didn't have much time next because, in a few days, when he got back, his little Eevee would hatch, and he would need to take care of it.

In that case, he'll keep this stone first and consider reviving it when there's a chance in the future.

He wondered whether fossil revival technology had become widespread by then.

The two continued forward and soon reached the entrance to the kingdom ruins.

The steps leading to the kingdom were full of cracks, covered with boulders and dust.

Raifort stepped forward and gently touched them, then blew the dust off her fingers.

"It seems that no one has visited this place for a long time. Just as well, I don't need to worry about someone else taking what I want ahead of time."

Having said that, Raifort looked up at the ruins of the kingdom standing in the darkness and flashed a slight smile back:

"Let's go. First, I want to find the secret chamber used to store secrets from the Paldea Empire period."

D nodded, and they climbed the ancient steps; after passing through a crumbling archway, they officially entered the city.

Compared to the outside, the residential buildings inside the city had all become a collapsed ruin; just this sight was enough to give a sense of the destructive power of those few Treasures of Ruin during the Paldea Empire period.

It seems they weren't called Treasures of Ruin for nothing.

Just then, D suddenly heard a rustling sound behind him.

As he prepared to turn and check, Koraidon reacted faster than he did.

"Agaa!"

With a mighty roar, a menacing aura surged from Koraidon, strong winds pushing back a few Duskull trying to stealthily approach from behind.

D accidentally locked eyes with one of the Duskull, and the next second, he felt a chill run through him.

"Be careful! Duskull can absorb human bio-energy; try to avoid looking them in the eye!"

Raifort's voice came through.

D quickly snapped back, glancing around, seeing a total of eight Duskull in front of him, always residing in this place and rarely seeing humans.

So even though Koraidon released its pressure, it didn't have much effect on them.

The eight Duskull pressed forward, and D ordered in a low voice: "Flamethrower!"

"Agassi!"

Koraidon immediately opened its mouth wide, channeling fiery Fire Type Energy to form a scorching flame that engulfed the Duskull.

The Duskull cried out in pain, letting out a dull growl; only then did they feel fear and drifted back into the darkness.

"Be cautious, there seem to be more than just Duskull among the Pokemon here!"

Raifort received information from Zoroark and reminded.

"Got it!" D nodded.

Raifort looked around, observing the structure of this place, and said: "This is the entrance to the kingdom. Based on the kingdom's architecture at the time, the main fortress should still be up there!"

The two continued upward, with Raifort identifying directions and seeking the target.

Meanwhile, D and Koraidon kept a watchful eye on the surroundings to prevent unexpected Pokemon attacks residing in this place.

As a tomb raider and history teacher, Raifort seemed to know a lot about past events like this.

Gradually, they successfully arrived at the small wooden door next to the main hall of the castle.

"Here it is!" Raifort looked at the small door, her face filled with excitement.

Just as she was about to rush forward to open the door and search for the purpose of their trip, D suddenly noticed movement from the piles of rubble on both sides of the path.

The next second, accompanied by two violent tremors, dust flew from the ground, and two gigantic Pokemon, akin to golems, suddenly leaped out.

They were Golurk!

One of the Golurk's heads rotated continuously, its eyes lighting up with yellow light, and its focus locked entirely on Raifort, who was aimed at the secret chamber.

The Golurk suddenly used High Horsepower and charged fiercely at Raifort, its right hand clenched into a fist and raised high.

"Watch out!"

D shouted, and Koraidon immediately dashed forward, using Dragon Tail to repel the Golurk.

Meanwhile, Raifort's Zoroark squared off against the other Golurk.

---

As a Dark type, Zoroark naturally has a type advantage over Golurk.

Though the battle didn't end as quickly as with Koraidon, its agile moves and type matchups quickly brought down the Golurk.

The eyes of the two defeated Golurk dimmed.

Raifort let out a sigh of relief: "You gave me quite a scare, good thing you're here. Looks like I found the right person this time."

D gestured forward with his chin: "Let's take care of business."

"Mhm!"

Raifort then stepped forward and pushed open the door.

D looked inside from the outside and realized it seemed to be a room used for storing books and archives in the past.

Dust-covered books and yellowed parchments were scattered everywhere. Raifort dove frantically into the room, searching for information related to the Treasures of Ruin.

With nothing better to do, D sat on the steps in front of the castle hall, waiting with Koraidon.

While waiting, D took out the "gem"-shaped stone again and played with it in his hand.

Koraidon watched the stone.

D raised an eyebrow and asked, "Does Koraidon know what this is?"

"Ar-ghuh," Koraidon seemed unsure.

Seeing this, D knew Koraidon didn't know.

He thought Koraidon, living in the past, might know something about it!

Come to think of it, once cleaned, this stone truly doesn't resemble a fossil. Instead, it looks more like a gem, somewhat similar to amber.

"Maybe I could make it into a pendant to wear around my neck as an accessory?" D thought suddenly.

He picked up the stone and measured it against his neck with his hand. Not bad, apart from being somewhat large, it suited quite well.

At this moment, Raifort's excited voice came from behind.

"Finally found it! The parchment recording the Treasures of Ruin! With this, I can find those four Ruinous Pokemon!"

D turned his head, recalling the legends of the Treasures of Ruin, he asked:

"Are you sure you want to unseal them? If the seals are broken and those Pokemon go berserk, what then for the Paldea Region?"

"Uh..." Raifort suddenly stammered.

With D's reminder, she suddenly thought of something.

Legends say that those four treasures-turned-Pokemon of ruin became Treasures of Ruin due to absorbing human desire and greed.

Thinking of this, Raifort hurriedly checked the contents recorded on the parchment.

She was already skilled in ancient writings, and it didn't take long for her to decipher the text.

The parchment said: The king invited a renowned Pokemon Tamer to subdue the four Ruinous Pokemon.

The tamer then used eight stakes to suppress the Ruinous Pokemon, imprisoning them in four shrines.

"Those with greed in their hearts must not unseal them, or disaster will descend upon the world again. Only when those without distractions unseal them will the Treasures of Ruin reveal their true forms."

Raifort murmured the ancient text.

Only those without distractions can unseal them...

Is she such a person?

Ahem... could she not be? Her purpose in finding the Treasures of Ruin was to possess them and collect them as precious.

Feeling a bit embarrassed, Raifort realized she couldn't give up so easily now that she was here.

Putting away the parchment, Raifort looked at D who was waiting: "Ahem, my business is done. Next up is helping you collect gold coins! Come on, if I guess right, the way to the Treasure Vault is this way!"

D glanced at Raifort's parchment but didn't ask much.

After all, his purpose here was to help Gimmighoul collect enough ancient gold coins to evolve into Gholdengo.

The two of them continued down the dilapidated road. After passing through most of the ruins, they finally found the castle's Treasure Vault.

Perhaps to deter theft, the Treasure Vault's door and walls were made of very sturdy material.

Even after thousands of years, rain and groundwater hadn't corroded them, keeping this place in its original state.

The door had a lock, and D initially considered having Koraidon forcefully remove it, but then he saw Raifort pulling out a small metal piece from her bag and fiddling with the lock.

With a click, the lock opened.

Raifort pushed the door open. Before D could feel surprised at her lock-picking skills, his eyes were caught by the dazzling pile of treasures before him.

Whoa! So many gold coins!

Standing at the door, Raifort smiled and said, "Looks like your wish can also be fulfilled now!"

Thinking about the imminent birth of Gholdengo, D couldn't help but smile, then walked forward with Koraidon to start loading the gold coins into his bag.

After loading for a while, he stopped and lay down on the pile of coins.

Raifort: "?"

D closed his eyes and said, "I always wondered when watching movies, what it feels like for pirates to sleep on a pile of treasure."

Raifort couldn't help but ask, "Do you know now?"

"Yes." D sat up, "It's hard and not comfortable at all."

"Pfft!" Raifort chuckled.

Then D started packing the pile of coins into his Space Backpack again.

Apart from the pile of gleaming coins, there were other gold and silver jewels.

Like the Golden Crown, Gold Necklace, a ruby the size of a pigeon's egg, a golden King's Scepter...

D wasn't much interested in these things. Like the crown and scepter might have been used by a dead king, holding them is unnerving.

Therefore, D said, "I just want the coins, you can take the rest!"

"You don't want them?" Raifort was somewhat surprised, she glanced at it and said, "Such a Golden Scepter could fetch tens of millions outside."

Tens of millions?

Upon hearing this number, D's heart moved slightly.

But when he looked up and saw Raifort's meaningful gaze, he suddenly understood something.

Good heavens, Raifort was testing him!

Just now, Raifort read the content on the sheepskin scroll without hiding it from him, so D heard about the information that only someone with a clear mind can lift the seal.

Choose a lifetime of wealth or choose four Ruinous Pokemon?

Almost without hesitation, D had the answer in his heart.

He wants the Treasures of Ruin; he wants to have his own Snow Leopard Pokemon!

Therefore, D firmly refused the other golden treasures: "No thanks, I just want these coins."

After all, his goal for this trip was these coins in the first place.

Upon hearing this, Raifort's face showed a sudden realization, and her tone was full of appreciation:

"Truly courageous... Alright, I understand. In that case, I'll take these. After I go back and thoroughly decipher these sheepskin scrolls, I might still need your help. Please take care then!"

"Alright." D nodded with a smile.

*

This underground ruins exploration ended successfully. D and Raifort left the underground cave along the way they came and returned to Asado Desert.

At this time, the sky was already bright.

The two, having not slept all night, were visibly tired.

Raifort drove, carrying D and Koraidon directly back to Cascarrafa City.

They stood at the intersection to say goodbye.

Raifort: "Do you have any plans next?"

D yawned: "Go home, and then sleep well. What about you?"

"I'm going to find a hotel to sleep for a while too. Well, thank you for today, let's cooperate again next time. See you later! I'm off!"

Raifort waved her hand, turned around and left, quickly disappearing on the streets of Cascarrafa City.

D also mounted Koraidon and started returning to the ranch.

Koraidon was a bit tired too, so to get home quickly, it sped up.

So in less than ten minutes, it carried D back to the ranch.

Back home, D released all the Pokemon from the Poke Ball.

D intended to give the coins in the backpack to Gholdengo, but he was too tired, he tossed the backpack full of coins into the living room and went to his room to sleep.

In a daze, D felt something hard and rather uncomfortable in his pocket. He blindly reached it, took it out, tossed it aside, and then fell asleep deeply.

After a while, brother Indeedee came in with a cup of hot milk.

Seeing D already asleep, brother Indeedee didn't disturb him further.

It picked up the messy clothes from the floor to wash them in the washing machine.

Just as brother Indeedee was about to leave, it saw the stone that D casually tossed onto the floor.

Brother Indeedee walked over, picked up the stone, then left the room, placing the stone into D's designated pile for storing Everstones, Fire Stones, Water Stones, and other items.

---

---

---

---

# 393: Chapter 390: Ultra-Rare Pokemon, Gholdengo, Is Born!

D slept until seven in the evening.

After waking up, he casually had a couple of bites of food to get by, then eagerly opened his backpack and began counting the haul from this trip.

"Cling clang!"

Along with the crisp and pleasing sound of gold coins ringing out, the pile of shiny ancient Paldea gold coins formed a small hill on the floor.

All the surrounding Pokemon couldn't help but be drawn over, their eyes filled with shock.

"Pawmo!"

"Mama!"

D grinned and scratched Pawmot's head, laughingly asked, "What do you think? Aren't these gold coins beautiful?"

"Pawmo!" Pawmot nodded repeatedly, its eyes reflecting the glimmering golden light.

"Why don't you go play on the side for a while, as I'm going to start counting the coins!"

D rolled up his sleeves and began counting the gold coins in front of him one by one.

"One, two, three, four..."

Time ticked away second by second, and after some time had passed, the gold coin hill was finally moved piece by piece to the other side by D.

"Seven hundred twenty-three, seven hundred twenty-four... seven hundred sixty-six."

"A total of 766 coins?"

Upon getting the count of the coins, D immediately began calculating his current total number of gold coins.

"Aside from these coins, I've already collected 230 before, 230 plus 766 equals 996."

Faced with this result, D couldn't help but widen his eyes and burst out with a curse.

"What the hell! Just 4 coins short of 1000?"

Are you kidding me?

D doubted his life a bit, looking uncertainly at the coins in front of him.

Could it be that he counted wrong? Maybe he missed four coins?

Unwilling to give in, D spent over half an hour re-counting the coins carefully and thoroughly.

In the end, he still found 766 coins.

Along with the coins he'd collected before, he had a total of 996 coins, just four short of 1000.

"..."

D felt a deep sense of frustration, laying himself flat on the floor, starting to doubt his life.

Damn king! So many Golden Crowns, Gold Necklaces, and Golden Toilets. But not more gold coins!

D covered his face and sighed.

"Pawmo?"

The surrounding Pokemon gathered around to check on him.

"I'm fine."

D sighed, hoping that Geeta would help him collect the final four coins.

With this thought, D revitalized himself.

Now, the most important thing is to give these coins to Gimmighoul and let it store them in the Treasure Chest.

Thus D went to the Pokemon Tavern and brought back the Treasure Chest where Gimmighoul was hiding.

As if feeling itself moving, Gimmighoul silently lifted the lid of the chest a bit with its head to observe outside secretly.

Seeing that the person hugging it was D, Gimmighoul immediately breathed a sigh of relief.

"Ding-a-ling!"

D glanced at it, saying mysteriously, "Are you ready? I've got a surprise for you."

"Ding-a-ling?!"

Hearing the word surprise, Gimmighoul's eyes immediately lit up.

Previously, D surprised it with what it dreamed of—gold coins!

And now D said there was another surprise, could he have found shiny gold coins again?

Gimmighoul's face was full of anticipation.

D walked into the room.

When Gimmighoul clearly saw the pile of gold coins on the floor, it jumped up in excitement, flipping the lid of the chest.

"Go ahead!" D said with a smile.

Filled with a sense of fantasy, Gimmighoul stared at the glittering coins, feeling like it was in a dream.

Only in dreams would something so wonderful happen!

After all, it was just a Normal Gimmighoul, always on the path of collecting coins but never achieving its dream.

But now... so many gold coins were truly laid out before it, Gimmighoul's immediate reaction was disbelief.

Instead of rushing forward, it uncertainly asked:

"Ding-a-ling?"

Understanding Gimmighoul's question, D nodded with a smile: "That's right, they're all yours."

"Ding-a-ling!"

Upon receiving D's affirmation, Gimmighoul jumped excitedly, leaping out of the chest and falling face-first onto the pile of coins.

Gimmighoul was filled with happiness, nibbling here and sniffing there, as if it were a fish swimming in a sea of coins.

"Whew..." Watching this scene, D's face also showed a smile.

It was worth it, jumping into quicksand and running to the underground cavern already!

After the excitement, Gimmighoul began diligently moving the coins into its Treasure Chest.

Seeing this, D joined in to help.

Who knows what the Treasure Chest is made of that it can hold so many coins, even though the volume of coins seems larger than the chest from the outside...

Yet the Treasure Chest was like a bottomless pit, swallowing all the coins.

Finally, Gimmighoul took a leap and jumped into the chest, half-submerged in coins.

Perhaps this is the magic of Ghost-type Pokemon? D couldn't help but wonder.

As far as he knew, besides the chest form of Gimmighoul, Gengar also has storage abilities.

These Pokemon have bodies that are alternate dimensional spaces, able to hold many, many things.

It's said that before space backpack technology was invented, some Trainers would store their travel luggage inside Gengar's body.

---

Bathing in the sea of Gold Coins, Gimmighoul at this moment only felt a surge of power emerging from the coins, continuously flowing into its body, allowing it to clearly feel that it was slowly becoming stronger.

But that power only lasted for a while before disappearing.

Almost there—instinctively, this thought popped into Gimmighoul's mind.

It looked at D and mentioned this matter.

"Ding!"

D understood and began to explain the reason to Gimmighoul: "That's evolution. Once you gather a thousand Gold Coins, you can evolve into a more powerful Pokemon, known as Gholdengo."

"Ding...

Gimmighoul's eyes turned into stars like Ogerpon.

It wanted to evolve, wanted to evolve into Gholdengo!

D sensed Gimmighoul's feelings, smiled, and nodded: "Yeah, it won't be long now!"

Next, they were just waiting for Geeta's reply.

However, this wait lasted until August.

League year 199, August 10th.

Larry rushed to the ranch with a briefcase.

His eyes were blackened, his face full of fatigue, looking like he hadn't rested for a long time.

Seeing D, Larry showed no surprise, he placed the backpack on the grass weakly and said:

"You're D, right? I've found the ancient Paldea Gold Coins you need."

"What?" Larry's voice was as small as a mosquito, so D didn't hear clearly.

Larry: "..."

He repeated: "These are the ancient Paldea Gold Coins you wanted. I've spent a week running through six cities to collect these coins, and I still couldn't gather a thousand, I'm sorry, I've tried my best."

The bag opened, revealing the pile of shiny Gold Coins inside, looking no less than one hundred.

Gimmighoul can finally evolve!

D was extremely excited, quickly thanking Larry: "It's okay, these are already enough! Thank you for your hard work, Mr. Larry!"

"It's my job." Larry's body began to sway weakly.

D: "?"

He couldn't help but ask: "How long have you been without rest?"

Larry weakly replied: "About four days." During the procurement of ancient coins, he temporarily received several other tasks, almost without having rested.

D: "... Why don't you rest at the ranch for a while?"

Larry shook his head: "No, you focus on your tasks, don't mind me, I'll just sit here and enjoy the sun."

As he said that, Larry sat on the grass, looking at the autumn-filled ranch, and those rare Pokemon, weakly sighing.

"This ranch is truly full of vitality, for ordinary people like me, your place is really stimulating."

D: "..."

"Please wait a moment." Seeing Larry in that condition, it seemed he hadn't had a chance to drink a sip of water along the way.

D turned and went to the living room to prepare a cup of spirit-restoring black tea for Larry.

However, when he returned, he saw Larry asleep on the soft grass.

Larry: zzZ!

D: "..."

Oh well, he decided not to disturb Larry, letting him rest well here!

Thus, D picked up the bag of coins and headed straight to the Pokemon Tavern.

During the day, the Pokemon Tavern was closed, with no guests inside, only Gimmighoul present.

D placed the Treasure Chest on the bar, then excitedly took out the last four Gold Coins from the backpack.

These are the last four coins!

Once accepted, Gimmighoul can evolve into Gholdengo!

At this moment, Gimmighoul also felt the imminent event about to occur.

With excitement all over its face, it looked at the coins in D's hand.

"Go ahead and take them!" D put those four coins into Gimmighoul's Treasure Chest.

The next second, Gimmighoul felt a powerful essence frantically flowing into its body.

"Ding!"

Gimmighoul couldn't help but exclaim.

Soon, the thousand coins started to swirl, twisting around the small Gimmighoul.

The light of evolution exploded.

Unlike the light emanated during normal Pokemon evolution, Gimmighoul's evolutionary light was dazzling gold!

This golden light instantly submerged both Gimmighoul and the Treasure Chest completely.

D watched this scene with passionate eyes.

Truly deserving to rank number 1000 in the Pokedex, evolving with such panache?

At this moment, D only regretted that smartphones had not yet been invented.

Otherwise, he would have recorded the entire process and shared it online proudly!

Because this Gholdengo might be one of a kind in this era.

The only one in the world!

As the golden light enveloping Gimmighoul disappeared, the once weak Gimmighoul was nowhere to be found, replaced by a brand new Pokemon about 1 meter 20 tall with a dazzling Gold body.

Gholdengo was born!

D watched the Gholdengo before him with excited eyes.

Gholdengo lifted its head with longing, looking brightly at D.

The next second, Gholdengo jumped high, hugging D's neck, its voice full of obvious warmth and affection.

"Ding~!"

D immediately sensed Gholdengo's sentiment, it was grateful for his help in collecting the coins needed for evolution.

"Very well!" D repeated thrice: "Congratulations, Gholdengo, let's take care of each other from now!"

"Ding!"

Gholdengo happily nodded, indicating no problem.

---

Next, it's about where Gholdengo will go.

Before evolving, Gimmighoul is naturally timid and doesn't like to go out in front of humans.

But after evolving, Gholdengo's temperament undergoes a dramatic change.

The Pokédex says that Gholdengo is lively and cheerful, and very friendly towards both humans and Pokemon.

With such a personality, there's no way Gholdengo would continue hiding in the Pokemon Tavern like during its Gimmighoul days.

Thinking of this, D said, "Let's go, let's head out together! It's time to officially introduce you to everyone!"

"Jingling~!"

Gholdengo knew there were many Pokemon living in the ranch; it had found out through secret observations before evolving.

Alcremie, Pawmo, Dragonite, Ogerpon...

Gholdengo also wanted to play with them.

So, Gholdengo let go of D's hand, walked ahead of him, and ran out of the tavern full of expectations, turning back at the door to look back.

D was slightly startled, then realized that Gholdengo wanted to interact with the outside world.

He smiled slightly, lifted his foot to follow Gholdengo's steps, and led it into the ranch.

Standing on the grass, D whistled toward the surroundings.

Soon, a large group of Pokemon came running from all directions.

Koraidon, Cyclizar, Pawmo, Alcremie, Dragonite, Dragonair, two Eevees, Arboliva...

When all the Pokemon were gathered, D cleared his throat and introduced them:

"From now on, this Pokemon is also our new companion, its name is Gholdengo!"

Pokemon had different reactions to the new companion.

Dragonite and Dragonair were attracted by Gholdengo's golden glowing body and found it amazing.

Koraidon stood with arms crossed on the sidelines, looking as if it was used to things like this.

Alcremie and Ogerpon happily welcomed Gholdengo, and the addition of a new companion made Ogerpon so happy that it hopped twice on the grass.

Pawmo scratched its head, showing a happy expression as well.

For it, the more companions, the better, as the ranch would become livelier, and Pawmo loved lively places.

As for the Pokemon that welcomed it, Gholdengo also displayed its friendliness.

It danced a mysterious dance on the ground, like a boy breakdancing.

The next second, a magical scene happened.

Suddenly, a cool rain of gold coins fell from thin air.

The golden coins looked particularly dazzling in the sunlight.

The Pokemon lifted their heads to gaze at the sky, then reached out their hands.

The coins in the sky seemed to be guided, landing in their palms.

A cheerful smile appeared on Gholdengo's face.

D was dumbfounded by the scene, mouth agape.

Wow... so impressive!

Is this what it means to be wealthy, just casually raining gold coins?

No wonder the Pokédex says Gholdengo can immediately form good relationships with anyone...

Looking at it now, those words were indeed true.

Because D saw Ogerpon and the others pulling Gholdengo's hand, surrounding it in the middle, and taking it to play in Snorlax Park.

D looked down at the shiny new coin in his palm.

He bit it hard with his teeth.

Yep, it's real!

Now it seems Gholdengo really can generate coins right on the spot!

"Are they coins made with Steel Type Energy?" D wondered in his heart.

Things like this really defy science!

What does it feel like to suddenly have a wealthy one in the ranch?

D vaguely realized that he probably would no longer have to suffer from poverty in the future.

*

At 4 PM, Larry woke up from his sleep.

He opened his eyes and found himself lying in a bed.

Larry got up somewhat confused, left the room, and went downstairs.

"Awake?" D, brewing tea on the sofa, saw Larry and asked.

Larry was slightly stunned, then realized what had happened.

After a nap, he finally recovered some spirit: "Thank you, D, for the disturbance today."

"You're welcome, have some tea and snacks!" D said with a smile.

Larry, poor overworked brother.

Seeing Larry's dark circles and weary appearance, D was reminded of himself as a corporate drone before crossing over, and couldn't help but feel sympathy for Larry.

"Were the coins I brought useful?" Larry sat on the sofa; even seeing a legendary Pokemon like Calyrex, he wasn't too surprised.

As a member of society, years of work had long helped him develop a heart that views everything as normal.

D nodded: "I've already used them, and I must sincerely thank you. If not for the coins you sent me, I'd have been in hot water."

He was just 4 coins short before, almost drove him crazy.

Larry took a sip of tea: "You're welcome, it was the chief's assignment."

D smiled.

And Larry continued: "I have another favor to ask."

D was slightly surprised, and then said: "Go ahead, if I can help."

Larry: "I want to battle you in a Pokemon match."

D was a bit slow to react: "Hmm?"

Larry explained: "For me, this is part of my job too."

Besides, this was arranged by the chief, and as a Gym Leader, he had to come and participate in the trial.

D understood, but didn't know what to say.

"Would you like to rest a bit more?" D asked.

Larry shook his head: "No, let me finish this task quickly! I want to finish work sooner."

D: "...Alright."

Larry took a simple sip of tea, then stood up and followed behind D to the ranch.

D initially planned to introduce Larry to some trials in the ranch.

But when he saw Gholdengo's figure, he suddenly changed his mind.

Because D thought of something very interesting.

So next, let's see whether the endurance of a working adult has any effect on Gholdengo!

---

---

---

---

# 394: Chapter 391: How Can There Be Such a Perfect Pokemon as Gholdengo?!

Guts, a Normal Type move, has a power of 70 in the game, but if the user is poisoned, burned, or paralyzed, its power doubles.

For a corporate drone, Guts can be considered an essential skill.

However, when facing Gholdengo, which is entirely made of gold, Guts is completely countered by the Ghost and Steel Type Gholdengo.

This even gave birth to a meme—

"Larry (taking out Staraptor's Poke Ball): May I use moves that are popular among working adults?"

"The opponent's Staraptor used Guts!"

"It seems ineffective against Gholdengo..."

Thinking of this, D nodded and said, "Then let's begin!"

Larry nodded in agreement.

The two of them stepped onto the battle ground in the pasture.

Larry took out a red and white ball from his suit pocket: "In that case, let's settle this in one round!"

D called out Gholdengo, just in time to hear Larry's words, and smiled, nodding in agreement: "Alright."

When Larry saw the gold-themed Pokemon, his usually expressionless face showed a hint of bewilderment due to exhaustion.

He paused the action of tossing his Poke Ball: "What kind of Pokemon is that?"

D: "Gholdengo, a Pokemon evolved from Gimmighoul."

"Gholdengo..."

Upon hearing this, Larry instantly realized D's purpose for collecting those gold coins.

He glanced at Gholdengo a few times, then shifted his gaze to D.

"You're indeed not just an ordinary person, no wonder the chief sees potential in you, I should have known."

D: "..."

Without saying much, Larry directly threw out his Poke Ball: "Let's get to work then, please guide me!"

With a flash of white light, a strong Staraptor appeared in front of Larry.

Seeing this scene, D was a bit surprised.

Well, looks like Larry is serious!

He originally thought Larry would send out any random Pokemon to handle the battle, but now Larry directly sent out his ace, Staraptor.

Great.

Seldom did D feel such spirited excitement, even though Gholdengo had just evolved, he had taken the opportunity during Larry's unconsciousness to familiarize himself with Gholdengo's moves.

Though few in number, in terms of attributes, the Steel and Ghost Type Gholdengo held a significant advantage against the Flying and Normal Type Staraptor.

Moreover, from Larry's demeanor, it seemed to be his first time encountering a Pokemon like Gholdengo.

The last appearance of Gholdengo was during the grand expedition of the kingdom a thousand years ago.

In that case, he'll take a gamble that Larry doesn't know Gholdengo's attributes!

The referee, Indeedee, walked over to the center of the battlefield, raised its right hand, and then blew the whistle hanging from its neck.

"Whee~!"

The sound of the whistle marked the official start of the battle.

"Staraptor, use Quick Attack!"

On command, the opposing Staraptor spread its wings and took flight, transforming its body into white light, charging towards Gholdengo with lightning speed.

Gholdengo stood still, unperturbed as the Quick Attack hit, without moving an inch.

Seeing this, Larry and Staraptor were both slightly taken aback.

"Is that Gholdengo a Ghost Type Pokemon? No, it's not just Ghost, it must have Steel Type too!! I should have realized, Gimmighoul is a Ghost Type Pokemon." Larry shook his head secretly.

After all, in everyday life, Gimmighoul is still quite a rare Pokemon.

Quick Attack was ineffective, and Staraptor, with its experience in regular battles, habitually distanced itself from Gholdengo.

However, D wasn't about to give it that chance.

"Gholdengo, use Metal Sound!"

"Jingle!"

Upon receiving the command, Gholdengo abruptly grabbed Staraptor's feet, and the thousand gold coins making up its body began to vibrate fiercely.

So many coins colliding with each other created a piercing sound resembling thunder.

Staraptor felt as if the sound was invading its ears, nearly blowing off its top, and instinctively the feathers all over its body stood on end.

"Staraptor, maintain your composure!" Larry's voice came from behind: "Fall back and use Brave Bird!"

"Gholdengo, don't give it that chance! Use your signature move, Make It Rain!" D's voice followed closely.

On the field, both Pokemon moved simultaneously.

Staraptor hadn't fully recovered from the Metal Sound, it struggled to flap its wings, trying to distance itself and then use its powerful Brave Bird to attack.

However, Gholdengo didn't give it that chance.

It simultaneously extended its hands, performing a motion like a magician throwing cards forward.

Brilliant gold light flashed, and countless gold coins appeared around Staraptor, attacking it in wave after wave like a tide.

Larry felt as if his eyes were blinded by the scene, he couldn't help but murmur: "So, so many coins!"

Staraptor's body was heavily hit by countless coins, unable to rise again.

Metal Sound had significantly lowered its special defense, and the powerful move, Make It Rain, with a power of 120, directly defeated the Staraptor.

The battle concluded, and Gholdengo struck a cool pose.

Larry took a deep breath, retrieved Staraptor using his Poke Ball.

"... I lost, for a mundane person like me, you're really too exhilarating."

D shook his head and smiled, saying, "Mr. Larry is quite remarkable too. This battle was won by using the advantage of information asymmetry. If you had known the attributes of Gholdengo in advance, the result might have been uncertain."

If he remembered correctly, besides Staraptor, Larry also had Pokemon like Tropius, Oricorio, and Altaria.

But these are the Pokemon Larry would use when serving as a member of the Elite Four.

"Either way, you still won." Larry looked up at the sky and added, "It's getting late, and today is a day without overtime, so I will take my leave now."

D: "..."

Goodness, Larry really doesn't dawdle at all, D couldn't help but think to himself, then he extended an invitation:

"It's almost dark, and the Squawkabilly Flying Taxi might have stopped running at this hour. Why not stay overnight at the ranch?"

The Chief had also told him to enjoy the rural scenery at D Ranch after finishing work, but Larry had his own plans.

Thinking of this, Larry shook his head and declined, "It's okay. Traveling to other places might be enjoyable, but returning home is the most comfortable, right?"

D was slightly startled, then he understood what Larry meant.

It really suited Larry's personality.

"Alright,"

D nodded, and before Larry left, he picked up a gold coin left by Gholdengo after using Make It Rain from the ground and handed it to Larry.

"Here, for you, as thanks for helping me collect the last few ancient gold coins."

Larry glanced at the gold coin, recalling the events that had just occurred, then reached out to accept this commemorative coin.

"This Gholdengo, I think it's the only Gholdengo in the Paldea Region." Larry suddenly remarked, "At least until today, I've never seen a Pokemon like Gholdengo."

"After all, evolving a Gimmighoul into Gholdengo isn't easy!" D sighed.

Larry nodded, then, carrying his briefcase, he turned and walked out of the ranch.

Walking on the country road outside the ranch, Larry paused for a long time, looking up at the vast sky and the distant horizon.

After working in society for so long, he almost forgot the feeling of roaming the world in his youth.

At that moment, Larry had an insight.

Maybe, that's why he chose to become the League's Flying Elite in the first place?

To spread his wings and soar freely in the blue sky, like those Flying-Type Pokemon.

But now, he's no longer young, and those activities are more suited for energetic young people.

Thinking of this, a smile appeared on Larry's lips, and he then took big strides forward.

The work was already done, and he didn't need to work overtime today, so it was time to go home.

*

The sky gradually darkened, and the Pokemon Tavern was brightly lit at night.

Pokemon gathered here to celebrate the arrival of a new member at the tavern.

D sat on a chair with Koraidon and several other Pokemon, watching Gholdengo being surrounded by enthusiastic Pokemon, generously scattering gold coins, momentarily unsure of what to say.

Is this the world of rich people? Giving gold coins away at the slightest disagreement.

First meeting, give gold coins; when happy, give gold coins; making a friend, give gold coins.

Gholdengo as a Pokemon is really too extravagant, isn't it?

Beside them, Pawmot and others were equally wide-eyed, staring at the enthusiastic Gholdengo.

Having lived with D for a long time, they had learned some human norms.

For example, when humans want something, they don't steal it, they use "money" to buy it.

Judging by D's expression, the Pokemon also understood that the gold scattered by Gholdengo was money.

They had never seen so much money on D...

"Pawmo. So extravagant." Pawmot couldn't help but say.

Umbreon used its paw pad to pick up a gold coin from the crack in the floor and brought it to D.

D held it in his hand, suddenly feeling that wherever Gholdengo went, there would always be gold coins to collect.

If a Meowth that loves collecting gold coins were here, wouldn't it just die from happiness?

D Ranch, a paradise for Meowth!

An ordinary gold coin like this one is about the size of two nuggets.

And on the market, a nugget can be sold for a high price of ten thousand league coins.

This means that a single gold coin is worth five thousand league coins.

Throughout the day today, Gholdengo has given out at least a hundred gold coins.

D did a quick calculation in his head, and was shocked to find that Gholdengo's one-day cash flow reached a total of five hundred thousand league coins!

Gholdengo, although it can't destroy the world like those legendary Pokemon, with its power, it can definitely disrupt the economic system of an entire region in a short time.

In response, D could only comment with four words — terrifying as hell!

To prevent this from happening, that night, as the Pokemon Tavern was closing, D sought out Gholdengo.

"Gholdengo, you can't just throw around gold coins like this!" D said earnestly to it.

"Ding-a-ling...?" However, Gholdengo was quite troubled by this.

But it liked giving coins to everyone!

D saw what Gholdengo meant, he shook his head and explained, "It's not that you can't give, it's about not producing so many coins all at once."

D pointed to the scattered coins on the tavern floor: "Look, so many coins have gone to waste."

"Ding-a-ling..." Gholdengo looked at those coins, not wanting to waste them, but it didn't know what to do either, so it looked at D for help.

D then said, "So, from now on you should do this: only give gold coins to people or Pokemon you like, as a token of your friendship, this way you can avoid extravagance and waste."

"Ding-a-ling!" Gholdengo's eyes lit up; it was a great idea!

It nodded repeatedly, patting its chest in assurance, expressing that it understood and would never waste again!

"That's more like it!" D patted Gholdengo on the head, "Then that's it for today! Do you want to stay in the tavern or come back to the room with us?"

Hearing this, Gholdengo glanced back at the familiar tavern, then decisively chose to stay there.

"Ding-a-ling!"

"Alright." Seeing this, D wasn't surprised, as Gholdengo obviously loved it here.

He nodded and said, "From today, your job is to stay in the tavern as the lucky cat of the entire ranch!"

Although it didn't know what a lucky cat was, Gholdengo liked this job.

It happily nodded, "Ding-a-ling!"

D smiled, "Well then, goodnight, I'm going back to sleep, you should rest early too!"

"Ding-a-ling~!"

D left the tavern, but when he returned to the living room, he saw the household Pokemon each holding several gold coins, even Calyrex did.

D: "..."

Alcremie happily explained, "Mamaa!"

Calyrex translated for it, "This is a gift from Gholdengo to us, that Pokemon is truly amazing, able to do such a thing, it reminds me of an old acquaintance..."

"Never mind, since it's a gift from Gholdengo to you, take good care of it!"

After speaking, D looked at Calyrex and asked, "Old acquaintance?"

Calyrex nodded slightly, then explained to D:

"It was a Pokemon called Melmetal. Back when I was a king in the Galar Region, I foresaw future events and gathered two Hundred Battle Heroes in advance."

"But at the time, the Hundred Battle Heroes lacked suitable weapons, that's when that Melmetal provided us with enough iron, which helped Zacian and Zamazenta forge the sword and shield, enabling them to successfully repel the Darkest Day."

"Only after that, did I fall into a deep sleep due to exhaustion of power, losing memory upon awakening, with no knowledge of where Melmetal went."

"I see..." D said while recalling the details of Melmetal, comforting, "Don't worry, Melmetal has a long lifespan, it is definitely still alive somewhere in this world, maybe hiding in some ruins, having a nice long sleep!"

Moreover, according to Melmetal's lore, it will never disappear.

Because when Melmetal's lifespan comes to an end, its body will slowly rust and disintegrate.

In the end, Melmetal will become tiny fragments, which will resurrect as Meltan in its initial stage, beginning its life anew.

---

---

# 395: Chapter 392: Unsung Heroes, the Pokemon's Shopping Adventure!

"Let's hope so!" Calyrex sighed deeply.

Seeing Calyrex, an idea popped into D's mind: perhaps he should go find that Melmetal and invite it to live on the ranch as well.

Though Melmetal was the unsung hero who provided iron for Zacian and Zamazenta to fend off the Darkest Day, it remained unrecorded in history.

D wasn't sure what Zacian and Zamazenta planned, nor whether they'd come looking for Calyrex after defeating Eternatus.

But if given a chance, D hoped they could all come to the ranch, including Melmetal.

While D harbored a slight desire to collect Legendary Pokemon, he mostly wished for these heroes to retire happily at the ranch, under the World Tree.

With this in mind, D began, "When there's time, let's go to the Galar Region to find Melmetal!"

Calyrex was slightly startled and gazed into D's eyes, understanding his thoughts instantly.

This little believer wanted the heroes to live a better life!

Calyrex chuckled softly, "Yes, I would very much like to meet Melmetal again."

"Alright, it's a promise then," D grinned, sealing the agreement with Calyrex.

*

The next day dawned bright and breezy, filling the countryside with the joy of harvest in this golden season.

A batch of Berries ripened in the Berry Garden; early in the morning, D took Koraidon and Cyclizar to pick Berries, boxing the excess to load onto a pickup for sale.

Meanwhile, other usually idle Pokemon gathered at the Snorlax Park.

Pokemon like Pawmo, Ogerpon, Alcremie, Glaceon, Umbreon jumped onto Snorlax's belly one after another.

Larger Pokemon like Dragonite and Garganacl couldn't climb onto Snorlax's belly and had to stay on the open ground nearby.

And piled among the Pokemon was a heap of sparkling Gold Coins.

They gathered there to discuss something very important.

D had always taken care of them, often bringing gifts from his travels outside.

Now, with Gholdengo's help, they all had money and decided to buy a gift for D, just like he did for them.

Pawmo looked troubled: "Pamo…"(But I've never been to the town and don't know how to buy things from humans…)

The other Pokemon didn't know either. They exchanged puzzled glances.

Then, Dragonite stepped forward confidently:

"Rawr-rawr!"(I know! Just give them money, and you can buy things! Like humans buying milk from the ranch!)

Sister Indeedee's eyes lit up, promptly asking, "Dee-dee?" (Then who should we send to complete this task?)

It was a real problem since, apart from D, humans didn't understand what they said.

The Pokemon glanced at each other.

Suddenly, the always-attentive Ogerpon had an idea.

"Boni-boni!"(How about I go? I can wear a mask, then they won't know I'm a Pokemon!)

It often did this back in Kitakami.

Alcremie: "Maa-maa!"(Good idea!)

Dragonite nodded as well: "Rawr-rawr!"(Then I'll take you there!)

It was very familiar with the town from making daily deliveries.

Brother Indeedee looked calmly at the other Pokemon, saying, "Dee-dee."(In that case, let's stay at the ranch to avoid D noticing anything unusual.)

Sister Indeedee fretted slightly: "Dee-dee?"(But is this money enough to buy a gift?)

The other Pokemon had no concept of money, and were naturally puzzled by this question.

Then, Gholdengo confidently patted its chest and said, "Ding!"(Leave it to me! If it's not enough, I can make lots and lots of Gold Coins!)

So, after some careful planning, the Pokemon quickly divided the tasks among themselves.

Dragonite would take Ogerpon and Gholdengo to the town, while the other Pokemon stayed at the ranch to prevent D from discovering anything.

Sister Indeedee found a small bundle, packed the Gold Coins inside, and hung it on Ogerpon's arm.

While D was busy in the Berry Garden, Dragonite loaded Ogerpon and Gholdengo on its back and quietly slipped away from the ranch from the rear.

The remaining Pokemon stayed at Snorlax Park, waiting for them to return.

Busy in the Berry Garden, D suddenly looked across the river.

"Did I just hear a Dragonite's growl?" he wondered aloud.

Koraidon and Cyclizar were equally perplexed.

"Graa…"

"Grro?"

"Never mind, it didn't sound like a fight," D shook his head, dismissing the thought, as he instructed Cyclizar to help load the large basket of Berries onto the pickup.

Meanwhile, Dragonite, Ogerpon, and Gholdengo had already reached the town.

To conceal its Pokemon identity, Ogerpon donned an Eevee mask D had bought at a fireworks festival.

Gholdengo and Dragonite feigned being its Pokemon.

The trio strutted down the streets of Porto Marinada Town, drawing looks from passersby.

Ogerpon was a bit nervous, whispering, "Boni?"(Why does it feel like everyone's looking at us?)

Dragonite seemed unconcerned: "Rawr!"(Maybe because I'm a Dragonite!)

Delivering goods frequently, it was used to humans staring or even chasing it through several streets just to see it.

Dragonite didn't understand humans, so it simply ignored them.

Hearing Dragonite's explanation, Ogerpon relaxed, thinking its Pokemon identity hadn't been discovered!

Meanwhile, Gholdengo was brimming with enthusiasm.

Seeing people watch it, it cheerfully waved back at them.

"Ding!"

People who hadn't seen Gholdengo before gasped in surprise.

"That Pokemon's greeting us!"

"Wow! Its body is so shiny and beautiful!"

Gholdengo nodded proudly, yes, it was indeed that impressive!

The three Pokemon continued forward.

Suddenly, a fervent-eyed boy blocked their path.

Ogerpon jumped in surprise, almost letting out a Pokemon growl beneath its mask.

The boy, excitedly glancing at Dragonite and Gholdengo, then turned to Ogerpon and said:

"You're a Trainer too, right? Amazing! You caught a Dragonite and this golden Pokemon... I challenge you! Please battle my Pokemon!"

ps: Added a chapter, end of month, asking for monthly votes!
