# 396: Chapter 393: The Flower of Gracidea

Battle?

They aren't here to battle today, so Ogerpon instinctively shook its head to refuse, then continued walking forward with Dragonite and Gholdengo.

Seeing that the masked girl rejected his challenge, the boy was slightly stunned.

But that Dragonite looks very powerful. Since I've encountered it, it would be a shame not to battle such a strong Trainer.

The boy hurriedly caught up, following behind the Trainer: "Please have a Pokemon battle with me, just one match, please!"

Ogerpon, Dragonite, and Gholdengo stared blankly at the persistent boy in front of them.

The three Pokemon exchanged glances; it seemed they could only move forward by defeating this human.

"Boo..."

Ogerpon wanted to take off its mask and battle itself, but suddenly remembered it was disguising as a human Trainer.

So Ogerpon quickly put on the mask again and nodded.

Seeing the Trainer agree, the boy's eyes lit up, and he immediately pulled out a Poke Ball from his pocket.

"Nice to meet you!"

Ogerpon imitated his gesture and extended its hand.

Dragonite at first didn't react, until it noticed Ogerpon's puzzling gaze and realized it was its turn to act.

Dragonite stepped forward with a fierce presence.

"Wow!"

Watching this scene, a question mark popped up above the boy's head.

What is going on? This Trainer and her Pokemon seem so odd?

He couldn't help but ask, "Can't you speak?"

Ogerpon didn't know how to respond, so it could only shake its head.

Can't speak? Is this girl mute? The boy was bewildered, how would she command the Pokemon in battle?

Only then did the boy realize his rudeness and quickly apologized to the girl in front of him, "I'm very sorry! I didn't know your situation!"

Ogerpon didn't understand why he was rambling, it waved its hand signaling the battle could start.

The boy had many questions but still couldn't resist the temptation and led the strange girl and her Pokemon to the battle square in the town center.

In this world, Trainer culture is prevalent, and Trainers on their travels could have a Pokemon battle at any time.

Because of this, almost every town and city has multiple battle venues.

Porto Marinada Town has them too.

At this moment, Ogerpon mimicked D's stance, standing at one end of the battle arena, Dragonite pretending to be its Pokemon stood ahead.

Seeing the Trainer was ready, the boy felt his fighting spirit ignite.

He held the Poke Ball and then strongly threw it.

"Come on, my partner! The dancer in the water, Quaquaval!"

With a flash of white light, a gracefully posing duck Pokemon appeared on the battlefield, dancing.

"You go first!" the boy said, slightly curious about how this speechless girl would command her Pokemon to battle.

But the next second, he saw the Dragonite on the opposite side move.

It flapped its wings, creating a strong Hurricane sweeping towards the opposite side.

Countless fierce winds struck Quaquaval, immediately rendering it unable to battle.

??!

"Wh-what happened?" The boy couldn't react in time.

Was that Hurricane just now? What power!

Seeing the opponent defeated, Ogerpon couldn't help but let out a happy Growl, then turned around with Dragonite and Gholdengo to leave.

Now they could continue selecting a gift for D!

The boy left in place was baffled.

What on earth... is all this?

The three Pokemon returned to the street, occasionally stopping in front of glass windows gazing at the dazzling array of goods inside.

This time no one interrupted them, so the gift selection process was quite smooth.

Soon, Ogerpon selected a delicate bouquet.

The flower shop owner, a lady in a checkered apron, looked at the masked girl and gently introduced:

"This is the famous Gracidea flower!"

"Bonnie?" Hearing the flower's name, Ogerpon couldn't help but mumble.

The shop owner was slightly stunned, then looked incredulously at the masked "girl" in front of her and asked, "Are you... a Pokemon?"

Only then did Ogerpon realize it had exposed itself, quickly covering its mouth with its hand.

This gesture amused the shop owner.

"Don't worry, though I don't know why you pretend to be a human, buying this flower must be to gift it to someone very important to you, right?"

Seeing the other party had seen through its identity and seemed without malice, Ogerpon simply stopped pretending and happily nodded, "Bonnie Bonnie!"

Dragonite and Ogerpon beside it echoed in agreement.

Looking at these three Pokemon, a gentle smile appeared on the woman's face.

She could see these three Pokemon lived very happily.

"If that's the case, then give this bouquet to that person! It's called Gracidea flower, originating from the very far Sinnoh Region away from here."

"People there on birthdays or anniversaries and other important days express gratitude by making bouquets of these flowers."

The three Pokemon quietly listened.

Originally, Dragonite wasn't satisfied with the flower Ogerpon picked because the ranch also had many flowers, D might not like it.

However, upon hearing this, its original thought changed.

Giving such a flower to D couldn't be more suitable!

Gholdengo had the same thought.

Rather than flowers, Gholdengo preferred shiny golden objects.

Therefore, it initially planned to buy something of that color for D to thank him for all the Gold Coins he gave to help it evolve.

But now... this flower is perfect!

Once the gift selection was complete, it was time for payment.

Ogerpon rummaged through its clothes and handed over the bundle that its sister Indeedee had given it.

The florist reached out to receive it, initially thinking it contained berries the three Pokemon gathered, but upon opening, was almost blinded by the gold light inside.

Her face showed shock: "These, these are...?"

The three Pokemon looked at each other.

Thinking it wasn't enough money, Gholdengo quickly used Steel Type Energy to create a dozen more Gold Coins.

Witnessing this moment, the woman was almost scared to faint.

Heavens! What did she see? This Pokemon can make Gold Coins!

"No, this is too valuable..." She quickly tied the bundle and returned it.

The three Pokemon looked puzzled, completely confused.

What's going on? Can't these Gold Coins be used to buy flowers?

The woman explained, "Well, I won't charge you, the bouquet is my gift to you, these Gold Coins are too valuable, you should keep them safe!"

Upon hearing that, the three Pokemon finally understood what happened.

They glanced at each other, then happily left the dozen Gold Coins Gholdengo just made, and rushed out of the shop with the flowers.

The woman's head was bewildered, she grabbed the coins wanting to chase after them, only to see the two Pokemon already mounted Dragonite, flying away.

---

# 397: Chapter 394: Eevee Egg Hatch and Building Eevee Paradise

When D received the bouquet from the Pokemon, he was both surprised and delighted.

"For me?" he asked.

"Buneary!"

"Bonsly!"

"Wooo!"

"..."

The Pokemon in front of him nodded in unison, their faces lighting up with expectant and happy smiles.

D never imagined a day would come when a man like him would receive flowers. In his memory, bouquets were typically given to girlfriends, wives, mothers, or teachers.

D was deeply moved. He reached out and patted and hugged each of the Pokemon in front of him.

These Pokemon he raised didn't disappoint him!

D felt so happy that even his steps felt much lighter.

He hummed a little tune as he returned to the living room, getting ready to place the bouquet in the most conspicuous spot.

Although flowers may wither, the bond between him and these Pokemon would last forever.

However, just as D's gaze swept over the bouquet, information suddenly surfaced in his mind.

[Gracidea Flower: A very precious flower. When given to certain Pokemon, it can change their form. On birthdays or anniversaries, some humans also use it to express gratitude by gifting it in a bouquet.]

"Gracidea Flower?" D was slightly taken aback.

Isn't that Shaymin's favorite flower?

Shaymin, the mythical Pokemon of the Sinnoh Region.

They often live together in flower gardens, possessing magical powers that can purify toxins in the atmosphere and transform barren lands into gardens.

The legends of the Sinnoh Region speak of Shaymin's tales.

It's said that when people rejoice at the end of the harsh winter with the changing of seasons, offering gratitude to the heavens, Shaymin will appear to bloom flowers on desolate lands.

Among all the flowers, Shaymin loves the Gracidea Flower the most.

Because this flower can transform them from Land Forme to Sky Forme.

What's more concerning to D is that Shaymin is a Pokemon that migrates with the blooming of the Gracidea Flower.

So here's the question, if he plants a field of Gracidea Flowers on his ranch, would it attract wild Shaymin?

D immediately wanted to try, but unfortunately found that the current season was autumn, and with winter approaching, the entire land would soon be covered in snow and ice, unsuitable for planting.

Spring and summer are the blooming seasons for the Gracidea Flower.

If that's the case, then he'll just wait until next spring to plant them!

For now… there was another matter waiting for him.

D went upstairs, turned on his computer, and through a forum private chat, informed Raifort of Gimmighoul's evolution into Gholdengo.

Raifort quickly replied.

She first congratulated D and said she would visit to see the Pokemon if given the chance, mentioning that she is currently investigating the locations of shrines imprisoning the Treasures of Ruin and would collaborate with him in the future.

D replied with an acknowledgment, logged off, and placed a call to the town's antique shop owner, informing him about Gholdengo's birth.

This was something he had promised the owner before.

After hearing the news, the antique shop owner immediately expressed excitement and wanted to see Gholdengo.

D agreed and took Gholdengo to the antique shop while buying groceries in town in the afternoon.

"This is unbelievable… this is Gholdengo!"

The antique shop owner's eyes shone as he circled Gholdengo.

"This golden body, this dazzling brilliance, this powerful posture, this Pokemon is truly perfect!"

"Seeing Gholdengo, my life is complete!"

D was quite speechless, "Isn't that an exaggeration?"

"Not at all, not one bit!" The antique shop owner's voice was full of awe, "The last appearance of Gholdengo was a thousand years ago, and now only legends of the Pokemon remain."

"Some say it's a treasure all explorers dream of, and acquiring Gholdengo grants one a lifetime of gold."

Hearing this, D nodded slightly, as it was true—Gholdengo indeed had the ability to create gold.

Having fulfilled his promise, D bid farewell to the antique shop owner and returned to the ranch with Gholdengo.

With everything settled regarding Gholdengo, he had nothing much left to do.

Oh wait, he almost forgot something!

Calculating the time, those Eevee Eggs upstairs might be hatching soon!

To witness Eevee's hatching firsthand, D started keeping a watch on the seven Eevee Eggs from that day onward.

Time quickly passed to August 15, 199th year of the League calendar.

That afternoon, D was in the kitchen making chicken soup when the Umbreon watching over the eggs upstairs suddenly ran down the stairs, shouting:

"Vee! Vee! Vee! Vee!"

D was amused by Umbreon's silly running posture, but the next second, he realized something.

"The egg hatched?" D asked quickly.

Umbreon nodded: "Vee!"

"Sister, come watch the pot for me!" D called out, then headed upstairs with Umbreon.

By the time they reached the room, the first little Eevee had already hatched.

With dark, clear eyes, fluffy light-colored fur, and paws with pinkish pads…

Eevee was clumsily crawling out of the egg with a piece of eggshell on its head.

The newly hatched Eevee was so cute!

At that moment, D felt his heart melt.

He quickly approached, joined by Glaceon and Umbreon, to watch over the little Eevee.

Soon, the little Eevee crawled out of the eggshell, looking at the human and two Eevees with its shiny black eyes.

"Its tail has a saw-like pattern, so it's a male Eevee." D observed its tail and identified its gender.

Glaceon, seeing such a cute Eevee, immediately forgot all its previous pettiness.

It looked at Eevee with joy, softly saying, "Vee!"

Umbreon was also curious about this Eevee. Silently, it approached and sniffed out the smell on Eevee's body.

"Hello, Eevee!" D greeted it with a smile.

The little Eevee looked at him with its dark eyes, then at the two big Eevees beside it, and responded somewhat clumsily, "Vee… Vee!"

Just then, the older Indeedee arrived with milk prepared for the newly hatched Pokemon.

Having been professionally trained, they knew how to care for newly hatched Pokémon.

Seeing this, D made room and watched as the older Indeedee fed the little Eevee with a smile.

"From now on, he's your little brother!" D told the two adult Eevees beside him.

However, just as he finished speaking, the remaining six eggs followed suit with cracking sounds.

Soon, one by one, little Eevees emerged with eggshells on their heads.

The newly hatched Eevees were still quite tiny, seemingly curious about everything in the outside world.

With so many Eevees, the older Indeedee couldn't look after them alone, so D hurriedly called the younger Indeedee downstairs for more bottles of milk.

Perhaps smelling the milk, the little Eevees, having used a great deal of strength to hatch, all gathered around, hungry.

"Vee… Vee!"

Their tender cries echoed in the room.

These Eevees were calling while trying to climb onto D.

D, in a fluster, handled them, holding a bottle of milk in one hand.

Goodness, he wasn't even married yet and was going to have to become a "milk dad"!

Eventually, D sat cross-legged on the carpet, enclosing all the little Eevees in his arms, adjusting the bottles with pre-installed nipples for them to suck.

Though a bit of a hassle, holding these fluffy little Eevees brought an unexpected sense of joy! So thought D, the milk dad.

---

---

---

---

# 398: Chapter 395: The Three Eevee Brothers!

The little Eevee who had filled their bellies just curled up in D's arms and started getting drowsy.

A few of them soon fell fast asleep, their soft paw pads pressed against D's legs.

The others were also very sleepy, but didn't doze off right away. Instead, they leaned on their companions, squinting their eyes in a daze and occasionally nodding their little heads.

Looking at this pile of Eevee, D felt his heart melt.

He reached out to scratch the chins of a few Eevee and lowered his voice. "Go to sleep."

Hatching Pokemon in reality isn't quite the same as in the games. Pokemon that hatch from eggs can't battle right away, and compared to a normal Eevee, their bodies are more like a baby's.

The books say newborn Pokemon like this need plenty of sleep and nutrition to get through their infant stage.

But unlike humans, a Pokemon's infant stage is very, very short, probably only lasting about a week, after which they can grow to their normal size.

Only after all the Eevee had fallen asleep did D line them up and place them on a soft blanket.

Then D started checking the patterns on the little Eevee's tails to determine their gender.

"Male, male, male, female, male, female, female..." D muttered. "Four boy Eevee and three girl Eevee in total, girl Eevee really are rare!"

If he remembered right, the male-to-female ratio for Eevee was 7:1, so getting three females out of this clutch of eggs was already pretty good.

"Mm, I'll evolve these four boy Eevee into Flareon, Jolteon, Vaporeon, and Leafeon later."

"As for the three female Eevee, I'll evolve them into Sylveon and Espeon, and give one an Everstone to hold. That way I'll have collected all the Eeveelutions!"

Absolutely perfect!

D turned to look at the two adult Eevee at his side and said to them, "From now on, you two will be the leaders of this group of Eevee!"

"Bui?!"

Glaceon and Umbreon were stunned. They lay by the bed, looking at that nest of little Eevee with troubled expressions on their faces.

But they'd never been leaders before?

"You'll figure it out bit by bit," D said. He wanted to say more, but then his gaze fell on the Indeedee sister beside him, and a bad thought suddenly popped into his head.

Uh oh! He'd called the Indeedee sister up here—who's watching the pot downstairs?!

D hurried down to check, and saw Flutter Mane at the stove, flusteredly smacking at the cooktop with its winglike claws.

D: "..."

Good grief, the Indeedee sister found an "illiterate" to watch the pot!

He quickly stepped in to take over the kitchen, checked the chicken soup in the pot, and seeing it wasn't ruined, turned it down to a low simmer.

Seeing D come over, Flutter Mane finally let out a sigh of relief, as if a huge weight had been lifted.

*

The little Eevee slept like logs and didn't wake up until evening.

When they woke, D had the Indeedee Sibling help him feed them milk again.

After they finished drinking, the Eevee finally started curiously exploring this brand-new world.

The seven little Eevee sat obediently on the wooden floor, big dark eyes shining as they looked up at D in front of them, the Indeedee Sibling, and Pawmot and the others.

D pointed at himself and said, "I'm D."

All the Eevee: "Bui?"

"This is the little sister Indeedee, and this one is the big brother Indeedee."

The Eevee looked over at the Indeedee Sibling standing to the side.

"Ai~" The Indeedee sister greeted them warmly.

The Indeedee brother kept his composure, nodded steadily, and said, "Ai."

The Eevee blinked and let out happy growls. "Bui!"

D didn't need to introduce the rest of the Pokemon.

Because Pawmot, Alcremie, and Ogerpon were already impatiently introducing themselves.

But with so many Pokemon all at once, their round of introductions completely overwhelmed the little Eevee.

Very soon, one or two little Eevee shook their heads, crying "Bui bui" as they tried to escape this "meet-and-greet."

Influenced by them, the other Eevee followed suit and started running all over the room.

One Eevee ran under the bed, two Eevee ran under the cabinet, and a few others ran out of the room.

The hallway outside the door led to the stairs down to the first floor.

D was startled, afraid an Eevee might tumble down the stairs, and hurriedly directed the Indeedee Sibling beside him to help catch them.

As Psychic Type Pokemon, this kind of thing was easy work for the siblings.

Their eyes lit up with a blue glow as they used Telekinesis, and they quickly caught all the fleeing Eevee.

The little Eevee looked utterly bewildered.

D set them down on the blanket. Thinking that after napping all afternoon, they probably weren't sleepy at all now, he simply took them downstairs and put them on the sofa.

"Play here for a while."

"Bui!"

The next second, the little Eevee clambered up along him, crawling onto D's shoulders and head.

They seemed to feel very close to this human who was taking care of them.

Seeing this, D brought out some toys and played with them for a while.

Around eight or nine o'clock, the little Eevee seemed to have tired themselves out and started getting sleepy again.

D also happened to feel a bit tired, so he took them back to his room and put them on the bed.

"You'll all sleep here tonight."

The Eevee didn't answer. They just trembled and looked at the Koraidon beside D, looking absolutely terrified.

---

Koraidon was quite speechless: "Ah-ga-szzz..."

D couldn't help but laugh: "It's Koraidon, it's our companion, it won't hurt you."

"Bamo Bamo!"

Pawmot also nodded repeatedly, agreeing with D's words, and reassured them not to be afraid. If danger arose, it would protect them!

But the little Eevees were still very scared, and when D got into bed, they all scrambled into the covers to hide.

D: "..."

It is widely known that the covers can ward off all demons and monsters.

Seems like this is not only applicable in human society, even Pokémon understand this.

D lay on his side, gently gathered the Eevees into his arms, and said with a smile: "Sleep, sleep!"

The narrow yet warm bed made the Eevees feel very at ease, so they soon fell asleep snugly.

D watched the scene with a gentle gaze.

Young Pokémon are truly adorable!

So small, with a scent that is both fragrant and soft, and furry to the touch.

*

The Eevees' juvenile stage quickly passed, and as days went by, their bodies gradually grew, and their fur changed.

Meanwhile, Cyclizar's birthday arrived, and D brought Pokémon from the ranch to celebrate its birthday, and let it enjoy a second species strength-increasing birthday cake.

And the day after Cyclizar's birthday, the Eevees fully matured, turning into standard-looking Eevees.

For trainers, this is the time when Eevee can start learning moves and try battles.

But to D, a seven-day-old Eevee was still quite small, so he wasn't in any hurry to train them towards set evolution goals.

After growing up, the Eevees no longer sleep as long as they did when they were young.

They began having a keen curiosity about their living place and expended great energy exploring every corner of the house.

Thus, D was able to find them anywhere in the house.

Under the sofa, under the bed, inside cabinets, down Slugma's heating channel, behind the TV...

Sometimes, when he went to the toilet, he'd be shocked by a little Eevee leaping out of the bathtub.

D: "..."

If this continues, it's truly a hassle, and D was even more resolved not to let this group of Eevees out yet to play freely in the ranch.

Because he was worried that with Eevee's activity level, they'd run straight out of the ranch, and then get lost or never return due to other reasons.

Just like Pawmot did, sleeping on a rock and being carried away by Bombirdier, flying far away.

Nevertheless, when he didn't allow the Eevees to go out, they started deeper exploration of the house.

That morning, D went out, as the house was nearly out of food ingredients, so he went to town to buy some to replenish the stock.

In the living room, two gentle female Eevees were lounging on the sofa, taking a leisurely nap.

And two other Eevees were chasing and playing around the sofa.

Glaceon and Umbreon sat squatting on the floor nearby, watching the group of mischievous little ones, exchanging a glance, wordless.

"Bu-ee..." Umbreon let out a mature sigh.

It felt that when it was young, it wasn't as naughty, nor did it like squeezing into dark corners and coming out covered in dust.

Meanwhile, three lively male Eevees formed an exploration team, proceeding to explore D's "Treasure Vault."

This place was where D stored items, like those Mew and Teddiursa sent back, and some precious stones, all put here by D.

Three brave Eevees burrowed into the pile of odds and ends, digging with their heads into boxes searching.

Soon, one Eevee discovered several stones that constantly attracted its attention.

"Bu-ee!"

This little Eevee let out an exclamation, and the other two Eevees came over upon hearing, using their forelimbs to cling to the box rim, peering inside.

One Eevee used its paw to rummage through the box's contents, but accidentally slipped in, falling head over heels into the box.

"Bu-ee!"

In the scramble, this Eevee's paw inadvertently touched a piece of orange-yellow stone, and the next second, before the other two Eevees knew, it saw a bright light erupting from the Eevee.

The energy from that piece of orange-yellow stone was fully absorbed into its body.

When the glow faded, a Pokémon with fiery red fur and a large, fluffy tail appeared before the two Eevees.

"Bu-ee!"

Witnessing this scene, the two Eevees were shocked and couldn't help but let out a cry.

Upon hearing their voice, the "Eevee" sprang out of the box with a thud.

It was about to say something but soon felt its body changing.

"Bu, Bu-ee?!"

Flareon looked down at its form in a panic.

The two other Eevees looked bewildered, and one ran up and lightly patted the creamy-colored tuft of fur on Flareon's forehead.

At this point, Flareon also received relevant information from its genetic inheritance.

Oh! So that's it! It evolved into Flareon and can now use fire-breathing moves!

Realizing it had become stronger, Flareon got really excited, opening its mouth to let out an enthusiastic growl: "Bu-ee!"

The two other Eevees looked on, bewildered.

---

They learned from Flareon's words that Flareon transformed through a stone in the box to become like this?

"Eevee?" an Eevee asked uncertainly.

"Eevee!" Flareon nodded repeatedly, indicating it felt so powerful now!

Upon hearing this, the two Eevee couldn't sit still.

They both dove into the box and each picked a stone that attracted them.

And as soon as they touched the stones, the next second, two dazzling beams of light erupted from their bodies.

The two Eevee absorbed the energy from the stones, and their forms rapidly began to change.

When the light faded, a Jolteon with yellow fur and a Vaporeon with fins and gills appeared in front of Flareon.

Flareon looked at the two companions and then at itself, immediately surprised to discover that they all looked different!

"Eevee Eevee!"

The Eevee were very pleased with their transformation.

They all ran out of the room, eager to show D their new forms.

So, when D returned to the ranch from the town, carrying large and small bags, he noticed the absence of three Eevee, replaced by Flareon, Jolteon, and Vaporeon.

D: ?

What happened?

The trio of Vaporeon, Flareon, and Jolteon gathered around, excitedly recounting their miraculous adventure.

"Eevee Eevee!" (We discovered many magical stones during our exploration that transformed us into more powerful Eevee!)

D couldn't understand them initially, but Calyrex happened to be in the living room watching TV, casually translated for him, and transmitted through telepathy.

These words left D bewildered.

What exploration? Who goes on adventures in their own home?!

"You three little rascals!" D said helplessly, gritting his teeth as he poked their heads with his finger.

However, the three Eevee looked a mix of aggrieved and saddened.

"Eevee..." (Because D always forbids us from going out to play, we can only play in the room...)

On hearing this, D swallowed all the words he was about to say.

It was indeed his fault, but he was only worried about the Eevee running out of the ranch.

Even though there's Glaceon and Umbreon to watch over them, there are so many Eevee that if the two adult Eevee get distracted, there's no telling how many mischievous Eevee could escape.

Looking at the group of Eevee in front of him, D ultimately relented.

He sighed helplessly, saying, "Alright, from today onwards, you can also go outside to play on the grass like everyone else!"

"Eevee?!"

Upon hearing this, the little Eevee's eyes lit up instantly.

D nodded: "But you must remember, don't run out of the ranch, stay within the fenced area, or you'll never be able to come home if you encounter bad people."

Upon hearing this, the little Eevee felt a bit scared, but couldn't suppress their curiosity about the outside world.

So they repeatedly nodded and promised: "Eevee!"

They would never run beyond the fence!

D opened the door, accompanied by Glaceon and Umbreon, watching this group of Eevee rush out of the room.

Flareon and Jolteon ran across the grass, with Vaporeon following its instincts to the lake in the center of the ranch, diving in.

The remaining four Eevee scattered throughout the ranch.

Some were attracted by the flowers in the flower field, heading over to watch Combee collect honey from the blooms.

Others were captivated by the crops in the fields, running along the ridges.

Two Eevee lay leisurely beneath a fruit tree, enjoying the cool autumn breeze.

"Phew—" Seeing this scene, D let out a long breath of relief.

With Vaporeon, Flareon, Jolteon, Glaceon, and Umbreon already evolved, only Leafeon, Espeon, and a Sylveon were left.

Leafeon's evolution is relatively simple, requiring only a Leaf Stone or a place rich in Grass Type Energy, like near a Moss Rock.

However, Espeon and Sylveon are a bit more complex.

Espeon needs a certain level of friendship, then evolves during the day while soaking up sunshine.

Sylveon requires learning Fairy Type Moves and reaching a certain friendship level to evolve.

To prevent the remaining Eevee from evolving into other forms, D contemplated for a moment and planned to start specific training tomorrow to guide them towards evolving into Espeon and Sylveon.

---

---

---

---

# 399: Chapter 396: D's Little Classroom—Teaching the Eevee

But letting Eevee evolve requires respecting their opinions.

We'll put aside the opinions of those Eevee who have already evolved.

Even though D wants the remaining Eevee to evolve into forms not yet owned on the ranch, before taking action, he needs to ask for their opinions thoroughly.

D focused his mind and, once the Eevee had played outside and gotten tired, he gathered all the Eevee in the living room.

Then, D took out the book that recorded all Eevee attributes and introduced the potential evolution forms to the Eevee.

Nine Eevee squatted in a line on the floor, with only Glaceon and Umbreon looking neutral, while the others were full of curiosity.

"Ahem," D cleared his throat, drawing all Eevee's attention to himself.

Koraidon brought out a small blackboard, and D cut out images from the book and categorized them on it.

"Initially, I didn't plan on telling you about this now, but three mischievous Eevee accidentally ran into my Treasure Vault and touched the Evolution Stones I stored there, evolving ahead of time," D explained.

He originally planned to let them evolve when they were older, at least one or two years old.

Upon hearing this, the other Eevee glanced at the middle, where stood Flareon, Jolteon, and Vaporeon.

"Eee..."

The trio of fire, water, and lightning showed embarrassed expressions, stuck out their tongues, and tried to Charm D into letting them off the hook.

"Eee-vee~"

D sighed helplessly, then refocused on the main topic.

"Eevee, I'm talking about you." D swept his gaze over the Eevee before him: "Every Eevee has infinite evolution possibilities."

"Evolution is the process of becoming stronger, just like Flareon, Jolteon, and Vaporeon."

"Currently, humans have discovered eight Eevee evolution forms."

The Eevee listened attentively.

D tapped the pictures on the blackboard: "First, let me introduce Flareon, like this one here."

Saying this, D pointed to Flareon in the group.

"Flareon, as the name suggests, has the ability to control fire, can spit high-temperature flames from its mouth to attack, and can roast berries with fire."

"Eee!" Under the gaze of all the Eevee, Flareon nodded repeatedly, indicating that D was right.

To prove it, Flareon took a deep breath and released a small flame.

The flame hit the carpet, instantly setting the woolen carpet on fire.

D: "!!!"

"Flareon!" D felt his breath tighten.

Seeing the burning carpet, Flareon realized it had made a mistake, "Eee..."

Fortunately, Vaporeon used Water Gun in time to extinguish the flame.

Looking at the burned carpet, D blackened face issued a command to the Pokémon: "No one is allowed to use moves at home from now on!"

"Eee, Eevee!" Already guilty, Flareon quickly nodded, indicating it understood.

"Since it's your first time, and I hadn't taught this before, I'll let it go this time. But you absolutely can't do it next time, understand?"

"Eee!"

D then continued with the lesson: "I believe you all understand Flareon's abilities, next, let me introduce Vaporeon."

Unlike the lively, impulsive Flareon, the evolved Vaporeon became gentle, like flowing water.

Upon hearing its introduction, Vaporeon happily wagged its tail.

"Eevee evolves into Vaporeon, losing its fur but gaining the ability to live in water, freely control water, and flexibly use Water Gun to attack opponents. In life, it can also water berry trees and extinguish fires."

The Eevee curiously looked at Vaporeon, some seemed interested, while others showed less interest.

"Next, I'm introducing Glaceon." D turned his gaze to Glaceon.

"Glaceon, the fresh snow Pokémon, uses ice and snow powers to attack. In summer, staying near Glaceon means you don't need to worry about the heat or sunstroke! In short, Glaceon is also a reliable Eevee."

Glaceon didn't speak but nodded slightly, appreciating D's special introduction!

"Now, Jolteon, evolved through the Thunder Stone. It can use strong electricity to paralyze opponents, demonstrating great strength."

D looked at Jolteon; the Pokédex says Jolteon tends to be neurotic, making bonding difficult for Trainers.

Yet, Jolteon was quite gentle, perhaps due to D hatching it from an egg.

Jolteon even voluntarily gathered electricity, showcasing its power for the other Eevee to see more clearly.

"Lastly, Umbreon," D continued: "Umbreon, evolved under the moonlight, is a nocturnal Pokémon, most active under the moonlit late night."

---

"At the same time, that black fur on its body can also help it camouflage itself, keeping opponents from spotting it in the dark."

Umbreon let out a clear, melodious cry: "Bui～"

The blue rings on its body emitted a faint blue glow, looking incomparably mysterious.

D smiled slightly, then turned his gaze to the remaining four Eevee that hadn't evolved yet.

"Next I'm going to introduce you to Eevee evolutions that haven't appeared on the ranch yet."

Hearing this, the four Eevee all showed expectant expressions.

"Bui?" What kind of Eevee would it be?

"The first one, Leafeon." D pointed at the drawing of Leafeon on the blackboard. "It's a Grass Type Pokemon and can perform Synthesis like a flower. And precisely because of Synthesis, there is always a refreshing air and a unique grassy fragrance around Leafeon."

"Bui bui?" One little Eevee stood up and asked curiously.

Seeing this, D ate a few pieces of Translation Nuts Cake, then finally understood its question.

This little Eevee was asking: What does grassy fragrance smell like?

D thought for a moment, then asked Koraidon to call Gogoat over, and had Gogoat use Grassy Terrain.

The next second, the room was flooded with a green light.

The air also took on a very unique grassy scent.

Watching the little Eevee twitch their black noses and sniff, D said, "It's roughly this smell."

"Bui!" One little Eevee really liked it.

Another little Eevee, however, looked troubled.

Seeing this, D quickly had Gogoat withdraw the energy.

"Then next, I'm going to introduce Espeon. It's the complete opposite of Umbreon—it can absorb sunlight and convert it into Psychic Power. Not only that, Espeon is also very good at using Future Sight."

The Eevee looked at the image of the Eevee with pale purple fur, and their eyes lit up at once.

"Bui!" (Wow!)

That beautiful coat shimmered with a velvety sheen, looking extremely smooth. Not only that, there was also a very beautiful Ruby on Espeon's forehead.

So pretty!

D smiled faintly and continued, "Next I'm going to introduce Sylveon."

The Eevee shifted their gazes to the next photo.

The Eevee in that photo was a beautiful one with white and pink fur, with bows and lovely ribbons on its head.

"This is Sylveon, a Fairy Type Pokemon. Don't be fooled by how gentle and cute it looks—Sylveon's power is also very impressive. Even when facing Dragon Type Pokemon several times bigger than itself, they will leap at them without the slightest fear," D explained.

He observed the Eevee's reactions, then asked, "So, have you all already decided in your hearts what kind of Eevee you want to become in the future?"

At this point, D suddenly remembered something, so he added:

"Of course, it's fine if you don't evolve. Although an unevolved Eevee can't spit fire or water, it can develop in the direction of Partner Eevee."

"And Eevee can also use a very powerful Move—Extreme Evoboost. In short, even if it doesn't evolve, Eevee's potential is still limitless."

Extreme Evoboost, a Z-move from the Alola Region, works by calling upon the eight Pokemon that Eevee can evolve into and borrowing their power, merging the strength of Eevee's nine forms into one. It's Eevee's ultimate trump card.

In the games, this Move also raises Eevee's Attack, Defense, Special Attack, Special Defense, and Speed by two stages each.

It's just that to use this Move, the Trainer needs to wear a Z Ring and also hold Eevee's exclusive Z Crystal.

The Eevee listened very earnestly to this explanation.

They stared at the photos on the blackboard, faces full of hesitation, and for a long time couldn't make up their minds.

D wasn't in a hurry. Although he really wanted to collect every Type of Eevee, if an Eevee didn't want to evolve into a particular evolution, he couldn't very well force it to evolve...

On this matter, D chose to respect the Eevee's wishes.

However, before the remaining four little Eevee could decide, the already evolved Flareon ran up.

"Bui bui!" (D, D, I want to evolve into Partner Eevee!)

Black lines popped up on D's forehead. "You've already evolved! Eevee that have already evolved can't devolve!"

"Bui..." Flareon was so dejected.

Seeing it like this, D couldn't bear it. Thinking about it, this was partly his fault. If only he had introduced the evolution forms to the Eevee earlier, or put those Evolution Stone away properly.

But what was done was done; there was no way to change it now.

He could only comfort it: "Don't be sad, Flareon is really strong too. When winter comes you can help us make a fire and keep warm—you're also a very capable Eevee."

Flareon became very excited. "Bui bui?" (Really? Then does D like me?)

D smiled and rubbed its head. "Of course. I'll even have to ask you to help warm up my bed then!"

Flareon in winter, Glaceon in summer—practically perfect.

"Bui!" Hearing this, Flareon no longer wanted to evolve into any other Eevee; it really liked how it was now.

And at this moment, the remaining four little Eevee had also made their decisions.

"Bui!" (I like the smell of grass; I want to evolve into Leafeon!)

---

"Vee vee?" (Can Espeon really use Future Sight?) an Eevee asked.

D nodded: "That's what the book says."

The Pokedex entry for Espeon records such information.

It is said that Espeon originally had no special abilities, but it awakened its predictive power in order to protect itself.

Its entire coat of fur is extremely sensitive, using its fine, short fur to sense the flow of air, detect subtle changes in the air, and accurately predict upcoming weather and opponents' actions, and so on.

Receiving a positive response, the little Eevee happily nodded: "Vee vee paw!" (Then I want to evolve into Espeon, so I can know in advance when D is coming home!)

D raised an eyebrow and asked curiously: "Why do you want to know when I'm coming home?"

The little Eevee instinctively replied: "Vee... vee!" (So I can sneak some ice cream from the fridge without getting caught... oops! I accidentally said it out loud!)

Eevee quickly covered its mouth,

D sighed: "I only stop you from eating too much because I'm worried you'll get a stomach ache. If you get sick, it's going to be really uncomfortable."

Eevee drooled: "Vee!" (But ice cream is so tasty!)

D: "..."

Oh well, if it gets a stomachache, I can get my sister's Indeedee to use Lunar Dance, and if that doesn't work, we can visit Miss Joy at the Pokemon Center in town.

"So, what about you two?" D turned his gaze to the other two Eevee.

One of the Eevee looked at Koraidon, eyes filled with determination: "Vee!" (I want to evolve into Sylveon!)

D was curious: "Why?"

Eevee was full of fighting spirit: "Vee!" (Because Sylveon can defeat Dragon-type Pokemon!)

Upon hearing this, D understood.

Oh, so that's why it's been waiting here!

He remembered a few days ago when he took Eevee to sleep, Koraidon saw so many Eevee on the bed and wanted to jump up too, but at the time, D thought the bed was too crowded and was worried about squishing the little Eevee, so he refused Koraidon.

As a result, Koraidon got jealous, feeling that the Eevee had stolen its D, so it deliberately pulled an Eevee off the bed, making it sleep by its side.

The poor little Eevee was almost scared to death, wanting to get back on the bed, but Koraidon wouldn't let it go, so in the end, the Eevee started crying.

The crying woke D, who had to spend a lot of effort to calm it down.

Ever since, that Eevee regarded Koraidon as a villain.

D glanced at the speechless Koraidon, and couldn't help but laugh: "You can try, but Koraidon's very powerful, and not just an ordinary Dragon-type Pokemon. It's very difficult to defeat."

"Vee!" (I will work hard!)

Eevee remained full of fighting spirit.

Koraidon: "Ah gah ss..."

D then turned his gaze to the last Eevee: "And you? What kind of Eevee do you want to evolve into?"

This Eevee looked left and right, seemingly undecided.

After a while, it finally spoke: "Vee..." (It seems like only a partner Eevee…)

D: "What kind of Eevee do you want to evolve into? Besides a partner Eevee, you can evolve into other types as well, and I will help you grow in that direction."

However, this Eevee shook its head, and after a moment, as if it had made up its mind, said firmly:

"Vee!" (I want to be a partner Eevee!)

D was slightly taken aback and asked: "You don't want to evolve into another type of Eevee?"

Eevee shook its head vigorously: "Vee vee!" (No! I want to be an awesome partner Eevee!)

More importantly, it didn't want to evolve into the same-looking Eevee as others; it wanted to be the unique Eevee in D's heart and in the ranch!

So, it planned to stay as an Eevee and keep D company!

---

---

---

---

# 400: Chapter 397: Giratina and the Frozen Sky Bouquet, Blessing of the Sea

Hearing Eevee's words, D was slightly taken aback.

He hadn't expected Eevee to have such thoughts.

But on second thought, from a certain perspective, Pokemon and human offspring are hardly any different.

Just like children living in the same family, every child wishes to be unique.

And every Eevee also hopes to be different.

So, they don't want to evolve into the same Eevee as others.

It's not just this Eevee wanting to become a partner Eevee; perhaps other Eevee share this sentiment too.

D's gaze swept across the group of Eevee in front of him, and the Eevee all looked up at him.

"I understand," D exhaled softly and said with a smile, "So next, I will help you in the direction of your target Eevee, but before that, you need to wear this stone."

As he spoke, D took out the Everstone he had prepared and gave it to the few Eevee that hadn't evolved yet to wear.

The Eevee looked down at the stone on their necks, all wearing confused expressions: "Eevee?"

D explained, "This is the Everstone, and it prevents you from evolving suddenly due to environmental factors."

After all, Eevee's genes are too unstable, and there are several places on the ranch that can cause Eevee to evolve.

Like the World Tree with abundant Grass Energy and around the Gogoat, the Ice Cave for Glaceon, etc.

Inside that cave lies the treasure Ice Stone of Glaceon, and if little Eevee touches it, the ranch might soon have a second Glaceon.

Thus, the Everstone is very necessary for the Eevee.

As for the time of evolution... D felt it was too early for Pokemon to evolve just a few days after hatching.

So he planned to let these Eevee evolve when they are about one or two years old.

But before that, he had to make preparations in advance.

The evolution method for Leafeon is simple, but Espeon and Sylveon are a bit more special.

"Find a time later to teach Eevee to learn Baby-Doll Eyes," D thought to himself.

Baby-Doll Eyes, a Fairy Type move, is also one of the prerequisites for evolving into Sylveon.

After arranging things with the Eevee, D carried a bag of items and went out.

He found Dugtrio, invited Calyrex, and with the two Pokemon, came to the lake on the ranch.

After passing through the passage prepared by Giratina, the person and the two Pokemon entered the Reversed World.

In the chaotic and disorderly Reversed World, a tall and thriving towering tree stood.

That was the World Tree.

It had been more than twenty days since Calyrex last expedited the World Tree, and carefully calculating, tomorrow was the day to expedite it again.

Thinking of this, D's gaze swept over the World Tree.

In the next second, relevant information appeared in his mind.

[World Tree (Mature Stage): A seedling tree that absorbed a large amount of Power of Grass, due to the Gogoat, it mutated during growth, with vast Life Force brewing within, eventually emerging, said to support a world and nurture all things with Natural Energy, takes another 64054 years to fully grow.]

"The growth progress is already over half!" D took a deep breath, for the past few months, Calyrex would regularly come and nurture the World Tree each month.

Currently, Calyrex can shorten the World Tree's growth phase by about four thousand years each month.

At this pace...

"64054 divided by 4000... it takes about 16 more months," D calculated simply in his mind.

It's August 22nd in the League Calendar of 199, in sixteen months it will be December of 201, more than a year from now.

This speed is fast already, after all, if it were at the original speed, he might not live to see the day the World Tree matures.

The time for nurturing the World Tree is tomorrow, D came to the Reversed World today for another matter.

The outside season isn't suitable, and it's impossible to plant Gracidea flowers, so D intends to plant a batch in the Reversed World first and see.

He didn't disturb Giratina, who was sleeping among blooms, and went straight with Dugtrio and Calyrex to an undeveloped Sky Island.

The trio divided the work, with Dugtrio tilling the land, D sowing the seeds, and finally Calyrex expediting the blooming of the Gracidea flowers.

Soon, a beautiful sea of pink flowers appeared before the three of them.

Dugtrio's eyes flickered as it gazed at the scene: "Dug dug... " (So beautiful...)

Calyrex was also somewhat moved: "I too see such beautiful flowers for the first time."

"Yes." D nodded, smiling as he said, "After all, these are the magical flowers that can attract Mythical Pokemon!"

Shaymin, although lacking the presence compared to Mew, Jirachi, Celebi, Diancie, and other Mythical Beasts, is still a genuine Mythical Pokemon.

At this moment, Giratina was also drawn over, looking at the Sky Island covered with pink flowers in a deep voice:

"What are these flowers?"

"Gracidea flowers," D replied.

He looked at Giratina and suddenly thought of a scene from a movie special.

That special was called "Giratina and the Sky Warrior."

At that time, a Shaymin would accidentally enter the Reversed World and then have a thrilling encounter with Giratina.

---

Giratina felt a chill under D's ambiguous gaze, widening its eyes as it demanded, "Why are you staring at me?"

D snapped back to reality, shaking his head with a smile, "Nothing, just suddenly thought of something."

Giratina pondered, "What is it?"

D kept it vague, "Just take good care of this flower sea! You'll know when the time comes."

By then, who knows what expression Shaymin will have upon seeing this modified Reversed World.

D left with Calyrex and Dugtrio.

Leaving Giratina alone, looking bewildered at the pink flower sea before it.

It flapped its wings, stirring up a breeze.

Suddenly, countless petals were lifted by the wind, fluttering magnificently through the sky.

Giratina stared at this scene, then flew forward to sniff the flowers, discovering to its surprise, that they had quite a pleasant scent.

Then, Giratina lay down boldly upon them, and began to snore away.

*

In the evening, D was in the Pokemon Tavern counting the fruit wine in the barrels when he suddenly heard the sound of sirens outside.

He stepped out the door to see Miss Jenny passing by the ranch with a Growlithe.

"What's going on?" D asked in surprise.

Miss Jenny stopped the police car, and the Growlithe jumped down from the seat beside.

Miss Jenny followed suit, stepping out of the car and said to D:

"I'm leaving the car at your ranch for a bit, need to take Growlithe to patrol the back mountain later."

Autumn had arrived again, and to avoid another dreadful forest fire like last year, the Porto Marinada Town police station had strengthened patrols of the back mountain.

"Just leave it there, I'll keep an eye on it for you!" D squatted down, petting Growlithe's head as he asked, "Didn't the town call a forest ranger over?"

"They did." Miss Jenny replied helplessly, "But there's only one ranger, the back mountain is too big, he may not be able to manage it all, so while we're free now, we're just helping out a bit, the fire department sent some people too."

D nodded, understanding they were concerned about fire outbreaks.

"Thank you for your hard work!"

Miss Jenny smiled slightly, "It's our job, well, I'll leave you to it, we're heading out now!"

D nodded, watching Growlithe follow Miss Jenny off towards the back mountain.

Seems like the jobs of the police and firefighters in this world are quite taxing!

D shook his head, turned back to the Pokemon Tavern, filled the empty wine barrels, and brought over some berries and sandwiches.

Once guests arrive, the tavern can start operations.

After he finished that, D went home.

It was now four-thirty in the afternoon, as far as he knew, Miss Jenny would be patrolling until at least nine in the evening.

Though this season the students at Paldea Academy should be on summer vacation, right?

No, this world is different from the one he lived in his previous life, those students probably wouldn't stay home resting during summer vacation, they would surely travel to various cities, challenging gyms.

After all, the gym leaders in the Paldea Region have no holidays before November arrives.

Only during the League Conference held in the Paldea Region can those gym leaders wrap up a year's work, enjoying more than two months of vacation time.

D shook his head, though Geeta treated him well, there's no way he'd agree to become a gym leader.

In comparison, the lifestyle of a free person is much more carefree!

With that thought, D took out a cookbook titled "300 Recipes" from his bag and began studying.

This was something he bought from town in the morning, the purpose being to learn new recipes to create more delicacies for Pokemon, developing more useful dishes.

After all, with his own limited ideas, the dishes he made were quite repetitive... if the Pokemon wouldn't tire of them, he certainly would.

"Mushroom sauce... required ingredients, shiitake, button mushrooms, king oyster, bamboo shoots..."

"Spicy meat stew, ingredients are fatty meat, salad oil, minced garlic, ginger paste..."

"Wine-braised fully ripe Paldea tomato? Vodka-steamed Kalos eggplant? Paldea crab meat and ham dumplings... Paldea Seafood Rice..."

Looking at the pictures of the delicacies above, D thought they looked quite appetizing.

Mushroom juice, spicy meat soup, wine-braised fully ripe Paldea tomato, and vodka-steamed eggplant have the necessary ingredients at home.

However, the ingredients for the crab meat and ham dumplings are not available because this dish requires using raw ham slices to replace the dumpling skins to wrap the filling, which looks very extravagant.

"Never mind, I'll just try out these dishes today."

D stood up, took the ingredients from the refrigerator, and then began preparing the dishes.

As time passed, and the sky outside gradually darkened, the Pokemon who had been playing outside all day came back home, and D had prepared dinner for the day.

[Mushroom Juice (C): Prepare mushrooms like juice, make a rich soup, it's a village specialty drink, very appetizing.

Cooking Effect: Mushroom Hunter. If consumed by a Pokemon with the Pickup ability, it will become better at finding mushrooms, with a high probability of picking mushrooms back for the Trainer.]

D: "..." What is this?

It only works for Pokemon with the Pickup ability, and the only Pokemon with the Pickup ability in the ranch is Teddiursa.

However, Teddiursa is still traveling outside and hasn't returned for a long time.

But this dish gave D a new idea.

Perhaps he could raise a group of Pokemon with the Pickup ability and send them out to pick mushrooms and items, just like the Felyne expedition teams in Monster Hunter.

The Felynes hired by hunters form expedition teams to go out and help hunters gather herbs and mushrooms and bring them back.

It feels somewhat similar to the Pickup ability of Pokemon.

D continued to look at the next dish.

[Spicy Meat Soup (C+): A rich broth harmonized with meats, beef soup with chicken essence and soy sauce added, slowly simmered to fully unleash the pungency of ginger, and upon drinking, offers boundless endurance.

Cooking Effect: Sprint Lv1. After consuming, humans and Pokemon will experience reduced stamina consumption during running battles for a certain time, lasting for 15 minutes.]

Reduced stamina consumption? D raised an eyebrow; the effect of this spicy meat soup is quite useful and can be tried.

Next is the wine-braised fully ripe Paldea tomato, its effect is appetizing. As the name suggests, humans and Pokemon who have no appetite can eat it, and then they'll want to eat.

Quickly braised small tomatoes in white wine, tasting sour and sweet, the blend of tomato's tanginess and white wine's aroma indeed whets the appetite.

D decided to make this dish for whichever Pokemon wouldn't want to eat in the future.

The next one is vodka-steamed eggplant, which makes the body warm, similar to the effect of hot soup noodles.

Lastly, the Paldea Seafood Rice.

Made with prawns and mussels along with rice, it smells appetizing.

[Paldea Seafood Rice (B): A dish made with prawns and mussels, rice fully absorbs seafood broth, a bounty of the sea, a dish full of Paldea's distinctive flavor.

Cooking Effect: Sea's Blessing. When consumed by humans, it greatly increases the probability of catching rare fish while fishing; when consumed by Pokemon, it gains the blessing of the sea, greatly enhancing the effect of training in the ocean, lasting 3 hours.

Review: Paldea Seafood Rice, a distinctive dish full of the sea's bounty!]

Oh! It finally came out!

Looking at this effect, D was delighted to finally have a new dish that helps Pokemon!

And it's an all-purpose dish that works for both humans and Pokemon.

Eating it boosts the probability of catching rare fish, which is like divine assistance for anglers.

In the Pokemon World, rare fish don't just refer to ordinary fish; they can also mean fish-type Pokemon.

Ordinary fish-type Pokemon include Magikarp, Feebas, Goldeen, and Basculin, among others.

Rare fish-type Pokemon include Mudkip, Carvanha, Relicanth, and maybe even Gyarados and Milotic.

If you're feeling bold, it's not impossible to fish up Kyogre.

After all, in the anime, Lana caught a small Kyogre with a fishing rod.

And when consumed by Pokemon, it can greatly enhance the effect of ocean training.

This might not be very useful for land-based Pokemon.

But it's completely different for those Dragonairs eager to evolve into Dragonite.
